# 04a. OPNsense core issue tracker review (github.com/opnsense/core)

Research input for **sensr**, the from-scratch Rust remake of OPNsense on a NixOS base. This document reviews all 7,984 issues (no pull requests) of `opnsense/core` as of 2026-11-15 so that sensr fixes OPNsense's known pain points and includes what users asked for and never got. The companion `opnsense/plugins` tracker is covered in 04b.

**How to read this document**

- Section 1: statistics by state, label, area and year.
- Section 2: every one of the 272 currently open issues with a sensr disposition.
- Section 3: every feature request OPNsense closed as `not_planned` and every `help wanted` issue closed without completion (1,228 issues), grouped by area. Section 3b adds the help-wanted issues that the inactivity bot closed as "completed" although nothing shipped.
- Section 4: the 100 most-wanted feature requests (reactions + comments) and whether OPNsense eventually shipped them.
- Section 5: recurring bug classes in closed bugs, with sensr's architectural mitigation and the regression test to write.
- Section 6: the 40 most impactful items for sensr, with stable IDs `ISS-C-001..040`.

**Method.** Issues were fetched with the GitHub API (`fetch_issues.py`), classified by area with a title-prefix / keyword classifier (`classify.py`), clustered into bug classes (`bugclasses2.py`), and then every issue in sections 2 and 3 was read (title + body excerpt) and given a one-line disposition; the last comments of ~750 issues were fetched to detect bot timeouts and to verify shipping status for section 4. Dispositions use a fixed vocabulary:

| tag | meaning |
|---|---|
| **ADOPT** | sensr should implement this as a feature or hard requirement |
| **DESIGN-AVOIDS** | sensr's architecture prevents the problem by construction (mechanism named) |
| **N/A-LINUX** | FreeBSD / pf / PHP / OPNsense-implementation-specific; does not apply to a Linux + Rust + nftables design |
| **OUT-OF-SCOPE** | not a core firewall-distro concern (plugin territory, vendor-specific, cosmetic to OPNsense only) |
| **TEST** | a bug whose main lesson is a regression / integration test sensr must have |
| **SUPPORT** | user question or misconfiguration; where it reveals a UX/documentation gap the gap is noted |

**sensr design assumptions used for the dispositions** (so the dispositions are consistent; these are inputs to, not conclusions of, this review):

- Linux kernel, nftables (no pf/ipfw), no PHP, no `config.xml`.
- One typed, versioned, declarative config model (Rust structs; schema versions with forward migrations tested in CI against fixture configs of every prior version). Apply is transactional: validate whole config -> plan diff -> apply atomically -> roll back on failure. GUI and API are generated from the same schema.
- API-first: every GUI action is a REST/JSON call documented in OpenAPI; API keys with scopes; RBAC per endpoint and verb; audit log; CLI is a thin API client; whole-config declarative apply for IaC.
- Networking via netlink (systemd-networkd or own daemon): VLAN, bonding, bridge, PPPoE, kernel WireGuard, strongSwan (vici), OpenVPN, FRR, multi-WAN by fwmark + policy routing + per-WAN health checks, nftables flowtables, CAKE/HTB/fq_codel shaping.
- IPv6 first-class: DHCPv6-PD client with prefix-lifetime tracking, RA daemon under reconciler control, NPTv6, track-interface, dynamic prefixes in rules via nft sets.
- Kea v4/v6 primary DHCP with lease API, dnsmasq optional; Unbound with DoT/DNSSEC/overrides/blocklists.
- HA: VRRP (keepalived-style) + conntrackd + config replication over the API.
- Suricata IDS/IPS; nftables-based dual-stack captive portal; local/LDAP/RADIUS/TOTP/OIDC auth.
- Observability: structured logs (journald + remote syslog/OTLP), Prometheus metrics, NetFlow/IPFIX, per-rule counters.
- Upgrades: NixOS generations (atomic, rollback); config history git-like with remote push.


## 1. Statistics

Dataset: 7984 issues (pull requests excluded) from `opnsense/core`, fetched 2026-11-15 via the GitHub API; bodies truncated to 1200 chars.


### 1.1 By state / state_reason

| state | state_reason | count |
|---|---|---:|
| closed | completed | 6387 |
| closed | not_planned | 1297 |
| open | - | 267 |
| closed | duplicate | 28 |
| open | reopened | 5 |

Note: `completed` is over-counted; OPNsense-bot auto-timeouts (180 days inactivity) were historically closed as `completed` as well (see section 3b).


### 1.2 By label

| label | total | open | closed completed | closed not_planned | closed duplicate |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| support | 2284 | 129 | 1415 | 736 | 4 |
| help wanted | 1850 | 1 | 632 | 1217 | 0 |
| cleanup | 1246 | 27 | 1208 | 10 | 1 |
| feature | 1192 | 27 | 1141 | 24 | 0 |
| bug | 930 | 3 | 927 | 0 | 0 |
| incomplete | 700 | 26 | 418 | 240 | 16 |
| roadmap | 148 | 3 | 145 | 0 | 0 |
| upstream | 116 | 3 | 101 | 12 | 0 |
| (no label) | 1085 | 57 | 1008 | 13 | 7 |

Label semantics as used by the OPNsense team (inferred): `support` = user question/misconfiguration (closed quickly, usually not_planned); `help wanted` = 'reporter must supply more info or a PR, otherwise the bot times it out' (NOT 'we want contributors'); `incomplete` = report lacks reproduction; `cleanup` = internal refactor task; `roadmap` = team-planned major item; `upstream` = FreeBSD/third-party bug.

## 2. Every currently open issue (272)

Type and area are the reviewed values (subagent-reviewed per issue; the pre-classification was corrected where the text said otherwise). `w` = sensr importance weight 0-3. Disposition tags: **ADOPT** (implement as feature/requirement), **DESIGN-AVOIDS** (architecture prevents it), **N/A-LINUX** (FreeBSD/pf/PHP-specific), **OUT-OF-SCOPE**, **TEST** (lesson is a regression test), **SUPPORT** (user question; UX lesson noted).

Open by type: {'bug': 146, 'feature': 91, 'support': 15, 'other': 20}. Open by label: {('support',): 128, ('-',): 57, ('incomplete',): 26, ('feature',): 26, ('cleanup',): 25, ('bug',): 3, ('upstream',): 3, ('cleanup', 'roadmap'): 2, ('feature', 'roadmap'): 1, ('help wanted', 'support'): 1}.

Disposition tag mix: {'ADOPT': 136, 'DESIGN-AVOIDS': 79, 'N/A-LINUX': 24, 'SUPPORT': 13, 'TEST': 11, 'OUT-OF-SCOPE': 9}.

| [#10906](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10906) | Kea: adding an interface does not trigger a firewall reload, so its automatic DHCPv6 rules never appear | bug | support | 2026-09 | 0/9 | 3 | DESIGN-AVOIDS: single transactional apply recomputes all derived state (auto DHCP/DHCPv6 rules) from the whole config; no per-service partial reloads that miss dependents. |
| [#10783](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10783) | Firewall rules with protocol=any generate invalid pf syntax, silently aborting all filter reloads | bug | incomplete | 2026-08 | 0/2 | 3 | DESIGN-AVOIDS: nftables ruleset rendered from typed enums (Protocol::Any omits the match) and checked with nft -c in the plan step; apply never silently aborts. |
| [#10347](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10347) | Auto-kill matching states when a firewall rule is modified or disabled | feature | - | 2026-05 | 0/5 | 2 | ADOPT: optional per-rule 'flush states on change' using conntrack mark = rule id, so editing/disabling a rule can kill its states at apply time. |
| [#10627](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10627) | GUI settings mismatch with config.xml | support | support | 2026-07 | 0/3 | 2 | SUPPORT: anti-lockout on wrong interface -> DESIGN: anti-lockout derived from the GUI/SSH listen interfaces in config, shown as a visible generated rule. |
| [#10968](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10968) | Scheduled rules do not work after reboot | bug | support | 2026-10 | 0/3 | 2 | ADOPT: schedules evaluated by a timer service that (re)applies rule state at boot and on every schedule edge; integration test for reboot inside a window. |
| [#10383](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10383) | Allow Invert with multiple Destination/Source in fw rule | feature | support | 2026-06 | 0/2 | 2 | DESIGN-AVOIDS: rule src/dst accept a list of aliases with negation applied to the union (nft anonymous set / concatenated sets), not single-alias-only invert. |
| [#10621](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10621) | Problems with floating rules after upgrade from 26.1 to 26.7 | bug | support | 2026-07 | 0/2 | 2 | DESIGN-AVOIDS: one rule model with explicit ordering and migration tests; floating vs interface rules are the same struct with a scope field, so upgrades can't reorder. |
| [#10524](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10524) | Antispoof rules break firewall loading on unassigned interface | bug | support | 2026-07 | 0/1 | 2 | DESIGN-AVOIDS: nftables ruleset generated from the typed model, checked with nft -c before atomic swap; antispoof emitted only for existing interfaces |
| [#10920](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10920) | Enabling dnsmasq does not automatically generate firewall rules to allow access to port 67 | bug | support | 2026-09 | 0/1 | 2 | DESIGN-AVOIDS: enabling any listener service (DHCP/DNS/portal) emits auto-generated, visible allow rules on its interfaces via the same derived-rule mechanism. |
| [#10346](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10346) | pf generates spurious TCP RSTs due to incorrect window scale validation | bug | support | 2026-05 | 1/3 | 1 | N/A-LINUX: pf window-scale/state tracking RST bug; Linux conntrack TCP window tracking has nf_conntrack_tcp_be_liberal; expose it as an advanced toggle. |

## 3. Declined / abandoned requests (1228 issues)


This is the most valuable input for sensr: things OPNsense users asked for and the project declined, never got to, or where the reporter gave up. Selection criteria: (a) label `feature` and `state_reason = not_planned`; (b) label `help wanted` and closed with any reason other than `completed` (in practice `not_planned`, i.e. bot timeout or explicit decline). Note on OPNsense's labelling: `help wanted` on this tracker almost always means "the reporter must supply more information or a PR; otherwise the bot closes it after 180 days", so this set contains many support questions and incomplete bug reports next to genuine feature requests. Every item was read and given a disposition; support questions were kept (they are listed with `SUPPORT:` and the latent UX requirement) because they show where OPNsense's model is confusing.

Recurring declined/abandoned themes (details per area below):

1. **Auto-generated firewall rules are opaque**: users repeatedly asked to see, reorder, disable or override the hidden automatic rules (anti-lockout, DHCP, ICMPv6, bogons, NAT reflection helpers) and were declined (#7127, #7783, #7372). sensr should render every auto-rule as a visible, individually toggleable object.
2. **Multi-WAN return-path correctness** for VPN and services on a secondary WAN (WireGuard/IPsec/OpenVPN replies leaving via the default gateway, `reply-to` edge cases): #6013, #6140, #6719, #6993, #7364, #9806. sensr: conntrack-mark based reply routing independent of rule semantics.
3. **HA awareness of dependent services** (PPPoE/dialup, WireGuard, OpenVPN clients, IPsec, RA) on backup node: #7333, #6473, #8471, #9366, #9873 and the open #10936. sensr: VRRP notify hooks drive service placement.
4. **Kea gaps** after the ISC -> Kea migration: per-reservation options, client classes, out-of-pool reservations, DDNS, lease registration into Unbound (#7475/#7307 saga), domain-search per subnet.
5. **IPv6 prefix lifecycle**: PD-only WANs, /62 and odd prefix lengths, RFC 9096 deprecation on prefix change, RA and firewall following a changed prefix, DS-Lite, MAP-E/T, NAT64.
6. **Auth/API/IaC**: separate OTP login field, OIDC/SAML with group mapping, API-key creation via API, interface assignment via API, RBAC per endpoint, secret leakage in API responses, optimistic concurrency.
7. **Logging and disk safety**: flow logs filling disks, size-based retention, drop-free remote syslog, reporting load on the dataplane.
8. **Scheduled / unattended upgrades and installs**, deferred to the Business Edition (#1798, #7787).


Breakdown: feature-labelled closed not_planned = 24; help-wanted closed without completion = 1217 (of which labelled `support` 707, `incomplete` 219). Reviewed type mix: {'bug': 570, 'feature': 444, 'support': 174, 'other': 40}. Disposition tag mix: {'ADOPT': 642, 'DESIGN-AVOIDS': 240, 'TEST': 143, 'SUPPORT': 139, 'N/A-LINUX': 36, 'OUT-OF-SCOPE': 28}.


Within each area, items are sorted by sensr weight (3..0), then engagement. Format: `#N` [type, year, rx/c, labels] title -- disposition.


### 3.1 Firewall rules / pf (66)

- [#7127](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7127) [feature, 2024, 2/38, incomplete] **Allow custom Firewall rules before auto generated rule / allow auto generated rules to be configured/deleted** -- w3 ADOPT: every auto-generated rule is visible, individually disable-able and overridable; user rules may precede them (c=38). Anti-lockout opt-out explicit.
- [#7783](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7783) [feature, 2024, 7/13, -] **[Feature Request] Web GUI option to disable auto firewall rules from /usr/local/etc/inc/filter*** -- w3 ADOPT: GUI/API toggles per auto-rule family (ICMPv6, DHCP, VRRP, anti-lockout, local-out) with warnings; duplicate of #7127 (rx=7).
- [#7372](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7372) [bug, 2024, 1/15, support] **Anitlockout rule opens ports on WAN interface** -- w3 ADOPT: anti-lockout rule bound to the designated management interface (never WAN role); if LAN missing, prompt at console instead of guessing. TEST: 4-NIC fresh install.
- [#6633](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6633) [bug, 2023, 0/2, support] **Firewall rules automatically reload potential gap** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: nftables ruleset replaced atomically in one transaction (nft -f with flush inside); no window where rules are partially loaded.
- [#7266](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7266) [support, 2024, 1/17, support] **OPNSense dropping sessions** -- w2 SUPPORT: state drops from gateway flap/state reset -> UX: expose conntrack events + reason (gateway switch, reload, timeout) in log; don't flush states on reload by default.
- [#9652](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9652) [feature, 2026, 0/18, support] **[26.1] New rules UI: ability to have a floating rule on a single interface** -- w2 ADOPT: rule model allows multi-interface and single-interface rules uniformly with explicit priority ordering; no separate 'floating' concept needed
- [#6776](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6776) [bug, 2023, 3/11, support] **Interface:"Block private networks" and "Block bogon networks" does not work properly with ipv6.** -- w2 ADOPT: bogon v6 set must not include fc00::/7 unless 'block private' checked; keep bogon/private sets separate and consistent v4/v6; unit test set contents
- [#6676](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6676) [bug, 2023, 0/12, support] **WAN address does not mean a singular address** -- w2 ADOPT: distinct rule address macros: 'WAN primary address' vs 'all WAN addresses (incl. VIPs)'; document semantics in schema field help
- [#7817](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7817) [feature, 2024, 6/6, -] **[Feature Request] Bulk Copy Firewall Rules To Interface** -- w2 ADOPT: bulk select rules and copy/move to another interface (API: batch clone with interface rewrite); rx=6
- [#7139](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7139) [bug, 2024, 0/10, support] **Interface 'Block bogon networks' Blocking Legitimate ipv6 ICMP Traffic** -- w2 TEST: bogon/private-network block rules are family-specific (inet6 set never matches ICMPv6/link-local); unit test rule generation for fe80::/10 exemption
- [#8040](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8040) [feature, 2024, 0/10, support] **Set TCP flags to "Any flags" by default on allow-rules for "any" protocol** -- w2 ADOPT: default rule matches any TCP flags (ct state new) rather than SYN-only; TCP flag match is an explicit advanced option.
- [#6502](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6502) [feature, 2023, 1/8, support] **Allow custom Firewall rules before auto generated rules** -- w2 ADOPT: user rules can be placed before auto-generated rules (explicit 'pre-system' chain position); auto rules visible and individually overridable.
- [#5543](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5543) [feature, 2022, 1/7, support] **Is there a way to manually adjust state timeouts?** -- w2 ADOPT: expose per-protocol conntrack timeouts (nf_conntrack_tcp_timeout_established etc.) and per-rule ct timeout policies in advanced rule options.
- [#7724](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7724) [bug, 2024, 0/7, support] **wrong matching shown in firewall state table** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: state->rule attribution uses ct mark = rule UUID index set at rule match, not positional rule numbers that shift on reload.
- [#8330](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8330) [feature, 2025, 2/5, support] **Specify Multiple Non-Contigous Ports Per Firewall Rule** -- w2 ADOPT: port fields accept lists/ranges inline (80,443,8000-8100) compiled to anonymous nftables sets; no alias needed (rx=2).

- [#6858](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6858) [feature, 2023, 3/2, -] **mass edit in firewall rules** -- w2 ADOPT: bulk edit of selected rules (enable/disable/log/category/gateway/interface) via batch PATCH API; GUI multi-select (rx=3).
- [#8444](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8444) [bug, 2025, 0/5, support] **There were error(s) loading the rules: pfctl: DIOCADDRULENV: Device busy** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: single serialized nftables apply path (one applier task, netlink transaction); concurrent apply requests queue, never race on the kernel.
- [#9966](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9966) [feature, 2026, 0/5, feature] **Firewall Rules [new] Wireguard group rules not showing in Inspect** -- w2 ADOPT: rule inspect view for an interface shows effective rules including group/global rules in evaluation order, with source grouping
- [#5621](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5621) [feature, 2022, 0/4, incomplete] **Feature Request: multiple source / destination / service options per Firewall Rule** -- w2 ADOPT: rule source/destination/service accept multiple entries (list fields) compiled to anonymous nftables sets; no need for single-use aliases.
- [#6116](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6116) [feature, 2022, 2/2, incomplete] **Warn if firewall rule has IP with non-zero bits after subnet mask** -- w2 ADOPT: validation warning/error when host bits set in CIDR (e.g. 2001:db8::1/64) in rules/aliases; offer normalize (rx=2).
- [#9360](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9360) [feature, 2025, 0/4, support] **Automatically Generated Rules unflexible, please consider improvement** -- w2 ADOPT: auto-generated rules (default deny, self-out) have per-rule log toggle and are visible/orderable relative to user rules in the ruleset view

- [#9592](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9592) [feature, 2026, 0/4, support] **Automatically create reject-all sister rules for firewall rules with schedules to make stateful cutoff working properly** -- w2 ADOPT: scheduled rules kill matching conntrack entries when schedule ends (ct kill by rule mark), configurable per rule; no manual sister rules.

- [#9338](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9338) [bug, 2025, 0/3, incomplete] **WAN Gateway Unreachable with "Block bogon networks" Enabled After Upgrade to 25.7.6** -- w2 TEST: bogon blocking must never block the interface's own gateway/link subnet; test rule generation with gateway inside a bogon range (e.g. CGNAT 100.64/10).
- [#5867](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5867) [feature, 2022, 0/2, incomplete] **Firewall rid retention (non match altering rule changes)** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: rules have stable UUIDs in config model; log entries carry UUID, which survives any edit including log/description changes.
- [#6024](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6024) [feature, 2022, 0/2, incomplete] **Feature request:  Show group membership of interfaces in rules section** -- w2 ADOPT: rule view per interface shows which interface groups it belongs to and merges/greys inherited group+floating rules in evaluation order.
- [#7169](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7169) [feature, 2024, 0/2, incomplete] **Firewall source, destination, redirect target drop down list DHCP hosts addition** -- w2 ADOPT: rule editor source/dest picker offers DHCP reservations and leases (resolved to dynamic nftables set elements)
- [#7967](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7967) [support, 2024, 0/2, incomplete] **Create default, usually disabled rule for outside access** -- w2 SUPPORT: how to allow WAN GUI access -> UX: admin access policy page (interfaces/allowed sources) generating the rules; no port-forward hack.

- [#10065](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10065) [bug, 2026, 0/2, support] **Alias and interface group with same name generates invalid pf rules** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: alias, interface-group and interface names live in one namespace with uniqueness validation in the config model; generated nftables uses prefixed set names.

- [#8078](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8078) [bug, 2024, 0/1, support] **Failure to add floating rules to new VLAN or group** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: floating rules compile to an nftables chain evaluated for all interfaces; new VLANs/groups inherit automatically. - [#9568](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9568) [feature, 2025, 0/1, support] **Firewall rule simulator: GUI tool to test which rule(s) match a synthetic packet (by interface, src/dst IP and port, pro** -- w2 ADOPT: rule simulator API: given iif/src/dst/proto/port, evaluate the compiled ruleset (nft --check or own evaluator) and report matching rule; GUI tool

- [#9645](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9645) [feature, 2026, 0/4, support] **option to only show interface groups in firewall rules** -- w1 ADOPT: firewall rule view filter/toggle to show only interface groups or hide member interfaces; sidebar grouping for M*N VLAN setups.

- [#7338](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7338) [bug, 2024, 0/3, support] **Normalization broken (ignoring the port)** -- w1 ADOPT: normalization/TOS/MSS rules honor all match fields incl. ports (nftables mangle chain generated from same rule matcher as filter).

### 3.2 NAT (31)

- [#6650](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6650) [bug, 2023, 0/74, support] **NAT reflection is broken** -- w3 ADOPT: NAT reflection must work out of the box on clean install (hairpin via DNAT+masquerade rules); e2e test across LAN/VLAN clients (c=74).
- [#10121](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10121) [bug, 2026, 1/26, support] **After the fix of #9702 bug, Some DNAT rules start to work but others stop working (bug?/bugs?)** -- w3 TEST: DNAT regression suite covering port-forward variants (interface address, VIP, alias dest, reflection, port ranges) run on every change (c=26).
- [#5941](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5941) [bug, 2022, 0/9, support] **NAT reflection for port forward rules not created, manual rules don't work.** -- w3 ADOPT: NAT reflection (hairpin) per port forward: DNAT in prerouting + SNAT to interface address for same-subnet clients, generated automatically; e2e test.
- [#6530](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6530) [bug, 2023, 0/8, support] **Outgoing IPv6 NAT uses wrong address after WAN gets new address from DHCPv6** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: outbound NAT uses nftables 'masquerade' or interface-address sets updated from netlink addr events, never a frozen literal address; TEST WAN v6 change
- [#7448](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7448) [bug, 2024, 0/5, support] **Port Forward reply to not getting set correctly** -- w3 ADOPT: DNAT from non-default-route interface (VPN) marks conntrack so replies route back via ingress interface (ct mark + ip rule), independent of policy routing.
- [#9763](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9763) [bug, 2026, 2/5, support] **Destination NAT: Reflection Fails If Redirect Target Is Alias** -- w2 TEST: NAT reflection with alias redirect target (set-based DNAT map) must generate hairpin SNAT; add to DNAT regression suite (rx=2).
- [#6021](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6021) [bug, 2022, 0/5, support] **Port forwards apply to all interfaces if destination=any** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: DNAT rules always scoped to the configured ingress interface (iifname); 'destination any' cannot capture other interfaces' traffic; test.
- [#7022](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7022) [bug, 2023, 0/4, support] **Nat reflection not working as it should** -- w2 TEST: NAT reflection with multiple VLANs and reverse proxy target; include in DNAT regression suite (#10121).
- [#8371](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8371) [bug, 2025, 0/3, support] **NPTv6 does not work with Track Interface** -- w2 ADOPT: NPTv6 internal/external prefix may reference a tracked interface (prefix resolved at runtime, nftables set); TEST with PD rotation
- [#8798](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8798) [bug, 2025, 0/3, support] **Syntax error after appyling label** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: nftables rules generated from typed AST with quoting; labels/descriptions never interpolated raw. Fuzz test descriptions with spaces/quotes.
- [#6444](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6444) [bug, 2023, 0/2, incomplete] **NAT port forward Rules Require Manual Sates Reset to function** -- w2 ADOPT: on DNAT rule add/change, offer/auto-flush conntrack entries matching the new rule's tuple so existing states don't bypass it.
- [#8213](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8213) [bug, 2025, 0/2, incomplete] **1:1 NAT used with ALIAS - issue on modification** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: aliases referenced by UUID everywhere (1:1 NAT included); renaming never breaks references.
- [#8265](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8265) [feature, 2025, 0/2, incomplete] **Port-Forward listen all interfaces** -- w2 ADOPT: DNAT rule interface selector allows 'any' (nftables prerouting without iifname match) so new VLANs need no group update
- [#8779](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8779) [bug, 2025, 0/2, incomplete] **Inconsistent NAT Port Forward and Firewall Rule Generation Causes Silent Failure Until Rule Recreation on OPNsense** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: associated filter rule derived from NAT object at render time (not a stored copy), so it can never drift; test edit paths.
- [#9851](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9851) [bug, 2026, 0/2, incomplete] **NAT Reflection Bug** -- w2 ADOPT: reflection SNAT matches any internal source network reaching the DNAT target (ct status dnat + oifname), not only the exit interface's subnet.
- [#10023](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10023) [bug, 2026, 0/2, incomplete] **Bug Report: Hybrid Outbound NAT rule with `protocol=any` and `interface-address` translation does not persist across reb** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: ruleset rendered identically at boot and on apply from the same config (one renderer, no boot-time special path); boot-render test.
- [#10265](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10265) [bug, 2026, 0/2, support] **NAT: Predefined ports overwrite alias with same name** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: port fields are typed enum {well-known-port / alias-ref(UUID) / literal}; names never collide or silently rewrite.
- [#6512](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6512) [support, 2023, 0/10, support] **Wrong Destination MAC when using NAT in conjunction with Virtual IP** -- w1 SUPPORT: off-subnet VIP + NAT to modem status IP needs SNAT -> UX: 'access ISP modem on WAN' preset generating VIP + outbound NAT.
- [#7671](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7671) [feature, 2024, 1/5, support] **Add a Separator to NAT list page and rule page** -- w1 ADOPT: separators/sections in NAT rule grids (same grouping feature as filter rules).
- [#6242](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6242) [feature, 2023, 0/4, incomplete] **Ability to schedule NAT rules** -- w1 ADOPT: schedules attachable to NAT rules (same schedule object; timer-driven nftables rule toggle).
- [#8788](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8788) [bug, 2025, 0/4, support] **Multiple BINAT's with same Internal not generating pf rules** -- w1 ADOPT: multiple 1:1 NAT mappings to the same internal IP allowed (DNAT per external; SNAT picks primary/explicit); test N-external->1-internal.
- [#9190](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9190) [support, 2025, 0/4, support] **Aliaes not working in NAT port forward** -- w1 SUPPORT: alias in port forward not matching (type/refresh) -> UX: NAT rule view shows resolved alias contents + match tracer.
- [#6057](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6057) [bug, 2022, 0/3, -] **Port Forwarding Rules, "Add associated filter rule" no longer an option after rule is created** -- w1 ADOPT: port forward's associated filter rule link is editable any time (create/unlink/relink) via a reference field in the NAT object.
- [#8070](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8070) [support, 2024, 0/3, support] **Regression in opnsense NAT/NAT-PMP with using tailscale on client devices behind opnsense router with CGNAT WAN** -- w1 SUPPORT: NAT-PMP/Tailscale behind CGNAT -> UX: miniupnpd/NAT-PMP reports external address correctly even if WAN is private; document CGNAT limitations
- [#8345](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8345) [bug, 2025, 0/3, support] **Loopback interface is gone in NAT** -- w1 ADOPT: loopback 'lo' selectable in DNAT (output-chain redirect for locally generated traffic, e.g. DNS to alt port).
- [#5963](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5963) [feature, 2022, 0/2, incomplete] **Outbound NAT IPv4+IPv6 address family option missing** -- w1 ADOPT: outbound NAT rule address family 'inet+inet6' emitting both nftables families (single rule, dual-stack)
- [#6735](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6735) [feature, 2023, 0/1, support] **Firewall/NAT/one-to-one GUI form / Aliases for the field "External Network"** -- w1 ADOPT: 1:1 NAT external/internal fields accept aliases (nftables map for binat); same address picker component everywhere.
- [#7345](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7345) [bug, 2024, 0/1, support] **port forward on port 80 overrides proxy rules on port 443 e.g. relayd** -- w1 TEST: DNAT on port 80 must not affect locally-terminated 443 (reverse proxy on firewall); test port-forward + local listener coexistence.
- [#7450](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7450) [support, 2024, 0/1, support] **Port NAT via OpenVPN: normal fw rule is not hit while floating rule works fine** -- w1 SUPPORT: DNAT via VPN provider needs matching inbound rule on tun interface and reply-to; -> UX: DNAT rule auto-creates linked filter rule on the same interface
- [#7809](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7809) [bug, 2024, 0/1, support] **NAT Port Forward "Redirect Target IP" does not allow using built in interface addresses** -- w1 ADOPT: redirect target field accepts interface-address tokens (LAN address, VIPs) resolved to dynamic sets; same address type everywhere.
- [#9701](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9701) [feature, 2026, 0/1, -] **Feature-Request: Quality-of-life improvement - Set Static-Port NAT also for 3478 (STUN)** -- w1 ADOPT: outbound NAT 'static port' quick preset for UDP 3478/5349 (STUN/TURN) and SIP; documented in NAT help.

### 3.3 Aliases / tables / GeoIP (53)

- [#10324](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10324) [bug, 2026, 0/18, support] **cron "Update and reload firewall aliases" not work for URL** -- w2 ADOPT: URL-table refresh schedule as cron expression per alias (exact hour), and 'refresh now' API; cron job calls the same API as GUI.
- [#7157](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7157) [bug, 2024, 0/11, support] **host alias resolve doesnt work for a specific host with CNAME response** -- w2 TEST: host alias resolver must follow CNAME chains to A/AAAA (use proper resolver lib, not getaddrinfo quirks); test alias with CNAME target.
- [#5995](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5995) [feature, 2022, 0/10, support] **Firewall: Alias: Allow adding descriptions to every entry** -- w2 ADOPT: alias entries are objects {value, description} in schema; grid shows description per entry (c=10).
- [#9013](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9013) [feature, 2025, 5/5, incomplete] **Find all rules to an Alias** -- w2 ADOPT: alias grid column 'used by N rules' with drill-down list of referencing rules/NAT/aliases; reference index maintained in config model (rx=5).
- [#6619](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6619) [feature, 2023, 2/7, support] **Feature request: proper administration of aliases** -- w2 ADOPT: alias management shows usage/references (rules, NAT, other aliases), unused-alias filter, per-entry description/comments, bulk edit.
- [#7481](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7481) [feature, 2024, 1/7, support] **[Feature] Global Aliases** -- w2 ADOPT: one global alias object type referenced everywhere (rules, NAT, Unbound ACLs, IPsec, captive portal); no per-feature duplicate lists.
- [#9105](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9105) [feature, 2025, 1/7, support] **Alias in OpenVPN Server Local Network field (and any other field where an IP adress or range can be put in)** -- w2 ADOPT: network-list fields across VPN schemas accept alias references (expanded at render time); schema-level 'NetworkOrAlias' type; rx=1
- [#8063](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8063) [feature, 2024, 3/4, -] **Add an option to block private/random MAC addresses** -- w2 ADOPT: MAC alias supports prefix masks (e.g. x2/x6/xA/xE locally-administered bit) via nftables ether saddr & mask to block randomized MACs; rx=3
- [#9438](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9438) [feature, 2025, 2/5, support] **Could you please add .trim() to the FW alias network/host tokenizer?** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: alias content parser trims/normalizes whitespace and newlines before validation; same normalizer used by GUI and API
- [#9841](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9841) [feature, 2026, 3/3, incomplete] **Please add default alias RFC1918** -- w2 ADOPT: built-in read-only aliases: rfc1918, rfc4193 ULA, link-local, bogons, cgnat 100.64/10, multicast
- [#6404](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6404) [feature, 2023, 0/5, incomplete] **[SOLVED] firewall alias not updating in a timely manner** -- w2 ADOPT: API endpoint to force-refresh a single URL-table alias on demand, plus refresh interval in seconds/minutes (not fractional hours).
- [#8067](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8067) [feature, 2024, 2/3, -] **Option to put static-dhcp mapped clients into alias.** -- w2 ADOPT: reservation field 'member of aliases[]' so static clients auto-populate firewall aliases/nftables sets
- [#6176](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6176) [bug, 2022, 0/4, support] **URL table aliases "Loaded#" can mislead** -- w2 ADOPT: alias status shows two distinct numbers: entries parsed from source and entries actually loaded in nftables set, plus last-apply error.
- [#6762](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6762) [bug, 2023, 0/4, incomplete] **Hostsname on Aliases not updated** -- w2 TEST: host alias re-resolution must update nftables set on TTL expiry; integration test with DNS record change propagates within TTL.
- [#8299](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8299) [feature, 2025, 3/1, -] **Tags for dynamic IPv6 prefixes** -- w2 ADOPT: dynamic-v6-host aliases (prefix-from-interface + host suffix) usable in every address field (NAT, overrides, gateways) via a typed AddrRef union; same as #9297
- [#9180](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9180) [feature, 2025, 0/4, -] **FeatureRequest [core] - linking DHCP Reservations to Firewall Aliases** -- w2 ADOPT: link Kea reservations to firewall aliases (reservation 'aliases[]' field or alias type 'dhcp-reservations'); same as #8067
- [#10066](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10066) [bug, 2026, 0/4, support] **URL Table (IP) aliases may refresh successfully but later drift to smaller pf runtime tables than their source** -- w2 TEST: URL-table alias loaded set element count must equal parsed source count after refresh and after rule reload; integration test with 50k-entry list.
- [#6264](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6264) [feature, 2023, 0/3, -] **Support other GeoIP sources** -- w2 ADOPT: pluggable GeoIP sources: MaxMind, IPdeny/db-ip free CSV feeds, custom URL per country mapping; no mandatory account.
- [#5890](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5890) [feature, 2022, 0/2, incomplete] **URL Table alias update % criteria** -- w2 ADOPT: URL-table alias refresh guardrail: reject/keep-last-good when new fetch shrinks below N% of previous or is empty; alert via log/metric.
- [#7395](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7395) [feature, 2024, 1/1, support] **Alias for all my Networks** -- w2 ADOPT: built-in dynamic alias 'all local networks' (every configured interface subnet incl. tracked IPv6 prefixes) auto-updated as interfaces change.
- [#8159](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8159) [bug, 2024, 0/2, support] **Firewall --> Aliases --> Hosts does not follow CNAMEs** -- w2 TEST: FQDN alias resolution follows CNAMEs (deb.debian.org -> fastly) and loads all A/AAAA; regression test with CNAME-only name.
- [#9297](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9297) [feature, 2025, 1/1, -] **Find a better way to integrate dynamic IPv6 aliases into the settings** -- w2 ADOPT: first-class 'dynamic v6 host' address type (interface prefix + suffix) across rules/NAT/overrides without separate alias objects; same as #8299
- [#9308](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9308) [feature, 2025, 1/1, support] **System-wide Alias Usage (e.g., in Routes and WireGuard Endpoints)** -- w2 ADOPT: alias references accepted system-wide (static routes, WG endpoints/AllowedIPs, IPsec TS) via shared NetworkOrAlias schema type with dynamic re-render
- [#7693](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7693) [feature, 2024, 0/1, support] **Auto create Alias from Wireguard?** -- w2 ADOPT: auto-provided dynamic aliases per WG instance/peer (tunnel address, AllowedIPs) usable in rules without manual duplication
- [#9577](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9577) [support, 2026, 0/21, support] **Sporadic failures of firewall rules** -- w1 SUPPORT: alias IP 'breached' (CIDR/refresh/ordering confusion) -> UX: per-log-entry 'which rule/set matched' and alias element lookup (c=21).
- [#8391](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8391) [feature, 2025, 2/9, -] **Spamhaus ASN Drop list - json format** -- w1 ADOPT: URL alias type for ASN lists with pluggable parser (JSON/plain), ASN->prefix resolution via RIPEstat/bgpq, refresh + per-field extraction.
- [#6788](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6788) [feature, 2023, 0/9, support] **Increase Aliases/Grouping to handle more complex networks.** -- w1 ADOPT: alias names up to 64+ chars with '.', '-' allowed (nftables set names are free-form); schema validation shows allowed charset.
- [#8219](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8219) [feature, 2025, 0/7, support] **[Feature Request] Bogons currently exclude parts of RFC1918 and they (maybe) should not OR have an available RFC1918 ali** -- w1 ADOPT: ship built-in aliases for RFC1918, RFC6598 CGNAT, ULA, link-local, bogons (documented contents) so users don't hand-maintain them.
- [#10114](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10114) [bug, 2026, 0/6, support] **[Firewall:Aliases] MAC-type alias is pulling large inventory of past IP addresses** -- w1 ADOPT: MAC alias resolves from live neighbor tables (ARP/NDP) and current DHCP leases only, with TTL-based expiry of stale IPv6 addresses.
- [#6570](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6570) [support, 2023, 0/5, support] **OPNsense BGP Alias lists show empty [SSL: CERTIFICATE_VERIFY_FAILED]** -- w1 SUPPORT: BGP/ASN alias feed TLS cert verify failure -> UX: alias fetch errors surfaced in GUI with cause (CA, proxy), per-alias opt-in CA bundle.
- [#9035](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9035) [support, 2025, 0/5, incomplete] **Drop lists don't work** -- w1 SUPPORT: DROP list not blocking (rule order/direction) -> UX: built-in 'threat feed' alias preset (Spamhaus DROP) with one-click block rule + verification tool.
- [#5918](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5918) [support, 2022, 0/4, support] **Aliases URL Tables (IPs) does not work correctly** -- w1 SUPPORT: URL-table alias content vs loaded set mismatch -> UX: diagnostics view shows fetch status, parse errors, and live nftables set contents per alias.
- [#7056](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7056) [support, 2023, 0/4, support] **Feature: Firewall rules based on domain name instead IP** -- w1 SUPPORT: FQDN-based rules exist as host aliases -> UX: rule editor destination field accepts FQDN inline and auto-creates a resolved set; doc it.
- [#6174](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6174) [bug, 2022, 0/3, incomplete] **URL table aliases do not correctly resolve URLs with sub-domains...** -- w1 TEST: URL-table alias URL validation must accept multi-label hostnames; regression test fetch of http://a.b.c.d.tld/list.txt is actually requested.
- [#6136](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6136) [bug, 2022, 0/2, incomplete] **Alias "Maintain a set of table entry counters" implementation** -- w1 TEST: per-entry counters must work for an alias included inside another alias; test nested alias with counters enabled on inner member.
- [#6237](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6237) [feature, 2023, 0/2, incomplete] **Packets and bytes counters totals** -- w1 ADOPT: alias diagnostics grid supports totals row for packets/bytes (computed server-side from per-element nftables counters).
- [#6327](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6327) [bug, 2023, 0/2, incomplete] **FIREWALL: ALIASES - Last updated (URL Table)** -- w1 ADOPT: URL-table alias tracks last_success and last_attempt separately with last error message.
- [#7102](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7102) [feature, 2023, 0/2, incomplete] **Sorting alias entries** -- w1 ADOPT: alias entry editor with numeric sort button and drag/drop ordering; entries are an ordered list in schema.
- [#7541](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7541) [bug, 2024, 0/2, support] **Comma seperated IP blocks copy paste as Alias return Invalid range error** -- w1 ADOPT: alias bulk-paste parser accepts comma/space/newline separated entries with clear per-entry validation errors (same validator in GUI and API).
- [#9267](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9267) [bug, 2025, 0/2, support] **Alias with CIDR Ranges Not Showing # Loaded** -- w1 ADOPT: alias status 'loaded' count must count CIDR elements as loaded (nftables set with interval flag); diagnostics show set elements verbatim.
- [#9457](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9457) [support, 2025, 0/2, support] **The firewall passes packets from a country that is included in an alias used in a blocking rule** -- w1 SUPPORT: GeoIP alias miss (db staleness/other rule) -> UX: GeoIP db version/date shown, per-IP country lookup tool, rule-match tracer.
- [#9576](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9576) [feature, 2026, 0/2, support] **Improve Geoip csv db download** -- w1 ADOPT: GeoIP setup with guided provider presets (MaxMind key, free CSV feeds), test-download button and clear error messages; docs generated from schema.
- [#9783](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9783) [feature, 2026, 1/1, support] **Alias number of time it is used in firewall rules** -- w1 ADOPT: alias usage count column plus 'show referencing rules' modal (duplicate of #9013 requirement).
- [#9882](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9882) [bug, 2026, 0/2, support] **Alias network input field does not strip trailing whitespace, causing valid CIDR entries to fail validation** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: schema-level normalization trims whitespace before validating CIDR/IP strings in both GUI and API (single validator).
- [#5820](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5820) [bug, 2022, 0/1, support] **Aliases panel showing wrong unique IP entries when using ipsets.** -- w1 TEST: alias diagnostics must show per-alias member sets correctly when one alias nests another (nftables set-in-set/concat); regression test for nested alias counts.
- [#6548](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6548) [feature, 2023, 0/1, -] **Bug:  Firewall Aliases Don't Allow Host Exclusions When Using Qualified Domain Names** -- w1 ADOPT: nested aliases with exclusion (NOT) operator for host/FQDN aliases too: resolved FQDN sets subtracted at set-build time before nftables load.
- [#8419](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8419) [feature, 2025, 0/1, -] **Trim IPs / Network Ranges when copy/pasting** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: schema-level trimming/normalization of IP/CIDR inputs (same as #9882).
- [#9417](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9417) [support, 2025, 0/1, support] **url table aliastable is blocking an innocent IP** -- w1 SUPPORT: IP blocked though absent from list (CIDR containment) -> UX: 'which set element matched this IP' lookup in alias diagnostics (nft get element).
- [#9469](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9469) [feature, 2025, 0/1, support] **Could you please re-arrange the alias content box controls from underneath it to above it?** -- w1 ADOPT: alias editor toolbar (text/token mode toggle, import, count) pinned above content with virtualized list for large aliases
- [#9498](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9498) [feature, 2025, 0/1, support] **Indicate whether the alias is used in any rule or not** -- w1 ADOPT: alias grid shows in-use indicator and unused filter (same reference index as #9013).
- [#9562](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9562) [bug, 2025, 0/1, support] **The list of countries in the GeoIP alias dialog does not include some of those that the API returns** -- w1 ADOPT: GeoIP country list derived from the loaded database's actual country codes (incl. XK Kosovo), not a hardcoded ISO list.
- [#6219](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6219) [support, 2022, 0/2, support] **Are alias table entry and rule statistics counters written to disk or kept only in RAM?** -- w0 SUPPORT: counters live in kernel/RAM -> UX: document that nftables counters are RAM-only and that no periodic disk writes occur (flash-friendly).
- [#8050](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8050) [other, 2024, 0/1, -] **[Feature Request] Add port information for STUNS/TURNS 5349** -- w0 ADOPT: well-known port list shows protocol hint (TCP/UDP) for each entry incl. 5349 STUNS/TURNS; keep list data-driven from IANA.

### 3.4 Interfaces (VLAN/LAGG/bridge/PPPoE/wireless) (68)

- [#9398](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9398) [bug, 2025, 3/23, support] **Wrong / confusing MSS clamping behaviour with PPPoE MTU 1492** -- w3 ADOPT: MSS clamping per interface derives from MTU for v4 (MTU-40) and v6 (MTU-60) separately; nftables tcp option maxseg set rt mtu; test PPPoE 1492 v6
- [#6134](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6134) [bug, 2022, 0/18, support] **Interface not brought down / Routes not removed when disabling interface in Web UI** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: declarative networkd state: disabling an interface removes addresses/routes/rules in the apply diff; test disable -> no routes remain (c=18).
- [#9225](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9225) [bug, 2025, 0/7, support] **Regression in 25.7.2: PPPoE Path MTU Discovery broken – TCP needs MSS clamp, UDP impacted** -- w3 ADOPT: PPPoE MTU 1492 propagates to route MTU + auto MSS clamp (nftables tcp option maxseg set rt mtu); TEST: PMTUD ICMP not dropped, UDP >1472 fragments ok
- [#6816](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6816) [feature, 2023, 0/2, support] **Don't restart OpenVPN connections etc when DHCP WAN IP address is the same** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: DHCP renew with unchanged address is a no-op diff in the planner; dependent services restart only when address/gateway actually changes
- [#9914](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9914) [bug, 2026, 0/2, incomplete] **Changing WAN from DHCP to Static does not work** -- w3 TEST: interface addressing mode change DHCP->static tears down client, flushes dynamic addr/routes and applies static+gateway in one transaction
- [#8773](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8773) [bug, 2025, 0/1, -] **Web UI Allows Firewall Group to be Deleted when Rules are Defined for the Group** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: schema references are typed; deleting an interface group referenced by rules (incl. rules ON the group) fails validation with a dependency list.
- [#8110](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8110) [bug, 2024, 0/19, support] **Disabled interface sets IPv6 addresses automatically** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: unassigned netdevs kept administratively down with accept_ra=0/autoconf=0 (sysctl per netdev) until assigned; TEST no addresses on unassigned ports
- [#8838](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8838) [bug, 2025, 3/9, -] **Can't disable IPv6 on interface** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: disabling v6 on an interface validates dependents (RA, DHCPv6, NPTv6) with clear referential error or cascade; no hidden ISC leftovers
- [#7068](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7068) [bug, 2023, 0/11, support] **WAN does not initialize correctly after hard reboot due to power loss while behind ISP modem.** -- w2 ADOPT: DHCP WAN client must keep retrying and re-run gateway/route setup when upstream modem comes up late; test boot-before-modem scenario
- [#8914](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8914) [bug, 2025, 0/9, support] **MTU on interface not reset to default value when field is reset/cleared** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: clearing MTU reverts to default on apply because planner diffs desired vs actual kernel state and resets removed attributes; test MTU clear
- [#8017](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8017) [feature, 2024, 0/7, -] **Allow changing description string in Interfaces > Assignments** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: interface description is a mutable field; references use stable IDs so renaming/relabeling never requires delete+recreate
- [#9959](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9959) [bug, 2026, 0/6, -] **If you had ISC DHCP Enabled on a interface and remove ISC after deprecation, you can not change a interface previously c** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: removing a DHCP backend migrates/deletes its config via versioned schema migration; no dangling references block interface changes
- [#7144](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7144) [bug, 2024, 0/5, support] **Firewall group list does not display group membership and does not permit editing of existing groups** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: interface group membership is a list on the group object in the schema; GUI and API read the same field, no stale legacy data path. TEST: migration of groups.
- [#8646](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8646) [bug, 2025, 2/3, incomplete] **PPPoE fails to reconnect automatically after reboot in 25.1+ — repeated timeouts, inconsistent ACNAMEs, and late reconne** -- w2 ADOPT: PPPoE client with fast retry backoff and AC-name tolerance (ignore AC mismatch unless pinned); integration test reboot->PPPoE up within 30s
- [#7349](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7349) [bug, 2024, 0/4, support] **PPPoE on bridge device broken between OPNsense 23.7 and OPNsense 24.1.4-amd64** -- w2 ADOPT: PPPoE over bridge device must be supported and ordered after bridge creation at boot; integration test PPPoE parent=bridge
- [#7425](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7425) [bug, 2024, 0/4, support] **Sudden nightly reconnect using PPPoE since version 24.1.6** -- w2 TEST: PPPoE session must not be reset by nightly jobs (log rotate, backup, cert renew); integration test asserting PPPoE uptime across all scheduled jobs.
- [#7792](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7792) [feature, 2024, 3/1, -] **[Feature Request] Add ATT Bypass WPA Supplicant Functionality into GUI** -- w2 ADOPT: 802.1X supplicant (wpa_supplicant wired EAP-TLS with cert from cert store) as a WAN interface option; covers AT&T gateway bypass.
- [#5794](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5794) [bug, 2022, 0/3, support] **pppoe connection breaks on heavy throughput ("LCP: no reply to 1 echo request")** -- w2 ADOPT: PPPoE LCP echo keepalive must survive saturation (tunable echo interval/failure count, prioritize LCP in shaper); auto-redial on LCP timeout
- [#6526](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6526) [bug, 2023, 1/2, incomplete] **Autonegotiation Issue with 23.6.1 on Intel X553** -- w2 ADOPT: per-interface speed/duplex/autoneg settings (ethtool -s) in the interface model, applied declaratively; needed for modems with broken autoneg.
- [#9400](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9400) [feature, 2025, 1/2, support] **Interface Enumeration Improvements** -- w2 ADOPT: no hardcoded LAN/WAN/OPTn; logical interfaces are user-named with role tags (wan/lan/mgmt) used by templates and rule macros
- [#10110](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10110) [feature, 2026, 0/3, support] **[Enhancement] GUI 'disable' option for VIPs** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: every schema object has a uniform 'enabled' flag (VIPs included); disabling keeps config and drops the address from the plan.
- [#5604](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5604) [feature, 2022, 0/2, support] **Allow adding bridged interfaces on initial setup** -- w2 ADOPT: installer/first-boot wizard can create a bridge (LAN+wifi members) and assign it as LAN before any IP config
- [#6025](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6025) [feature, 2022, 0/2, incomplete] **Assign Gui Interface** -- w2 ADOPT: first-boot wizard lets you pick which interfaces may reach the management GUI/SSH (generates allow rules per chosen interface)
- [#8131](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8131) [feature, 2024, 0/2, incomplete] **DHCP Lease Time & Duration - Overview Details Include** -- w2 ADOPT: interface overview shows DHCP lease details (server, obtained, expires, T1/T2, remaining) from the client's lease state
- [#8605](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8605) [feature, 2025, 1/1, support] **[Virtual IP] Offer the possibility to disable a VIP** -- w2 ADOPT: 'enabled' flag on virtual IP / additional address entries so they can be staged or disabled without deletion
- [#5664](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5664) [bug, 2022, 0/1, support] **Changing device (network port of an interface) does not work in Assignments** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: reassigning WAN device to ppp0 failed on stale VLAN parent check; whole-config validation resolves device refs by typed ID, not name strings
- [#5882](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5882) [bug, 2022, 0/1, -] **openssh becomes unresponsive when interface definitions change** -- w2 ADOPT: on interface address/MTU change, notify or restart bound listeners (sshd, GUI) via ListenAddress=any or systemd socket re-bind; integration test
- [#5895](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5895) [bug, 2022, 0/1, support] **opnsense 22.7 rc2 not responsive in KVM guest machine** -- w2 ADOPT: interface reconfigure/delete is a bounded, async apply job with timeout and progress; GUI never blocks on a hung backend; TEST on KVM virtio.
- [#6240](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6240) [support, 2023, 0/1, support] **wan get dchp lease from lan network** -- w2 SUPPORT: WAN got lease from LAN after adding NIC (device renumbering) -> DESIGN: stable interface identity by MAC/path, not enumeration order
- [#7874](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7874) [feature, 2024, 0/1, -] **[Feature Request] Add IEEE 802.1X Auth (via wpa_supplicant) Configuration Options to WebGUI** -- w2 ADOPT: 802.1X wired client auth config in interface schema (dup of 7792): EAP method, identity, cert/key refs, ca.
- [#8056](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8056) [bug, 2024, 0/1, support] **DHCP server active when it isn't** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: typed references between interface and DHCP server config; disabling interface validates against actual DHCP enable flag, no stale XML keys
- [#8375](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8375) [feature, 2025, 0/1, -] **PPPoE Uptime Stats** -- w2 ADOPT: PPP session metrics: connected-since, reconnect count, last disconnect reason; exported as Prometheus metrics and shown on interface page
- [#8621](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8621) [bug, 2025, 0/1, -] **WAN connection lost after PPPoE link re-negotiation** -- w2 ADOPT: PPPoE reconnect must atomically re-run route/NAT/DNS hooks on new address; health-check WAN after reconnect and alert if default route missing
- [#8785](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8785) [bug, 2025, 0/1, support] **Uniqueness should be enforced across Interface descriptions and Group names** -- w2 ADOPT: single namespace validation across interface names, group names and alias names (all usable as nft set/chain refs); reject collisions at validation.
- [#8098](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8098) [support, 2024, 0/18, support] **no more traffic on uplink and strange way to recover** -- w1 N/A-LINUX: FreeBSD NIC driver stall after update; on Linux provide link/driver diagnostics (ethtool stats, carrier flaps) and a watchdog to bounce a dead uplink.
- [#9222](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9222) [support, 2025, 0/15, support] **PPPoE unstable from v25.1.3 to most recent v25.7.3_7** -- w1 SUPPORT: PPPoE instability across releases -> UX: PPP negotiation log with LCP echo stats, option to tune echo interval/failure and MRU
- [#7386](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7386) [support, 2024, 0/12, support] **[24.1.5] interface with tagged VLAN not working on Deciso DEC4630** -- w1 SUPPORT: tagged VLAN dead after upgrade on specific hardware (driver issue) -> UX: show driver/offload capability per NIC and allow disabling rx-vlan-offload
- [#7601](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7601) [support, 2024, 0/12, support] **HTTP/TCP packets originating from Opnsense towards LAN interfaces seem to disappear** -- w1 SUPPORT: locally-originated TCP to LAN vanishing after upgrade (likely offload/VLAN issue) -> UX: built-in pcap on interface with 'this host' filter
- [#6859](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6859) [bug, 2023, 0/11, support] **Poor throughput of OPNsense installed in a VMware VM using vmxnet3** -- w1 N/A-LINUX: FreeBSD vmxnet3 driver throughput; Linux vmxnet3 is mature. Still: ship offload/multiqueue defaults tuned per driver and a perf smoke test in VM CI.
- [#5526](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5526) [support, 2022, 0/8, support] **LAGG is not working correctly** -- w1 SUPPORT: LACP bond shows 2.85G not 20G (single-flow hashing) -> UX: bond page shows per-member link speed, LACP partner state and xmit hash policy
- [#8012](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8012) [support, 2024, 0/8, support] **Automatically Generated Firewall Rules Interfere with WiFi Connectivity** -- w1 SUPPORT: wifi-as-client interface blocked by auto rules; -> UX: WAN-type interface template auto-allows DHCP client replies (nftables ct state + udp 68)
- [#6034](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6034) [support, 2022, 1/6, support] **ARP network problems** -- w1 SUPPORT: ARP resolution failures after upgrade on HA pair (likely proxy-ARP/VIP overlap) -> UX: neighbor table viewer with per-interface ARP diagnostics
- [#7133](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7133) [support, 2024, 0/7, support] **IGC1 interface under bridge0 is flapping leading to lack of communication via the interface each couple of hours after u** -- w1 N/A-LINUX: igc driver flapping under FreeBSD bridge; on Linux ship bridge + STP defaults and expose link-flap counters per port in diagnostics.
- [#8080](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8080) [bug, 2024, 0/7, support] **WPA broken for wifi after 24.7 update** -- w1 N/A-LINUX: FreeBSD wifi/WPA breakage; sensr uses hostapd on Linux. AP mode is low priority but if shipped, integration-test WPA2/WPA3 on upgrade.
- [#8231](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8231) [bug, 2025, 0/7, support] **Having an Interface with mixed VLAN Traffic (tagged & untagged), untagged outbound traffic is lost** -- w1 TEST: parent interface with untagged traffic + VLAN children keeps untagged forwarding/NAT working; regression test on bridge/VLAN mixed setups.
- [#8018](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8018) [bug, 2024, 0/6, -] **Crash when reassigning a wireless interface** -- w1 TEST: reassigning a wireless device to another logical interface must not crash apply; integration test device swap across interface types
- [#6811](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6811) [support, 2023, 0/5, support] **PPPoE Connection Issues** -- w1 SUPPORT: PPPoE regression vs ISP (worked on macOS/other) -> UX: PPPoE session log (PADI/PADO/LCP/IPCP) viewable in GUI with raw negotiation details
- [#7606](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7606) [support, 2024, 1/4, support] **PPPoE not working anymore** -- w1 SUPPORT: PPPoE drop triggered by client VPN disconnect; -> UX: PPPoE log must never be empty on failure, record LCP/PADT reason codes
- [#8427](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8427) [support, 2025, 0/5, support] **Bridge0 is flapping every 2 or 3 days on a one year Deciso appliance with the business OPNSense 24.10.2** -- w1 SUPPORT: transparent bridge flapping every 2-3 days (STP/hw) -> UX: bridge STP state, topology-change counter and member link-flap events in logs
- [#5764](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5764) [support, 2022, 0/4, incomplete] **CRC-Offload on KVM-Host** -- w1 SUPPORT: virtio checksum offload with VRRP in KVM -> UX: offload toggles per interface via ethtool with hypervisor-aware defaults and docs.
- [#7465](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7465) [bug, 2024, 0/4, -] **PPPoE set idle timeout not working when INT value is 0.** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: PPPoE idle timeout 0 rejected by validator; typed Option<u32> with 0=disabled validated identically in GUI/API from one schema
- [#7610](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7610) [bug, 2024, 0/4, support] **External static ip setup as VirtualIP accepts traffic for 30 seconds and then stops** -- w1 TEST: alias IP in a different subnet on WAN keeps working (proxy ARP / correct source selection); integration test with off-subnet VIP for 5 minutes.
- [#7120](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7120) [bug, 2024, 0/3, support] **Disabling hardware TCP segmentation offload doesn't disable VLAN_HWTSO** -- w1 N/A-LINUX: FreeBSD VLAN_HWTSO flag; on Linux ethtool offload toggles (tso/gro/rx-vlan-filter) are explicit per-device and inherited by VLAN children predictably
- [#7831](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7831) [bug, 2024, 1/2, support] **OPNsense 24.7: "ping: sendto: No buffer space available" with Tor relays and pfatt** -- w1 N/A-LINUX: FreeBSD ENOBUFS under load with netgraph bypass; on Linux expose qdisc/driver drop counters and conntrack saturation metrics for overload diagnosis.
- [#9660](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9660) [support, 2026, 0/3, support] **NIC Numbers versus Interface Numbers Assigned** -- w1 SUPPORT: physical port numbering vs device name mismatch -> UX: installer shows MAC, link state and 'blink/identify' per port; stable predictable names
- [#6064](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6064) [support, 2022, 0/2, incomplete] **Outbound NAT and routing breaks when setting custom MAC address on WAN/outside interface (using Web GUI)** -- w1 SUPPORT: MAC spoof breaks routing (upstream ARP cache) -> UX: MAC override field with warning + 'send gratuitous ARP on change'.
- [#8026](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8026) [support, 2024, 0/2, support] **DEC3852 Unifi 10GB SFP+ Fiber and Ethernet Modules + Direct-Atach Cable not usable** -- w1 N/A-LINUX: FreeBSD axgbe SFP+ module compatibility; on Linux expose ethtool module info/EEPROM in diagnostics and document an SFP compatibility list.
- [#9394](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9394) [support, 2025, 0/2, support] **Additional VLAN's fail to get IP address when using a PPPOE VLAN for internet connection** -- w1 SUPPORT: new VLAN clients fail DHCP when WAN is PPPoE-over-VLAN -> UX: DHCP server status per interface and auto-check that Kea listens on new VLANs
- [#9517](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9517) [bug, 2025, 0/2, incomplete] **WoL Fails on Intel 82567LM-3 (em0) after Clean Shutdown (S5) on OPNsense 25.x (FreeBSD 14.3 Regression)** -- w1 ADOPT: WoL enable on shutdown (ethtool wol g) as a per-interface setting applied in the shutdown path; test clean shutdown keeps WoL armed.
- [#6524](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6524) [support, 2023, 0/1, support] **VLan non-functional under 23.1** -- w1 SUPPORT: VLAN passed no traffic on new release (likely driver/hw VLAN filter); -> UX: per-VLAN RX/TX counters and offload status visible in interface overview
- [#7064](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7064) [bug, 2023, 0/1, support] **WAN IP collision causes permanent internet loss** -- w1 ADOPT: IPv4 address conflict detection (ARP DAD via networkd DuplicateAddressDetection) with log/alert and automatic retry when conflict clears.
- [#7072](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7072) [bug, 2023, 0/1, support] **Insert my currently connected MAC address (use with care) using wrong MAC for interfaces other than WAN** -- w1 TEST: 'insert connected MAC' helper must read MAC from the interface being edited, not WAN; unit test per-interface MAC lookup
- [#7346](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7346) [bug, 2024, 0/1, support] **TAP Child lose Vlan TAG after reboot** -- w1 TEST: VLAN children on TAP/bridge members keep their tag across reboot; integration test for VLAN-on-tap persistence in networkd config
- [#8767](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8767) [support, 2025, 0/1, support] **Fresh install, no ping and no dns. Hardware issue?** -- w1 SUPPORT: 4-NIC mini PC no connectivity (NIC driver) -> UX: interface page shows driver, link state, carrier, rx/tx errors to localize hardware issues
- [#7984](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7984) [support, 2024, 0/7, support] **incorrect WAN IP address on PPPOE** -- w0 SUPPORT: confusion over PPPoE WAN address vs peer; -> UX: PPP interface view shows local IP, peer IP and negotiated DNS distinctly
- [#6301](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6301) [support, 2023, 2/3, support] **Is Realtek Plugin Still Needed?** -- w0 N/A-LINUX: Realtek vendor driver plugin; Linux r8169/r8125 in-tree, so no plugin needed.
- [#5725](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5725) [other, 2022, 0/2, incomplete] **missing labels for interfaces.php** -- w0 OUT-OF-SCOPE: incomplete report about missing form labels in interfaces.php; sensr forms generated from schema always carry labels
- [#7025](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7025) [feature, 2023, 0/2, -] **Infiniband lagg type support** -- w0 OUT-OF-SCOPE: IPoIB bonding is niche; Linux bonding over ib devices works where kernel supports it, no special handling in core

### 3.5 IPv6 / DHCPv6-PD / RA / NPTv6 (72)

- [#6263](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6263) [bug, 2023, 0/38, support] **Multiple DHCPv6 instances send advertisements** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: one Kea v6 + one RA daemon as systemd units (no duplicate processes); on prefix change RA deprecates old prefix (lifetime 0), Kea subnets replaced
- [#6962](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6962) [bug, 2023, 1/20, support] **[23.7.7] dhcpc6 does not receive dhcp6 advertise from ISP over PPPOE if pf is on** -- w3 TEST: DHCPv6 client traffic (UDP 546/547, ff02::1:2) over PPPoE always permitted by base nftables chain regardless of user rules; e2e on pppoe netdev
- [#10070](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10070) [bug, 2026, 0/20, support] **LAN IPv6 prefix assignment (identity association/track interface) fails to use /64** -- w3 TEST: fresh install PPPoE + /56 PD -> LAN gets first /64 automatically; CI e2e of the canonical IPv6 home setup; same as #9523
- [#9261](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9261) [bug, 2025, 2/16, support] **Clients losing IPv6 connectivity overnight on ISPs with nightly PPPoE reconnects due to RFC 9096 violation** -- w3 ADOPT: RFC 9096 compliant RA: deprecated prefix advertised with lifetime 0 for >= 2h (or until clients drop), MaxRtrAdvInterval cap; TEST macOS-like client recovery
- [#9523](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9523) [bug, 2025, 0/18, support] **My ISP delegates a /62 IPv6 prefix, but rtadvd.inc generates a /62 Router Advertisement prefix.** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: track-interface always carves /64 from any delegated prefix (/62,/60,/56) with validated subnet-id; RA prefix length fixed at 64; TEST /62 PD
- [#9153](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9153) [bug, 2025, 0/17, support] **IPv6: PPPoE empty /var/etc/dhcp6c.conf** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: DHCPv6 client config rendered from schema regardless of parent type (PPPoE/VLAN); TEST render on pppoe parent is non-empty and valid
- [#9873](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9873) [feature, 2026, 0/17, support] **IPv6 / CARP / RADVD splitbrain issue** -- w3 ADOPT: RA daemon HA-aware: only MASTER advertises; on failover BACKUP sends RA with same prefix; MASTER demotion sends lifetime-0 RA (c=17).
- [#7018](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7018) [bug, 2023, 1/11, support] **radvd not deprecating prefixes when pppoe connection is manually disconnected** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: RA daemon config reconciles on PPP down: deprecated prefixes advertised with lifetime 0 for a period (RFC 9096), then removed; same as #9261
- [#7165](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7165) [feature, 2024, 8/3, -] **KEA DHCPv6 Integration** -- w3 ADOPT: Kea DHCPv6 first-class (IA_NA pools, IA_PD delegation, reservations by DUID/MAC, options) at parity with v4 from day one
- [#7380](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7380) [bug, 2024, 0/11, support] **Not getting IPv6 address on WAN interface at boot** -- w3 TEST: DHCPv6 client over PPPoE starts after PPP link up (systemd ordering/netlink carrier) and retries SOLICIT; boot e2e acquires PD without manual reload
- [#9853](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9853) [bug, 2026, 0/6, support] **DHCPv6 Prefix Delegation not working on IPoE (non-PPPoE) connection** -- w3 ADOPT: PD-only WAN assigns ::1 of first /64 (or routes PD via LL next-hop) so routing works without IA_NA; same as #6202/#6233; TEST IPoE PD-only
- [#5591](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5591) [bug, 2022, 0/4, support] **21.7.8 sets invalid default route for IPv6** -- w3 TEST: RA-learned default route only accepted on WAN-role interfaces (accept_ra per interface, LAN accept_ra=0); regression test on LAN RA from another router
- [#8505](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8505) [bug, 2025, 0/3, support] **RADVD re-advertises expired prefixes** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: RA daemon receives deprecate-then-remove schedule from prefix-change reconcile; expired prefixes never re-advertised with nonzero lifetime; TEST
- [#8370](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8370) [bug, 2025, 0/2, support] **No IPv6 connection outgoing (without manual intervention)** -- w3 TEST: with PD-only WAN (no IA_NA), firewall sources outbound v6 from a tracked-prefix address and default route via RA LL gateway; e2e in CI netns lab
- [#5735](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5735) [bug, 2022, 0/1, support] **DHCPv6 creates leases with old address after WAN reset** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: on PD prefix change, track-interface addresses, Kea v6 subnets and stale leases for old prefix are replaced in one reconcile; TEST prefix rotation
- [#6435](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6435) [support, 2023, 1/44, support] **Impossibility to use IPv6 since 23.X** -- w2 SUPPORT: v6 reachability lost after upgrade (44 comments, unclear cause) -> ADOPT: ipv6 diagnostics page (ndp table, RA received, routes, addr lifetimes) + upgrade smoke test
- [#4983](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4983) [feature, 2021, 0/21, -] **Add MAP-T/MAP-E support** -- w2 ADOPT: MAP-E/MAP-T (RFC7597/7599) and DS-Lite (B4) as WAN IPv4aaS modes using Linux ip6tnl/nat46 modules with DHCPv6 option 94/95 parsing
- [#5572](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5572) [support, 2022, 0/16, support] **Can't get IPV6 to work, trying to add 'Prevent Release', but it doesn't exist.** -- w2 SUPPORT: user couldn't find 'prevent release' -> UX: DHCPv6-PD client form with ISP presets (PD size, IA_NA/IA_PD only, no-release) and live client state
- [#6475](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6475) [support, 2023, 0/16, support] **IPV6 Routing problem on LAN if re0** -- w2 SUPPORT: LAN v6 unreachable on re0 (NIC/driver or NDP) -> TEST: NDP/ICMPv6 essential rules (RS/RA/NS/NA, MLD) always present in nftables base chain
- [#9815](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9815) [feature, 2026, 0/16, support] **radvd: disable IPv6 address autoconfiguration for Virtual IP** -- w2 ADOPT: per-prefix RA flags (AdvAutonomous off for ULA/virtual IP, on-link only); same as #7513
- [#6233](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6233) [feature, 2023, 0/11, feature] **Allow an interface to track itself when getting a prefix via DHCPv6** -- w2 ADOPT: interface may track its own delegated prefix (assign ::1 of a sub-/64 to WAN) for ISPs that delegate without IA_NA (NTT Flet's); DS-Lite tunnel source
- [#6555](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6555) [feature, 2023, 3/6, support] **DHCPv6 support for virtual IP range** -- w2 ADOPT: Kea v6 reservations/pools on static ULA (virtual IP) prefixes alongside tracked GUA (multiple subnets per interface, shared-network); same as #9427
- [#6292](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6292) [bug, 2023, 0/7, support] **DHCP v6 - VLAN priority not honored** -- w2 ADOPT: DHCPv6 client must set 802.1p VLAN priority (egress-qos-map / SO_PRIORITY) when configured; TEST: pcap asserts PCP=6 on SOLICIT
- [#5474](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5474) [support, 2022, 0/6, support] **No IPv6 Default gateway, no IPv6 internet when manually assigned.** -- w2 SUPPORT: no v6 default route behind AT&T bypass -> ADOPT: default v6 gateway learned from RA (accept_ra=2 on WAN) shown in gateway list with source 'RA' and lifetime
- [#5672](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5672) [bug, 2022, 0/6, support] **DHCPv6 client configuration - UI option for overriding config is totally ignored** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: no 'custom config file path' escape hatches; every DHCPv6 client option typed in schema (advanced section) so GUI fields are never silently ignored
- [#7059](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7059) [bug, 2023, 0/6, support] **WAN DHCPv6 configuration set to configuration file override prevents start of dhcpd6 on startup** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: no custom-config-file mode; track-interface dependents start from declared state; TEST LAN Kea v6 starts when WAN PD not yet acquired
- [#9144](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9144) [support, 2025, 1/5, incomplete] **IPv6 regression, update 25.7 -> 25.7.2, WAN IF no longer reliably acquires IPv6** -- w2 SUPPORT: WAN v6 unreliable after upgrade on virtio -> TEST: DHCPv6 acquisition e2e in CI per release (netns lab, simulated ISP); explicit 'use v4 connectivity' semantics
- [#9326](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9326) [bug, 2025, 0/5, -] **IPv6 traffic silently dropped on WG interface when no tunnel IPv6 address is set** -- w2 ADOPT: WG interface without v6 address still routes v6 (Linux needs link-local or accept; auto-assign fe80 or set addr_gen); test v6 forwarding over address-less wg
- [#5975](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5975) [bug, 2022, 0/4, support] **ipv6 renew (?) takes the pppoe  interface down** -- w2 TEST: DHCPv6 renew/RA events on PPPoE never trigger PPP session restart; link events handled per address family
- [#6202](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6202) [feature, 2022, 0/4, support] **IPv6 - workaround for RFC6603 issue** -- w2 ADOPT: PD-only WAN (RFC6603 exclude / no IA_NA) assigns a /64 from the delegated prefix to the WAN or loopback so NAT66/VPN endpoints work; same as #6233
- [#7513](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7513) [feature, 2024, 0/4, support] **Allow Separate Prefix-Specific Router Advertisement Options on a Single Interface** -- w2 ADOPT: RA config per prefix (A/L flags, lifetimes) on one interface: e.g. GUA autonomous + ULA on-link-only; typed prefix list in RA schema
- [#8016](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8016) [bug, 2024, 2/2, -] **radvd RAs get out of sync with tracked 6rd interface addresses** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: single prefix-change event source (netlink addr/prefix watch) drives RA, Kea, NPTv6, firewall sets for all WAN types incl. 6RD/tunnels
- [#8681](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8681) [bug, 2025, 0/4, support] **OPNsense Forwards Link-Local Router Advertisements Across Interface** -- w2 TEST: RAs received on WAN are never re-sent/forwarded on LAN (per-netdev RA daemon, forwarding sysctl correct); pcap test on LAN shows only our RAs
- [#5545](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5545) [bug, 2022, 0/3, support] **Incorrect reverse ip6.arpa zone for DHCPv6 registration in DNS** -- w2 TEST: ip6.arpa zone nibble alignment for prefixes with trailing zero nibbles (/64 -> 16 nibbles); same as #8747
- [#5645](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5645) [bug, 2022, 0/3, incomplete] **radvd not starting up after upgrade** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: RA daemon config generated from validated state; service start failure surfaces stderr in GUI apply result instead of red icon
- [#6560](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6560) [bug, 2023, 0/3, support] **IPv6 Neighborhood solicitation on carp interfaces** -- w2 ADOPT: on VRRP MASTER transition send unsolicited NA/gratuitous ARP for all VIPs (keepalived garp/ndisc) and test NS handling for VIPs in hypervisor setups.
- [#6713](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6713) [bug, 2023, 0/3, support] **IPv6 router advertisement from one vlan is distributed to all other vlans** -- w2 TEST: RA emitted only on the configured VLAN netdev, never parent/sibling VLANs; pcap test on parent interface shows no RAs
- [#7964](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7964) [bug, 2024, 2/1, -] **Unable to configure NPTv6 tracking interface with 6RD** -- w2 ADOPT: NPTv6 external prefix may track any dynamic-prefix source (DHCPv6-PD, 6RD, SLAAC) via nftables set updated on prefix change
- [#8747](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8747) [bug, 2025, 0/3, -] **DHCPDv6 ip6.arpa generation not aligned to subnet** -- w2 TEST: generated ip6.arpa DDNS zone name must be nibble-aligned to subnet prefix length (/64 -> 16 nibbles); unit test on prefix->zone fn
- [#9402](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9402) [bug, 2025, 0/3, incomplete] **KEA DHCPv6 - Windows 11 24H2 & 25H2 DHCPv6 T1, T2, Valid and Preferred Lifetimes bug mitigation** -- w2 ADOPT: Kea v6 preferred-lifetime/valid-lifetime/T1/T2 exposed per subnet (workaround for Win11 DHCPv6 client bug); same as #8692
- [#9992](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9992) [bug, 2026, 0/3, support] **dnsmasq sending RAs even if "Router advertisements" option is disabled** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: single RA source per interface enforced in schema (RA daemon xor dnsmasq ra); dnsmasq dhcp-range gets 'ra-only/off' flags consistent with toggle; TEST
- [#6317](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6317) [bug, 2023, 0/2, incomplete] **Invalid IPV6 DNS servers for DHCP v6 can cause radvd to crash in a boot loop** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: DNS server fields typed Ipv6Addr (reject '::7' shorthand unless full address); RA config validated before daemon start, old config kept on failure
