- [#8692](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8692) [feature, 2025, 0/2, incomplete] **Seems to be an issue with DHCPd6 preferred-lifetime (187 seconds; very short)** -- w2 ADOPT: Kea v6 preferred-lifetime, valid-lifetime, renew/rebind timers exposed per subnet with sane defaults (prefer >= 50% valid)
- [#8803](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8803) [bug, 2025, 0/2, support] **Is there a way to stop OPNsense from sending RA's with lifetime 0?** -- w2 ADOPT: RA daemon lifecycle: send lifetime-0 RA only on real shutdown/disable, never on restart or boot; test RA sequence across reboot (no deprecating RA at startup).
- [#9629](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9629) [feature, 2026, 0/2, support] **Add option to suppress default IPv6 DNS (Option 23) in Dnsmasq** -- w2 ADOPT: DHCPv6 option 23 (DNS) and RA RDNSS each explicitly toggleable/overridable per interface, not auto-forced
- [#10211](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10211) [bug, 2026, 0/2, support] **Incorrect prefix selection when multiple IA_PD prefixes (GUA + ULA) are received** -- w2 ADOPT: when multiple IA_PD prefixes received, track-interface selects by policy (prefer GUA, optional ULA too) and RA advertises each with correct flags
- [#10219](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10219) [bug, 2026, 0/2, support] **Change of "System DHCP Unique Identifier" not effected until dhcp6c restart** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: DUID is a schema field; apply plan knows DHCPv6 client must restart when it changes (dependency graph), done in the same transaction
- [#6371](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6371) [feature, 2023, 0/1, support] **DSLite Support** -- w2 ADOPT: DS-Lite WAN mode (IPv4-in-IPv6 ip4ip6 tunnel to AFTR learned via DHCPv6 option 64), as native interface type.
- [#6515](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6515) [feature, 2023, 0/1, support] **IPv6 Track Interface use Router Advertisement to trigger premature reload** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: track-interface reconcile keyed on actual PD prefix change (lease diff), not on every RA/renew event; debounce prevents premature reloads
- [#6746](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6746) [feature, 2023, 0/1, support] **Make broken DHCPv6 config more obvious** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: DHCPv6 client options typed in schema (no freeform config); client state machine + last error shown in interface status
- [#7576](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7576) [bug, 2024, 0/1, -] **DHCPv6 broken radvd config when switching from static IPv6 address** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: changing interface v6 mode static->DHCPv6 regenerates client + RA config from the new declared state in one transaction; TEST mode switch
- [#8166](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8166) [feature, 2024, 0/1, support] **allow opnsense to give up on getting a prefix delegation from ISP after configurable time** -- w2 ADOPT: RA for static ULA/virtual-IP prefixes advertised independently of PD state; PD wait timeout configurable, RA never blocked by missing PD
- [#8389](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8389) [bug, 2025, 0/1, -] **Reporting: Traffic - not counting IPv6 traffic** -- w2 ADOPT: traffic accounting (flow export + interface counters) is dual-stack by default; TEST: IPv6 download appears in per-host traffic report.
- [#5626](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5626) [support, 2022, 0/21, incomplete] **No IPv6 route or address to client (radvd/DHCPv6) issue** -- w1 SUPPORT: clients lost v6 after upgrade -> UX: RA/DHCPv6 status page showing advertised prefixes, lifetimes, and PD state per interface
- [#2540](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/2540) [feature, 2018, 4/11, -] **Autoconfigure 6RD based on DHCPv4 option 212** -- w1 ADOPT: 6RD auto-config from DHCPv4 option 212 (request option, derive prefix/BR/mask); netlink sit tunnel re-created on lease change
- [#6247](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6247) [support, 2023, 2/11, support] **IPv6 MLD listener report going to wrong interface** -- w1 SUPPORT: MLD reports egress wrong interface (hypervisor bridge) -> ADOPT: per-interface MLD/IGMP snooping/mcast toggles and mcast route visibility
- [#6884](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6884) [support, 2023, 0/10, -] **IPv6 Issues with COX Fiber on 23.7.X** -- w1 SUPPORT: /56 PD stopped working after ISP migration (unknown) -> UX: DHCPv6 client debug view with SOLICIT/ADVERTISE/REPLY timeline and received IA_PD contents
- [#5856](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5856) [support, 2022, 1/7, support] **Wrong IPv6 WAN address in traceroute in multi-WAN environment (WAN + wireguard WAN)** -- w1 SUPPORT: ICMPv6 TTL-exceeded source address in multi-WAN (routing table choice) -> UX: per-interface v6 source address selection documented; policy routing table per WAN
- [#10282](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10282) [bug, 2026, 0/7, support] **radvd: DNSSL can't be disabled without disabling RDNSS** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: RA RDNSS and DNSSL are independent Option<> fields in schema; each can be empty; TEST DNSSL off with RDNSS on
- [#8827](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8827) [support, 2025, 0/5, support] **DHCPv6 with DNSmasq** -- w1 SUPPORT: dnsmasq DHCPv6 ranges/constructor confusion -> DESIGN: v6 ranges expressed as ::start-::end within tracked prefix in schema, same for Kea/dnsmasq
- [#9971](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9971) [bug, 2026, 0/5, support] **RADVD RDNSS entry is not checked for "suffix only" with unexpected behavior** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: RDNSS typed as Ipv6Addr or 'interface address'; suffix-only inputs rejected or explicitly expanded with prefix (documented), never copied verbatim
- [#8478](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8478) [support, 2025, 0/4, support] **Wan loose IPv6 after a time** -- w1 SUPPORT: WAN v6 lost after 30 min (RA lifetime/DAD?) -> UX: addr lifetimes + RA received log on interface status; ADOPT: accept_ra persistence on WAN
- [#7670](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7670) [bug, 2024, 0/3, support] **[24.7] dhcpd6 stops working when renewing IP on 6to4 Tunnel** -- w1 TEST: Kea v6 survives tunnel (6to4/6rd) address renew; dependents reconfigured, not killed; chaos test on tunnel endpoint change
- [#8751](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8751) [feature, 2025, 1/2, support] **[Feature Request]  "Parse Prefix" option for host alias** -- w1 ADOPT: host alias option 'network from prefix' (mask resolved AAAA to /64 or /56) so dynamic-prefix site-to-site rules match whole prefix.
- [#8891](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8891) [feature, 2025, 0/2, support] **Network Alias from a FQDN with custom prefix** -- w1 ADOPT: FQDN alias with prefix-length option (treat resolved AAAA as /64 network) for dynamic IPv6 prefixes with temporary addresses.
- [#9444](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9444) [bug, 2025, 0/2, upstream] **Malformed response for NTP server address in DNSmasq** -- w1 ADOPT: DHCPv6 option 56 NTP suboptions (server addr/mcast/fqdn) typed; '[::]' interface-address placeholder resolved to actual GUA at generation; TEST pcap
- [#6143](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6143) [bug, 2022, 0/1, -] **Documentation and context help misleading: IPv6 prefix delegation** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: PD pool defined as (prefix, delegated length) with computed count; help text generated from schema; validate alignment
- [#6504](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6504) [feature, 2023, 0/1, support] **DHCPv6 with relayed prefix delegation only** -- w1 ADOPT: DHCPv6 relay for IA_PD only while local Kea v6 serves IA_NA/options on the same interface (per-interface relay/serve mix)
- [#8246](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8246) [support, 2025, 0/1, support] **Network on WAN interfgace is set to only own IP address for IPv6** -- w1 SUPPORT: WAN v6 alias with /128 netmask vs on-link prefix -> UX: validate alias prefix length against interface prefix and explain on-link implications
- [#8610](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8610) [support, 2025, 0/1, support] **IPv6 Issues** -- w1 SUPPORT: generic IPv6 setup question -> UX: guided IPv6 WAN wizard (PD size, track interfaces, RA mode) with live status
- [#6295](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6295) [support, 2023, 0/10, support] **Router Advertisements provide "fe80" address as gateway to clients** -- w0 SUPPORT: RA gateway is fe80 (correct per RFC4861) -> UX: explain in RA status page; optional RIO/route info; never a bug
- [#8066](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8066) [feature, 2024, 0/6, -] **Overwrite link-local address with my own ULA** -- w0 OUT-OF-SCOPE: link-local fe80 is mandatory per RFC4291; sensr allows setting a stable LL token (addrgenmode / ip token) but not removing LL

### 3.6 DHCP (ISC / Kea / dnsmasq) (80)

- [#9889](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9889) [bug, 2026, 0/11, support] **Failover WAN connection failing to renew DHCP due to missing route?** -- w3 ADOPT: DHCP client unicast RENEW must use an interface-bound socket/route (SO_BINDTODEVICE) so multi-WAN policy routing can't break renewal of the backup WAN
- [#8020](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8020) [bug, 2024, 0/7, support] **WAN DHCP refresh still drops connection/packets in 24.7.5_3?** -- w3 TEST: WAN DHCP renew to same IP must not trigger interface reconfigure, Unbound restart or route churn; event dedupe on unchanged lease
- [#5656](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5656) [bug, 2022, 0/6, incomplete] **DHCPD stops globally on subnet change for single interface.** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: transactional validate rejects interface subnet change that orphans a Kea pool (per-subnet error), never silently stops dhcpd globally
- [#10095](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10095) [bug, 2026, 0/6, incomplete] **dnsmasq DHCP assigns wrong subnet mask (/28) when subnet_mask field is empty in DHCP range** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: DHCP range netmask derived from interface prefix in schema (Option<Netmask> defaults to interface); validate range within subnet; TEST empty mask -> /24
- [#7191](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7191) [bug, 2024, 0/4, support] **DHCP renew drops connection - OPNsense Version 24.1_1** -- w3 TEST: WAN DHCP renew must not remove/re-add default route (lease renew with same gateway is a no-op to routing table); regression test on T1 renew
- [#7158](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7158) [feature, 2024, 2/1, -] **KEA DHCPv4** -- w3 ADOPT: Kea v4 per-subnet/per-pool options: domain-name, domain-search, valid-lifetime per pool, multiple pools per subnet
- [#7765](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7765) [bug, 2024, 1/2, incomplete] **KEA DHCP reservations - malformed MAC address check** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: typed MacAddr validation prevents malformed reservation reaching Kea; TEST: dash-separated MAC input normalizes, Kea config always loads
- [#9609](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9609) [bug, 2026, 0/3, support] **Kea DHCPv4 not successful running after update (dnsmasq/ISC re-enabled)** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: one DHCP backend per interface enforced in schema (enum), so an upgrade/migration can't leave two daemons contending for :67; migration tested in CI
- [#7477](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7477) [bug, 2024, 0/2, incomplete] **feature: Adding a duplicate IP to MAC reservation causes kea to stop working** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: schema validation rejects duplicate reservation IP/MAC per subnet before apply; Kea config generated only from validated state so it never fails to start
- [#6343](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6343) [bug, 2023, 0/17, support] **DHCP - Setting "Gateway" to "None" On a Specific Device Does Not Work** -- w2 ADOPT: per-reservation 'no router option' in Kea (host option-data routers empty / never-send); TEST: client receives no option 3
- [#7768](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7768) [feature, 2024, 10/6, -] **Support for DDNS in Kea** -- w2 ADOPT: Kea DDNS (kea-dhcp-ddns, RFC2136 with TSIG) first-class per subnet, plus built-in Unbound lease registration path
- [#8247](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8247) [feature, 2025, 0/12, support] **KEA DHCPReservation - permit empty "ip address" field, like in old isc-dhcp** -- w2 ADOPT: reservations without fixed IP (MAC -> hostname/options only, dynamic address) supported in Kea host entries; replaces isc 'host without fixed-address'
- [#8620](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8620) [feature, 2025, 0/11, support] **DNSmasq DHCP options incomplete, untyped and confusing** -- w2 ADOPT: dnsmasq (optional) DHCP options modelled as typed per-subnet fields (router, dns, ntp, domain) auto-derived from interface, not raw option lines
- [#7875](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7875) [support, 2024, 0/10, support] **ISC DHCPv4 need  restart on vlan interface** -- w2 SUPPORT: dhcpd not answering on VLAN until restart -> TEST: DHCP server restarts/rebinds when VLAN interface recreated or address changes (netlink event driven)
- [#8421](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8421) [bug, 2025, 0/8, support] **Kea DHCP Not honoring the order of the list of DNS ip's that shuld be provided to DHCP Clients** -- w2 TEST: ordered list fields (DNS servers, NTP) preserve user order through schema->Kea config; property test over list fields, no implicit sort
- [#6755](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6755) [feature, 2023, 0/7, support] **DHCP: add "allow unknown-clients" option** -- w2 ADOPT: Kea client-classes with 'known/unknown client' pool restriction (allow-unknown-clients equivalent via KNOWN/UNKNOWN classes) per pool
- [#8175](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8175) [bug, 2025, 0/7, support] **Removal of the exit_with_hooks() function and its calls from src/opnsense/scripts/interfaces/dhclient-script** -- w2 ADOPT: stable hook points for DHCP-client lease events (exec script/webhook with received options as JSON) so custom automations survive upgrades
- [#8323](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8323) [support, 2025, 0/7, support] **Kea dhcp is not always assigning an ip address to interface igb0 after reboot.** -- w2 SUPPORT: Kea not answering on igb0 after boot -> TEST: Kea starts after all interface addresses are up (ordering/netlink wait) and re-binds on late interfaces
- [#9427](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9427) [feature, 2025, 0/7, support] **Feature Request: Kea DHCP: Enable shared networks for DHCPv4 and DHCPv6** -- w2 ADOPT: Kea shared-networks for v4 and v6 (GUA tracked + ULA static on one interface) in schema; same as #8170/#6555
- [#7478](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7478) [support, 2024, 0/6, support] **[BUG] WAN interface periodically losing DHCP lease** -- w2 SUPPORT: WAN lease lost daily (ISP/modem quirk) -> ADOPT: DHCP client must fall back to re-DISCOVER and log cause; expose client state and lease timers in GUI
- [#7591](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7591) [feature, 2024, 2/4, support] **Kea: Support DHCP Vendor Option codes** -- w2 ADOPT: Kea option 43/vendor-encapsulated and option 60-class vendor options exposed as typed custom-option definitions (Unifi, SIP, PXE)
- [#9054](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9054) [feature, 2025, 0/6, -] **Passthrough of KEA DHCP client-class configurations to UI** -- w2 ADOPT: Kea client-classes in schema (test expression, options, next-server/boot-file per class) e.g. UEFI vs BIOS by option 93; pools restricted by class
- [#9090](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9090) [feature, 2025, 2/4, support] **Kea DHCPv4 GUI: add Option 42 (ntp-servers) field; clarify/retire legacy Option 4 (time-servers)** -- w2 ADOPT: Kea v4 option 42 ntp-servers as typed field; drop/hide obsolete option 4 time-servers behind advanced
- [#9838](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9838) [bug, 2026, 0/6, support] **Legacy Static ARP entries persist and block traffic after switching from ISC DHCP to Kea DHCP** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: static ARP/neighbor entries are derived state regenerated from current config on every apply (netlink neigh flush+set), never left over from removed backend
- [#10068](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10068) [feature, 2026, 0/6, support] **Feature request: dnsmasq ignore client id (global)** -- w2 ADOPT: DHCP server option to identify clients by MAC ignoring client-id (dnsmasq dhcp-ignore-clid / Kea match-client-id=false) globally and per subnet
- [#7435](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7435) [bug, 2024, 0/5, -] **KEA DHCP Reservation bypassed** -- w2 ADOPT: on adding a reservation whose MAC holds a dynamic lease, offer to delete that lease (Kea lease4-del) so client gets reserved IP on next renew
- [#9817](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9817) [bug, 2026, 0/5, support] **KEA - static DHCPv4 reservation - wrong (always the first) subnet prefilled** -- w2 TEST: 'create reservation from lease' prefill selects subnet containing the lease IP; unit test over multi-subnet fixtures
- [#7310](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7310) [feature, 2024, 2/2, -] **KEA: custom DNS for reservation** -- w2 ADOPT: per-reservation DNS server option in Kea v4 reservations (option-data domain-name-servers on host entry)
- [#7810](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7810) [feature, 2024, 3/1, -] **Kea: Support DHCP Classless Static Route option** -- w2 ADOPT: Kea option 121 classless-static-routes (and 249 MS variant) as typed list of (prefix, router) per subnet/reservation
- [#8737](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8737) [feature, 2025, 0/4, support] **GUI: dnsmasq support for non-connected / non-local subnets.** -- w2 ADOPT: DHCP (Kea/dnsmasq) subnets not tied to a local interface: shared-network scopes for relayed subnets behind L3 switches, with relay/giaddr matching.
- [#8947](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8947) [bug, 2025, 0/4, support] **Static mappings from ISC DHCPv4 still being used when ISC DHCPv4 is disabled.** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: single DHCP backend selection per interface in schema; neighbor/ARP view reads reservations only from the active backend
- [#7678](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7678) [feature, 2024, 1/2, incomplete] **Request MAC address validation when entering KEA reservation** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: MAC is a typed field (MacAddr) in schema; parser accepts colon/dash/dot forms, normalizes, rejects garbage at validate time
- [#8586](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8586) [feature, 2025, 0/3, support] **Merge IP-less static lease with the associated dynamic lease** -- w2 ADOPT: unified lease view joins dynamic lease with IP-less reservation by MAC (shows reserved hostname/description on dynamic lease row)
- [#9578](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9578) [bug, 2026, 0/3, support] **dnsmasq DHCP not being enabled when I add a subnet from CLI** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: CLI is thin API client so 'enable DHCP on new interface' goes through the same schema/apply path as the GUI; no divergent console wizard
- [#5849](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5849) [feature, 2022, 0/2, incomplete] **DHCP Static Mapping creates Alias** -- w2 ADOPT: reference DHCP reservations directly in firewall rules (dynamic alias type 'dhcp-host' resolved into nftables set) instead of manual host alias
- [#6546](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6546) [bug, 2023, 0/2, support] **DHCP Static Mappings allows duplicate entries** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: schema uniqueness constraints on reservation (MAC, IP, hostname per subnet) enforced at validate; duplicate hostname -> error, not two DNS records
- [#7132](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7132) [bug, 2024, 0/2, support] **Hosts across VLANS register into DHCP Dynamic DNS PowerDNS Zone** -- w2 ADOPT: DDNS (RFC2136) settings scoped per subnet/pool in Kea (ddns-qualifying-suffix, server per subnet), not global
- [#7871](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7871) [bug, 2024, 0/2, support] **DHPC Bug: ISC DHCP Range doesn't update automatically after changing Router IP address** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: validate fails when DHCP pool is outside interface subnet after IP change; apply plan offers to shift pool proportionally
- [#7950](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7950) [feature, 2024, 1/1, -] **[Feature request] Kia leases - create a reservation** -- w2 ADOPT: 'Create reservation' action on each Kea lease row, prefilled with MAC/IP/hostname
- [#8140](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8140) [bug, 2024, 0/2, incomplete] **KEA DHCP does not change "Auto collect option data" when changing the subnet** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: Kea subnet options derived from interface at apply time (auto mode = computed, not snapshot); interface IP change re-derives options
- [#8170](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8170) [feature, 2024, 1/1, -] **Kea: support shared networks** -- w2 ADOPT: Kea shared-networks in schema (multiple subnets on one interface/link, named group), with per-subnet pools and options
- [#8174](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8174) [feature, 2025, 0/2, support] **[Feature Request] Allow setting "option dhcp-option-overload" in DHCP web gui** -- w2 ADOPT: option 43 vendor-encapsulated-options with raw hex/typed suboptions per subnet (Unifi inform URL pattern); same as #7591
- [#8523](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8523) [feature, 2025, 0/2, feature] **dnsmasq: Offer dhcp-relay functionality** -- w2 ADOPT: DHCP relay and dnsmasq/Kea coexist via per-interface binding (bind-interfaces/bind-dynamic) enforced in schema; same as #5682
- [#8738](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8738) [bug, 2025, 0/2, cleanup] **Kea DHCP: Missing validation of "static routes" input field** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: static route option typed as list of (dest CIDR, router IP) in schema; bare IP rejected at validation, never reaches Kea
- [#9813](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9813) [bug, 2026, 0/2, support] **dnsmasq not using configured option[6] DNS** -- w2 TEST: explicit option 6 (DNS servers) in dnsmasq range/host overrides must override auto-derived router DNS; regression test after dnsmasq model migration
- [#5682](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5682) [feature, 2022, 0/1, support] **Feature Request: Relay and DHCP Server at the same time** -- w2 ADOPT: allow Kea server on some interfaces and DHCP relay on others concurrently (per-interface mode, not a global server-vs-relay toggle)
- [#6193](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6193) [bug, 2022, 0/1, support] **DHCP LAN: range changes to default after rerun wizard** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: setup wizard writes only the fields it shows via the same API; re-running it shows a diff/plan before apply so existing DHCP range is never reset
- [#6341](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6341) [feature, 2023, 0/1, support] **dhcp relay : distinguish between upstream and downstream networks (use -iu and -id instead of just -i)** -- w2 ADOPT: DHCP relay config distinguishes upstream (server-facing) and downstream (client-facing) interfaces per relay instance
- [#6450](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6450) [feature, 2023, 0/1, support] **Detect WAN link is back up after loss, configurable delay, renew WAN ip.** -- w2 ADOPT: link-state driven DHCP client (netlink carrier events) with configurable debounce delay and automatic renew/re-DISCOVER on carrier up
- [#7580](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7580) [support, 2024, 0/1, support] **WAN Reject Leases From Option Not Working** -- w2 SUPPORT: 'reject leases from' misconfigured -> ADOPT: DHCP client option to reject offers from given servers AND to reject RFC1918 offers on WAN with a clear log line
- [#7636](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7636) [feature, 2024, 0/1, support] **Allow editing/deleting static DHCP leases from the UI** -- w2 ADOPT: static reservations editable/deletable from the leases view (unified lease+reservation grid, edit opens reservation form)
- [#7951](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7951) [feature, 2024, 0/1, -] **[feature request] 'Auto collect option data' should show what it is going to set dhcp options to** -- w2 ADOPT: Kea subnet form shows effective (auto-derived) option values greyed out when auto-collect is on; preview of resolved options
- [#9331](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9331) [feature, 2025, 0/1, -] **Kea DHCPv4: "No Router" setting for reservations** -- w2 ADOPT: per-reservation 'routers: none' (explicit empty option 3 override) in Kea host entries; same as #6343
- [#9067](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9067) [feature, 2025, 0/18, support] **DNSMasq: Allow multiple tags for host** -- w1 ADOPT: dnsmasq host reservations accept multiple tags (set:a,set:b) as Vec<Tag> in schema
- [#8941](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8941) [feature, 2025, 0/11, support] **DNSmasq tags are only partially useful due missing modifiers** -- w1 ADOPT: dnsmasq tag conditions modelled as expressions (tag, !tag, wildcard) in schema for ranges/options, not multi-select only
- [#6186](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6186) [support, 2022, 0/8, incomplete] **22.7.9 breaks DHCPv4 for IPv6-enabled devices** -- w1 SUPPORT: v4 DHCP broke on wired v6-capable hosts after upgrade (unknown root cause) -> TEST: dual-stack client acquisition e2e in CI with real DHCP clients
- [#5610](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5610) [support, 2022, 0/7, support] **ipv4 dhclient fails to read address** -- w1 SUPPORT: VLAN-on-WAN dhclient never gets offer -> UX: WAN DHCP client state page showing last DISCOVER/OFFER timestamps + live pcap button
- [#9061](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9061) [feature, 2025, 0/7, incomplete] **Services: ISC DHCPv6: Leases - Hostnames** -- w1 ADOPT: DHCPv6 lease grid shows hostname (from FQDN option/DDNS or correlated DUID->v4 lease) alongside IAID/DUID; server-side search.
- [#8745](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8745) [bug, 2025, 0/6, -] **Kea DHCPv4: Unable to obtain lease when Socket Type is 'udp'** -- w1 ADOPT: Kea dhcp-socket-type raw vs udp exposed with help text (udp needs relay/unicast); TEST: both modes serve broadcast DISCOVER on Linux
- [#9756](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9756) [support, 2026, 0/6, incomplete] **Kea DHCPv4 does not respond to DHCPDISCOVER on VLAN interface despite correct configuration** -- w1 SUPPORT: Kea on VLAN never sees DISCOVER (AP/switch issue) -> UX: per-interface DHCP packet counters + 'listen test' diagnostic showing socket binds
- [#6103](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6103) [support, 2022, 0/5, support] **DHCP server leases not using set default lease time** -- w1 SUPPORT: per-interface default lease time ignored (client requested lease) -> UX: lease view shows requested vs granted vs valid-lifetime
- [#8704](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8704) [feature, 2025, 0/5, support] **Dnsmasq DHCP: allow option6 and option at the same time** -- w1 ADOPT: dual-stack option fields (e.g. domain-search) entered once, emitted to both v4 option and v6 option for dnsmasq/Kea
- [#8632](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8632) [bug, 2025, 0/4, support] **Importing CSV into dnsmasq for static IPs produces an error when changing "host" or "domain" fields of an IP** -- w1 ADOPT: reservation CSV import keyed by stable id/MAC with upsert semantics (update hostname/domain of existing IP), with dry-run diff.
- [#9215](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9215) [bug, 2025, 2/2, support] **Missing hostname (dhcp option 12) with KEA** -- w1 ADOPT: Kea echoes/sets option 12 host-name from reservation hostname (or ddns-replace-client-name) so clients receive assigned name
- [#7097](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7097) [feature, 2023, 1/2, -] **Allow overriding ipxe boot filename on DHCP static mappings** -- w1 ADOPT: per-reservation boot-file-name/next-server override in Kea host entries (iPXE script per host), inheriting subnet defaults
- [#9375](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9375) [feature, 2025, 1/2, support] **Add a way to change DHCP assignment direction** -- w1 ADOPT: Kea allocator choice per subnet (iterative low->high default, random, flq); v6 pools default to low addresses for readability
- [#9795](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9795) [feature, 2026, 1/2, support] **Feature Request [KEA DHCP]: Alias use in IP/Host Fields** -- w1 ADOPT: Kea subnet option fields (DNS, NTP, routers) accept host alias references, expanded at config generation
- [#6009](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6009) [feature, 2022, 0/2, support] **Make Use of aliases in dhcp** -- w1 ADOPT: MAC aliases reusable in Kea reservations/client-classes (shared MAC/host alias model across firewall and DHCP)
- [#6211](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6211) [feature, 2022, 0/2, incomplete] **Would like to sort SERVICES: DHCPV4: [LAN] static mapping reservations by IP address** -- w1 ADOPT: reservations grid with server-side sort by IP (numeric), MAC, hostname; show pool-gap view of free addresses
- [#7124](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7124) [bug, 2024, 0/2, incomplete] **DHCP client does not send "vendor-class-identifier" (option 60) in DHCP request.** -- w1 N/A-LINUX: ISC dhclient option naming quirk; sensr DHCP client exposes option 60 vendor-class-identifier as a typed field with its RFC name
- [#7340](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7340) [support, 2024, 0/2, incomplete] **DHCP server on one interface can receive requests from clients connected to another interface.** -- w1 SUPPORT: hypervisor bridge leaked DHCP broadcasts across segments -> UX: Kea lease view shows ingress interface; auto rules for DHCP are per-interface only
- [#7528](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7528) [feature, 2024, 0/2, -] **Kea: support hub and spoke HA configurations** -- w1 ADOPT: Kea HA modelled as named relationships (hot-standby, load-balancing, hub-and-spoke) in schema, not a single peer pair
- [#7674](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7674) [feature, 2024, 0/2, incomplete] **Kea DHCP Server - Sorting** -- w1 ADOPT: subnets grid default-sorted by CIDR, reservations by subnet then numeric IP; server-side sort on all columns
- [#8081](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8081) [bug, 2024, 0/2, -] **Kea refuses to make DHCP offer with ERROR: MBZ nibble 0x6 != 0** -- w1 ADOPT: Kea lenient-option-parsing / ignore-rai-link-selection toggles exposed for non-compliant clients (MBZ nibble) with per-subnet scope
- [#8420](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8420) [feature, 2025, 0/2, incomplete] **MAC address alias not supported in DHCP static mapping** -- w1 ADOPT: reservation MAC field accepts a MAC alias reference (typed union MacAddr / AliasRef), expanded at Kea config generation
- [#8636](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8636) [bug, 2025, 0/2, -] **[Kea] Unable to disable pushing Search Domains via DHCP** -- w1 ADOPT: explicit 'do not send domain-search/domain-name' toggle per Kea subnet instead of magic '.' value; validate accordingly
- [#7633](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7633) [feature, 2024, 0/1, -] **When in Services: ISC DHCPv4: Leases and setting a static IP have an option to set an Host Alias on the Services: ISC DH** -- w1 ADOPT: when creating reservation from lease, optional 'add to alias' field (shared host alias model, see #5849/#8067)
- [#8506](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8506) [feature, 2025, 0/1, support] **Add ability in the Kea config UI to enable randomize address allocation** -- w1 ADOPT: Kea allocator per subnet (iterative/random/flq) exposed as enum in subnet schema
- [#8685](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8685) [support, 2025, 0/1, support] **SERVICES: ISC DHCPV6: [LAN] Dynamic DNS Update IPv4 Hosted Name Server** -- w1 ADOPT: DHCPv6 DDNS (Kea D2) accepts IPv4 or IPv6 DNS server addresses; address fields are typed IpAddr not IPv6-only.
- [#9870](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9870) [bug, 2026, 0/1, support] **DHCRelay IPv6 doesn't start if DHCP server IP is reachable over an IPSEC WAN interface** -- w1 ADOPT: DHCPv6 relay accepts upstream server reachable only via routed tunnel (no link-local requirement); validator explains interface/server mismatch

### 3.7 DNS (Unbound / dnsmasq / DDNS) (101)

- [#8075](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8075) [bug, 2024, 4/40, support] **24.7.8 - ISC DHCPv4 does not register new leases in Unbound** -- w3 TEST: lease->Unbound registration end-to-end test (new lease, renew, expire) run in CI on each release; 40 comments shows regression pain
- [#8895](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8895) [bug, 2025, 0/24, incomplete] **DNSmasq: Fails to start during boot** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: DNS/DHCP units ordered after the interfaces they bind (netlink wait, bind-dynamic); WireGuard interfaces created before dependents; TEST boot with wg interface
- [#5550](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5550) [bug, 2022, 0/19, -] **Unbound: DHCP registration does not sanitize names causing unbound to fail** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: lease hostnames sanitized to valid DNS labels (or skipped with log) before Unbound local-data; Unbound config always passes checkconf; same as #9758
- [#8051](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8051) [bug, 2024, 0/17, -] **Unbound host overrides breaks when adding a wildcard entry** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: validate wildcard + explicit host coexistence in same domain and generate Unbound local-zone redirect correctly; config passes checkconf before apply
- [#7426](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7426) [bug, 2024, 1/11, support] **No DNS resolution for 10 mins when specific interface's state changes to UP** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: link-up events trigger a debounced, idempotent reconcile of only affected services; no per-interface hook scripts that can loop for 10 min
- [#7684](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7684) [bug, 2024, 0/11, incomplete] **Unbound not reliably starting on boot up** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: systemd unit ordering (After=network-online, bind-to interface addr with retries/wildcard listen); TEST: boot with slow interfaces still starts resolver
- [#9322](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9322) [bug, 2025, 4/3, support] **Unbound DNS crashes when internet connection is lost** -- w3 TEST: resolver survives WAN loss and recovers when link returns (infra-cache expiry, no crash); chaos test toggling WAN; systemd Restart=on-failure
- [#7376](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7376) [bug, 2024, 2/3, -] **24.1.5 - ISC DHCPv4 does not register new leases in Unbound** -- w3 TEST: lease-commit hook (Kea lease_cmds / hook script) must push A/PTR into Unbound without restart; integration test for new lease -> resolvable
- [#9758](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9758) [bug, 2026, 0/4, support] **whitespace character in kea dhcp static lease names causes unbound to break** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: reservation hostname is a typed DNS label (RFC1123) rejecting spaces; Unbound local-data generation escapes/skips invalid names so Unbound never crashes
- [#10155](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10155) [bug, 2026, 0/4, support] **bug: unbound changed default access list on upgrade from allow to deny** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: explicit versioned schema migrations tested in CI carry defaults (ACL allow/deny); no implicit default flips on upgrade; TEST upgrade fixture corpus
- [#10026](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10026) [bug, 2026, 0/3, support] **Management services (SSH/HTTPS) become unreachable after Unbound restart via pluginctl** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: resolver restart never touches nftables/NAT state; independent systemd units, ruleset changes only on config apply; TEST mgmt reachable after restart
- [#9587](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9587) [bug, 2026, 1/1, support] **Unbound DNS overrides not resolving until next Unbound restart** -- w3 TEST: adding an override applies immediately via unbound-control local_data (and cache flush of that name); e2e test resolves within 1 s of apply
- [#10144](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10144) [bug, 2026, 0/2, support] **unbound: apply can leave generated runtime files stale after #8888** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: apply = validate -> render ALL derived runtime files -> reload atomically in one transaction; saved config and running config cannot diverge
- [#6866](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6866) [bug, 2023, 0/1, -] **Unbound DNSBL update failure causes unbound to ignore/remove old DNSBL** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: blocklist refresh is transactional: keep last-good compiled list when any download fails; surface failure as alert, never unload
- [#7982](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7982) [bug, 2024, 0/1, support] **Unbound DNS fails to start after reboot - manual start works** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: resolver unit waits for interface addresses (or uses interface-automatic/ip-freebind) with systemd restart on failure; TEST boot ordering; same as #7684
- [#9830](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9830) [bug, 2026, 0/1, -] **Unbound dont use Port 853 for DNS over TLS (DoT)** -- w3 TEST: DoT forwarder generated as addr@853#authname; pcap/unit test asserts port 853 used for TLS upstreams on v4 and v6
- [#8952](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8952) [feature, 2025, 2/29, support] **unbound: per-subnet host overrides using views** -- w2 ADOPT: per-interface/subnet host overrides via Unbound views (access-control-view + view local-data); override record has optional 'scope: interfaces[]'
- [#6722](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6722) [bug, 2023, 7/19, support] **Blocked CNAME of allowed domain not able to easily be allowed** -- w2 ADOPT: DNS blocklist allowlist semantics: allowing a domain also permits its CNAME chain targets for that query (Unbound respip/cname handling); UI to allow from query log.
- [#5714](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5714) [bug, 2022, 0/16, support] **Unbound always uses DoT for Query Forwarding** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: forward-tls-upstream is an explicit per-forwarder field in schema (plain vs DoT), never implied by a global TLS setting
- [#6652](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6652) [bug, 2023, 0/16, support] **Unbound DNS aliases do not appear in list in web GUI** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: grid data comes from the same API the backend reads; typed parent reference (UUID) in alias model so entries cannot save but not list
- [#10228](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10228) [feature, 2026, 0/13, support] **[feature request] OpenVPN: register assigned client IPs on an DNS server** -- w2 ADOPT: VPN (OpenVPN/WireGuard/IPsec) client address assignments publish to Unbound local-data via event hook (client-connect -> DNS API) (c=13).
- [#5712](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5712) [feature, 2022, 0/12, -] **Unbound blocklist (DNSBL) per interface** -- w2 ADOPT: Unbound blocklist (RPZ/local-zone) applied per interface/client-set via views or tags (access-control-tag), not global only
- [#7819](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7819) [feature, 2024, 6/4, -] **Unbound: Allow multiple IPs for Host and Domain Overrides** -- w2 ADOPT: host/domain overrides accept multiple A/AAAA addresses per name (Vec<IpAddr>) for round-robin and dual-stack
- [#9736](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9736) [bug, 2026, 2/8, support] **Unbound Query Forwarding randomly stops working** -- w2 ADOPT: forward-zone health monitoring (per-upstream SERVFAIL/timeouts) with Prometheus metrics; TEST local-domain forward to dnsmasq under load; same as #7639
- [#10051](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10051) [bug, 2026, 0/10, support] **Unbound DNSBL prevents resolver startup: generated python-script path breaks unbound-checkconf** -- w2 N/A-LINUX: Unbound python module path in chroot; sensr feeds blocklists as RPZ/local-zone data via unbound-control, no python module; checkconf gate before apply
- [#7053](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7053) [bug, 2023, 0/9, support] **unbound stats page leads to memory leak -> OOM kill** -- w2 ADOPT: DNS query reporting stored in bounded SQLite/ring buffer with backpressure; TEST: reporting page under heavy query rate stays within memory budget
- [#8207](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8207) [feature, 2025, 1/8, support] **Unbound needs certificate picker to serve DoT/DoH** -- w2 ADOPT: Unbound DoT/DoH listener with certificate picker from cert store (ACME-renewable), auto reload on cert renewal
- [#5804](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5804) [feature, 2022, 0/8, feature] **Unbound Host overrides not honored for DHCP registered IPs** -- w2 ADOPT: Unbound DHCP registration uses per-subnet domain from Kea (ddns-qualifying-suffix) and host overrides take precedence over lease registrations.
- [#7272](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7272) [bug, 2024, 0/8, support] **Local nameserver still used for DNS even if advised not to do** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: system resolv.conf is derived strictly from declared 'system nameservers' list; 'use local resolver' is an explicit boolean, independent of what listens on :53
- [#7990](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7990) [bug, 2024, 1/7, support] **Regression: Update DNS with static entries without IP** -- w2 ADOPT: IP-less reservations register in DNS from the live lease (join by MAC) when 'register reservations' is on; TEST regression
- [#8439](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8439) [bug, 2025, 0/8, -] **Unbound repeatedly crashes due to Python mount issues in chroot environment** -- w2 N/A-LINUX: FreeBSD chroot + Python module mount quirk; sensr Unbound runs as systemd-sandboxed unit with no Python module, lease updates via unbound-control
- [#6673](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6673) [feature, 2023, 0/7, feature] **Alternate Hostnames to bypass the DNS Rebinding Attack checks not working** -- w2 ADOPT: rebind-protection allowlist (rebind-domain-ok / Unbound private-domain) is a resolver-level setting distinct from GUI alternate hostnames
- [#9124](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9124) [bug, 2025, 0/7, support] **Unbound: existing aliases are not shown in list unless the specific host is chosen in the table above** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: aliases grid lists all CNAMEs with parent column by default; master-detail filter optional; same as #6652/#5696
- [#10281](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10281) [bug, 2026, 0/7, support] **Unbound DNS Blocklist does not trust user-defined Certificate Authorities** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: one system trust store (user CAs merged into the bundle used by all HTTP fetchers: blocklists, updates, ACME); TEST fetch from internal CA host
- [#6311](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6311) [bug, 2023, 0/5, support] **Unbound is slow when IPv6 is allowed but a connection not present** -- w2 ADOPT: resolver do-ip6/prefer-ip4 auto-derived from whether a usable global v6 default route exists (re-applied on route change)
- [#6525](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6525) [bug, 2023, 4/1, -] **Unbound: local-zone should be configurable** -- w2 ADOPT: local-zone type (static/transparent/typetransparent/redirect) configurable per domain override and for system domain incl. home.arpa handling per RFC8375
- [#6789](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6789) [feature, 2023, 0/5, support] **Unbound or Dnsmasq to Override DNS Hostnames on the Interface level** -- w2 ADOPT: per-interface/per-client-set host overrides via Unbound views (access-control-view) so the same name resolves differently per segment
- [#7193](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7193) [other, 2024, 3/2, support] **Change default domain from OPNsense.local to OPNsense.internal** -- w2 ADOPT: default domain for new installs is `<host>.internal` (IANA private-use TLD), not .local (mDNS collision).
- [#6668](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6668) [support, 2023, 0/4, support] **Stuck ISP DNS Servers in WAN DHCP** -- w2 SUPPORT: ISP DNS stuck after unticking 'override by DHCP' -> DESIGN-AVOIDS: resolver config derived purely from current declared state on each apply, no stale files
- [#10015](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10015) [bug, 2026, 1/3, support] **Blank Domain Name in DNSMASQ static host reservation** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: reservation domain is Option<Domain> defaulting to instance domain at generation, so CNAMEs/aliases always get a FQDN
- [#10120](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10120) [bug, 2026, 1/3, support] **Unbound 1.24.2 becomes unresponsive repeatedly after OPNsense 26.1.6 upgrade — process alive, not answering queries** -- w2 ADOPT: resolver liveness probe (systemd watchdog / periodic local query) that restarts a hung Unbound and alerts; no in-process Python module (RPZ instead)
- [#6041](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6041) [bug, 2022, 1/2, upstream] **unbound stops responding reliably under certain conditions** -- w2 ADOPT: large blocklists loaded via unbound-control local_zones/RPZ in chunks or as RPZ zone file, with memory sizing warnings; TEST: 500k-entry list stays responsive
- [#7168](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7168) [bug, 2024, 1/2, support] **Unbound sometimes not updating IPV6 prefix for LAN interface** -- w2 TEST: firewall's own AAAA local-data updated on tracked-interface prefix change (addr netlink event -> unbound-control local_data_remove/add); same as #8052
- [#7207](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7207) [bug, 2024, 0/3, -] **unexpected Unbound DNS blocklist caching** -- w2 ADOPT: blocklist refresh honors configured interval/ETag/If-Modified-Since with no hidden min-age cache; 'force refresh' action in GUI/API
- [#7532](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7532) [feature, 2024, 1/2, -] **Unbound:  Make it easier to enable or disable blocklists** -- w2 ADOPT: blocklist enforcement global toggle + timed pause (e.g. 5 min) via API/GUI/dashboard widget, per-client bypass set
- [#7906](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7906) [bug, 2024, 0/3, -] **unbound: remote control certificate creation problem when system date is way off** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: unbound-control over unix socket (control-interface: path) needs no TLS certs; no time-dependent self-signed artefacts
- [#9874](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9874) [feature, 2026, 1/2, -] **Unbound: override `local-zone` setting when setting up Query Forwarding/DNS over TLS** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: adding a forward zone for a special-use domain (home.arpa, RFC1918 reverse) auto-emits matching local-zone nodefault/transparent; same as #6525
- [#5828](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5828) [bug, 2022, 0/2, incomplete] **Unbound : wilcard * refused in Host overrides** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: host override 'host' typed as DnsLabel / Wildcard; validator and help text come from the same schema so they can't disagree
- [#5916](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5916) [feature, 2022, 0/2, support] **Unbound DNS over TLS handshake fail ambiguity** -- w2 ADOPT: DoT upstream health view showing TLS verify failure reason (expired cert, SNI mismatch, unreachable) per forwarder, from Unbound logs/probe
- [#6611](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6611) [bug, 2023, 0/2, incomplete] **unbound not Register new DHCP leases** -- w2 TEST: new lease triggers Unbound local-data update via unbound-control (no restart); integration test resolves hostname within N s of lease grant
- [#8796](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8796) [feature, 2025, 0/2, feature] **Reporting: Unbound DNS - Only 1000 entries available in details** -- w2 ADOPT: DNS query log stored in bounded DB with full retention window searchable server-side (paged), not a fixed 1000-row view.
- [#8870](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8870) [bug, 2025, 0/2, support] **dnsmasq hosts not deleted after changing ip adress** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: dnsmasq hosts file is fully regenerated from declared reservations on every apply (no incremental append), so stale entries cannot persist
- [#8889](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8889) [feature, 2025, 0/2, support] **Better Support For Dual Stack Operation In Unbound Overrides** -- w2 ADOPT: host override holds both A and AAAA (Vec<IpAddr>) under one record; CNAME aliases attach to the record, not to an address family
- [#9203](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9203) [other, 2025, 0/2, incomplete] **OpenVPN interface not available in Unbound Network interfaces selection list** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: every VPN instance is a first-class interface in the model, so Unbound/Kea interface selectors list it automatically
- [#9209](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9209) [feature, 2025, 0/2, support] **Unbound DNS Aliases – Option to automatically link both A and AAAA records** -- w2 ADOPT: CNAME alias attaches to one dual-stack host record returning both A and AAAA; same as #8889
- [#9277](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9277) [bug, 2025, 0/2, support] **Dnsmasq domain override not used for static hostnames: possible regression?** -- w2 TEST: static reservations get the subnet/instance domain appended identically to dynamic leases (dnsmasq domain=/expand-hosts); regression test
- [#7231](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7231) [bug, 2024, 0/1, upstream] **Unbound Segfaults Every Several Days** -- w2 ADOPT: systemd Restart=on-failure for resolver with crash counter alert; coredump capture surfaced in GUI
- [#8052](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8052) [bug, 2024, 0/1, -] **Unbound: Require manual restart to populate system AAAA record** -- w2 TEST: system hostname A+AAAA local-data regenerated when interface IPv6 address (SLAAC/PD) arrives after resolver start; netlink addr event -> unbound-control local_data
- [#8223](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8223) [feature, 2025, 0/1, -] **Unbound: Do not add forwarding domains to private-domains** -- w2 ADOPT: forward zones do not auto-add to private-domain; separate explicit 'rebind-allowed domains' list
- [#9447](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9447) [feature, 2025, 0/1, support] **Unbound - Option to add local zone static entries for reverse DNS look ups** -- w2 ADOPT: local reverse zones (in-addr.arpa/ip6.arpa for all local prefixes incl. delegated PD) auto-declared static so PTR lookups answer locally, with manual PTR entries
- [#10169](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10169) [bug, 2026, 0/1, support] **[Dnsmasq] DNS queries containing the system domain don't get forwarded to the next dns server since v26** -- w2 TEST: forward zone for the system domain overrides local authoritative handling (dnsmasq local=/domain/ not emitted when a server=/domain/ exists); regression test
- [#8416](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8416) [bug, 2025, 0/18, support] **dnsmasq: strict-order functionality not working as expected** -- w1 ADOPT: dnsmasq upstream strategy exposed (strict-order vs all-servers) with retry-on-timeout semantics documented; prefer Unbound default
- [#8825](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8825) [feature, 2025, 0/16, support] **[Feature Request] Dynamic DNS alternate host name for registration for DHCP clients** -- w1 ADOPT: reservation 'additional DNS names' (CNAME/alias list) registered into Unbound/DDNS alongside the primary hostname
- [#5104](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5104) [feature, 2021, 3/12, feature] **Unbound for DoH** -- w1 ADOPT: Unbound DoH listener (http-port + TLS cert from cert store) alongside DoT, exposed as typed resolver listener config
- [#8633](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8633) [feature, 2025, 0/13, support] **Add the "domain" and "expand-hosts" options to dnsmasq** -- w1 ADOPT: dnsmasq 'domain' + 'expand-hosts' per instance/per interface (global local domain appended to short hostnames)
- [#8424](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8424) [feature, 2025, 3/7, -] **[Feature Request] unbound: support for Hyperlocal caching (RFC 7706)** -- w1 ADOPT: Unbound auth-zone root hyperlocal (RFC 8806) toggle with primary IP list and zonefile cache

- [#9558](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9558) [bug, 2025, 3/2, support] **Bring back the un-filtered list of aliases in the overrides section** -- w1 ADOPT: Unbound/DNS override alias pickers list all alias types (filter by applicability but allow override); don't silently hide host aliases (rx=3).
- [#6060](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6060) [feature, 2022, 0/4, -] **Unbound DNSBL: Show filter list URL/support site in GUI** -- w1 ADOPT: predefined blocklist catalog entries carry URL, maintainer, homepage, entry count and last-fetched; 'which list blocks domain X' lookup
- [#6249](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6249) [other, 2023, 0/4, support] **Potential race condition with DNS blocklist download when forcing requests over a WireGuard tunnel with static routes** -- w1 ADOPT: blocklist/update downloads retry with backoff and wait for network-online + tunnel-up targets; download jobs are retried not one-shot at boot
- [#7741](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7741) [bug, 2024, 1/3, incomplete] **Blocklist.site is defunct** -- w1 ADOPT: blocklist catalog as out-of-band data file with health status (last-modified age) and 'stale/defunct' marker; remove dead sources without a release
- [#8000](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8000) [feature, 2024, 3/1, -] **Unbound DNS: Overrides: optimize alias display** -- w1 ADOPT: host override grid shows alias count/inline alias chips with expandable rows; search covers alias names; same as #5696
- [#8386](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8386) [bug, 2025, 0/4, upstream] **Unbound-Control DNS over TLS Bug (unbound-control Shows tls-upstream: no Despite DNS over TLS Being Enabled)** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: single generated Unbound config (no split include files) so unbound-control introspection matches declared state; effective-config viewer
- [#8615](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8615) [bug, 2025, 0/4, -] **Unbound DNS Errors "Protocol Not Available"** -- w1 N/A-LINUX: FreeBSD socket option error (IP_RECVTOS?) in Unbound; on Linux ensure Nix build enables matching kernel options; smoke test logs clean
- [#6157](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6157) [feature, 2022, 0/3, incomplete] **Unbound dns over https DOH** -- w1 ADOPT: DoH upstream forwarding (Unbound lacks DoH forward; use built-in proxy or dnscrypt-proxy as optional forwarder) plus DoH listener; see #5104
- [#6241](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6241) [support, 2023, 0/3, support] **DNS Issues on 22.7.10_2** -- w1 SUPPORT: sporadic 5-10s DNS latency -> ADOPT: resolver latency metrics (Prometheus histogram per upstream) and a diagnostics page showing them
- [#7177](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7177) [bug, 2024, 1/2, incomplete] **UnboundDNS does not take an ip address and port for Host Overrides.** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: host override typed as IpAddr only (no port; DNS A/AAAA cannot carry ports); help text generated from schema explains; SRV override type added
- [#7464](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7464) [feature, 2024, 0/3, support] **allow use of aliases in `system > settings > general > dns servers` and other dns server input locations** -- w1 ADOPT: system/forwarder DNS server fields accept host alias refs (incl. dynamic IPv6 host aliases) resolved at apply
- [#7642](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7642) [feature, 2024, 0/3, -] **Feature Request: Implement Bootstrap DNS for Unbound** -- w1 ADOPT: DoT upstream by hostname with bootstrap resolver (resolve at apply + periodic re-resolve via unbound-control forward)
- [#7732](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7732) [feature, 2024, 0/3, incomplete] **Unbound blocklist (DNSBL): add "HaGeZi"** -- w1 ADOPT: curated predefined blocklist catalog (incl. HaGeZi multi/pro) shipped as data file updatable independently of release
- [#8527](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8527) [other, 2025, 0/3, support] **Regarding preconfigured blacklists in unbound** -- w1 ADOPT: blocklist catalog maintenance process with freshness checks (CI job flags lists not updated in 180d); same as #7741
- [#8717](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8717) [other, 2025, 0/3, support] **Dnsmasq "Domain" / "Edit Domain Override" shall rename to "DNS Redirect" / "Edit DNS Redirect"** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: schema naming: 'forward zone' (conditional forwarder) vs 'override record'; labels from the same schema in GUI/API/docs
- [#9997](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9997) [bug, 2026, 0/3, support] **unbound: Spurious 'PTR record already exists' warnings for all host override aliases** -- w1 TEST: PTR generation emits one PTR per IP without spurious duplicate warnings for CNAME aliases; unit test on local-data generator
- [#8627](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8627) [feature, 2025, 0/2, support] **Configurable dnsmasq timeout** -- w1 ADOPT: upstream query timeout configurable for forwarders (dnsmasq --dns-timeout equivalent, Unbound infra settings); never cache SERVFAIL-as-NXDOMAIN
- [#9010](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9010) [other, 2025, 0/2, support] **Reword the Unbound DNS "Register ISC DHCP4 Leases" text** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: one 'register DHCP leases' toggle in Unbound with backend-agnostic lease source (Kea/dnsmasq), no vendor-named setting
- [#9038](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9038) [feature, 2025, 0/2, support] **Dnsmasq log warning - No upstream servers configured** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: dnsmasq upstream list explicitly declared (or 'none'); generated config uses no-resolv when resolver-only, avoiding spurious warnings
- [#5696](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5696) [feature, 2022, 0/1, -] **Unbound: new MVC for host overrides --> add multi select to host overrides to be able to view/search all aliases** -- w1 ADOPT: flat searchable grid of all host overrides + aliases (CNAME) with server-side search across both tables
- [#6214](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6214) [feature, 2022, 0/1, -] **[FR] unbound: move override aliases grid to dropdown tab** -- w1 ADOPT: host-override aliases as a flat tab/grid with parent filter instead of master-detail only; same as #5696

- [#6737](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6737) [feature, 2023, 0/1, support] **Unbound Query Forwarding improvements, multiple IPs / Domain names per entry.** -- w1 ADOPT: Unbound forward-zone entries accept multiple upstream servers and multiple domains per entry (list fields in schema).
- [#7597](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7597) [feature, 2024, 0/1, -] **UnboundDNS: Host Override TTL setting unavailable!?** -- w1 ADOPT: per-host-override TTL field with resolver-level default

- [#8030](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8030) [feature, 2024, 0/1, -] **[OPNSense] Allow setting cache_ttl or disabling caching when fetching blocklists** -- w1 ADOPT: blocklist fetch cache TTL configurable + force-refresh; same as #7207
- [#8562](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8562) [bug, 2025, 0/1, support] **a port alias for multiple ports prevents the host DNS (Unbound) to be resolved** -- w1 TEST: alias names and DNS hostnames are separate namespaces; resolver must never consult alias table; TEST same name as port alias and host resolves
