### 3.8 Routing / gateways / multi-WAN (64)

- [#9806](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9806) [bug, 2026, 0/96, support] **BUG [URGENT] [Multi WAN] Gateways does not respect reply-to in 26.1.8** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: reply routing via conntrack mark saved at ingress (ct mark set iif table) + ip rule fwmark; multi-WAN return path independent of pf reply-to (c=96).
- [#7335](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7335) [bug, 2024, 0/28, support] **[ BE 23.10.2, 23.10.3, 24.4.2 ] Automatic fail-over to a Fallback Gateway fails [WORKAROUND]** -- w3 TEST: dual-stack multi-WAN failover: IPv4 gateway down must switch default route while dynamic IPv6 stays; scenario test with gateway groups (c=28).
- [#6576](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6576) [bug, 2023, 0/25, support] **No error message for non-supported multi WAN w/ single gateway IP setup** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: per-WAN routing tables + interface-bound probes support duplicate upstream gateway IPs; validator warns otherwise (c=25).
- [#7163](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7163) [feature, 2024, 3/20, -] **Multiple monitor IPs for gateway monitoring** -- w3 ADOPT: gateway health check with multiple monitor targets (ICMP/TCP/HTTP) and quorum/any/all policy per gateway (c=20, rx=3).
- [#9316](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9316) [bug, 2025, 0/13, incomplete] **when using gateway groups/failover OPNSENSE is not returning connections back to the primary/higher WAN** -- w3 ADOPT: failback to higher-tier gateway when it recovers: re-plan ip rules/default route, optionally flush conntrack entries pinned to the backup WAN. TEST: fail+recover.
- [#8189](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8189) [bug, 2025, 0/4, support] **[Security issue] Firewall rules with force gateway switches back to default gateway** -- w3 ADOPT: policy-routed rule option 'drop if gateway down' (no fallback to default route) enforced via fwmark table with unreachable default (security).
- [#6719](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6719) [bug, 2023, 0/2, incomplete] **ICMP reply sent to wrong interface on floating rule with wildcard interfaces** -- w3 ADOPT: replies to locally-destined traffic on secondary WAN egress the same interface (fwmark + ip rule per ingress ifindex); integration test dual-WAN ping
- [#9511](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9511) [bug, 2025, 0/2, support] **Multi-WAN failover doesn't automatically reload filter rules on gateway state change** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: policy routes point to gateway-group fwmark tables; health checker rewrites routing table on state change, nftables rules untouched. Test failover.
- [#9165](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9165) [bug, 2025, 0/18, support] **DPINGER Error 65 After Upgrade, Both Gateways Show Offline** -- w2 TEST: gateway monitor must bind to the correct source address after upgrade/IP change; integration test for health-check after WAN re-address (no EHOSTUNREACH loop).
- [#7516](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7516) [bug, 2024, 0/17, support] **after update to 24.1.8 the routing is no longer correct** -- w2 TEST: upgrade regression test comparing routing table before/after generation switch for static routes with gateway groups; routes are declarative and re-applied on boot.
- [#9605](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9605) [bug, 2026, 0/17, support] **If a disabled route is added all existing matching routes are deleted** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: apply computes diff of desired routes; a disabled route object contributes nothing and cannot delete connected routes (c=17). Test.
- [#7850](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7850) [bug, 2024, 0/11, incomplete] **Wireguard VPN gateway loss of connection** -- w2 ADOPT: gateway monitor keeps probing offline tunnel gateways and WG re-resolves endpoint / re-sends handshake after WAN change (netlink address-change trigger)
- [#6170](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6170) [bug, 2022, 0/10, support] **[BUG] Interface routes not installed in FRR routing table (Zebra)** -- w2 ADOPT: FRR integration: interface-gateway (point-to-point WireGuard) static routes are installed via zebra/netlink so BGP next-hops resolve; test with WG + BGP.
- [#7626](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7626) [feature, 2024, 0/9, support] **Gateway Group support for static routes** -- w2 ADOPT: static routes may target a gateway group (failover/ECMP via nexthop groups / policy rules), e.g. prefix over multiple IPsec VTI tunnels.
- [#8108](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8108) [bug, 2024, 0/9, support] **Skip rules when gateway is down doesn't work for gateway group** -- w2 ADOPT: policy rules targeting a gateway group whose members are all down are skipped (or fall to default) when 'skip when down' is set; TEST: all-down group.
- [#8086](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8086) [bug, 2024, 0/8, support] **Gateway monitoring does not work when multiple connections share the same gateway** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: per-interface probe binding, no shared host routes (same as #6342/#6576).
- [#8987](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8987) [bug, 2025, 0/8, support] **Bugs in gateway monitoring and default gateway selection in a multi-WAN failover scenario using Wireguard (Issue 1 of 4)** -- w2 ADOPT: default gateway selection never picks a disabled gateway; failover state machine unit-tested (disabled/down/up transitions) with WG gateways
- [#7556](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7556) [bug, 2024, 0/7, support] **No route to IPv6 gateway added when scatic "far" gateway added.** -- w2 ADOPT: 'far gateway' flag adds host route to gateway via interface (ip -6 route add gw/128 dev wan) before default route; TEST off-link v6 gateway
- [#7706](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7706) [bug, 2024, 0/7, support] **Outgoing lan rules with specific gateway not functional** -- w2 TEST: policy-routing rule with URL-table alias destination + explicit gateway keeps fwmark/ip rule after alias refresh; regression test.
- [#5866](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5866) [feature, 2022, 4/2, -] **Improve multi-WAN failover resiliency: multiple IP monitoring per gateway before taking down, and auto DHCP renewal when** -- w2 ADOPT: gateway health-check with multiple monitor targets + quorum, and auto DHCP renew/link re-up when gateway recovers (bridged VM NICs never lose link)
- [#8181](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8181) [bug, 2025, 0/6, support] **Problem with multi-wan PPPoE interfaces since 24.7.11** -- w2 TEST: two PPPoE WANs with identical peer gateway IP both usable (per-table routing); regression test in multi-WAN suite.
- [#6342](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6342) [bug, 2023, 0/5, support] **`Disable Host Route` has no effect in 23.x** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: monitor probes bound to interface (SO_BINDTODEVICE) need no host routes at all; 'disable host route' setting disappears.
- [#7637](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7637) [bug, 2024, 0/5, support] **Gateway monitoring remote IPs don't get removed from routing table when removed from monitoring** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: monitor-host routes are owned by the declarative routing state; apply diff removes routes when monitor IP is removed.
- [#5778](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5778) [bug, 2022, 0/4, support] **dpinger monitoring wrong interface in multi-wan with duplicate gateway ips** -- w2 ADOPT: gateway health checker binds to interface (SO_BINDTODEVICE) and source IP so duplicate gateway IPs on multi-WAN are monitored independently.
- [#5959](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5959) [bug, 2022, 0/4, support] **No static route entry for gateway with far gateway checked** -- w2 ADOPT: 'far gateway' installs onlink host route (ip route add GW dev IF onlink) before default route; integration test off-subnet gateway.
- [#6119](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6119) [bug, 2022, 1/2, incomplete] **Gateway Group GUI is confusing with mixed IPv4/IPv6 Gateways** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: gateway groups are per address family in schema (typed), GUI filters gateways by family and explains tiers; mixed family rejected at validate
- [#6981](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6981) [bug, 2023, 0/3, support] **Unable to use a Gateway group (LB/FO) for routing Wireguard VPN traffic.** -- w2 ADOPT: gateway groups must accept WireGuard tunnel gateways (far gateway/interface-only nexthop) with health probes via the tunnel; test LB/FO over 2 WG
- [#9571](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9571) [bug, 2025, 0/3, support] **Multi-WAN IPv6: SLAAC Gateway Monitor marks Public IP as Offline after time** -- w2 ADOPT: v6 gateway monitor sources probes from a stable global address (not temporary/privacy addr) on SLAAC WANs; use_tempaddr=0 on WAN
- [#5688](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5688) [feature, 2022, 0/2, incomplete] **Multi-WAN Load Balancing with Sticky Connections using Source/Destination.** -- w2 ADOPT: multi-WAN load-balance hashing policy selectable: per-flow, per-source, per-src+dst (nftables numgen/jhash with ct mark persistence).
- [#6203](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6203) [feature, 2022, 1/1, support] **Policy based routing - TLD based** -- w2 ADOPT: DNS-driven policy routing: Unbound ipset/nftset module populates per-domain sets used by policy-route rules (domain -> gateway).
- [#6486](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6486) [bug, 2023, 0/2, support] **No "route-to" rule for gateway group will be created when using dynamic gateway members** -- w2 ADOPT: gateway groups with dynamic-IP members (WireGuard/PPPoE) must resolve at apply time and re-plan on address change; TEST: group of two dynamic gws does policy routing.
- [#6573](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6573) [feature, 2023, 0/2, incomplete] **http-ping for gateway checking** -- w2 ADOPT: gateway health probes support ICMP, TCP connect, HTTP(S) GET and DNS query (see #7163 multi-target).
- [#7406](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7406) [bug, 2024, 0/2, support] **PPPoE IPv6 gateway always marked as offline if gateway monitoring is enabled** -- w2 ADOPT: v6 gateway health check sources from a global address (not LL) and supports monitor over PPPoE; auto-select probe target when gateway is fe80
- [#8988](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8988) [bug, 2025, 0/2, support] **Bugs in gateway monitoring and default gateway selection in a multi-WAN failover scenario using Wireguard (Issue 2 of 4)** -- w2 TEST: gateway monitor state transitions on WG failover (part 2 of 4 series); property tests for default-gateway election
- [#8989](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8989) [bug, 2025, 0/2, support] **Bugs in gateway monitoring and default gateway selection in a multi-WAN failover scenario using Wireguard (Issue 3 of 4)** -- w2 TEST: gateway monitor/default gateway selection WG failover (part 3 of 4); scenario tests covering return-to-primary after recovery
- [#8990](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8990) [bug, 2025, 0/2, support] **Bugs in gateway monitoring and default gateway selection in a multi-WAN failover scenario using Wireguard (Issue 4 of 4)** -- w2 TEST: gateway monitor/default gateway selection WG failover (part 4 of 4); ensure routes re-installed when gateway returns online
- [#9230](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9230) [feature, 2025, 0/2, incomplete] **More robust gateway monitoring** -- w2 ADOPT: robust gateway monitoring: multi-target quorum, TCP/HTTP probes, hysteresis, jitter/loss thresholds (consolidates #7163/#6573/#5786).
- [#9464](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9464) [bug, 2025, 1/1, support] **default route is lost since 25.7.8 upgrade** -- w2 TEST: default route presence after reboot/upgrade for static and DHCP WAN; routes are declarative state reconciled at boot, not scripts.
- [#5786](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5786) [feature, 2022, 0/1, support] **Multi Wan Failover, Add Stability Setting for Fail-Back** -- w2 ADOPT: gateway failback hysteresis: hold-down timer and flap damping before returning to preferred gateway.
- [#5815](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5815) [bug, 2022, 0/1, support] **Wrong behaviour with Dpinger while monitoring Gateways.** -- w2 ADOPT: own Rust gateway health checker with per-gateway state machine, exposing status via API/metrics consistently (no racing dpinger sockets).
- [#6160](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6160) [bug, 2022, 0/1, -] **static prefix routes to networks bigger than /64 won't survive reload / reconnect** -- w2 TEST: static IPv6 routes with prefix shorter than /64 survive interface reconnect/reload; routes re-applied from declarative state on link events.
- [#7320](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7320) [bug, 2024, 0/1, -] **Gateway Group GUI is confusing with mixed IPv4/IPv6 Gateways** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: gateway groups typed per address family; see #6119
- [#7536](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7536) [bug, 2024, 0/1, support] **Gateway groups are ignored if you use policy based routing in combination with an OpenVPN connection** -- w2 ADOPT: gateway groups over VPN client tunnels with failover/round-robin via nftables fwmark + nexthop groups; integration test two OpenVPN gateways
- [#7623](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7623) [bug, 2024, 0/1, support] **IP prefix assigned on a disabled interface was still being routed** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: disabled interface contributes no prefixes/routes in rendered networkd state (same as #6134).
- [#8583](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8583) [bug, 2025, 0/1, support] **Gateway monitoring does not set a interface as up again** -- w2 TEST: gateway recovers to 'up' autonomously without GUI poll (health checker is a daemon with event bus, not GUI-triggered).
- [#9003](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9003) [feature, 2025, 0/1, support] **Static Route and static fallback Route** -- w2 ADOPT: static routes with metric and optional gateway health tracking (route withdrawn when gateway down) for static fallback routes.
- [#9008](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9008) [bug, 2025, 0/1, support] **Policy based routing rule is being ignored** -- w2 TEST: policy-routing (fwmark + ip rule) persists across upgrades and rule-module migrations; upgrade-path scenario test with PBR rules.
- [#9570](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9570) [bug, 2025, 0/1, support] **Gateway Widget: WAN2 IPv6 (SLAAC) disappears from Dashboard after some time** -- w2 TEST: SLAAC-learned default gateway kept in gateway list across RA refresh (lifetime renewal, not delete/re-add); widget reflects it
- [#10045](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10045) [bug, 2026, 0/1, support] **Loopback route lost during WAN interface flap** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: loopback managed by networkd, independent of WAN events; reconcile loop re-asserts declared routes after link flaps. Test flap.
- [#10194](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10194) [bug, 2026, 0/1, support] **opnsense using for some traffic automatic generated rule instead specific rule for LAN interface, resulting using wrong ** -- w2 ADOPT: 'let out from firewall host' auto-rule scoped to locally-originated traffic only (meta skuid/oif output chain), never matching forwarded LAN traffic.
- [#8236](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8236) [bug, 2025, 0/5, support] **Crashing after 24.7.11 > 24.7.12 Upgrade – memory usage keeps increasing until crash** -- w1 N/A-LINUX: FreeBSD fib_algo memory leak. Still: Prometheus memory metrics + alert on monotonically growing RSS of core daemons, and crash capture.
- [#9251](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9251) [support, 2025, 0/5, support] **remove inactive gateway is set to active and not removed.** -- w1 SUPPORT: stale dynamic gateway lingering -> UX: gateways derived from interface model; DHCP/PPP gateways vanish when interface disabled, no orphan objects.
- [#8259](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8259) [bug, 2025, 0/4, support] **Wrong WAN address in gateway** -- w1 TEST: PPPoE gateway address shown in GUI equals kernel peer address after reconnect (netlink watch, no cached file).
- [#5555](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5555) [bug, 2022, 0/3, support] **Gateway monitoring fails for 2nd wireguard interface** -- w1 TEST: gateway monitor for second WireGuard interface works independently (one dpinger-like probe per gateway with bound source); test wg0+wg1 monitoring
- [#8217](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8217) [bug, 2025, 0/3, support] **WebUI Crashes when changing a route or syncing HA configs** -- w1 TEST: route add/remove and HA sync are async jobs; GUI never blocks on networkd; load test route changes while API stays responsive.
- [#6587](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6587) [bug, 2023, 0/2, incomplete] **FRR not updating connected IPs properly when removing Virtual IPs from loopback interfaces** -- w1 TEST: FRR redistribute-connected reflects loopback VIP removal (netlink event -> zebra); integration test add/remove loopback address with OSPF.
- [#7974](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7974) [bug, 2024, 1/1, -] **dpinger picks "default" IPv6 bind address for multipoint WireGuard tunnel** -- w1 ADOPT: gateway monitor source address selection per gateway honors the interface address matching the monitored family/prefix; explicit source override field
- [#9822](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9822) [bug, 2026, 0/2, support] **dpinger with default config reports incorrect latency** -- w1 TEST: health checker latency equals ping RTT for default payload size; unit test timing math and payload defaults.
- [#6315](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6315) [support, 2023, 0/1, support] **Multi Wan setup from same ISP only one works some times** -- w1 SUPPORT: same-ISP dual WAN with identical gateway IP -> UX: per-interface routing tables (ip rule + table per WAN) make this work; validator explains.
- [#6497](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6497) [support, 2023, 0/1, support] **Traffic landing up on Wrong Interface** -- w1 SUPPORT: policy-route override vs gateway group leaves stale conntrack entries -> UX: flush/rebind affected conntrack on route-policy change (kill-states option)
- [#7881](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7881) [bug, 2024, 0/1, -] **Misalignment between Gateway "Loss Interval" help and dpinger output** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: help text generated from the schema field doc, so units/defaults (loss interval in ms vs multiplier) can't drift from code.
- [#9555](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9555) [support, 2025, 0/1, support] **Opnsense documents on Proton road warrior guide** -- w1 SUPPORT: ICMP gateway monitor banned by ProtonVPN -> UX: per-gateway monitor settings (interval, disable, TCP/HTTP probe alternatives) in docs/wizard.
- [#9696](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9696) [feature, 2026, 0/1, -] **allow for tcptraceroute** -- w1 ADOPT: diagnostics traceroute supports ICMP/UDP/TCP (SYN to port) and source interface selection.
- [#9556](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9556) [feature, 2025, 0/1, support] **Feature request to change the order of the static routes** -- w0 ADOPT: static route grid with sort/search by destination/gateway/interface; order is irrelevant (kernel LPM + metric) and UI says so.

### 3.9 HA / CARP / config sync (28)

- [#7333](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7333) [feature, 2024, 11/31, incomplete] **Allow WAN Interface to be enabled/disabled based on CARP Status** -- w3 ADOPT: WAN failover with a single public IP: keepalived brings WAN interface/DHCP client up only on MASTER (notify scripts); shared MAC option (c=31, rx=11).
- [#6473](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6473) [feature, 2023, 0/5, incomplete] **IPSEC must stay on master node** -- w3 ADOPT: HA-aware services: IPsec/WireGuard/OpenVPN bound to a VRRP VIP start only on MASTER and stop on BACKUP via keepalived notify hooks
- [#8471](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8471) [bug, 2025, 0/2, support] **OpenVPN Instances as Client keeps connecting - waiting - reconnecting on both MASTER and BACKUP nodes** -- w3 ADOPT: HA-aware VPN client instances: run only on VRRP MASTER (keepalived notify), stopped on BACKUP (same as 6473)
- [#8971](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8971) [bug, 2025, 0/2, incomplete] **pfsync corrupts standby** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: replication transfers UUID-keyed typed objects, never positional interface indexes; schema validation on receiver rejects mismatches.
- [#8059](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8059) [bug, 2024, 1/23, support] **[pfsync multiqueue] tcp connections dropped when pfsync is active and multiqueue is enabled** -- w2 TEST: conntrackd state sync with multiqueue NICs must not drop TCP flows; HA soak test with RSS enabled measuring connection failures (c=23).
- [#5930](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5930) [bug, 2022, 0/15, support] **Enter Persistent CARP Maintenance Mode doesn't do anything** -- w2 ADOPT: persistent maintenance mode = explicit keepalived priority demotion persisted in config and shown in status; test that MASTER actually hands over.
- [#6581](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6581) [feature, 2023, 3/10, support] **Expose in GUI the CARP unicast options as an advanced setting** -- w2 ADOPT: VRRP unicast peer mode (unicast_src_ip/unicast_peer) as first-class HA option alongside multicast (rx=3).
- [#5612](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5612) [feature, 2022, 0/11, support] **Have CARP switch to active only when everything else is running** -- w2 ADOPT: VRRP priority gated on readiness: start keepalived after services healthy, track_script for critical services, startup demotion delay.
- [#6539](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6539) [feature, 2023, 0/10, support] **Firewall rule that is active depending on CARP status** -- w2 ADOPT: rule/service condition 'only when VRRP MASTER/BACKUP' (keepalived notify toggles an nftables flag set); usable by WireGuard, outbound rules (c=10).
- [#6122](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6122) [feature, 2022, 0/7, support] **Support CARP with IPv6 peer IP** -- w2 ADOPT: HA peer address IPv4 or IPv6 (config sync API and conntrackd over IPv6); validation accepts both.
- [#10002](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10002) [bug, 2026, 0/7, support] **Captive Portal in HA setups resolves system hostname to interface address instead of CARP VIP, breaking deterministic ac** -- w2 ADOPT: per-zone portal servername resolves to the zone's VRRP VIP (explicit 'portal address' field defaulting to VIP on that interface) in HA.
- [#10159](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10159) [bug, 2026, 1/6, incomplete] **HA Sync Issue: WireGuard "Depend on (CARP)" resetting to "None" on Backup** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: 'depends on VRRP instance' is a reference by VRRP UUID shared across nodes, so it replicates intact; WireGuard starts only on MASTER.
- [#7877](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7877) [bug, 2024, 1/4, support] **Enter Persistent CARP Maintenance Mode not working since 24.7** -- w2 TEST: maintenance mode must demote MASTER (observable via keepalived state) and persist across reboot; integration test with two nodes.
- [#9042](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9042) [bug, 2025, 0/5, support] **IP address conflict** -- w2 ADOPT: config validation rejects a VRRP/VIP address equal to an existing interface address on the same node (whole-config address uniqueness check)
- [#7436](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7436) [bug, 2024, 0/4, support] **Kea DHCP HA "This server name" is overwritten on Opnsense HA update on slave** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: node-local fields (Kea HA this-server-name, hostname) tagged in schema and excluded from HA replication by construction.
- [#9991](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9991) [bug, 2026, 0/3, support] **Kea DHCP High Availability: "This server name" overwritten on HA Sync** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: Kea HA server-name is a node-local schema field excluded from replication (duplicate of #7436).
- [#5554](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5554) [feature, 2022, 0/2, incomplete] **Exclude web interface listening interface from HA synchronisation** -- w2 ADOPT: HA config replication with per-section include/exclude and node-local fields (admin listen, hostname, interfaces IPs) marked in schema.
- [#6994](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6994) [bug, 2023, 0/2, incomplete] **System: High Availability: Settings should check whether web gui listens on specified interface** -- w2 ADOPT: HA settings validation: sync peer address must be reachable and the local API must listen on the sync interface; validate at apply, warn in GUI.
- [#7997](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7997) [bug, 2024, 0/1, -] **System > Configuration > Backups - settings not synchronised to peer in HA setup** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: HA replication syncs the whole typed config minus node-local fields; no per-section opt-in list to forget (backup settings included).
- [#8003](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8003) [bug, 2024, 0/1, -] **Firewall > Settings > Advanced - settings not synchronised to peer in HA setup** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: whole-config replication (see #7997) covers firewall advanced settings automatically.
- [#9183](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9183) [support, 2025, 0/1, support] **No connection on one of connected network for 120sec after rebooting master.** -- w2 ADOPT: HA failback is preemption-configurable with hold timers; BGP-advertised routes follow VRRP master state via FRR hooks so there is no 120s blackhole on reboot.
- [#10289](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10289) [bug, 2026, 0/1, -] **Deleted legacy rules remain on backup firewall in HA cluster** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: replication is full-state (desired config) with deletions, not additive merge; receiver prunes objects absent from sender.
- [#6556](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6556) [feature, 2023, 0/10, -] **Only add CARP FW Filter rules if VIP are configured** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: nftables ruleset generated from config; VRRP allow rules emitted only when a VRRP instance exists on that interface.
- [#8201](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8201) [feature, 2025, 0/9, support] **MAC Interface source in failover mode - CARP unicast - flood trafic** -- w1 ADOPT: VRRP unicast mode + BACKUP node keeps MAC alive (periodic ARP/ND via keepalived or dedicated keepalive) to avoid switch flooding.
- [#8124](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8124) [other, 2024, 0/8, support] **VRRP support: interoperability or proto non-grata?** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: sensr HA is keepalived/VRRP natively, interoperable with MikroTik and other VRRP speakers.
- [#6196](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6196) [support, 2022, 0/1, support] **Question - HA Misconfigurations** -- w1 SUPPORT: HA misconfiguration question -> UX: HA setup wizard + validator (unique VRIDs, peer reachability, matching interfaces) with status page.
- [#8651](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8651) [feature, 2025, 0/1, -] **Allow cron task exclusion in XMLRPC Sync** -- w1 ADOPT: per-object 'local only / do not replicate' flag on cron jobs (and any object) honored by HA replication.
- [#8383](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8383) [feature, 2025, 0/1, -] **feature: Leverage message bus for Firewall session DB sync to allow large scale Active/active/active clustering.** -- w0 OUT-OF-SCOPE: N-node active/active state sync over message bus; core targets 2-node VRRP + conntrackd multicast/unicast sync. Keep sync pluggable.

### 3.10 VPN: WireGuard (41)

- [#7364](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7364) [bug, 2024, 1/55, support] **Wireguard point-to-site handshake broken due to route not added in routing table** -- w3 ADOPT: WG endpoint route must be pinned to physical WAN (ip rule for wg fwmark) so default-via-tunnel never swallows handshake traffic; integration test; c=55
- [#9005](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9005) [bug, 2025, 0/8, incomplete] **After reboot OPNsense 25.7, WireGuard stops working (connection don't work)** -- w3 ADOPT: WG at boot fully configured (addresses, peers, routes, fwmark rules) in one networkd/netlink transaction; integration test reboot -> traffic both ways
- [#6140](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6140) [bug, 2022, 0/5, support] **Wireguard will always use the default gateway** -- w3 ADOPT: WireGuard replies egress the ingress WAN in multi-WAN: fwmark on wg UDP + ip rule per WAN (kernel wg has no reply-to); integration test two WANs
- [#6993](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6993) [bug, 2023, 0/4, upstream] **Wireguard selective routing gateways loop detected** -- w3 ADOPT: WG endpoint traffic pinned to physical WAN via ip rule for fwmark (wg fwmark + table), so policy routing default-via-wg never loops; test
- [#7977](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7977) [feature, 2024, 4/22, feature] **Import Wireguard client from another system and reuse the QR code and wg0.conf in 'edit peer', ability to edit dns serve** -- w2 ADOPT: peer model stores tunnel address, DNS, and client private key (optional) so existing clients can be imported and QR/.conf regenerated later; rx=4 c=22
- [#7679](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7679) [support, 2024, 0/20, support] **Unbound DNS leak - inconsistency** -- w2 SUPPORT: DNS leak via WG gateway group -> ADOPT: Unbound outgoing-interface bound to selected gateway/policy route; show which interface resolver queries egress
- [#10094](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10094) [bug, 2026, 0/18, support] **WireGuard Instance tunnel address applied as 0.0.0.X/8 instead of correct IP** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: tunnel addresses typed as IpNet at parse time; netlink address apply uses struct not strings; test 10.200.0.2/24 round-trip
- [#7468](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7468) [bug, 2024, 0/17, support] **Wireguard interface not coming online after reboot - "required IPv4 interface address could not be found, skipping"** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: WG netdev brought up by networkd regardless of underlay readiness; endpoint DNS resolved with retry (wg reresolve timer); test boot order
- [#8851](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8851) [bug, 2025, 0/16, support] **Multiple WireGuard instances: peer assignment broken for second instance** -- w2 TEST: peers assigned to second instance show in status and are applied to wg1; test multi-instance peer assignment and status by instance
- [#7355](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7355) [bug, 2024, 1/9, support] **Wireguard: No routes are configured if AllowedIPs are outside of the wireguard interface subnet** -- w2 ADOPT: routes for every AllowedIPs prefix installed via tunnel (not only those inside interface subnet) unless 'no routes' set; unit test route derivation
- [#7360](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7360) [feature, 2024, 6/2, -] **WireGuard Peer generator - Config download button** -- w2 ADOPT: WireGuard peer generator offers download of .conf file (named after peer) alongside QR; rx=6
- [#5592](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5592) [support, 2022, 0/6, support] **Traffic routed arbitrarily over the Wireguad Interface despite disabled WG gateway** -- w2 SUPPORT: traffic leaked via WG because AllowedIPs 0.0.0.0/0 installs routes -> UX: 'disable routes' (Table=off) default for policy-routed client tunnels; warn
- [#7439](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7439) [feature, 2024, 3/2, feature] **Wireguard Peer Generator - Improvements for better user experience** -- w2 ADOPT: peer generator: auto-next free tunnel IP, preset endpoint/DNS from instance, remember last values, one-click add+apply; rx=3
- [#9024](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9024) [bug, 2025, 0/4, support] **Newly created Wireguard peer is not active, despite the GUI suggesting so** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: apply diffs peers via netlink (wg set) immediately; status API reflects kernel state so GUI cannot show an unapplied peer as active
- [#9318](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9318) [bug, 2025, 0/4, support] **After startup, the WireGuard tunnel does not function; after restarting the service, everything is OK.** -- w2 ADOPT: WG endpoint FQDN resolved with retry at boot and periodic re-resolve; status shows resolved endpoint; test boot with unresolvable DNS
- [#7849](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7849) [bug, 2024, 0/3, incomplete] **Wireguard VPN and Handshake update** -- w2 ADOPT: WG status derives 'connected' from last-handshake age (< 180s) and rx counters, refreshed live via netlink, not cached
- [#7859](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7859) [bug, 2024, 0/3, support] **Wireguard cannot use same allowed-ips for multiple peers** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: validation rejects overlapping AllowedIPs across peers in one instance (WireGuard cryptokey routing is exclusive) with clear message.
- [#9017](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9017) [bug, 2025, 0/16, support] **After reboot OPNsense 25.7, WireGuard stops working (connection don't work)** -- w1 TEST: duplicate of 9005; reboot test for WG return traffic
- [#6566](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6566) [bug, 2023, 0/15, support] **WireGuard/OpenVPN: tun naming collision** -- w1 N/A-LINUX: /dev/tun naming collision between wireguard-go and OpenVPN; Linux kernel wg uses named netdevs (wg0) and OpenVPN tun names are explicit
- [#8680](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8680) [bug, 2025, 0/14, support] **TCP over IPsec over Wireguard using the Wireguard kernel module** -- w1 TEST: IPsec (ESP) over WireGuard with TCP payload; MTU/MSS stacking (1420-ESP overhead) and nftables flowtable interplay; integration test
- [#10131](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10131) [support, 2026, 0/13, support] **PROTON wireguard connections dropped and not restored** -- w1 SUPPORT: duplicate of 9066 (Proton WG drops behind CGNAT); keepalive + re-resolve + gateway re-probe
- [#8893](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8893) [support, 2025, 0/11, support] **Incomplete User Documentation for WireGuard Site to Site Configuration** -- w1 SUPPORT: split-tunnel S2S LAN clients blocked -> UX: WG wizard creates interface, gateway, routes and allow rules together; checklist shows missing pieces
- [#9377](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9377) [support, 2025, 0/8, support] **Wireguard reports successful handshake when no data is flowing** -- w1 SUPPORT: handshake shown but no data (revoked peer key) -> UX: status shows rx/tx deltas and 'last data received' separate from handshake age
- [#9066](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9066) [support, 2025, 0/7, support] **Proton Wireguard become stale and never restored - requires a new wan ip** -- w1 SUPPORT: WG stale behind CGNAT/LTE -> UX: persistent-keepalive default for client tunnels, endpoint re-resolve + re-handshake on WAN address change
- [#8580](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8580) [feature, 2025, 1/4, -] **Wireguard UI enhancement - Allow multiple PubKey:IPv4[IPv6] pairs in the Peer Tab and connect to each round-robin** -- w1 ADOPT: peer entry supports multiple endpoints (failover list) with health check and automatic endpoint switch; rx=1
- [#9636](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9636) [bug, 2026, 0/5, support] **Keepalive signal to wireguards peer does not follow the setting** -- w1 TEST: persistent-keepalive value propagates to kernel (wg show shows it); unit test netlink peer attributes vs config
- [#9591](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9591) [feature, 2026, 0/4, support] **Wireguard missing parameter(s) in generator   etc. Please have fields for all config items.** -- w1 ADOPT: peer/generator schema covers all wg-quick fields: MTU, DNS search, Table, PreSharedKey, PersistentKeepalive, PostUp hooks not included
- [#7396](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7396) [support, 2024, 0/3, support] **Wireguard GUI KeyGen Pair Issue** -- w1 SUPPORT: keypair confusion in GUI generator -> UX: keygen shows which key is private/public, derives pubkey live from private, validates base64 length
- [#7946](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7946) [feature, 2024, 2/1, support] **Adding Rosenpass to wireguard for PQC** -- w1 ADOPT: optional Rosenpass (PQ PSK rotation) integration for WireGuard peers in core schema; rx=2
- [#8854](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8854) [bug, 2025, 0/3, -] **WireGuard Peer Generator "limit" or bug?** -- w1 ADOPT: QR generator warns when config exceeds QR capacity (~2900 bytes) and offers .conf download instead of silently failing
- [#9589](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9589) [bug, 2026, 0/3, support] **QR-code generated for Wireguard peer QR generator is INVALID.   Yields Invalid section etc.** -- w1 TEST: generated QR/.conf parses with wg-quick and official apps (INI sections valid); golden-file + parser test
- [#9590](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9590) [feature, 2026, 0/3, support] **Wireguard Peer editing missing QR-Code.** -- w1 ADOPT: QR code and .conf regeneration available when editing an existing peer (requires stored client key, see 7977)
- [#9597](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9597) [bug, 2026, 0/3, support] **WireGuard server stops replying to some peers until service restart** -- w1 TEST: WG server replies to all peers over time (conntrack/UDP source port, roaming endpoints); long-running soak test with roaming peers
- [#9598](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9598) [bug, 2026, 0/3, support] **Wireguard peer generator forget to increment a new IPv6 address from previously used ones.** -- w1 TEST: peer generator allocates next free v4 AND v6 tunnel address from existing peers; unit test allocator both families
- [#8433](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8433) [support, 2025, 0/2, incomplete] **Static route errors** -- w1 SUPPORT: large files fail over WireGuard = MTU/MSS -> UX: auto MSS clamp on tunnel interfaces by default and MTU diagnostics tool.
- [#8996](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8996) [feature, 2025, 1/1, support] **Wireguard peer generator: improve compatibility, input label and error** -- w1 ADOPT: generated .conf uses 'comma space' separators, labels 'Address' not 'tunnel address', and shows validation errors inline; rx=1
- [#7454](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7454) [support, 2024, 0/1, support] **Some hosts hardly/not at all reachable through wireguard** -- w1 SUPPORT: some hosts slow via WG (MTU/PMTUD) -> UX: WG interface default MTU 1420 with MSS clamping on tunnel; MTU probe diagnostic
- [#8071](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8071) [support, 2024, 0/1, support] **Routes for enabled Wireguard peers are created irrespective of their connection status.** -- w1 SUPPORT: AllowedIPs routes installed regardless of handshake (WG semantics) -> UX: per-peer 'install routes' toggle; doc that AllowedIPs is a routing table
- [#8153](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8153) [bug, 2024, 0/1, support] **wireguard VPN: new peers aren't displayed in the peers list if there was a peer record with the same name/endpoint IP pr** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: peer grid reads from the typed config store with server-side search, no caching keyed by name/endpoint; test create-delete-recreate
- [#8327](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8327) [feature, 2025, 0/1, support] **Explain how to specify DNS search suffix in Wireguard documentation and Peer Generator tool** -- w1 ADOPT: peer generator has distinct DNS servers and DNS search domains fields rendering a correct DNS= line
- [#10056](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10056) [support, 2026, 0/8, support] **Wireguard (I believe) related crashes** -- w0 SUPPORT: random kernel crashes suspected WG -> UX: crash reports include kernel panic + module list automatically; Linux wg module stable

### 3.11 VPN: IPsec (44)

- [#9366](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9366) [bug, 2025, 0/7, support] **IPsec + HA CARP, IPsec start tunnel on Backup Node** -- w3 ADOPT: IPsec connections bound to a VRRP VIP start only on MASTER (keepalived notify -> swanctl initiate/terminate); test both roles.
- [#6013](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6013) [bug, 2022, 0/1, -] **IPSec traffic leaves on wrong interface (default GW) in multi-WAN setup** -- w3 ADOPT: IPsec local_addrs bound to chosen interface/VIP plus policy route (ip rule from local addr) so IKE/ESP egress the configured WAN in multi-WAN
- [#7582](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7582) [bug, 2024, 0/27, support] **IPsec very unstable in IPv6 when going from 24.1.8 to 24.1.9_4** -- w2 TEST: auto IKE/ESP allow rules generated per address family matching the connection's local/remote AF; integration test dual-stack IPsec after upgrade
- [#6370](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6370) [bug, 2023, 0/18, support] **IPsec 23.1 rekey_time always 14400s** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: schema migrations are versioned and tested in CI with fixtures asserting rekey/lifetime values survive; no silent default substitution
- [#6964](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6964) [bug, 2023, 0/16, support] **IPSec ESP not decrypted via IPv6 transport** -- w2 TEST: IPsec road-warrior over IPv6 transport must decrypt (xfrm policies for v6 outer, UDP encap 4500 v6); dual-stack integration test
- [#7416](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7416) [bug, 2024, 0/16, support] **Overlapping reqid allocated for old- and newstyle IPsec (also overlapping with automatic allocation)** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: single IPsec model; reqid allocated by the config planner from one namespace and asserted unique by validator
- [#8978](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8978) [feature, 2025, 0/16, support] **IPsec VTI /31** -- w2 ADOPT: VTI/XFRM tunnel addressing accepts /31 (RFC 3021) and /127 point-to-point; validator allows them
- [#6052](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6052) [bug, 2022, 0/10, support] **Slow boot / DNS timeout @ VTI with hostname** -- w2 ADOPT: boot ordering: strongSwan FQDN remotes resolved asynchronously (swanctl --load-conns non-blocking) so unresolved DNS never blocks interface setup
- [#8459](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8459) [feature, 2025, 0/9, support] **Support IKEv2 fragmentation via UI** -- w2 ADOPT: expose IKEv2 fragmentation (RFC 7383) and IKE fragment size in connection schema (swanctl fragmentation=yes); default on
- [#6238](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6238) [bug, 2023, 0/8, -] **IPsec VTI when tunnel goes down, reconnecting the tunnel doesn't bring back the static routes** -- w2 ADOPT: static routes via VTI/XFRM re-installed on updown event after reconnect; routes tied to tunnel device, not SA lifetime; test DHCP WAN IP change
- [#6250](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6250) [bug, 2023, 0/8, support] **Route Based VPN hangs on reboot - interface isn´t created on reboot for Gateway** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: VTI/XFRM interfaces created by networkd before strongSwan start; service ordering via systemd deps; boot test with route-based IPsec gateway.
- [#6217](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6217) [bug, 2022, 1/6, incomplete] **VTI interface is not created** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: route-based IPsec child creates its XFRM/VTI interface as part of the same transactional apply; interface exists before gateway step; test
- [#7767](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7767) [bug, 2024, 0/5, support] **After reboot unbound service starts after IPsec service wich causes permanent connection error when the ipsec remote gat** -- w2 ADOPT: systemd ordering strongswan After=unbound/network-online; strongSwan retries FQDN resolution instead of failing permanently
- [#7959](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7959) [bug, 2024, 0/5, support] **VPN ipsec - Manual policy in Security Policy Database not inserted in case of reboot** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: manual XFRM policies are part of the declarative config, applied at boot with everything else (not only via updown hooks); test reboot persistence
- [#5503](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5503) [bug, 2022, 0/4, support] **IPsec should prevent enabling duplicate Phase 2 remote subnet** -- w2 ADOPT: config validation rejects enabling two IPsec children with identical local/remote traffic selectors on the same node (whole-config uniqueness)
- [#9143](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9143) [feature, 2025, 0/4, -] **Support Configuring Additional Key Exchanges for IKEv2** -- w2 ADOPT: IKEv2 proposal schema includes additional key exchanges (RFC 9370, ke1_ ML-KEM) for strongSwan 6 post-quantum hybrid
- [#7205](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7205) [feature, 2024, 0/3, -] **VPN-IPsec-Connections - Add check for installing kernel routes policies with 0.0.0.0/0 in traffic selector** -- w2 ADOPT: validator warns when a child with 0.0.0.0/0 or ::/0 traffic selector has kernel policies enabled (would hijack all traffic); suggest route-based
- [#7238](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7238) [feature, 2024, 0/3, feature] **Firewall rules no longer automatically created for IPSec** -- w2 ADOPT: optional auto-managed firewall rules per IPsec connection (IKE/ESP on WAN + child TS pass rules) visible as system rules
- [#8900](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8900) [feature, 2025, 0/3, feature] **CRL Support for IPsec VPN** -- w2 ADOPT: local CA CRLs installed to swanctl x509crl automatically; per-connection 'revocation' policy (strict/ifuri/relaxed) in schema
- [#9339](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9339) [support, 2025, 0/3, incomplete] **API: IpSec: sessions: search_phase2** -- w2 ADOPT: IPsec session API exposes installed child SAs/traffic selectors (from vici list-sas) for policy-based tunnels, enabling RRI scripts.

- [#8200](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8200) [bug, 2025, 0/2, support] **IPsec site-2-site firewall rules not adopted dynamically** -- w2 ADOPT: auto rules reference remote peer via nftables named set updated by vici/updown on DynDNS change; no full ruleset reload needed
- [#5844](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5844) [bug, 2022, 0/1, support] **IPSEC traffic gets denied by "Default deny" when fragmentation comes in** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: nftables conntrack reassembles fragments (nf_defrag) before rule match, so fragmented TCP over IPsec hits the same state; test fragmented ESP payloads
- [#10119](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10119) [bug, 2026, 0/1, support] **HA: IPsec clients on primary disconnect when rebooting secondary firewall** -- w2 TEST: rebooting BACKUP must not disturb MASTER's IPsec SAs (no VIP flap, no conntrackd flush); HA integration test with mobile clients.

- [#6895](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6895) [bug, 2023, 0/9, support] **IPSec VPN tunnels won't reconnect because option "keyingtries" incorrectly written to .conf file** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: typed field (keyingtries: Forever/N) rendered by renderer with unit tests; swanctl.conf never gets a raw invalid string
- [#5518](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5518) [support, 2022, 0/7, support] **IPsec routed mode has changed, please update docs** -- w1 SUPPORT: routed IPsec docs outdated vs behaviour -> UX: VTI/XFRM tunnel docs generated from schema help text; IDs default to real endpoint IPs

- [#6061](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6061) [bug, 2022, 0/5, support] **IPSec: "Manual SPD entries" in conjunction with dynamic remotes may result in wrong endpoint adresses in the generated S** -- w1 ADOPT: manual SPD/passthrough entries with dynamic remote must be re-rendered on remote address change (vici updown hook); avoid static endpoint in XFRM policies

- [#9082](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9082) [bug, 2025, 0/5, support] **Wrong IPsec status in widget on an HA standby - probably problem lower down the stack, configd & friends** -- w1 ADOPT: IPsec status widget reads live swanctl/vici per node and shows 'standby, tunnels not started' on BACKUP, not stale cached state.

- [#7479](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7479) [bug, 2024, 0/2, incomplete] **IPSec - Tunnel Settings - cannot edit existing Phase2 entries** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: editing existing record re-validates with same schema as create; round-trip test load->save unchanged for every model
- [#7491](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7491) [support, 2024, 0/2, support] **IPsec: EAP-TLS IKEv2 fails with No trusted certificate found to verify TLS peer** -- w1 SUPPORT: EAP-TLS fails 'no trusted certificate' -> UX: IPsec connection shows which CA chain is loaded for EAP-TLS and warns if client cert CA not in cacerts
- [#9286](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9286) [bug, 2025, 0/2, support] **RADIUS Group Authentication Fails in IPsec VPN** -- w1 TEST: RADIUS group attribute mapping to peer config must survive upgrades; integration test RADIUS group constraint -> connection selection
- [#7122](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7122) [bug, 2024, 0/1, support] **Multiple (conflicting) SPIs created** -- w1 TEST: IPsec with multiple peers behind same head-end: unique IKE IDs, no duplicate SAs (swanctl uniqueness policy); interop test against VyOS/strongSwan.
- [#8414](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8414) [support, 2025, 0/1, support] **IPSEC - Auto generated rules are ignored, preventing tunnel renewal** -- w1 SUPPORT: auto IPsec rules hit default deny -> UX: per-rule counters on generated system rules and a 'why was this packet dropped' trace
- [#8760](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8760) [support, 2025, 0/1, support] **Unstable IPSEC VPN with new Configuration Method using Public IPV6 between 2 OPNSENSE Firewalls** -- w1 SUPPORT: unstable IPsec over dynamic public v6 -> UX: MOBIKE enabled by default, DPD settings explicit, peer-address-change events logged

### 3.12 VPN: OpenVPN (62)

- [#6758](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6758) [feature, 2023, 0/24, support] **VPN: OpenVPN: Instances missing a few options** -- w2 ADOPT: instance schema covers port-share, tcp-queue-limit, bcast-buffers, tun-mtu, fragment, mssfix plus a vetted 'extra directives' allowlist; c=24
- [#6559](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6559) [feature, 2023, 0/12, support] **Improve OpenVPN Compression Configuration to help mitigate VORACLE attack vector** -- w2 ADOPT: default allow-compression=no on all OpenVPN instances (VORACLE mitigation); explicit warning if user enables
- [#6976](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6976) [bug, 2023, 0/11, incomplete] **problem on /usr/local/opnsense/scripts/openvpn/ovpn_event.py** -- w2 ADOPT: learn-address hook updates nftables authgroup sets incrementally (nft add element) not full table rebuild; must stay O(1) per client for 150+ users
- [#7145](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7145) [bug, 2024, 0/10, support] **OpenVPN not applying CSO settings in some server modes (with workaround)** -- w2 TEST: CSO matched by CN (and optionally username) applied in all server auth modes incl. SSL/TLS+user; integration test fixed IP per client
- [#9205](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9205) [feature, 2025, 2/8, support] **OpenVPN: Allow manually configuring the dynamic IP pool range** -- w2 ADOPT: configurable ifconfig-pool range (or nopool) so static CSO addresses are reserved outside dynamic pool (same as 7567); rx=2

- [#7316](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7316) [feature, 2024, 0/8, support] **OpenVPN: Add More Advanced Options to Instances** -- w2 ADOPT: client instance schema covers auth-nocache, remote-cert-tls, nobind, resolv-retry, explicit-exit-notify, push-peer-info; typed extra-directive allowlist
- [#8579](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8579) [feature, 2025, 0/7, -] **Bind OpenVPN Server to multiple IP addresses** -- w2 ADOPT: OpenVPN instance binds to a set of interfaces/addresses (v4+v6) via multiple --local entries or one process per bind; or bind-any with nftables restricting
- [#6235](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6235) [bug, 2023, 0/6, support] **OpenVPN server DH length is not set when importing an old config** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: versioned schema migrations fill required fields with defaults on import; renderer rejects unset required params at validation not at daemon start
- [#8361](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8361) [bug, 2025, 0/5, support] **OpenVPN client Override has no option for topology** -- w2 TEST: CSO with push-reset must re-push topology (and other instance-level essentials); unit test CSO rendering includes 'push topology subnet'

- [#6770](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6770) [feature, 2023, 0/4, -] **OpenVPN Instances: Add more DNS options (which exist in "Servers")** -- w2 ADOPT: push options in instance schema: block-outside-dns, dhcp-option DOMAIN-SEARCH, register-dns; parity across server/client modes from one model

- [#8622](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8622) [feature, 2025, 0/4, support] **OpenVPN: Incompatible with the official OpenVPN P2P mode configuration** -- w2 ADOPT: native 'mode p2p' (static key or TLS, no --server) instance type accepting traffic from any source for dynamic routing (FRR over tun)

- [#7567](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7567) [feature, 2024, 0/3, -] **OpenVPN: working Static IP addresses for VPN clients** -- w2 ADOPT: OpenVPN instance pool range configurable (ifconfig-pool start/end, nopool) so static CSO addresses never collide with dynamic pool
