- [#8595](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8595) [bug, 2025, 0/1, -] **OpenVPN shows only one session when connecting with same user from internal network** -- w1 ADOPT: status keyed by client id (CID) from management interface so duplicate-cn sessions all appear; no UNDEF phantom rows
- [#8856](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8856) [support, 2025, 0/1, support] **OpenVPN TLS error witout Option "push block-outside-dns" for a rowadwarior** -- w1 SUPPORT: v4-only server with dual-stack client TLS timeout -> UX: export sets proto udp4 when server has no v6; warn on AF mismatch
- [#8878](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8878) [support, 2025, 0/1, support] **OpenVPN P2P Client automatic reconnect failes with TLS error** -- w1 SUPPORT: sporadic TLS error on P2P reconnect -> UX: expose reneg-sec, tls-timeout, and log TLS handshake failures with reason
- [#9649](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9649) [feature, 2026, 0/1, -] **Pushing route metric for individual routes to OpenPVN client** -- w1 ADOPT: OpenVPN server pushed routes with per-route metric (push "route net mask gw metric"); typed route list in instance.
- [#7071](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7071) [bug, 2023, 0/7, support] **OpenVPN: Client Specific Overrides - IP Error in generate file with use Topology P2P** -- w0 TEST: duplicate of 6924 (p2p CSO address rendering)
- [#6498](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6498) [support, 2023, 0/3, support] **Openvpn Connection Status Error** -- w0 SUPPORT: connection status page error (likely plugin/management socket) -> UX: status via management interface API with structured error, not PHP crash
- [#6695](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6695) [support, 2023, 0/2, incomplete] **Bad config file generation by opnsense** -- w0 SUPPORT: OpenVPN client config export missing auth-user-pass -> UX: export generated from typed instance model incl. auth mode; test exported profile.
- [#6972](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6972) [bug, 2023, 0/1, support] **OpenVPN: Not Working Client Specific Overrides with Topology P2P in split mode** -- w0 TEST: duplicate of 6924; CSO rendered for p2p topology; same test
- [#8191](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8191) [support, 2025, 0/1, support] **OpenVPN: Is This a Bug or a Configuration Issue?** -- w0 SUPPORT: S2S unreachable -> UX: built-in S2S troubleshooter showing routes installed by tunnel, iroute mappings, and rule hits on tun

### 3.13 VPN: other (L2TP/PPTP/GRE/GIF/VXLAN) (7)

- [#7833](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7833) [bug, 2024, 0/12, support] **VXLAN not auto turn up when reboot/start opnsense** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: VXLAN is a networkd netdev ordered after its underlay (WireGuard) in the dependency graph; boot brings it up; test reboot with VXLAN-over-WG
- [#9520](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9520) [feature, 2025, 0/11, support] **Allow IPv6 and dual stack addressing for GRE tunnels in GUI** -- w2 ADOPT: GRE/GIF tunnel inner addressing dual-stack (v4 and v6 tunnel addresses at once) in schema; ip6gre and gre both supported
- [#8251](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8251) [feature, 2025, 1/3, support] **Alias not accepted in Remote Address field for GIF/GRE Tunnel** -- w2 ADOPT: GRE/GIF/IPIP remote endpoint accepts FQDN or single-host alias, re-resolved on change (netlink update); validator restricts alias to single IP
- [#8593](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8593) [bug, 2025, 0/1, -] **GRE Tunnel Boot Race Condition** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: tunnel netdevs depend on underlay routes in planner ordering; networkd re-applies when route to peer appears; test GRE over routed fabric at boot
- [#9278](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9278) [support, 2025, 0/9, support] **VXLAN Bridge: One side does not learn MAC addresses over ARP** -- w1 SUPPORT: VXLAN bridge one-way MAC learning (learning off / FDB) -> UX: VXLAN device exposes learning, remote FDB entries, and bridge fdb viewer
- [#7851](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7851) [bug, 2024, 0/2, support] **Incorrect MTU when creating a GRE tunnel that runs over IPv6** -- w1 ADOPT: GRE over IPv6 outer sets MTU = underlay MTU - 44 (v6+GRE) automatically; MTU field shows computed default
- [#8202](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8202) [bug, 2025, 0/2, support] **L2TP connections fail to reconnect after a initial connection failure via a Parent Interface.** -- w1 ADOPT: L2TP/PPTP/PPPoE client links retry indefinitely with backoff when parent interface flaps; FQDN gateway allowed with periodic re-resolve

### 3.14 Captive portal (23)

- [#5816](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5816) [feature, 2022, 0/14, support] **Change Captive Portal Redirection Port** -- w2 ADOPT: captive portal redirect URL fully configurable (scheme, FQDN, port, path) so it can sit behind a reverse proxy; no hardcoded 8000/8001.
- [#8558](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8558) [bug, 2025, 0/8, -] **25.1.5_5 Captive Portal is unable to terminate active UDP connections** -- w2 ADOPT: on session end flush conntrack entries for the client IP (conntrack -D) in addition to removing from nftables set, so UDP/VPN flows die.
- [#7430](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7430) [support, 2024, 0/4, support] **Captive Portal not redirected to login page since using KEA-DHCP** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: portal session identity uses nftables sets by IP+MAC from neighbor table and Kea lease API; no dependency on a specific DHCP server's lease file.
- [#6661](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6661) [bug, 2023, 0/3, -] **Captiveportal crash on firewall reboot [OPNsense 23.1.11]** -- w2 TEST: captive portal must accept logins after cold boot (state DB and nftables sets re-created on start); integration test reboot->login.
- [#8833](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8833) [bug, 2025, 0/2, incomplete] **Captive-Portal cant working anymore!** -- w2 ADOPT: captive portal zone identified server-side from client IP/ingress interface (nft map), not from a client header; TEST: splash accept without zone header.
- [#9244](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9244) [bug, 2025, 0/2, incomplete] **Captive Portal: Sessions not deleted when voucher is expired** -- w2 ADOPT: voucher expiry ends the session immediately (session end = min(hard timeout, voucher expiry)); test voucher expiry -> session removed.
- [#5708](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5708) [feature, 2022, 0/1, support] **captive portal login** -- w2 ADOPT: captive portal post-login redirect: configurable fixed URL or original requested URL (preserve via redirect param).
- [#8333](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8333) [support, 2025, 0/1, support] **Captive Portal** -- w2 SUPPORT: pre-auth DNS blocked -> UX: zone setting 'allowed pre-auth destinations' (DNS servers, walled garden) rendered into nftables bypass set; default allows configured DNS.
- [#9604](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9604) [feature, 2026, 0/1, support] **Radius Auth shall use RFC attributes for VLAN assignments.** -- w2 ADOPT: captive portal consumes RADIUS reply attrs (Tunnel-Private-Group-ID, Filter-Id, Class) to tag client in an nft set/map for per-user policy routing.
- [#8304](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8304) [feature, 2025, 0/6, support] **Captive Portal Sessions - Technical Data Storage - GDPR compliance** -- w1 ADOPT: captive portal session/accounting log with configurable retention (GDPR), pseudonymization option and export; documented data fields.
- [#6675](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6675) [feature, 2023, 0/5, support] **Allow VoucherController.php API access from guest/captive network like AccessController.php** -- w1 ADOPT: voucher-create API callable from portal zone with scoped API key (RBAC scope 'captive.voucher.create'), optional per-zone exposure.
- [#9098](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9098) [bug, 2025, 3/1, -] **Return/enter key doesn't work on captive portal login page** -- w1 TEST: default portal template submits on Enter (real <form>); Playwright test for keyboard submit (rx=3).
- [#9676](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9676) [bug, 2026, 0/4, -] **Captive Portal: Anonymous auth not working correctly** -- w1 TEST: anonymous auth mode login via API returns session; contract test for each auth method (none/local/radius/voucher) per release.
- [#7438](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7438) [bug, 2024, 0/3, incomplete] **Captive Portal: "Allowed MAC-Address" ignored** -- w1 TEST: MAC allowlist must bypass auth; session list script must not crash on empty DB; tests for allowlist + empty-session listing.
- [#9095](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9095) [bug, 2025, 0/3, support] **Captive portal stops working properly if you enable periodic Captive portal backup** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: session DB is SQLite with WAL + online backup API; backups never lock or restart the portal daemon; test backup during active sessions.
- [#6921](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6921) [feature, 2023, 0/2, -] **Manually set Captive Portal a limit for "Concurrent User Logins"** -- w1 ADOPT: per-zone and per-user max concurrent sessions as integer (not boolean), enforced server-side on login.
- [#9479](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9479) [bug, 2025, 0/2, incomplete] **The captive portal does not load the custom template correctly** -- w1 ADOPT: custom portal templates validated on upload (required form ids/API contract) with versioned template API; default template downloadable and re-uploadable unchanged.
- [#9532](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9532) [bug, 2025, 0/2, support] **Captive Portal: excluding device from need to solve the captive portal doesnt work** -- w1 TEST: MAC/IP allowlist bypass persists across daemon restarts and config reloads; integration test allowlisted client without login.
- [#6887](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6887) [feature, 2023, 0/1, -] **Captive Portal Enhancement: Backend Integration and Markdown Support for Terms of Use** -- w1 ADOPT: server-enforced ToS acceptance (login API rejects without accepted=true) and Markdown-rendered ToS text per zone.
- [#6955](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6955) [support, 2023, 0/1, support] **Captive Portal not working when accessing webserver with IP and Port** -- w1 SUPPORT: portal intercept only on 80/443; access by IP:port isn't redirected -> UX: document intercept scope; configurable intercepted ports.
- [#7125](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7125) [bug, 2024, 0/1, support] **Captive portal CPU usage** -- w1 TEST: captive portal daemon CPU/memory stable over days (no timer/thread leak); soak test with simulated sessions and Prometheus metric check.
- [#8218](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8218) [bug, 2025, 0/1, -] **RADIUS requests from different captive portals are indistinguishable** -- w1 ADOPT: RADIUS requests carry per-zone NAS-Identifier/NAS-Port-Id/Called-Station-Id so multiple portal zones are distinguishable.
- [#8860](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8860) [feature, 2025, 0/1, -] **Add possibility to create Voucherdefinitions** -- w1 ADOPT: voucher templates (named validity/expiry presets) per zone as a config object; voucher generation picks a template.

### 3.15 IDS/IPS (Suricata) (28)

- [#7200](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7200) [bug, 2024, 1/5, support] **Suricata huge log files fill up the drive and crash the system** -- w3 ADOPT: EVE log via journald/socket with size-capped rotation; disk-usage guard disables file logging before root fills; alert on low disk.
- [#6814](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6814) [bug, 2023, 0/15, -] **Suricata stopped working after upgrade to 23.7(.2)** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: Suricata uses AF_PACKET/NFQUEUE, no netmap driver coupling; atomic NixOS upgrade rolls back if the IDS service fails health check (c=15).
- [#8984](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8984) [bug, 2025, 0/10, support] **Intrusion detection: template error, service can't start on 25.7** -- w2 TEST: Suricata config render + 'suricata -T' validation gate at apply; integration test on upgrade path (c=10).
- [#7405](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7405) [bug, 2024, 0/8, support] **OPNsense 24.1.6 Suricata 7 Cannot Allocate Memory** -- w2 ADOPT: Suricata memory settings (stream/flow memcap, workers) validated against RAM at apply; startup failure must not take down management (service isolation).
- [#8034](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8034) [support, 2024, 0/7, incomplete] **rules do not work** -- w2 SUPPORT: HOME_NET misconfig -> UX: HOME_NET/EXTERNAL_NET editable as typed fields (aliases allowed), shown in IDS settings; no yaml hand-edits.
- [#9268](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9268) [feature, 2025, 4/2, -] **Request for Enhanced GUI Support for Adding and Editing Custom Suricata Rules** -- w2 ADOPT: full-syntax custom Suricata rule editor with server-side syntax validation (suricata -T on temp file) and SID management (rx=4).
- [#8949](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8949) [bug, 2025, 0/5, support] **Suricata bridge interface incorrectly configured as 'eth0' in suricata.yaml instead of 'bridge0' (causing netmap errors)** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: Suricata config rendered from interface model using kernel ifnames (bridge0); render tests for bridge/VLAN/bond members.
- [#9819](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9819) [feature, 2026, 0/5, support] **[Feature Request] Suricata: add custom download rules** -- w2 ADOPT: custom ruleset sources (URL + optional auth/checksum) configurable alongside built-in providers (ET Open, Abuse.ch, etc.).
- [#9956](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9956) [bug, 2026, 0/5, upstream] **Suricata divert-to mode causes aborted HTTPS response streams (Axios / Node.js) – works with responseType: stream** -- w2 TEST: NFQUEUE IPS must not break HTTPS streams (verdict/segment handling); e2e test with Node/curl large chunked responses through IPS.
- [#9533](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9533) [bug, 2025, 0/4, support] **IPS webgui batch enable/disable rules too slow** -- w2 ADOPT: bulk rule enable/disable as single batch API call on SID sets; O(1) per rule, background compile, progress via job API.
- [#7026](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7026) [bug, 2023, 0/3, incomplete] **Opnsense GUI very slow after modify IDS rules** -- w2 ADOPT: rule enable/disable state stored as compact SID sets; ruleset compilation in a background job (Rust), GUI never blocks on 5k-rule edits.
- [#8122](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8122) [feature, 2024, 0/3, support] **I want IPS to work on certain subnets rather than interface binded.** -- w2 ADOPT: IPS via NFQUEUE lets rules select traffic (subnets/aliases/ports) to inspect, not whole interfaces; bypass set for trusted flows.
- [#9539](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9539) [bug, 2025, 0/3, support] **Suricata GUI rule updates deletes manually configured rulesets and corrupts directory** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: managed rule dir is generated; custom rules live in a separate declared directory/API objects and are never touched by downloads.
- [#7138](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7138) [feature, 2024, 0/2, -] **pfsense equivalent to SourceIP/SID whitelisting** -- w2 ADOPT: Suricata suppress/threshold per SID with by_src/by_dst IP or alias (threshold.config generated from typed model).
- [#8938](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8938) [feature, 2025, 0/2, support] **Suricata** -- w2 ADOPT: rule browser shows msg, classtype, severity, CVE refs, metadata (created/updated, deployment), with filters; stale-rule indicators from metadata.
- [#9001](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9001) [bug, 2025, 0/2, support] **Suricata IPS causes 100% CPU lockup on generic TCP traffic between internal interfaces** -- w2 TEST: IPS inline between two internal interfaces under TCP load (no 100% CPU spin); soak test with iperf through NFQUEUE.
- [#7948](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7948) [bug, 2024, 0/1, support] **IDS rulesets cannot be downloaded over IPv6** -- w2 TEST: ruleset downloader works IPv6-only (happy-eyeballs HTTP client); CI test with v6-only network namespace.
- [#9697](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9697) [bug, 2026, 0/1, support] **IPS in divert mode hold the session** -- w2 TEST: NFQUEUE IPS fail-open/fail-closed behavior configurable; queue bypass on Suricata stop; test that sessions aren't held when IPS stalls.
- [#8684](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8684) [bug, 2025, 0/11, support] **IDS (Suricata) Will Not Startup** -- w1 TEST: Suricata startup with large rulesets under memory limits; capture 'queue space' errors as startup failure with guidance (worker/memcap).
- [#9285](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9285) [bug, 2025, 0/7, support] **Custom rules not updating - Suricata** -- w1 ADOPT: custom rule files managed via API (upload/edit) and included in rule compile; SID collision detected and reported, rule reload via unix socket.
- [#10097](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10097) [bug, 2026, 0/5, incomplete] **Suricata Divert (IPS) mode not working on Proxmox virtio - ipfw receives no rules, zero traffic inspected** -- w1 N/A-LINUX: ipfw divert; sensr uses nftables queue to NFQUEUE, with diagnostics showing queue stats/packets inspected so 'zero traffic' is visible.
- [#7453](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7453) [other, 2024, 0/3, upstream] **Hyperscan Now Proprietary** -- w1 ADOPT: build Suricata against vectorscan (Hyperscan fork) in the Nix package set; track upstream license changes
- [#8077](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8077) [support, 2024, 0/3, incomplete] **Rules do not work update 11212024** -- w1 SUPPORT: user confused by IDS 'Home Networks' on a DHCP WAN -> UX: Suricata HOME_NET defaults to all LAN-side interface subnets with help text; dynamic WAN tracked via sets.
- [#5976](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5976) [support, 2022, 0/1, support] **Change timestamp format of suricata logs in opnsense** -- w1 SUPPORT: Suricata syslog timestamp format -> UX: remote syslog exporter supports RFC3164/RFC5424 format choice per destination.
- [#7211](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7211) [bug, 2024, 0/1, -] **Suricata start error: installed_rules.yaml No such file** -- w1 TEST: Suricata start with zero enabled rulesets must succeed (empty rule file generated); unit test config render with no rules.
- [#8092](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8092) [bug, 2024, 0/1, support] **Changes to enable/disable of Suricata IDS/IPS rulesets don't take effect with Download & Update Rules button** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: ruleset enable state is config; 'download & apply' is one transactional job that reports completion/failure in GUI (job API).
- [#8274](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8274) [feature, 2025, 0/1, -] **Suricata: Introduce more granularity into the user defined rules** -- w1 ADOPT: user-defined Suricata rule builder covers content/pcre/http.* /flow/threshold keywords with raw-rule escape hatch (see #9268).
- [#8173](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8173) [feature, 2025, 0/4, -] **[Feature Request] Enable SSL DPI on Suricata IPS** -- w0 OUT-OF-SCOPE: TLS interception/DPI not in core (privacy, CA on clients); Suricata still inspects TLS metadata (SNI, JA3/JA4, certs).

### 3.16 Traffic shaper / QoS (15)

- [#7092](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7092) [bug, 2023, 0/20, support] **Traffic shaper documentation does not work for VLAN.** -- w2 TEST: shaping on VLAN interfaces (tc on vlan device, IFB ingress) e2e test; docs generated per interface type (c=20).
- [#8029](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8029) [support, 2024, 0/11, support] **FQ_Codel not trraffic shaping upload** -- w2 SUPPORT: fq_codel upload not shaping (rule direction) -> UX: wizard creates both directions; status page shows live tc stats per pipe.
- [#5670](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5670) [support, 2022, 0/8, support] **upload traffic shapping dosn't work** -- w2 SUPPORT: upload shaping misconfig -> UX: shaper wizard per WAN (down/up rates, CAKE) with direction semantics explained; ingress via IFB handled automatically.
- [#7342](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7342) [bug, 2024, 0/7, support] **Firewall Shaper causes IPv6 address loss on WAN** -- w2 TEST: shaping on WAN must not drop/delay RA/DHCPv6/ND control traffic (priority class or bypass for ICMPv6/DHCPv6); e2e test with congestion.
- [#8547](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8547) [feature, 2025, 4/3, -] **Support variable rate traffic shaping for wireless 4G / 5G / Starlink / WISP WANs** -- w2 ADOPT: CAKE autorate (cake-autorate style) for variable-rate WANs: adaptive bandwidth from RTT measurements, as a shaper mode (rx=4).
- [#9875](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9875) [bug, 2026, 0/7, incomplete] **Traffic shaper ipfw hooks broken on 26.1.x — fq_codel pipe bandwidth not enforced** -- w2 TEST: shaper bandwidth enforcement e2e test (iperf through HTB/CAKE pipe measures <= rate) run in CI on every release.
- [#6615](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6615) [feature, 2023, 0/5, support] **Feature request: Alias and Category support for traffic shaper** -- w2 ADOPT: shaper rules use alias references and categories; grid shows resolved names (duplicate of #5728 alias part).
- [#5728](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5728) [feature, 2022, 0/3, support] **Traffic shaper should accept firewall aliases in dialog in order to shape IPv6 for dynamic prefixes** -- w2 ADOPT: shaper classifier rules accept aliases and dynamic IPv6 prefix sets (nftables classify/mark then tc filter on mark).
- [#6564](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6564) [feature, 2023, 1/2, feature] **Schedule support in Traffic Shaper** -- w2 ADOPT: schedules on shaper rules/pipes (time-varying rates via tc change on timer); same schedule object as firewall.
- [#6080](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6080) [bug, 2022, 0/15, support] **"Reset to Defaults" doesn't clear shaper pipe/queue** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: factory reset applies the default declarative config; tc qdiscs are part of rendered state and removed in the diff (c=15).
- [#9419](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9419) [bug, 2025, 0/7, support] **IPFW disable after applying any rule in web** -- w1 N/A-LINUX: ipfw toggled by pf reload; sensr shaping (tc) and filtering (nftables) are independent subsystems, applying one never resets the other.
- [#7295](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7295) [bug, 2024, 0/4, incomplete] **Setting queue limit in shaper has no effect** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: tc/qdisc parameters (limit, target, interval, quantum) rendered from typed shaper model with render tests; no hand-written ipfw lines.
- [#7296](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7296) [bug, 2024, 0/4, incomplete] **Fix setting pipe type in shaper settings.** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: pipe = HTB/TBF class, scheduler/AQM = child qdisc (fq_codel/CAKE/PIE) as separate typed fields; GUI options always map to a tc knob.
- [#9298](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9298) [bug, 2025, 1/3, incomplete] **OPNSense does not apply AQM on child queues (config_aqm Unable to configure flowset, flowset busy!)** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: AQM configured per child class qdisc natively in tc; shaper applied once per transaction (no repeated restarts).
- [#7499](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7499) [bug, 2024, 0/2, -] **Firewall->Shaper->Rules not showing all pipes and queues in Target selection dropdown when creating new Rule** -- w0 TEST: target dropdown lists all pipes and queues (API returns full list, grid paging not applied to pickers).

### 3.17 Auth / users / 2FA / SSH (53)

- [#6310](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6310) [feature, 2023, 18/30, incomplete] **Separate 2FA OTP field on login page** -- w3 ADOPT: login page has a dedicated OTP input (separate step or field) with autocomplete=one-time-code so password managers autofill; never concatenate.
- [#6110](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6110) [feature, 2022, 4/6, -] **OpenID or SAML authentication** -- w3 ADOPT: OIDC (and optionally SAML) as auth servers for GUI login, usable also by captive portal and VPN auth where protocol allows.
- [#9313](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9313) [feature, 2025, 0/6, feature] **Missing group-to-permission mapping for OpenID (Keycloak) integration (Business Edition 25.10)** -- w3 ADOPT: OIDC claim-to-group/role mapping (configurable claim name, group->RBAC role table) in core; admin role grantable from IdP groups.
- [#7162](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7162) [feature, 2024, 0/4, -] **API / UI permissions** -- w3 ADOPT: RBAC per API endpoint AND verb (read vs mutate), e.g. allow VPN status read but deny disconnect; GUI derives visibility from API scopes.
- [#8541](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8541) [bug, 2025, 0/15, support] **LDAP login to web GUI not possible anymore with v25** -- w2 TEST: LDAP GUI login integration test (AD + OpenLDAP containers) on every release; auth errors surfaced in audit log with backend reason.
- [#7911](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7911) [feature, 2024, 2/10, support] **Sensible to hide OTP seed in System > Access > Users > User Edit > OTP seed** -- w2 ADOPT: OTP seed is write-only in schema; GUI shows QR/seed only in the enrolment step, masked afterwards (see 6627, 10115).
- [#6228](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6228) [feature, 2023, 0/11, incomplete] **2FA - separate dialog box for OTP when connecting to vpn** -- w2 ADOPT: two-step 2FA for VPN auth: OpenVPN static-challenge / IPsec EAP second round / RADIUS Access-Challenge pass-through instead of password+OTP concatenation.
- [#6230](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6230) [bug, 2023, 0/11, support] **Auth Base has LDAP specific group handling, which is incompatible with the Radius Auth provider** -- w2 ADOPT: auth providers return a normalized Identity {user, groups[]} via a trait; RADIUS Class attr and LDAP memberOf each have their own parser; unit tests per provider.
- [#6871](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6871) [bug, 2023, 0/11, support] **LDAP backend makes wrong assumptions about user groups** -- w2 ADOPT: LDAP backend configurable for group DN vs name formats, memberOf vs search-by-member, no assumptions on CN=/OU= prefixes; TEST against Kanidm, AD, OpenLDAP, lldap.
- [#6980](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6980) [bug, 2023, 0/10, support] **User is not created using LDAP and Automatic user creation when User naming attribute is set to UserPrincipalName (eMail** -- w2 ADOPT: auto-created users from LDAP accept any naming attribute incl. UPN/email (allow @ and . in usernames); TEST: UPN login creates local user.
- [#7036](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7036) [feature, 2023, 0/7, feature] **OpenVPN didn't sent Calling-Station-Id in RADIUS request** -- w2 ADOPT: RADIUS auth from OpenVPN sends Calling-Station-Id=untrusted_ip and NAS-Port-Type; generic RADIUS client includes caller context for all services
- [#8481](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8481) [bug, 2025, 0/7, support] **LDAP user ("Automatic user creation") gets deleted every night** -- w2 ADOPT: auto-created remote users keyed by (username) with a list of allowed auth sources; cleanup job must never delete a user still present in ANY configured backend.
- [#5699](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5699) [feature, 2022, 0/6, incomplete] **Ability to set "NAS-IP-Address" for Radius Access Server** -- w2 ADOPT: RADIUS auth server option to set NAS-IP-Address / NAS-Identifier attributes (RFC 2865) in access requests.
- [#6837](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6837) [feature, 2023, 4/1, support] **Separate Input Field for 2FA Code During Login for Enhanced User Experience** -- w2 ADOPT: separate OTP field on login (dup of 6310); also apply to API key TOTP-less path and captive portal login form.
- [#6048](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6048) [bug, 2022, 0/4, support] **Group Edit dialog allows assigning privileges, Group Add does not** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: add and edit dialogs are the same generated form from the schema; no field-set divergence between create/update.
- [#8172](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8172) [bug, 2024, 0/4, incomplete] **The DNS-rebind check is not triggering when no hostname is supplied.** -- w2 ADOPT: GUI host-header check rejects bare-IP access when strict mode is on (configurable allowlist of IPs/names); TEST bare IP and alt names
- [#8425](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8425) [feature, 2025, 0/4, support] **Integration of LDAP user and groups from LDAP source** -- w2 ADOPT: LDAP group->local group mapping with on-demand or scheduled sync of groups and members (not only on login).
- [#8840](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8840) [bug, 2025, 0/4, support] **ssh authorized keys file deleted** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: authorized_keys are part of the user object and rendered from config; toggling password auth never touches key material. TEST: toggle and verify keys.
- [#5899](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5899) [feature, 2022, 0/3, -] **LDAP group style options are too limited / missing RFC 2307 support** -- w2 ADOPT: LDAP group mapping modes: memberOf, RFC2307 (memberUid/posixGroup), RFC2307bis (member DN); configurable group base/filter.
- [#6329](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6329) [feature, 2023, 0/3, support] **categories and filters in System: Access: Users** -- w2 ADOPT: server-side search/sort/filter on user grid (by group, auth source, expiry) plus CSV/API bulk import of users.
- [#9411](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9411) [bug, 2025, 0/3, -] **Login fails silently on multiple PHPSESSID cookies** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: SPA uses a single __Host- prefixed, path=/ session cookie (or bearer tokens); duplicate cookie -> clear and show explicit error, never silent fail.
- [#9748](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9748) [feature, 2026, 0/3, support] **Microsoft Entra ID support for OIDC group claims** -- w2 ADOPT: OIDC auth server: configurable groups claim name, optional extra scopes, Entra 'groups overage' via Graph fallback documented; map claim values to local groups.
- [#6627](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6627) [support, 2023, 0/2, support] **otp seed working on multiple devices** -- w2 ADOPT: TOTP enrolment flow: show seed/QR once, require a verification code to activate, then hide seed; re-enrolment regenerates it.
- [#7496](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7496) [bug, 2024, 0/2, incomplete] **Automatic Ldap group syncing not working openldap memberof is deprecated** -- w2 ADOPT: LDAP group sync supports search by (member=userDN)/(memberUid=uid) when memberOf overlay is absent (OpenLDAP); same as 5899.
- [#8255](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8255) [support, 2025, 0/2, incomplete] **RequireMsgAuth et/ou limitProxyState is in mode <Disable/Audit> problem** -- w2 ADOPT: RADIUS client implements Message-Authenticator on all requests (BlastRADIUS, RFC 2869) and validates it on responses; required for Windows NPS after KB5040268.
- [#8388](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8388) [bug, 2025, 0/2, support] **Granular Kea Privileges Not Allocating** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: RBAC is per-API-endpoint derived from OpenAPI tags; GUI pages request only endpoints they use, so privilege sets can't miss sub-pages
- [#8590](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8590) [feature, 2025, 1/1, -] **Feature request: Enable/disable TOTP for SSH and web access individually** -- w2 ADOPT: auth policy per access channel (GUI, API, SSH, console): choose auth server chain and MFA requirement independently.
- [#10020](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10020) [feature, 2026, 0/2, support] **Improved Dnsmasq and Kea System Access Privileges granularity** -- w2 ADOPT: RBAC scopes fine-grained per endpoint (e.g. dhcp.leases:read only) for read-only integration API keys (Home Assistant)
- [#10166](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10166) [feature, 2026, 0/2, support] **OpenVPN: track “Last successful login” per user and optionally deny inactive users after X days** -- w2 ADOPT: per-user last-successful-auth timestamp (all services) in user model + policy to disable accounts inactive N days; audit log backed
- [#5799](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5799) [feature, 2022, 0/1, -] **Add a clone button to easily duplicate authentication servers** -- w2 ADOPT: generic 'clone' action on every grid (auth servers included) generated from the schema; copy object with new name.
- [#5935](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5935) [bug, 2022, 0/1, -] **LDAP failover not working with multiple LDAP access servers** -- w2 ADOPT: LDAP connect/bind timeouts configurable and short (e.g. 3s), multiple servers tried in order; TEST: failover with unreachable primary.
- [#6112](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6112) [bug, 2022, 0/1, -] **Radius Authentication Request not sending/omitting:"ratNASIPAddress"** -- w2 ADOPT: always send NAS-IP-Address (and NAS-Identifier) in RADIUS Access-Request (needed by Azure MFA NPS IP whitelist).
- [#7579](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7579) [bug, 2024, 0/1, support] **LDAP Active Directory User change CN (User distinguished name) does not get updated (failed authentication)** -- w2 ADOPT: LDAP-linked users store an immutable identifier (objectGUID/entryUUID) not the DN; DN is re-resolved at bind time; editable in GUI.
- [#7882](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7882) [bug, 2024, 0/1, support] **WebUI: "Wrong username or password." when using Radius based account** -- w2 TEST: GUI login via RADIUS with local fallback; login path and 'Tester' must share the same auth code; log the failing backend reason in audit log.
- [#7941](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7941) [bug, 2024, 0/1, -] **Error when creating a new user and linking it to one or more groups** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: user+group membership written in one config transaction; no ordering between separate writes. TEST: create user with groups, verify membership.
- [#7998](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7998) [support, 2024, 0/1, support] **User login restriction - Block after multiple login attemps on Web UI possible?** -- w2 ADOPT: login rate limiting / lockout per user and per source IP for GUI, API and SSH (configurable thresholds, audit-logged, auto-unlock).
- [#6640](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6640) [support, 2023, 0/9, support] **TOTP + High-Availability (HA) -> How that works together?** -- w1 SUPPORT: TOTP in HA works when users+seeds+time are synced -> UX: HA sync covers users/TOTP secrets, docs state clock-sync requirement.
- [#8937](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8937) [feature, 2025, 0/8, support] **Send OTP QR Code via Email from LDAP User Profile** -- w1 ADOPT: self-service OTP enrolment portal for remote (LDAP/OIDC) users; optional 'send enrolment link by email' using directory mail attribute.
- [#7074](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7074) [support, 2023, 0/7, incomplete] **Automatic synchronization with Active Directory not work** -- w1 ADOPT: optional periodic LDAP sync job that imports/disables users per group filter, with dry-run and audit log; ships in core, not gated behind a paid edition.
- [#6820](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6820) [bug, 2023, 0/5, support] **System-Access-Tester: Encoding issue from Microsoft NPS server** -- w1 ADOPT: RADIUS reply attribute (Class/Filter-Id) group names decoded as UTF-8 (fallback Latin-1) before sync; TEST: umlaut group names.
- [#8145](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8145) [bug, 2024, 0/5, support] **home directories not protected** -- w1 ADOPT: admin/API users are not Unix accounts by default; shell users get 0700 homes and a dedicated primary group; CIS-style baseline test.
- [#10034](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10034) [bug, 2026, 0/5, support] **"User OTP seed" not working** -- w1 ADOPT: self-service page (password change, OTP seed regen) gated by per-group policy in schema; docs generated from same flags. TEST: group-gated OTP self-service.
- [#6307](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6307) [feature, 2023, 0/4, support] **Showing expiration date column in System:Access:User list view** -- w1 ADOPT: user grid shows expiration date, last login, auth source as sortable columns (generated grid picks fields from schema).
- [#9303](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9303) [support, 2025, 0/3, incomplete] **After upgrading to OPNsense 25.7.5, the "Import" button for importing users from Active Directory (configured as an LDAP** -- w1 SUPPORT: LDAP user-import button missing after upgrade -> UX: import action always visible on LDAP auth servers, disabled with reason tooltip when unavailable.
- [#6935](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6935) [support, 2023, 0/2, support] **User synchronization from LDAP server** -- w1 ADOPT: LDAP user import dialog queries the directory live (paged search) each time; optional scheduled user/group sync job.
- [#6979](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6979) [bug, 2023, 0/2, incomplete] **LDAP using Google can not get the memberOf attribute** -- w1 ADOPT: after bind-as-user, fetch memberOf with the service bind (Google LDAP denies self-read); configurable 'read attributes with bind DN'.
- [#7326](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7326) [support, 2024, 0/1, support] **Extended query don't work after import user** -- w1 SUPPORT: LDAP extended-query behaviour after user import -> UX: show evaluated LDAP filter and matched DNs in the auth tester; document import-vs-bind semantics.
- [#7583](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7583) [bug, 2024, 0/1, support] **OpenVPN: Wrong RADIUS_NAS_PORT_TYPE implementation value Ethernet instead Virtual** -- w1 ADOPT: RADIUS requests from VPN services set NAS-Port-Type=Virtual(5), not Ethernet; shared RADIUS client sets attributes per calling service
- [#8250](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8250) [feature, 2025, 0/1, -] **Authentification Servers - Radius - Authentification Type EAP-TTLS-PAP** -- w1 ADOPT: RADIUS auth server supports EAP-TTLS/PAP and PEAP-MSCHAPv2 in addition to PAP/MSCHAPv2 (rust radius client with EAP tunnel).
- [#8285](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8285) [feature, 2025, 0/1, support] **Last User password change** -- w1 ADOPT: user object has password_changed_at (and last_login) metadata exposed via API; optional password max-age policy.
- [#9471](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9471) [bug, 2025, 0/1, support] **Cosmetic: privilege title in user editor is out of sync with the main menu path** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: privileges are derived from API endpoint scopes and the menu tree is generated from the same registry, so names cannot drift.
- [#10073](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10073) [feature, 2026, 0/1, -] **System: Access: Tester convert to MVC** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: auth tester is an API endpoint (/auth/test with server, user, password) and the generated GUI page calls it; nothing to migrate.
- [#8146](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8146) [bug, 2024, 0/6, support] **toor / root, multiple uid=0 accounts** -- w0 N/A-LINUX: FreeBSD 'toor' second uid-0 account; NixOS base has a single root and shell users are opt-in.

### 3.18 Certificates / trust (32)

- [#7935](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7935) [bug, 2024, 0/4, support] **OpenVPN revoked certificates can connect** -- w3 ADOPT: CRL changes trigger regeneration + SIGHUP/reload of all OpenVPN/IPsec consumers; crl-verify file kept in sync; integration test revoked cert rejected
- [#6356](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6356) [bug, 2023, 0/3, incomplete] **system_advanced_admin.php crashes when ssl-cert is removed** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: deleting a CA/cert referenced by webgui/VPN is rejected by referential integrity in the config model; dangling refs can't crash pages; test deletion with refs.
- [#9049](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9049) [bug, 2025, 0/15, -] **Certificate list gives error after deleting expired certificate** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: cert store is typed; deleting one entry can't corrupt the list; API returns per-entry parse errors instead of failing the whole grid. Test: delete expired cert.
- [#7845](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7845) [support, 2024, 2/12, incomplete] **associating user and certificate** -- w2 SUPPORT: cannot link existing cert to user -> UX: user form offers 'select existing cert' and 'issue new client cert' with the user as subject; OpenVPN export picks it.
- [#6130](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6130) [bug, 2022, 2/7, upstream] **openvpn: update p12 export algorithm** -- w2 ADOPT: PKCS#12 export uses AES-256-CBC + SHA-256 MAC (OpenSSL 3 defaults), with optional legacy-compat toggle
- [#7750](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7750) [feature, 2024, 6/2, -] **Open VPN certificate expiration notification** -- w2 ADOPT: certificate expiry monitoring: dashboard widget, Prometheus metric (cert_expiry_seconds), configurable notification (mail/webhook) N days before expiry.
- [#9295](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9295) [bug, 2025, 0/5, incomplete] **Unable to clean up / delete revoked certificates.** -- w2 ADOPT: revoked certs stay in CRL but are deletable from the active store once no service references them; revocation and deletion are distinct typed states.
- [#7519](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7519) [feature, 2024, 0/4, support] **VPN Hardening (ciphers/encryption & config parameters)** -- w2 ADOPT: CA/cert generation supports ECDSA P-256/P-384 and Ed25519 with sane defaults; OpenVPN/IPsec profiles expose cipher/tls-min settings with secure presets.
- [#9399](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9399) [bug, 2025, 0/4, support] **The API needs an endpoint to switch or activate a webgui certificate** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: API-first: webgui active certificate is a config field settable via API; ACME/external renewals automatable
- [#9448](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9448) [bug, 2025, 0/4, support] **OpenVPN on Opnsense 25.7.7_4 fails when creating a server using a cert with CA + Intermediate CA** -- w2 ADOPT: OpenVPN server renders full CA chain (root+intermediate) inline when cert depth > 1; integration test intermediate-CA server start
- [#5629](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5629) [feature, 2022, 0/2, incomplete] **Correct keyUsage filed in creating self-signed CA** -- w2 ADOPT: generated CAs set keyUsage=keyCertSign,cRLSign (critical), basicConstraints CA:TRUE, SKI/AKI; server certs set serverAuth EKU; test with openssl verify.
- [#6646](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6646) [feature, 2023, 0/2, incomplete] **Feature Request : Certificate Renew for OpenVPN** -- w2 ADOPT: one-click 'renew' for internal CA-issued certs (same key or new key) with automatic reload of consuming services (OpenVPN/IPsec/GUI)
- [#7176](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7176) [bug, 2024, 0/2, -] **Certificate store does not flag server certificates as in-use, when allocated to a OpenVPN instance (server instance)** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: typed references make cert 'in use' computed from the schema graph; delete of a referenced cert rejected by validator for every consumer
- [#7622](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7622) [feature, 2024, 0/2, incomplete] **Certificate Renew/Reuse** -- w2 ADOPT: one-click certificate/CA renew via API (reuse CSR/subject, optional key rotation) that keeps all referencing services bound to the renewed cert.
- [#8484](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8484) [feature, 2025, 0/2, support] **Allow WebUI to use self-signed certs generated by external CA** -- w2 ADOPT: imported certs infer usage (server/client) from EKU/keyUsage rather than requiring the issuing CA+key present; any imported cert selectable for webgui TLS.
- [#10247](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10247) [bug, 2026, 0/2, incomplete] **Version: 26.1.7 - Component: System → Trust → Certificates - Title: Certificate form: Key length resets to 2048, lifetim** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: form constraints come from the schema (lifetime cap applies only to publicly-trusted server certs, not CAs/VPN); GUI cannot drift from backend validation.
- [#6470](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6470) [feature, 2023, 0/1, -] **Trust: Certificates: Add a local certificate** -- w2 ADOPT: certs can reference external PEM paths or be pushed via API (ACME/scripts); private keys stored in a separate 0600 secrets store, not inline in the exported config.
- [#9470](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9470) [other, 2025, 0/15, incomplete] **Handling of TLS certitificates in OPNsense** -- w1 ADOPT: default webgui cert is generated at first boot with the host's actual FQDN as CN/SAN; generic 'provide trusted cert' flow with ACME built in. Docs explain.
- [#6303](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6303) [support, 2023, 1/11, support] **OpenVPN-Gui 2.6 unable to decrypt .p12 file created in Client Export** -- w1 SUPPORT: OpenVPN 2.6 cannot read legacy-encrypted p12 -> same fix as 6130: modern PKCS#12 ciphers by default
- [#9960](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9960) [bug, 2026, 0/5, -] **/api/trust/ca/search returns incorrect digest and key_type** -- w1 TEST: API test asserting CA search metadata (digest, key_type, key size) matches the parsed X.509; derive metadata from the cert at read time, never from stored form input.
- [#6007](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6007) [feature, 2022, 2/2, support] **Add support for SSH Certificates** -- w1 ADOPT: SSH server supports TrustedUserCAKeys / SSH CA certificates in the admin-access settings, alongside per-user authorized keys.
- [#7089](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7089) [feature, 2023, 2/2, incomplete] **Filter Certs by Issuer CA** -- w1 ADOPT: certificate grid has server-side filter/sort by issuer CA, expiry, usage; grid filtering is generic in the GUI framework.
- [#8205](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8205) [bug, 2025, 1/3, -] **System/Trust/Certificates: Buttons for "show certificate info" and "download" do nothing when clicked** -- w1 TEST: e2e test for cert info/download actions after a config restore; cert blob parsing is server-side via API, not client-side JS.
- [#7621](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7621) [support, 2024, 0/3, incomplete] **Certificate Renew/Reuse** -- w1 SUPPORT: how to renew a cert -> UX: 'Renew' action on cert/CA rows (reissue with same key or new key, keep refs); duplicate of 7622.
- [#8011](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8011) [feature, 2024, 1/1, -] **Hide / mark revoked certificates** -- w1 ADOPT: revoked/expired certs get status badges and are filtered out of selectors (OpenVPN export, service cert pickers) by default.
- [#9271](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9271) [feature, 2025, 0/2, -] **Extended key usage for certificates** -- w1 ADOPT: cert issuance form exposes EKU multi-select (serverAuth, clientAuth, codeSigning, emailProtection, ipsecIKE, OCSPSigning) plus custom OIDs.
- [#9376](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9376) [feature, 2025, 1/1, support] **Add CA v3 constraints options to CA creation dialog** -- w1 ADOPT: CA creation dialog exposes pathlen constraint, name constraints (permitted DNS/IP), and EKU restrictions for the CA.
- [#6694](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6694) [bug, 2023, 0/1, -] **certificates with subjectKeyIdentifier generating a warning in 23.7** -- w1 TEST: import certs with subjectKeyIdentifier/AKI extensions and assert no spurious warnings; parse X.509 via a real library (rustls/x509-parser), not string matching.
- [#7746](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7746) [other, 2024, 0/1, -] **Exporting certificates in OpenVPN module** -- w1 ADOPT: cert/export filenames include CN + serial/short fingerprint; bulk export of all client bundles per user as one zip
- [#8245](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8245) [feature, 2025, 0/10, support] **[Feature Request] Add Polarproxy.** -- w0 OUT-OF-SCOPE: TLS decryption proxy (PolarProxy) for Suricata is plugin territory; core only exposes a clean cert-store API for such plugins.
- [#5888](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5888) [support, 2022, 0/3, support] **Squid in SSL-Inspection Mode doesn't  display several Sites, there are CA- or Certificate Errors** -- w0 OUT-OF-SCOPE: Squid SSL-bump site breakage is a proxy-plugin matter; sensr core does not do TLS interception.
- [#5969](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5969) [support, 2022, 0/1, support] **Proxy doesn't provide any certificate with Log SNI information only activated** -- w0 OUT-OF-SCOPE: Squid SNI-only inspection mode behaviour; proxy plugin territory.

### 3.19 API / MVC / configd / CLI (24)

- [#9345](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9345) [feature, 2025, 12/5, support] **Feature: API support for interface assignment (physical port → WAN/LAN) and IP configuration** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: interface assignment + IP config are schema fields, so Packer/Terraform/Ansible can bootstrap a fresh box via API or declarative config file.
- [#7324](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7324) [feature, 2024, 3/11, support] **Add interface assignments to API** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: interface assignment (logical name -> physical port) is part of the typed config model, hence fully API/IaC manageable from first boot.
- [#5776](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5776) [feature, 2022, 7/2, -] **Feature request: create api user from api or shell** -- w3 ADOPT: API keys are first-class API objects: create/revoke keys (with scopes) via API and CLI for IaC bootstrap, not only via GUI.
- [#9497](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9497) [bug, 2025, 1/6, support] **API documentation erroneously provides UUID parameters whereas the UI uses the ID** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: single generated OpenAPI spec from the schema, every object addressed by UUID in GUI and API alike; no legacy numeric IDs.
- [#9671](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9671) [feature, 2026, 1/3, support] **Add Core API support to create interfaces** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: interfaces and VLANs (with user-chosen device names) are config objects created via API; Ansible/Terraform full-config possible by design.
- [#10115](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10115) [bug, 2026, 0/3, incomplete] **Auth/User searchAction leaks password hashes, apikeys, otp_seed via API** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: schema marks secret fields (hashes, API secrets, TOTP seeds) as write-only; serializers strip them by default. TEST: no secret in any GET.
- [#6586](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6586) [bug, 2023, 0/2, support] **Simulateneous editing of firewall rules can lead to unexpected results** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: all mutations address rules by UUID and carry config version (optimistic concurrency / ETag); stale edits are rejected with reload prompt.
- [#7954](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7954) [bug, 2024, 0/2, support] **No Race Condition Handling: Two parallel admin-actions at the same time: 2nd Update overwrites the first update** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: config writes are transactional with optimistic concurrency (If-Match/version token); concurrent saves conflict explicitly instead of overwriting. Test it.
- [#8417](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8417) [bug, 2025, 0/2, incomplete] **Can't modify/update/change WAN gateway using API** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: every GUI mutation is an API call with OpenAPI schema, so no 'GUI-only' endpoints; gateway set/update tested by API contract tests.
- [#6938](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6938) [feature, 2023, 0/1, support] **allow API to enable/disable Floating / Interface firewall rules** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: all firewall rules (floating, group, interface, auto-generated) live in one schema, so API enable/disable covers every rule uniformly.
- [#7315](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7315) [feature, 2024, 0/1, support] **Add api endpoints to assign static dhcp to mac address** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: API-first: every GUI object (incl. v6 reservations) has CRUD endpoints in OpenAPI; Terraform provider generated from spec
- [#8475](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8475) [feature, 2025, 0/1, -] **API Support for Interfaces: Settings** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: every setting (incl. IPv6 enable, advanced network tunables) is in the schema and thus readable/writable via API.
- [#7608](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7608) [bug, 2024, 0/7, -] **Fatal memory exhaustion error when updating firewall rules via REST API** -- w2 TEST: API set_rule on large configs must stay bounded in memory/time; load-test rule CRUD with 10k rules + aliases; return proper 5xx JSON, never HTML fatal.
- [#9169](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9169) [bug, 2025, 0/6, support] **WireGuard API endpoint not working** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: service control API returns actual result (systemd job status), never 'OK' without action; contract test per service endpoint
- [#5593](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5593) [support, 2022, 0/4, support] **PFStatistcs API not returning values** -- w2 SUPPORT: user couldn't call pf stats endpoint -> UX: OpenAPI spec with required params + examples for every endpoint; 4xx with explicit 'missing param' errors.
- [#8148](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8148) [support, 2024, 1/3, incomplete] **Can't create Nat Port Forward via API..** -- w2 SUPPORT: NAT API usage -> UX: OpenAPI with examples for every endpoint, GUI 'show API call' for each form, CLI mirrors API (rx=1).
- [#5490](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5490) [feature, 2022, 0/3, support] **configctl openvpn support** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: API-first means every OpenVPN instance has start/stop/restart/status endpoints and the CLI exposes them; no ad-hoc control channel
- [#7232](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7232) [feature, 2024, 2/1, -] **Enhance Command-Line Interface Management for Configuring Network Interfaces** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: CLI is a thin API client over the typed config model, so interfaces can be modified in place from the shell without delete/recreate
- [#7298](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7298) [bug, 2024, 0/3, incomplete] **API kea/dhcpv4/getSubnet not responding with actual Subnets** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: API generated from schema returns full typed subnet objects (cidr, pools[]) with list/get symmetry; OpenAPI contract tests catch empty fields
- [#8787](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8787) [feature, 2025, 0/1, -] **unbound: add option to manually clear cache via API** -- w2 ADOPT: resolver API endpoints: flush cache (all/zone/name), reload, dump stats; apply of forwarder change offers keep-cache option
- [#8880](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8880) [bug, 2025, 0/1, support] **OpenVPN GUI Instance Configuration vs API** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: GUI and API read the same typed model; booleans are JSON true/false, selections are plain values; no divergent representations
- [#9446](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9446) [bug, 2025, 0/1, support] **The API endpoint do delete a certificate does not work** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: OpenAPI spec generated from code; contract tests ensure every documented endpoint exists; docs can't drift
- [#9599](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9599) [feature, 2026, 0/1, support] **Feature Request: [Wireguard] Granular API privilege management** -- w2 ADOPT: RBAC scopes per API resource instance (e.g. peers of WG instance X) so automation keys can be restricted to one instance
- [#9073](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9073) [bug, 2025, 0/4, support] **configctl control over wireguard inoperable after upgrade to OPNsense 25.7.1_1-amd64** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: CLI is the same API client as GUI; service start/stop/restart endpoints exercised by contract tests for every service

### 3.20 GUI / UX / i18n (97)

- [#6195](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6195) [feature, 2022, 0/3, incomplete] **CSP security issue due to it being ineffective** -- w3 ADOPT: strict CSP from day one: no inline scripts/eval, hashed/nonced bundles, trusted-types; SPA built to comply; CI check for CSP header.
- [#8374](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8374) [bug, 2025, 0/12, support] **WebUI Display Issues After 25.1.1 Upgrade** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: GUI bundle versioned with backend in one closure; no plugin/core version conflict page since plugins are built into the same generation.
- [#8686](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8686) [bug, 2025, 0/12, support] **wireguard peer status: confusing status icon** -- w2 ADOPT: WG peer status icons carry text label + tooltip (handshake age, rx/tx); status states explicit: never/stale/active
- [#7590](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7590) [feature, 2024, 1/10, feature] **In-progress accessibility fixes for screen reader users** -- w2 ADOPT: WCAG 2.1 AA accessibility as a SPA requirement: ARIA labels on generated forms/grids/dialogs, keyboard nav, axe-core checks in CI.
- [#5758](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5758) [feature, 2022, 0/9, support] **New option WebGUI enable/disable in "opnsense-shell"** -- w2 ADOPT: console/CLI menu option to toggle GUI/API reachability per interface (temporarily allow WAN mgmt) for headless cloud/VM bootstrap.
- [#8669](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8669) [other, 2025, 8/1, -] **WebUI Design: VPN->IPsec->Log File: The third time I disconnected all my users** -- w2 ADOPT: service control buttons kept out of log viewers; restart actions require confirmation and are visually separated; rx=8
- [#8723](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8723) [feature, 2025, 0/8, support] **Make the menu retractable** -- w2 ADOPT: collapsible/icon-only sidebar with remembered state; content area uses full width on 1080p.
- [#6132](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6132) [feature, 2022, 0/7, support] **Better UI notification when configd crashes** -- w2 ADOPT: GUI health banner when backend daemon/apply service is unreachable or degraded; apply must fail loudly, never silently time out.
- [#10137](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10137) [feature, 2026, 0/7, support] **[Cleanup] Tabulator: make the order of grid entries consistent (or editable)** -- w2 ADOPT: grids default to a deterministic server-side sort (name/sequence) and remember user sort per grid; ordered collections expose drag-drop sequence.
- [#5721](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5721) [feature, 2022, 0/6, feature] **Accessibility improvment** -- w2 ADOPT: generated forms emit <label for> / aria-labelledby for every input and checkbox; axe-core a11y lint in CI (dup of 7590).
- [#8912](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8912) [bug, 2025, 0/6, support] **Hidden fields in the "add alias" form are being stored (which may lead to exposed passwords from browser autofill)** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: forms generated from schema only submit fields valid for the chosen variant (tagged enum); backend rejects unknown fields, nothing hidden is stored.
- [#6192](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6192) [bug, 2022, 0/5, support] **Can't select more than one entry in lists** -- w2 ADOPT: all grids support multi-select (checkbox column, shift-click) with bulk delete/enable/disable; TEST: multi-select across pages.
- [#7393](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7393) [feature, 2024, 0/5, -] **Customizable fields/columns for firewall rule lists** -- w2 ADOPT: rule grid with user-selectable columns (all schema fields incl. TCP flags, gateway, tag, category) persisted per user.
- [#6760](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6760) [bug, 2023, 0/4, -] **system_advanced_admin.php crashes after switching the protocol** -- w2 ADOPT: switching GUI HTTP<->HTTPS/port validates certs, applies transactionally and redirects the browser to the new URL; auto-rollback if new listener fails health check.
- [#7252](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7252) [feature, 2024, 0/4, incomplete] **GUI: 7 rows** -- w2 ADOPT: server-side per-user preferences (default rows per page, per-grid overrides, theme) so grid settings persist across pages and sessions.
- [#7303](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7303) [feature, 2024, 2/2, -] **Make tables in GUI horizontally scrollable to make viewable on mobile** -- w2 ADOPT: generic grid component scrolls horizontally in a container and offers column hide/priority on narrow viewports (mobile-usable leases table).
- [#8294](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8294) [feature, 2025, 0/4, support] **Web UI search is a bit weak** -- w2 ADOPT: global search indexes menu entries AND schema field labels/help text (generated from schema), deep-linking to the field.
- [#9585](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9585) [bug, 2026, 0/4, cleanup] **Unbound DNS > Domain Overrides / Deleting multiple selected** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: bulk delete is a single batch API call (array of ids) in generated grid framework; TEST multi-select delete on every grid
- [#5811](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5811) [feature, 2022, 0/2, incomplete] **Too Easy to disable VPN** -- w2 ADOPT: destructive one-click toggles (disable VPN server) require confirmation, especially when the admin session itself traverses that VPN
- [#7634](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7634) [bug, 2024, 0/2, incomplete] **Webgui - jquery error after upgrade from 23.7 to 24.1** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: SPA assets are content-hashed and shipped in the same Nix closure as the backend; no stale cached JS after upgrade. Service-worker cache busting tested.
- [#8210](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8210) [bug, 2025, 1/1, -] **[ all editions ] UI : regard red-green weakness** -- w2 ADOPT: status indicators never rely on colour alone: distinct icon shape + text label; colour-blind-safe palette (dataviz tokens); a11y check in CI.
- [#9784](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9784) [feature, 2026, 0/2, incomplete] **Proper Logout** -- w2 ADOPT: SPA detects 401 on any API call / session expiry and redirects to login preserving the return URL; idle-timeout countdown warning.
- [#5970](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5970) [bug, 2022, 0/1, support] **Make gateway group name editable or make the group name field not a field** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: objects referenced by UUID, so display names (gateway group) are freely renamable; generated forms mark truly immutable fields read-only.
- [#6113](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6113) [feature, 2022, 0/1, support] **Chainable/Stackable default searches in grid.** -- w2 ADOPT: grid search supports multiple stacked field:value filters (server-side, URL-encoded, shareable) in addition to free text.
- [#6117](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6117) [feature, 2022, 0/1, -] **Show hostname in lease removal dialogue** -- w2 ADOPT: generated delete-confirm dialogs always show the row's identity fields (IP, MAC, hostname) for every grid, not just 'Are you sure?'
- [#6257](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6257) [feature, 2023, 0/1, -] **Accessibility Red Green Visual impairment** -- w2 ADOPT: status indicators never rely on color alone (icon shape + text label); colorblind-safe palette and a11y audit of generated GUI
- [#7539](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7539) [feature, 2024, 0/1, -] **Request: Please add some information to the firewall deletion popup** -- w2 ADOPT: generic delete-confirm shows rule description/identity (same requirement as #6117); applies to all generated grids
- [#8114](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8114) [feature, 2024, 0/1, -] **[Feature Request] UI: Fire "Wait" prompt and Greyout on possibly unresponsive / long tasks** -- w2 ADOPT: every apply is an async job with progress/status endpoint; GUI shows blocking 'applying...' overlay with steps and ETA, and reconnect logic if mgmt IP changes.
- [#8498](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8498) [feature, 2025, 0/1, -] **UI Logic, systemwide: Replace DISABLE checkboxes by unchecked ENABLE checkboxes** -- w2 ADOPT: schema style rule: boolean fields are always positive 'enabled' (default documented); linter rejects 'disable_*' field names.
- [#9525](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9525) [feature, 2025, 0/1, support] **Paradigm/design pattern** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: sensr is a client-rendered SPA over a JSON API; server never produces HTML. Rendering cost on the client, firewall CPU untouched.
- [#9901](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9901) [feature, 2026, 2/14, -] **ui: width of sidebar menu above 768px** -- w1 ADOPT: responsive sidebar: collapsible/off-canvas between 768 and ~1100px rather than full-width; tested via Playwright viewport matrix.
- [#9440](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9440) [feature, 2025, 0/8, support] **Could you please do this behind the scenes rather than have the user edit the value manually?** -- w1 ADOPT: validation errors offer one-click 'fix for me' when a deterministic normalization exists (e.g. identifier sanitizing); normalize at parse
- [#8298](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8298) [feature, 2025, 0/7, -] **Using NIST supported language: 'Allowlist'** -- w1 ADOPT: use allowlist/blocklist/passlist terminology consistently in schema, GUI, API and docs from the start.
- [#9323](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9323) [bug, 2025, 0/6, support] **Kea DHCPv4 Leases list too short** -- w1 TEST: grid component layout tests (height fills container) in Playwright for every generated grid including modal-embedded ones
- [#9925](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9925) [other, 2026, 0/6, incomplete] **Clearer text description in Wireguard configuration** -- w1 ADOPT: schema help texts include v4 and v6 examples and consistent 'interface' terminology; help text reviewed as part of schema
- [#8936](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8936) [bug, 2025, 0/5, support] **lighttpd - Connection refused** -- w1 N/A-LINUX: lighttpd->php-fastcgi socket refused; sensr GUI/API is one Rust binary under systemd, no FastCGI hop. Keep: GUI health in journald + systemd watchdog.
- [#9436](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9436) [bug, 2025, 0/5, support] **Alias screen search function cannot find by partial names results that begin with an underscore** -- w1 TEST: server-side grid search must be plain substring match incl. leading '_' and regex metachars; test search tokens with _ and .
- [#9788](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9788) [feature, 2026, 0/5, incomplete] **[Feature] Descriptions using an HTTP url should be HTML url link** -- w1 ADOPT: description fields render http(s)/mailto URLs as safe links (rel=noopener, no HTML injection) in grids and detail views.
- [#9917](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9917) [other, 2026, 0/5, support] **Rules [new] - minor improvements** -- w1 ADOPT: rule grid polish list (consistent icons, selection, tooltips); treat as UX acceptance checklist for rule grid component.
- [#6358](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6358) [bug, 2023, 0/4, -] **WebGUI: Unbound Blocklists console error saving** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: typed API responses (OpenAPI client) with null-safe generated form binding; a backend validation payload cannot crash the form JS.
- [#6416](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6416) [feature, 2023, 0/4, incomplete] **Use CSP frame-ancestors with GUI for whitelisted URLs** -- w1 ADOPT: configurable CSP frame-ancestors allowlist (default 'self') to embed GUI in dashboards like Home Assistant; no X-Frame-Options.
- [#6484](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6484) [bug, 2023, 0/4, support] **Lateral bar weird behavior on iPad Pro** -- w1 TEST: SPA navigation sidebar on tablet viewports (iPad Safari/touch) in Playwright device matrix; responsive menu with no hover dependence.
- [#7688](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7688) [feature, 2024, 0/4, feature] **Redirect to login screen in all open browser tabs when session of WebGUI becomes invalid** -- w1 ADOPT: SPA detects 401 on any API call and redirects to login; BroadcastChannel propagates logout to all tabs.
- [#7798](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7798) [bug, 2024, 0/4, incomplete] **Services: Intrusion Detection: Administration Download busy wheel keeps on turning even after download is finished** -- w1 ADOPT: long-running actions (rule download) are API jobs with status endpoint; SPA polls/streams job state and clears the spinner on completion; e2e test.
- [#9516](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9516) [bug, 2025, 0/4, -] **Form submission issue: Return key triggers wrong action on backup/restore page** -- w1 ADOPT: generated forms have one well-defined default submit action; Enter key triggers primary action; e2e test for restore form.
- [#5903](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5903) [bug, 2022, 0/3, incomplete] **IPsec: Tunnel Settings - Phase 2 list all hides last entry** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: grid page size 'all' handled server-side as no-limit in one shared grid component; unit test -1/all semantics
- [#6508](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6508) [feature, 2023, 1/2, support] **Favicon isn't displayed when bookmark to the dashboard is saved in Safari favourites.** -- w1 ADOPT: ship favicon.ico + SVG/PNG icons + apple-touch-icon + web manifest so bookmarks/PWA installs show icons in all browsers.
- [#8716](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8716) [feature, 2025, 0/3, incomplete] **Feature Request: Support for Arabic language in the OPNsense GUI** -- w1 ADOPT: i18n via Weblate/Crowdin with RTL support (Arabic, Hebrew) in the SPA: dir=auto, logical CSS properties.
- [#9420](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9420) [support, 2025, 0/3, support] **Webgui stopped responding immediately after install** -- w1 SUPPORT: GUI unreachable after install -> UX: console menu shows GUI listen addresses + health check and offers 'reset GUI access' (anti-lockout, HTTP fallback).
- [#9614](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9614) [bug, 2026, 0/3, support] **cleanup: GUI improvement: change misleading icon for VPN management (2 minutes, one LOC)** -- w1 ADOPT: shared icon vocabulary in the design system: disconnect=link-slash, delete=trash, disable=toggle; reviewed once, used everywhere.
- [#10167](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10167) [bug, 2026, 0/3, support] **Autocomplete not working as expected on login screen** -- w1 ADOPT: login form uses standard autocomplete=username/current-password/one-time-code attributes; TEST: Playwright checks browser autofill heuristics.
- [#5500](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5500) [feature, 2022, 0/2, incomplete] **Search box does not support aliases (no results)** -- w1 ADOPT: GUI command palette searches aliases, pages, services and synonyms/package names (ntp->Network Time, ssh->Administration) via API search endpoint.
- [#5706](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5706) [bug, 2022, 0/2, incomplete] **List quantity filter value vs label display.** -- w1 ADOPT: grid page-size persisted as value with label lookup; TEST: 'All' survives reload. Generic grid component means one fix everywhere.
- [#7170](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7170) [feature, 2024, 0/2, incomplete] **Alias sorting in firewall drop down lists** -- w1 ADOPT: alias/network dropdowns in rule editors sorted alphabetically with search-as-you-type (server-side search endpoint).
- [#7352](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7352) [support, 2024, 0/2, support] **[ BE 23.10.2, 23.10.3 ] "VPN: Wireguard" submenu missing** -- w1 SUPPORT: WireGuard menu missing on business edition (plugin not installed) -> DESIGN: WireGuard is in core, always present
- [#8112](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8112) [feature, 2024, 0/2, incomplete] **[Feature Request] Possible to show category in firewall rule table/list** -- w1 ADOPT: category column + category filter in rule grid (same as #7393 column chooser).
- [#8149](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8149) [feature, 2024, 0/2, support] **Option to hide disabled firewall rules in GUI** -- w1 ADOPT: rule grid filter 'hide disabled' (server-side enabled=true filter), remembered per user.
- [#8674](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8674) [bug, 2025, 0/2, incomplete] **ISC DHCPv6 address sort** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: grid sorting of IP columns is typed (IpAddr compare, not lexical) in the server-side grid framework; TEST ::21 < ::200
- [#8958](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8958) [bug, 2025, 0/2, support] **Language switch is not working** -- w1 ADOPT: language is a per-user preference applied instantly client-side (SPA i18n) with system default fallback; TEST: switch zh->en->zh.
- [#9176](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9176) [feature, 2025, 0/2, incomplete] **Feature request** -- w1 ADOPT: optional login-page branding (logo image + banner/MOTD text) stored in config; sanitised, no external resources.
- [#9742](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9742) [bug, 2026, 0/2, support] **search bar in Interfaces:Neighbors:Automatic Discovery does a partial match** -- w1 ADOPT: grid search defaults to substring match with exact-match toggle; same framework behaviour for all grids
- [#10142](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10142) [other, 2026, 0/2, incomplete] **System: Settings: Administration: Unclear documentation, help text** -- w1 ADOPT: help text is generated from schema field docs incl. examples ('fqdn, e.g. fw.example.internal'); DNS-rebinding allowlist validated as FQDN list.
- [#10161](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10161) [bug, 2026, 0/2, incomplete] **System: Settings: Administration http/HTTPS always Same value** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: HTTP and HTTPS listen ports are distinct schema fields with distinct form widgets; generated forms can't alias two fields. Test redirect-port settings.
- [#10165](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10165) [bug, 2026, 0/2, -] **[Dnsmasq:Ranges] Tabulator visual corruption on 19th entry** -- w1 TEST: grid rendering with 20+ rows and optional columns (visual regression test); pick a grid library with virtualization
- [#6501](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6501) [bug, 2023, 0/1, support] **Web-UI not responding on Link-Local (fe80)** -- w1 ADOPT: management listener binds [::]:443 incl. link-local scope so http://[fe80::x%iface] works; TEST GUI reachable on LL address from LAN
- [#6624](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6624) [feature, 2023, 0/1, support] **base url for GUI** -- w1 ADOPT: SPA and API served under a configurable base path (e.g. /fw/) so the GUI can sit behind a path-routing reverse proxy; relative asset URLs.
- [#7051](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7051) [bug, 2023, 0/1, -] **WebUI bug on mobile - save button hidden by bottom copyrigth bar** -- w1 ADOPT: form actions in a sticky, safe-area-aware bar; no fixed footer overlapping content on mobile; Playwright mobile viewport tests.
- [#7613](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7613) [bug, 2024, 0/1, support] **When Cloning a sprocket in opnsense's UI, the modal that's created retains the 'editing' title. leading to confusion** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: generated form dialogs carry explicit mode (create/edit/clone) and title; clone = new object prefilled. TEST: dialog title per mode.
- [#7915](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7915) [feature, 2024, 0/1, -] **Improve sidebar collapsed vs. expanded state** -- w1 ADOPT: SPA sidebar collapsible/pinned with remembered state and compact icon mode on mobile.
- [#7973](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7973) [bug, 2024, 0/1, support] **Firewall alias first page list view does not display category color dot consistently** -- w1 TEST: grid cell renderers (category color) consistent on first load vs refresh; Playwright snapshot of first page
- [#8027](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8027) [feature, 2024, 0/1, -] **On the Firewall - Rules - Interface Windows, make the icons for enabled and disabled logging more distinguishable** -- w1 ADOPT: rule-grid logging indicator uses distinct icon shape + tooltip for on/off, not only color shade
- [#8076](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8076) [support, 2024, 0/1, support] **Size down the wide of Interface Statistics coloums** -- w1 SUPPORT: statistics table too wide -> UX: grids have responsive/collapsible columns and user-selectable column visibility
- [#8113](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8113) [bug, 2024, 0/1, -] **UI: DHCPd>Table Views: Prevent word-wrapping to make content readable** -- w1 ADOPT: grid cells for addresses/MACs/IDs use nowrap + monospace + ellipsis with tooltip; horizontal scroll instead of wrapping (see 7303).
- [#8365](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8365) [feature, 2025, 0/1, -] **Change PHP session name to be dynamic** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: session cookie name includes a per-instance identifier (or use __Host- cookie scoped by host:port tokens) so several boxes behind SSH forwards coexist.
- [#8799](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8799) [feature, 2025, 0/1, -] **CSV export: option to save with UTF-8 BOM to fix broken non-english when opening in Excel** -- w1 ADOPT: CSV export option 'UTF-8 with BOM' (Excel-friendly) on all grid exports; import tolerates BOM.
- [#9047](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9047) [bug, 2025, 0/1, -] **Visual bug: opnsense-dark theme, vertical line appears in WebGUI when using menu** -- w1 TEST: visual regression tests (Playwright screenshots) for dark theme menus/dividers in the SPA.
- [#9113](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9113) [bug, 2025, 0/1, -] **Table visualisation shows error** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: config migrations are schema-versioned and tested in CI, so upgraded configs cannot produce grid rendering errors from missing/renamed fields.
- [#9431](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9431) [feature, 2025, 0/1, support] **Could you please make the Protocl/Source/Destination drop down lists taller on the FW screen?** -- w1 ADOPT: searchable select widgets with tall (viewport-relative) dropdowns and type-ahead for all rule pickers
- [#9437](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9437) [feature, 2025, 0/1, support] **Could you please add a keyboard shortcut to close the alias edit pop-up with Escape?** -- w1 ADOPT: all modal/edit dialogs close on Escape, submit on Ctrl+Enter; generated-form framework behaviour
- [#9654](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9654) [feature, 2026, 0/1, feature] **wizard: better defaults handling** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: wizard is a generated form over schema defaults; missing nodes fall back to typed defaults, never to empty strings.

- [#9733](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9733) [feature, 2026, 0/10, support] **[REQUEST] Rules [new] - When Statistics are empty they dont show up** -- w0 ADOPT: statistics column always renders (0 pkts / 0 B) for consistent row layout.
- [#9877](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9877) [support, 2026, 0/5, support] **Log search/filtering interface not visible under some browsers** -- w0 SUPPORT: log search UI invisible on Palemoon -> UX: SPA targets evergreen browsers only, with explicit unsupported-browser banner instead of silent breakage
- [#9900](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9900) [feature, 2026, 0/4, -] **feature: enable theme plugins to provide custom menu systems** -- w0 OUT-OF-SCOPE: theme plugins replacing menu structure; sensr offers theme tokens (colours/fonts) and a user-selectable nav layout (side/top) in core, not pluggable DOM.
