- [#7497](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7497) [feature, 2024, 1/1, support] **Rename "Firmware" menu entry to something else, move it to the top of the list along with settings.** -- w0 ADOPT: name the update page 'System Updates' (not 'Firmware'); place it under System alongside Settings.

- [#10082](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10082) [bug, 2026, 0/2, -] **first page of alias list fails to render on initial load until resize event fires** -- w0 TEST: grid component renders first page without needing resize event; Playwright test for alias grid initial render.
- [#7409](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7409) [bug, 2024, 0/1, -] **Gateway name is truncated in firewall rules page** -- w0 ADOPT: grid cells show full gateway name on hover/tooltip and never truncate identifying names silently.

### 3.21 Dashboard / widgets (28)

- [#8134](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8134) [bug, 2024, 0/3, support] **GUI shows Low Hard drive usage but Plugins failing because there is no space** -- w2 ADOPT: disk widget shows every mounted FS (incl. /var, tmpfs logs) with alerts at thresholds; services log ENOSPC as a user-visible notification.
- [#9293](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9293) [bug, 2025, 0/3, incomplete] **Dashboard** -- w2 ADOPT: live traffic graph keeps a bounded ring buffer and pauses streaming when tab hidden (visibilitychange); TEST: 24h soak with heap limit.
- [#9434](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9434) [bug, 2025, 0/3, incomplete] **Dark mode dashboard charts use unreadable text** -- w2 ADOPT: chart theme derived from CSS variables (dataviz tokens) so dark mode text/legends/axes are readable; visual regression test per theme.
- [#8953](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8953) [bug, 2025, 0/9, upstream] **Image file upload does not work for large images** -- w1 ADOPT: upload size limits are explicit (server returns 413 with a human message); large dashboard images are stored as files, not base64 in config.
- [#6911](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6911) [support, 2023, 0/6, support] **CPU temperature reporting in dashboard** -- w1 ADOPT: temperature widget reads hwmon/coretemp per-core and package sensors with labels; show source so users can compare with `sensors`.
- [#8252](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8252) [bug, 2025, 0/6, support] **Announcements widget: Increase the timeout** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: no widget calls external feeds synchronously; remote fetches happen in a backend job with cache, widget reads cached JSON instantly.
- [#10172](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10172) [feature, 2026, 0/5, -] **[REQUEST] Alight the sort order of Interface & Interface stats widget** -- w1 ADOPT: one canonical interface ordering (config order, user-adjustable) consumed by every widget and page.
- [#7069](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7069) [feature, 2023, 0/3, support] **Hide ZFS datasets in Lobby: Dashboard: System Information: Disk usage** -- w1 ADOPT: disk widget shows real mountpoints only (filter tmpfs/overlay/nix store bind mounts), with expandable detail; ZFS datasets collapsed.
- [#5509](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5509) [bug, 2022, 0/2, incomplete] **Gateways widget not displaying stats** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: dashboard widgets and status pages read the same API endpoint (gateway health JSON), so they cannot disagree; TEST: widget renders RTT/loss.
- [#5519](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5519) [feature, 2022, 0/2, incomplete] **Feature Request: Services Widget Not Running at Top** -- w1 ADOPT: services widget sorts failed/stopped-but-enabled units first and flags them even if hidden (systemd unit state via API).
- [#5549](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5549) [other, 2022, 0/2, incomplete] **Change The Use of the Dark Red On the Dashboard For Showing Resource Utilization** -- w1 ADOPT: dashboard gauges use neutral colour for utilisation and reserve red/amber for configurable thresholds (dataviz semantics).
- [#7212](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7212) [feature, 2024, 0/2, incomplete] **Interface speed on "Interfaces" widget when the interface is a PPPoE type** -- w1 ADOPT: interfaces widget shows parent device link speed/duplex for virtual interfaces (PPPoE, VLAN) from netlink ethtool of the underlying NIC.
- [#7653](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7653) [bug, 2024, 0/2, incomplete] **Firewall widget initial load only one log entry** -- w1 ADOPT: live firewall log widget seeds with last N entries from the log API then streams via WebSocket/SSE; TEST: initial fill = N.
- [#7754](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7754) [feature, 2024, 0/2, incomplete] **Dashboard widget initial height** -- w1 ADOPT: persist widget width+height in the per-user dashboard layout and reserve that box before data loads to avoid layout jumps.
- [#8167](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8167) [feature, 2024, 0/2, incomplete] **Feature Request on Interface-Widget on Dashboard: Sort Interfaces by Drag&Drop or by IP-Subnets** -- w1 ADOPT: interfaces widget order configurable (drag-drop or by role/name/subnet) and shared with the interface stats widget (see 10172).
- [#8287](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8287) [bug, 2025, 0/2, incomplete] **Dashboard widget unstable at specific viewport size** -- w1 TEST: widget responsive mode switch (table vs chart) uses hysteresis/container queries; Playwright sweep over viewport widths asserts no oscillation.
- [#8369](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8369) [feature, 2025, 1/1, -] **Dashboard: Traffic Graph Widget Customizations** -- w1 ADOPT: widgets resizable independently in width and height on the grid, with per-widget options (graph interfaces, time window).
- [#8660](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8660) [bug, 2025, 0/2, -] **1440p Dashboard Constant Resizing** -- w1 TEST: dashboard layout stable at 2560x1271 and all common resolutions; scrollbar appearance must not toggle breakpoints (use scrollbar-gutter: stable).
- [#8869](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8869) [feature, 2025, 0/2, support] **Get back the tabular "Interface Statistics" and "Firewall" to the dashboard.** -- w1 ADOPT: offer both tabular and chart variants for interface statistics and firewall widgets; user picks per widget instance.
- [#9233](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9233) [feature, 2025, 0/2, support] **[Feature] Add vitual interfaces' uptime in Dashboard** -- w1 ADOPT: interface status includes 'up since' timestamp for all links incl. PPPoE/WireGuard (netlink carrier change time), shown in widget + API.
- [#5679](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5679) [feature, 2022, 0/1, -] **Feature request: Dashboard widget for Intrusion Detection alerts** -- w1 ADOPT: dashboard widget for recent Suricata alerts (count by severity, last N) fed by EVE JSON API.
- [#7885](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7885) [feature, 2024, 0/1, feature] **system: dashboard grip snapping improvements** -- w1 ADOPT: dashboard widget layout snaps to a fixed grid in both axes (gridstack-like), with keyboard-resizable widgets.
- [#7913](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7913) [feature, 2024, 0/1, -] **dashboard: live log widget: add filter for severity** -- w1 ADOPT: live log widget settings: severity filter, unit/source filter, regex; backed by structured journald fields over WebSocket.
- [#8637](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8637) [bug, 2025, 0/1, support] **WAN packet capture does not reset on reboot** -- w1 ADOPT: interface counters are read from the kernel (reset at boot) and widget shows 'since boot' explicitly; optional user-resettable delta baseline.
- [#8659](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8659) [bug, 2025, 0/1, -] **Swap widget will show way too many decimals** -- w1 ADOPT: shared byte/number formatting helper (SI/IEC, fixed significant digits) used by every widget; TEST: 8191.96 MiB renders 8.0 GiB.
- [#9663](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9663) [feature, 2026, 0/1, feature] **Interfaces: Neighbors: Automatic Discovery - Dashboard Widget** -- w1 ADOPT: dashboard widget listing recently discovered neighbors (ARP/NDP + Kea leases) sorted by last_seen, from the neighbor-discovery API
- [#7958](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7958) [feature, 2024, 0/6, support] **[Feature Request] Make titles clickable in the Announcements dashboard widget** -- w0 OUT-OF-SCOPE: vendor announcement feed widget; if sensr ships a release-notes widget, titles link to the post.
- [#7668](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7668) [other, 2024, 0/3, incomplete] **Lots of empty space in services widget** -- w0 ADOPT: widget grid uses responsive columns (CSS grid auto-fill) so compact list widgets fill their width without dead space.

### 3.22 Logging / reporting / monitoring (76)

- [#5469](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5469) [bug, 2022, 1/19, support] **"/var/log/flowd.log" not rotating - filling up disk** -- w3 ADOPT: flow collector stores into size-bounded storage (SQLite/parquet with retention + max-size) with journald-like vacuum; TEST: disk usage stays under cap.
- [#5759](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5759) [feature, 2022, 0/10, -] **Option to preserve % of disk space so that logging does not fills up disk space and causes DoS** -- w3 ADOPT: log retention by both age AND size (journald SystemMaxUse/KeepFree, flow DB cap) with a disk-free floor; alert when breached.
- [#6107](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6107) [bug, 2022, 0/3, support] **Log Drops** -- w3 ADOPT: firewall logging via nflog with sized buffers and drop counters exposed as metrics; remote syslog over TCP/TLS with disk-backed queue. TEST: burst without loss.
- [#8272](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8272) [bug, 2025, 0/1, support] **Graphs in Reporting: Traffic causes rapid drop in FW throughput** -- w3 ADOPT: reporting queries run in low-priority collector service (cgroup CPU weight, ionice) off the dataplane; pre-aggregated rollups. TEST: throughput while graphing.
- [#7080](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7080) [feature, 2023, 12/6, incomplete] **core integration of telegram notifications like pf*** -- w2 ADOPT: core notification framework (events -> email/Telegram/webhook/ntfy/Matrix) for alerts like gateway down, HA failover, upgrade (rx=12).
- [#8177](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8177) [support, 2025, 0/12, support] **/var ramdisk no longer possible, so what should I do?** -- w2 ADOPT: optional tmpfs for volatile logs/flows/metrics with size cap and periodic flush to disk; wear-conscious defaults for SSD/eMMC boxes.
- [#10016](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10016) [bug, 2026, 0/10, support] **filter log widget "waiting for data" after reboot: latest.log symlink missing** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: firewall log streamed from nflog/journald via API, no latest.log symlinks or file rotation state; TEST: widget streams after reboot.
- [#7421](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7421) [bug, 2024, 0/6, support] **Host traffic report data ~1/2 expected rate** -- w2 ADOPT: per-host traffic accounting from flow records counts bytes once per direction (no double/half counting on VPN interfaces); TEST: known stream vs report.
- [#8054](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8054) [feature, 2024, 0/6, -] **KEA dhcp client hostname to syslog** -- w2 ADOPT: DHCP lease events logged as structured JSON incl. hostname, MAC, IP, subnet (Kea hook or lease-commit); ship to syslog/OTLP
- [#8089](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8089) [bug, 2024, 0/6, -] **Audit Log file shows proxy IP instead of client IP (possible solution included)** -- w2 ADOPT: trusted-proxy list setting; audit log and auth rate-limiting use X-Forwarded-For/PROXY protocol only from trusted proxies, else peer IP.
- [#10192](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10192) [bug, 2026, 0/6, incomplete] **crash_reporter.php is too liberal with sensitive information and needs editing capabilities for submitted data** -- w2 ADOPT: crash/diagnostic report shows and lets user redact content before sending; secrets scrubbed by schema markers; opt-in only.
- [#6590](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6590) [feature, 2023, 1/4, support] **Adding PF Rule Number to UI Firewall Rules tables for external log analysis/correlation** -- w2 ADOPT: firewall log entries carry rule UUID + human label; GUI rule grid shows UUID and nftables handle for correlation; export field documented.
- [#6839](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6839) [bug, 2023, 0/5, support] **Firewall - live view does not use description information from rule instead of "nat rule" as label** -- w2 ADOPT: NAT rule log entries carry rule UUID and description like filter rules (nftables log prefix per NAT rule).
- [#7752](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7752) [bug, 2024, 0/5, incomplete] **RRD not collecting data after reboot** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: no RRD/cron shell collectors; metrics are scraped by a Rust collector service into an embedded TSDB, systemd-managed and restarted on failure.
- [#7081](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7081) [feature, 2023, 0/4, -] **Improve Firewall - Log Files - Live View filtering (multiselect, missing OpenVPN/Wireguard interfaces/groups, direct inp** -- w2 ADOPT: live firewall log filter: multi-select interfaces incl. VPN interfaces and groups, free-text expression input
- [#7175](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7175) [bug, 2024, 0/4, support] **Logging Target UDP still use UDP when changed to TCP** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: remote log target rendered from typed config on each apply with service restart planned by diff; TEST: UDP->TCP change verified by tcpdump in CI.
- [#7814](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7814) [feature, 2024, 0/4, support] **Syslog-NG - Expand Capabilities of GUI logging configuration** -- w2 ADOPT: log pipeline accepts extra sources (file tails, plugin units) declared in config, forwarded to remote targets with same filtering; config-backed, not hand-edited.
- [#9499](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9499) [bug, 2025, 0/4, support] **netflow insight aggregator dies and cannot be restarted, reason: DatabaseError: database disk image is malformed** -- w2 ADOPT: flow aggregator uses crash-safe storage (WAL mode / write-tested) with automatic integrity check and repair-or-rotate on startup; never requires manual delete.
- [#9737](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9737) [bug, 2026, 0/4, support] **Firewall logging: Action "block" shown instead of "reject"** -- w2 ADOPT: log action field distinguishes drop vs reject (and reject type); nftables log prefix encodes verdict.
- [#7327](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7327) [feature, 2024, 0/3, -] **Allow Exporting Unbound DNS Reporting** -- w2 ADOPT: DNS query log as structured JSON (client, qname, qtype, rcode, blocked-by) exported via syslog/OTLP; Prometheus counters for blocked/allowed
- [#7441](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7441) [feature, 2024, 0/3, -] **SNMP-Support for OpenVPN instances** -- w2 ADOPT: Prometheus metrics per OpenVPN instance (connected clients, rx/tx bytes, per-client session) from management interface; SNMP via exporter optional
- [#6138](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6138) [bug, 2022, 1/1, support] **Numerical statistics often do not have units** -- w2 ADOPT: every metric in API/GUI carries a unit (schema annotation); formatting helper renders units/prefixes consistently; lint for unitless numbers.
- [#6810](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6810) [feature, 2023, 0/2, -] **Let us select the interface in "Interfaces: Diagnostics: DNS Lookup"** -- w2 ADOPT: diagnostics (dns lookup, ping, traceroute) accept source interface/address and gateway selection (SO_BINDTODEVICE)
- [#7219](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7219) [bug, 2024, 0/2, -] **Kea log file search results are inconsistent and unpredictable when the search string contains punctuation** -- w2 ADOPT: log viewer search treats input as literal substring by default, with explicit regex toggle; test punctuation (dots/colons) in queries
- [#7290](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7290) [feature, 2024, 0/2, incomplete] **Detailed rule info; include MAC** -- w2 ADOPT: firewall log entries include source MAC (nftables log with ether saddr) so 0.0.0.0/broadcast sources are identifiable
- [#7683](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7683) [bug, 2024, 0/2, incomplete] **"download selection" in Log broken - somehow** -- w2 ADOPT: log export streams the full filtered result set from the API (NDJSON/CSV, server-side filter), not the rendered page. TEST: export count equals filter count.
- [#8469](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8469) [feature, 2025, 0/2, incomplete] **RRD: QoL improvement adding charts for Interface errors & drops** -- w2 ADOPT: collect and graph interface rx/tx errors, drops, and queue drops from netlink stats alongside bytes/packets (also Prometheus metrics).
- [#5581](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5581) [support, 2022, 0/1, support] **Multiple 'remote log file' ports/sources** -- w2 ADOPT: multiple remote log targets each with its own filter (unit/facility/severity), port and transport (UDP/TCP/TLS syslog, OTLP).
- [#6840](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6840) [feature, 2023, 0/1, support] **Firewall - live view contains a link to jump to the edit dialog of triggered rule** -- w2 ADOPT: live log entry links to rule editor via rule UUID (deep link /firewall/rules/{uuid}).
- [#8756](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8756) [feature, 2025, 0/1, -] **Unbound complete Query and Answer Logging for Security Analysis** -- w2 ADOPT: optional full DNS query+answer logging (dnstap from Unbound -> structured JSON/OTLP) for security analysis, rate-limited
- [#7780](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7780) [support, 2024, 1/7, support] **Display error with Interface Statistics** -- w1 SUPPORT: interface statistics attribute VLAN traffic to parent/untagged; -> UX: per-VLAN counters via netlink stats, clearly label parent counts as aggregate
- [#8553](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8553) [bug, 2025, 0/8, -] **Interface packet counter showing erroneous data** -- w1 TEST: packet counters must use 64-bit netlink stats, no 32-bit wrap/misparse; unit test stats parsing against large values
- [#6527](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6527) [feature, 2023, 0/7, -] **Feature Request: Option to specify the Y axis value maximum on the Reporting\Traffic Graphs** -- w1 ADOPT: traffic graphs allow fixed Y-axis max (e.g. link speed) or auto, persisted per user; baseline at zero by default.
- [#9103](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9103) [bug, 2025, 0/6, -] **Unable to View Firewall Log Files in OPNsense** -- w1 TEST: log pipeline health check (journald -> parser -> API); GUI shows 'no log source' diagnostics instead of empty view.
- [#5524](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5524) [bug, 2022, 0/5, -] **"latest.log" symlink not being re-created on "Reset Log Files"** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: journald as log store, 'reset logs' = journalctl --vacuum; no latest.log symlinks exist to break.
- [#6689](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6689) [support, 2023, 0/5, support] **system_log ssl3_get_record:wrong version number / tls_early_post_process_client_hello:unsupported protocol** -- w1 SUPPORT: TLS handshake noise from scanners in system log -> UX: webgui TLS handshake failures logged at debug level, rate-limited, with client IP; not in main log.
- [#7658](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7658) [bug, 2024, 0/5, incomplete] **Cannot determine port number that OPNSense is specifying.** -- w1 ADOPT: reports show numeric ports by default with optional service-name hint (IANA table) on hover; never replace the number.
- [#7808](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7808) [bug, 2024, 0/5, upstream] **Incorrect loglevels for gateway log** -- w1 ADOPT: gateway monitor logs routine state at info, transitions at notice, loss at warning; log-level lint (duplicate of #5753).
- [#10209](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10209) [bug, 2026, 0/5, incomplete] **Reporting Insight returns no data due to NetFlow provider case mismatch** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: report providers are typed Rust enums shared by API and backend, no string-matched class names across PHP/Python. TEST: every provider returns data.
- [#7392](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7392) [feature, 2024, 0/4, -] **Better nvd3 graph extents in Reporting** -- w1 ADOPT: reporting graphs default Y-axis from zero with sane nice-number extents; toggle for auto-zoom; consistent units.
- [#10059](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10059) [feature, 2026, 0/4, support] **Rule ID format affecting Security Onion ingest** -- w1 ADOPT: one canonical rule id format (UUID with dashes) for built-in and user rules alike in logs and API; documented for SIEM ingest.
- [#5614](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5614) [bug, 2022, 0/3, support] **OPNsense 22.1.x OpenVPN clients are tied together in traffic reporting** -- w1 TEST: per-instance traffic stats keyed by stable instance ID not by device index that may be reassigned on restart
- [#7947](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7947) [bug, 2024, 0/3, support] **netflow on pppoe interface not working** -- w1 N/A-LINUX: netgraph netflow node on pppoe; Linux IPFIX via nf_conntrack/ipt_NETFLOW or softflowd on ppp devices, must handle dynamic ifindex of ppp links
- [#5508](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5508) [feature, 2022, 0/2, incomplete] **Feature Request: Firewall log time display short form option** -- w1 ADOPT: log viewer timestamp format user preference (short/local/ISO/relative) applied across live log and widgets.
- [#5744](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5744) [bug, 2022, 0/2, support] **Jump to last page in log files doesn't work** -- w1 ADOPT: log viewer uses cursor-based pagination from journald with total count so first/last page jumps work; TEST: >> lands on last page.
- [#5753](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5753) [bug, 2022, 0/2, support] **Messages with wrong severity appear after editing Gateways.** -- w1 ADOPT: structured logs with correct severity; expected reconfigure steps log at info, never warning/error; lint test for log levels.
- [#5827](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5827) [bug, 2022, 0/2, -] **Reporting Insight - Wireguard and ZeroTier switched interface** -- w1 TEST: reporting keyed by stable interface ID, not ifindex order; test two tunnel interfaces created in swapped order
- [#6608](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6608) [support, 2023, 0/2, incomplete] **SNMP Traffic originated by WAN IF IP to LAN IP in a non configured LAN. LAN IP is not configured in Opnsense** -- w1 SUPPORT: unexplained WAN->LAN flows in log -> UX: log entries link to the originating rule/service and show process/conntrack reason (e.g. SNMP, NAT reflection).

- [#7677](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7677) [bug, 2024, 0/2, incomplete] **RFC2136 Address has not changed logged as Error** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: structured logs with correct severity levels enforced by typed log macros; 'no change' is info not error
- [#7761](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7761) [feature, 2024, 0/2, incomplete] **Make reporting health options per graph sticky** -- w1 ADOPT: per-user UI preferences store (server-side) for graph options: granularity, inverse, show-table per graph.
- [#7790](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7790) [bug, 2024, 0/2, incomplete] **RREPORTING: HEALTH: Min/Max/Average values** -- w1 TEST: min/max/avg aggregations in reporting ignore null samples and sign-invert correctly for inverse series; unit tests on aggregation code.
- [#7988](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7988) [bug, 2024, 0/2, incomplete] **Captured packets in "View capture" window are not sorted by timestamp when selecting multiple interfaces** -- w1 ADOPT: multi-interface packet capture viewer merges by timestamp (single pcapng with interface column), not grouped per interface
- [#8280](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8280) [feature, 2025, 0/2, -] **[Request] Reverse (DNS) lookup for Reporting > Insight > Details (again)** -- w1 ADOPT: reverse DNS resolution toggle on all flow/report detail tables (async, cached) in NetFlow/IPFIX reporting
- [#9014](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9014) [bug, 2025, 0/2, incomplete] **Netflow Listening Interfaces dropdown shows interface** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: selectors show description + device name from one typed interface model; no UUID leak into GUI dropdowns
- [#9519](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9519) [bug, 2025, 1/1, support] **Packet Capture: Packet count does not take filtered packets into account** -- w1 ADOPT: packet capture count applies to matched packets (BPF filter applied before -c, as tcpdump does); TEST: capture 10 with port filter.
- [#9580](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9580) [feature, 2026, 1/1, support] **Firewall / Livelog** -- w1 ADOPT: live log cells clickable to add filter terms (IP, port, proto, rule) and 'filter out'; filters as URL state.
- [#5480](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5480) [feature, 2022, 0/1, -] **Allow Top Talkers table to be sorted in other ways** -- w1 ADOPT: top-talkers view with sortable columns (in/out/total, over selectable window) and freeze/pause; data from flow aggregation API.
- [#5741](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5741) [bug, 2022, 0/1, support] **Unable to use Live View** -- w1 TEST: live log stream via WebSocket/SSE must not saturate connection on high log rate; server-side rate limiting and backpressure test.
- [#6261](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6261) [feature, 2023, 0/1, support] **Feature Request: OCSF formatted logging** -- w1 ADOPT: structured JSON firewall/auth logs with stable field names; export mapping to OCSF/ECS via OTLP exporter rather than custom parsing.
- [#6657](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6657) [support, 2023, 0/1, support] **Firewall rules inspect data reset daily (~03:00)** -- w1 SUPPORT: rule counters reset by nightly reload -> UX: counters persisted/aggregated into Prometheus metrics so reloads don't zero visible stats.
- [#7807](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7807) [bug, 2024, 0/1, -] **Interface packets probably incorrect** -- w1 TEST: interface packet counters read from netlink rtnl_link_stats64 must track bytes; test counters increment under ping load on every interface type (incl. VLAN/ppp)
- [#8127](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8127) [bug, 2024, 0/1, -] **The units display on the Reporting: Traffic page is incorrect** -- w1 ADOPT: single unit formatter with correct SI prefixes (M not m; KiB vs kB explicit) used across reports; unit tests on formatter.
- [#8861](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8861) [feature, 2025, 0/1, -] **Possibility to disable console log messages in the Web UI** -- w1 ADOPT: console kernel log level (printk / dmesg -n) configurable in schema; quiet console by default with serial still showing errors.
- [#9496](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9496) [support, 2025, 0/1, support] **Live log had not worked until all items on the plain view filter were checked off** -- w1 ADOPT: live log view defaults to 'all' when filter set is empty and shows an explicit 'filters active, N hidden' banner.
- [#9502](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9502) [bug, 2025, 0/1, support] **Log filter selection has no effect** -- w1 TEST: log viewer date/severity filters bound to API query params and reflected in URL; Playwright test for filter change updating results.
- [#9725](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9725) [bug, 2026, 0/1, -] **Unbound DNS Reporting Displays Description Instead of Blocklist Name.** -- w1 TEST: blocked-query reports show blocklist source id, not user description; keep stable source key in log records
- [#9852](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9852) [bug, 2026, 0/1, support] **GUI Packet Capture does not display IPv6 traffic on a PPPoE interface, although the same traffic is clearly visible when** -- w1 TEST: GUI packet capture on PPPoE netdev shows v6 (no ethernet-only BPF assumptions; link-type aware filters)
- [#4122](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4122) [feature, 2020, 0/0, feature] **Syslog-NG: remote logging application matching** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: structured journald fields (SYSLOG_IDENTIFIER/_SYSTEMD_UNIT) matched exactly for remote log target filters, no prefix matching.
- [#7840](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7840) [bug, 2024, 1/8, -] **Log filled with "pf: ICMP error message too short (ip6)"** -- w0 N/A-LINUX: pf debug message spam; sensr: kernel/nft trace logging is opt-in per rule (nftrace) and rate-limited.
- [#6093](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6093) [bug, 2022, 0/6, support] **logger spins, burning lots of CPU** -- w0 N/A-LINUX: BSD logger spinning; sensr logs via journald with structured fields; no shelling out to `logger`.
- [#7365](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7365) [bug, 2024, 0/3, incomplete] **FW: Log Live View - Drop down list is empty and below the display field** -- w0 TEST: live log view filter dropdowns populated and positioned correctly (component tests for select widgets in the SPA).

### 3.23 Firmware / upgrades / installer / kernel (38)

- [#6167](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6167) [bug, 2022, 0/39, support] **firmware: freeradius3 package stuck on update** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: no per-package half-upgrade states; the whole closure switches atomically or not at all, and the old generation stays bootable.
- [#7275](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7275) [bug, 2024, 0/10, support] **Update issues from 23.7.12 to 24.1.1** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: atomic generation switch with post-boot health check (API reachable, management IP up) and automatic rollback to previous generation on failure.
- [#7787](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7787) [feature, 2024, 2/6, -] **Create OpnSense Installer Automation (Config- or Solution File, SSH for Ansible,...)** -- w3 ADOPT: unattended install: installer reads a declarative answer file (disk, NIC assignment, admin key, initial config) from USB/cloud-init/HTTP; CLI-only images.
- [#6127](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6127) [support, 2022, 0/5, support] **Network-Interfaces not found after update to 22.7.6** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: atomic NixOS generation upgrade with automatic rollback if interfaces named in config are missing after boot; use stable NIC naming (MAC/path).
- [#9930](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9930) [bug, 2026, 0/12, support] **Bug: Upgrade to 26.1.4 failed, because of surricata log file** -- w2 ADOPT: pre-upgrade preflight checks disk/tmpfs free space; disabled services (Suricata) produce no log files; /var/log tmpfs sized with headroom and alert on fullness.
- [#9495](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9495) [other, 2025, 0/8, support] **Clarification on Recommended Update Policies for OPNsense** -- w2 ADOPT: publish a written release/support policy: channel names (stable/security), support window per release, security-only backport channel.
- [#6353](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6353) [bug, 2023, 1/5, support] **Upgrade to 23.1 with NIC tunables** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: kernel/driver tunables are typed per-NIC settings validated against the new kernel at build time; failed boot auto-rolls back to previous generation.
- [#7820](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7820) [feature, 2024, 0/6, support] **Feature Request: Offline Package Install Via CLI When Device Cannot Connect to Internet** -- w2 ADOPT: offline upgrade/plugin install via signed closure bundle (nix copy --to file) uploaded through GUI/USB; CLI `sensr upgrade --from <bundle>`.
- [#7262](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7262) [bug, 2024, 0/5, support] **No NAT working on 24.1.2 - cant downgrade to 24.1.1 using opnsense-revert:  opnsense has a missing dependency: suricata-** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: NixOS generations roll back the whole system atomically; no package-dependency downgrade failures.
- [#9921](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9921) [bug, 2026, 0/5, support] **Error after Upgrade / starting with opnsense 26.1.** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: generation switch runs post-switch health check (SSH + API reachable) and auto-reboots/rolls back; upgrade never leaves SSH rejecting logins silently.
- [#6441](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6441) [feature, 2023, 1/2, support] **AutoUpdate important Security Updates** -- w2 ADOPT: opt-in unattended security-only auto-upgrade (NixOS autoUpgrade on a security channel) with maintenance window, auto-rollback on failed health check.
- [#9348](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9348) [feature, 2025, 0/2, support] **Improvements to Management: Status: Firmware** -- w2 ADOPT: update page streams progress phases (download/build/switch/health-check) with distinct states and a persisted log; distinct icons/colours per state.
- [#9567](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9567) [support, 2025, 0/2, support] **Update Error 25.7.10 on VM** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: upgrade log always available in GUI; failed switch rolls back; no 'unknown error, see logs' without the log being one click away.
- [#9894](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9894) [bug, 2026, 0/2, support] **Upgrade to 26 failed** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: upgrade = build new generation then atomic switch; a hang during download never leaves a half-applied system; old generation bootable.
- [#4516](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4516) [feature, 2020, 0/1, feature] **firmware: proxy settings for pkg.conf** -- w2 ADOPT: system-wide HTTP(S) proxy setting consumed by nix substituter fetches, update checks, and all outbound service fetches (blocklists, GeoIP).
- [#6305](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6305) [support, 2023, 0/1, support] **VIDCONSOLE INVALID** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: console settings validated against detected devices before apply; boot-time failure rolls back to previous generation; recovery console in installer.
- [#7578](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7578) [bug, 2024, 0/1, -] **shadowsocks configuration migration failing in a non-standard case.** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: versioned schema migrations are pure functions tested in CI against corpus fixtures (including odd inputs); migration failure blocks upgrade with a clear error.
- [#7797](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7797) [bug, 2024, 0/1, -] **Netdata - two issues when upgrading today from snapshots - upgrade incomplete** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: plugin packages have no install-time scripts creating users/groups; declarative users in the Nix module; whole closure switches atomically.
- [#8543](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8543) [bug, 2025, 0/18, support] **Resizing on the first boot on disks with MBR scheme** -- w1 ADOPT: first-boot root FS grow works for GPT and MBR (systemd-repart/growpart) on ARM64 SBC images; CI boots images with resized disk.
- [#5515](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5515) [bug, 2022, 0/8, support] **Post-22.1 errors from pkg during update check** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: no mutable package cache with permissions to corrupt; Nix store is immutable and update check is a pure HTTP query of the channel.
- [#6553](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6553) [support, 2023, 0/7, support] **Bug: problem of mounting the OPNsense kernel via netboot** -- w1 ADOPT: publish netboot artefacts (kernel+initrd+squashfs, iPXE script) for the installer image as part of release CI.
- [#7824](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7824) [other, 2024, 0/7, -] **system: snapshot default names and other ideas** -- w1 ADOPT: generation/snapshot default names: ISO date + short label (e.g. 2026-11-15-pre-upgrade); user-editable description; list sorted by creation.
- [#7726](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7726) [feature, 2024, 2/3, -] **Update the boot code on OpnSense kernel updates as well** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: systemd-boot/bootloader is managed by the NixOS generation switch; no manual zpool/bootcode upgrade step exists for the user to forget.
- [#8972](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8972) [support, 2025, 0/5, support] **error install update 25.1.11** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: Linux kernel has in-tree r8169/r8125 Realtek support; no driver plugin install before networking works.
- [#8036](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8036) [support, 2024, 0/4, support] **`opnsense-bootstrap`: Very slow download speed for packages** -- w1 ADOPT: substituter/mirror selection with a bandwidth test and status page; CDN-backed binary cache with regional mirrors for upgrades.
- [#9553](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9553) [bug, 2025, 0/4, support] **Health check - Reporting mismatches when update is available** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: Nix closure integrity is content-addressed (nix store verify); health check compares against the running generation, not the latest remote version.
- [#9685](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9685) [feature, 2026, 1/3, incomplete] **Add Init7 Mirror to Mirror List** -- w1 ADOPT: community binary-cache mirror program with geo/latency-based mirror choice and documented mirror onboarding.
- [#9964](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9964) [feature, 2026, 0/4, -] **ZFS ARC / Change ARC to 10% instead of default 50%** -- w1 ADOPT: tune FS cache for a firewall appliance (ZFS ARC max ~10-25% or ext4/btrfs default) so memory stays available to conntrack/Suricata; expose in system tuning.
- [#5751](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5751) [feature, 2022, 0/3, support] **Serial port access** -- w1 ADOPT: admin settings expose serial console port selection (enumerated ttyS*/ttyUSB*) and baud, applied via kernel cmdline + serial-getty units.
- [#6075](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6075) [support, 2022, 0/3, support] **No console menu when connecting via USB console port on DEC3860 version 22.7.5** -- w1 ADOPT: ship serial-getty on all detected serial ports by default (ttyS0, ttyUSB*, ttyACM*) so a console menu is always reachable on appliance USB consoles.
- [#6239](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6239) [support, 2023, 0/3, support] **opnsense-bootstrap fails on AWS EC2 instance** -- w1 OUT-OF-SCOPE: opnsense-bootstrap over stock FreeBSD on EC2; sensr ships a NixOS image/AMI with cloud-init style first-boot config instead.
- [#6536](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6536) [bug, 2023, 0/3, support] **DEC840 USB console** -- w1 ADOPT: serial-getty on all udev-detected USB serial consoles at boot; no hardcoded tty names. Test on appliance hardware matrix.
- [#8727](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8727) [feature, 2025, 0/3, -] **Add /etc/profile.d/proxy.sh for upstream proxy to use pkg** -- w1 ADOPT: single proxy setting (with no_proxy) applied to nix substituters, update checks and all fetchers; duplicate of 4516.
- [#6721](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6721) [support, 2023, 0/2, support] **is impossible install or bootstrap freebsd (aws marketplace) to opnsense on EC2?** -- w1 OUT-OF-SCOPE: EC2 bootstrap of OPNsense; sensr publishes cloud images (AMI/qcow2/raw) from CI with first-boot config via cloud-init/user-data.
- [#7558](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7558) [bug, 2024, 0/2, support] **Installer ignores LAN domain setting** -- w1 TEST: setup-wizard e2e test asserting the entered LAN domain ends up in system.domain and in DHCP/DNS option output.
- [#9028](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9028) [feature, 2025, 0/1, -] **opnsense-importer: dynamic UFS partition detection** -- w1 ADOPT: config importer/recovery scans all block devices for a labelled config partition or /sensr/config.json on any FS instead of hardcoded partition names.
- [#10017](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10017) [feature, 2026, 0/1, -] **Improve compatibility with USB drives in configuration importer (opnsense-importer)** -- w1 ADOPT: recovery config importer probes partitionless USB (whole-device FS) and partitions alike via blkid; duplicate theme of 9028/8538.
- [#5510](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5510) [other, 2022, 0/2, incomplete] **Console boot screen messed up** -- w0 N/A-LINUX: FreeBSD vt/VGA boot console rendering issue; systemd-boot + kernel console on Linux.

### 3.24 Backups / config (30)

- [#6051](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6051) [bug, 2022, 0/8, incomplete] **Big part of system configuration lost at night** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: transactional apply with validation + rollback and git-like config history; a plugin apply can't wipe interfaces/gateways. TEST: partial-write fuzz.
- [#7043](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7043) [feature, 2023, 1/3, incomplete] **Timed config change** -- w3 ADOPT: 'commit-confirm' apply: new config auto-rolls back after N minutes unless confirmed via API/GUI (like JunOS commit confirmed).
- [#7143](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7143) [bug, 2024, 0/4, support] **Password used for external backup in Google drive is in plain text** -- w3 ADOPT: secrets (backup encryption passphrase, API tokens) stored as typed Secret fields, redacted in logs/API responses/exports by construction; test asserts redaction.
- [#9866](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9866) [bug, 2026, 0/9, support] **Firewall Groups Cannot Be Restored** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: partial restore of any subtree is schema-validated; malformed input yields a typed error, never a runtime crash; fuzz test subtree restore.
- [#7384](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7384) [bug, 2024, 0/8, -] **nextuid get reset after importing from a backup** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: user UIDs are allocated from a persisted counter inside the versioned config model; restore validates uniqueness and bumps next-id; test for collision.
- [#6049](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6049) [bug, 2022, 0/5, support] **Sensitive files are world-readable, permissions are all over the place** -- w2 ADOPT: define file-permission policy in the Nix module: config store/keys 0600 root-only, secrets dir 0700; CI test asserts no world-readable secret paths.
- [#9990](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9990) [bug, 2026, 0/5, support] **Migration fails because of missing password** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: restore validates whole config against schema with per-field errors before apply; optional secrets are typed Option<Secret> so migrations can't require them.
- [#9390](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9390) [feature, 2025, 0/4, support] **Backup/Export/View Configuration Drift to Default Installation** -- w2 ADOPT: export 'diff from defaults' view: schema has typed defaults so backups omit defaults by design and GUI can show only user-modified settings.
- [#8043](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8043) [bug, 2024, 0/3, incomplete] **Fatal error: Uncaught TypeError: Cannot access offset of type string on string in /usr/local/etc/inc/rrd.inc** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: typed restore validates whole file against schema before import; metrics data separate from config, never embedded. TEST: restore without metrics.
- [#9740](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9740) [feature, 2026, 0/3, support] **Change tracking in logs with "Change reason" text entry** -- w2 ADOPT: optional/required 'change reason' on every config commit stored in the git-like history and audit log; per-object blame view.
- [#6493](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6493) [feature, 2023, 1/1, support] **Let me comment my changes (to the config)** -- w2 ADOPT: every config commit carries an optional message + author (git-like history); API `apply` accepts `message`; history view shows diff per commit.
- [#7180](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7180) [feature, 2024, 0/2, support] **Restore to other hardware could handle VLAN mappings better** -- w2 ADOPT: restore-on-other-hardware wizard maps physical NICs and auto-rebinds VLAN parents; show dependent VLAN/LAGG/bridge children per mapping before apply.
- [#7939](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7939) [feature, 2024, 0/2, incomplete] **Feature Request - Add the ability to import firewall rules using JSON or XML from the GUI** -- w2 ADOPT: partial config import/export: select a subtree (e.g. firewall.rules) as JSON, validate against schema, merge or replace, via API and GUI.
- [#8158](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8158) [bug, 2024, 0/2, incomplete] **Restore Backup config "Port Forwarding" and "Automatically generated rules" - "Default deny / state violation rule"** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: port forward and its associated filter rule are one typed object with reference; restore re-renders both consistently.
- [#8164](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8164) [bug, 2024, 0/2, support] **shaper settings change interface** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: shaper rules reference interfaces by UUID/logical name; config import maps interfaces explicitly, no positional optN names.
- [#9319](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9319) [bug, 2025, 0/2, incomplete] **Firewall Categories wiped after NAT Outbound Save/Apply (25.7.5–25.7.6)** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: single typed config store with transactional writes; saving one section cannot truncate another's data.
- [#6956](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6956) [feature, 2023, 0/1, -] **Create option for scrubbed config download** -- w2 ADOPT: config export with 'redact secrets' option (schema marks secret fields; redaction is automatic and complete).
- [#6970](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6970) [bug, 2023, 0/1, support] **Firewall aliases seem to not be applied after config restore** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: transactional apply rebuilds all alias sets from the declarative config on restore; no stale on-disk alias cache to go out of sync.
- [#8538](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8538) [feature, 2025, 0/9, -] **Support more removable media filesystes for config import** -- w1 ADOPT: config import from removable media supports FAT32/exFAT/ext4/NTFS(read) in installer and recovery console; also USB config seed on first boot.
- [#6455](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6455) [feature, 2023, 1/2, -] **More friendly filename for GDrive backups** -- w1 ADOPT: remote backup filenames use ISO-8601 UTC timestamp + hostname + config version (e.g. sensr-fw1-2026-11-15T00-00Z.json).
- [#7444](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7444) [bug, 2024, 0/3, support] **Backup to Google Shared Drive does not follow maximum Backup Count** -- w1 ADOPT: remote backup retention (count/age) is enforced by the backup driver with a listing of remote objects; integration test with mock S3/WebDAV for pruning.
- [#10084](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10084) [feature, 2026, 0/3, -] **System: Configuration: Backups migrate to MVC** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: backup/restore is fully API-driven from day one (local history, remote targets, restore with subtree selection); GUI generated from the schema.
- [#6711](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6711) [bug, 2023, 1/1, support] **Traffic Insight stopped working after config restore. Solved with NetFlow Data Reset.** -- w1 ADOPT: restore flow lists which runtime stores (NetFlow DB, RRD/metrics) are not in config and offers reset/reinit; never leave stale data silently breaking views.
- [#6759](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6759) [support, 2023, 0/2, support] **Restore Config on other Hardware routes traffic to default Gateway instead of direct interface, reset and configure manu** -- w1 SUPPORT: interface reassignment after restore on new HW -> UX: reassignment wizard re-validates gateway/route bindings referencing old NICs and warns.
- [#9861](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9861) [feature, 2026, 0/2, support] **Firewall: Import/Export add header check to .csv** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: imports are typed JSON with schema id + version header validated before parsing; wrong-file imports fail with clear error.
- [#6674](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6674) [support, 2023, 0/1, support] **Google Shared drive backup** -- w1 SUPPORT: Google Shared Drive upload fails -> UX: remote backup targets (S3/WebDAV/git) with explicit test-connection button and clear API error surfacing.
- [#8209](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8209) [support, 2025, 0/1, support] **System/Configuration/Backups: Google Drive autobackup setup/test fails with "Invalid P12 key, openssl_pkcs12_read() fail** -- w1 SUPPORT: P12 key rejected after upgrade -> UX: credential material validated at save time with explicit error; prefer OAuth/JSON key or S3 credentials over P12.
- [#8422](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8422) [feature, 2025, 0/1, -] **Backup - allow changing the time** -- w1 ADOPT: configurable backup schedule (cron/systemd timer) rather than fixed 00:00; backup must never cause network interruption (no apply, snapshot only).
- [#7052](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7052) [other, 2023, 0/4, support] **Make Google shared drive an optional plugin, like NextCloud, update Cloud Backup documentation** -- w0 OUT-OF-SCOPE: Google Drive specific backend; sensr core ships S3/WebDAV/git push, vendor clouds as plugins; keep docs generated from the schema.
- [#8119](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8119) [feature, 2024, 0/1, -] **Final size estimate for 'Backup Count'** -- w0 ADOPT: backup settings page shows current config size x retention count estimate and actual remote usage.

### 3.25 System / services / hardware (42)

- [#7197](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7197) [bug, 2024, 1/7, support] **Removal of a cron job command during a firmware update enables automatic firmware updates** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: cron jobs reference commands by stable typed ID; a removed command makes the job invalid (apply refuses) instead of silently falling back to the first entry.
- [#5797](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5797) [feature, 2022, 0/8, support] **Can't control the address the Admin lighttpd is listening; it's on all alias IP addresses, too** -- w2 ADOPT: admin web server listen config as explicit address list (incl. selecting one of several VIPs), not only per-interface; frees other VIPs for NAT.
- [#6748](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6748) [bug, 2023, 0/6, -] **Monit: E-mail server password with special chars ! and " won't work** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: generated service configs use typed escapers per target format; fuzz tests with special chars (! ") in secrets.
- [#7050](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7050) [bug, 2023, 0/5, support] **ddclient sends updates every 5 minutes even though the IP address has not changed** -- w2 ADOPT: built-in DDNS client caches last-pushed address per host and only updates on change (or forced interval); backoff on provider errors to avoid abuse bans.
- [#7704](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7704) [bug, 2024, 1/2, -] **Monit has issues with complex passwords** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: typed config renderers escape/quote every value for the target daemon (monit, etc.); TEST: fuzz passwords with /, quotes, $ in all templates.
- [#5680](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5680) [feature, 2022, 0/2, support] **Improve resilience by monitoring and restarting services, or the whole system** -- w2 ADOPT: systemd Restart=on-failure + WatchdogSec for all daemons, hardware watchdog support, and optional 'reboot if gateway unreachable N min' policy.
- [#7861](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7861) [support, 2024, 0/2, support] **Base Updates keep nuking /etc/auto_master** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: /etc is generated from the declarative config each generation; custom files live in a declared 'extra files' section and survive upgrades by design.
- [#8087](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8087) [bug, 2024, 0/2, incomplete] **KEA writes logs, even though it says it's no longer running in GUI** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: service enable flag in declared config maps 1:1 to systemd unit state on apply; disabled service is stopped+masked, journald log retention is global
- [#8504](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8504) [feature, 2025, 1/1, -] **Feature Request: Support hardware watchdog Intel TCO Watchdog Timer** -- w2 ADOPT: hardware watchdog support via systemd RuntimeWatchdogSec (iTCO_wdt, sp5100, etc.) toggle in system settings; also covers hangs during upgrade/reboot.
- [#10153](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10153) [other, 2026, 1/11, -] **services: migrate legacy ntpd pages to MVC/API** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: NTP (chrony) is a typed API model with generated GUI from the start; no legacy static pages exist to migrate.
- [#5737](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5737) [other, 2022, 5/6, incomplete] **Enable C-states to reduce power consumption and heat generation** -- w1 N/A-LINUX: FreeBSD C-state sysctl. Linux: ensure intel_idle/cpuidle enabled and expose power governor setting in system tunables (rx=5).
- [#9405](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9405) [feature, 2025, 0/11, support] **Make type editable in tunables** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: tunables are typed: kernel cmdline/modprobe params vs sysctl are distinct schema fields applied to the correct target; type editable in GUI.
- [#6761](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6761) [support, 2023, 0/10, support] **OPNsense 23.7 randomly hangs up with out of SWAP** -- w1 N/A-LINUX: FreeBSD process-spawn storm; sensr runs services as systemd units with restart rate limits and resource limits (MemoryMax) to contain runaway processes.
- [#8341](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8341) [bug, 2025, 2/8, support] **NTP Service Fails to Start When Using WireGuard on the Same Interface** -- w1 TEST: ntpd/chrony bound to an interface must start when a WG instance shares it (listen on specific addresses, not all); integration test; rx=2
- [#10263](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10263) [bug, 2026, 0/9, support] **ntpd: configured server peer on directly-connected subnet never reaches sync; pool-resolved peers on the same daemon wor** -- w1 ADOPT: NTP peer configuration (chrony `server` with iburst/prefer) respects local-subnet peers; expose chronyc sources in GUI to show reach/stratum per peer for debugging.
- [#5902](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5902) [feature, 2022, 0/8, feature] **Feature: Console menu option for changing keyboard layout** -- w1 ADOPT: console menu and GUI expose keyboard layout (console.keyMap) for VGA console; preinstalled images allow changing it without reinstall.
- [#6482](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6482) [bug, 2023, 0/7, upstream] **Random reboot after connected to internet (NTP / set time issue)** -- w1 N/A-LINUX: FreeBSD crash on NTP time step; sensr uses chrony with makestep at boot; test large time jump at boot does not break timers/services.
- [#7282](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7282) [bug, 2024, 0/6, support] **Missing SSH host key algorithms in configuration dropdown** -- w1 ADOPT: SSH hostkey/KEX/cipher/MAC dropdown lists generated at build from the shipped OpenSSH (`ssh -Q`), never hard-coded in the GUI.
- [#9208](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9208) [bug, 2025, 1/5, -] **[Monit] Allow dhcp interfaces in service settings** -- w1 ADOPT: monitoring (monit-like health checks) allowed on any interface incl. DHCP ones; resolve address at check time, not config time
- [#8839](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8839) [bug, 2025, 0/4, incomplete] **Monit ignores “From” email address in OPNsense 25.1** -- w1 TEST: monitoring alert mail From address from settings must appear in rendered config; template-render unit test per field (duplicate theme of 7502).
- [#7502](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7502) [feature, 2024, 1/2, -] **Allow configuration of sender email address under Services > Monit > Settings** -- w1 ADOPT: global notification/SMTP settings include a configurable From address and auth; shared by alerts, monitoring, cert-expiry mail.
- [#7518](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7518) [bug, 2024, 0/3, support] **Cron: Restart IPsec service does stop but not start the service** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: service restart is `systemctl restart` of a unit; scheduled actions reference the unit, and failures are reported in the job log with alerting.
- [#9888](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9888) [support, 2026, 0/3, incomplete] **No SSH Login as root** -- w1 TEST: passwords with every printable ASCII char work for GUI, SSH (PAM) and API; config renderer never shell-interpolates secrets.
- [#5512](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5512) [feature, 2022, 0/2, incomplete] **Feature request: Remove or hide swap space** -- w1 ADOPT: swap is optional at install (declarative: none/zram/file); dashboard hides the swap gauge when no swap is configured.
- [#8896](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8896) [feature, 2025, 1/1, -] **NTP: Accommodate LF Line Breaks in Custom GPS Initialization** -- w1 ADOPT: NTP GPS reference-clock init strings accept configurable line ending (CRLF/LF) and hex escapes; validated and shown as rendered bytes.
- [#10314](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10314) [bug, 2026, 0/2, incomplete] **Issue: wol binary fails to send magic packets on VirtIO interfaces with narrow subnets (/28)** -- w1 ADOPT: WoL sends directed broadcast with SO_BROADCAST and correct subnet broadcast (/28 etc.) or raw ethernet frame on chosen interface; unit test
- [#5641](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5641) [feature, 2022, 0/1, -] **Cron: don't show trashcan for internal cronjobs that can't be deleted by admin** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: system-owned scheduled jobs are read-only entries (no delete affordance) distinct from user cron jobs in the schema; generated grid respects it.
- [#7771](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7771) [feature, 2024, 0/1, -] **[OpenSSH] Make the PerSourcePenalties configuration available in GUI** -- w1 ADOPT: expose sshd PerSourcePenalties/PerSourceNetBlockSize in SSH settings schema (plus general login rate-limit, see 7998).
- [#7916](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7916) [support, 2024, 0/1, support] **Crashes after some time requiring a power cycle** -- w1 N/A-LINUX: FreeBSD hard lock; sensr ships systemd hardware watchdog + softdog, kdump/pstore capture, and a diagnostics bundle export.
- [#8336](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8336) [bug, 2025, 0/1, -] **Restart Wireguard Cron job not working** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: scheduler actions are API calls validated at save time; 'restart wireguard' is a real endpoint; test every schedulable action
- [#8438](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8438) [feature, 2025, 0/1, -] **OpenVPN restart service with Cron** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: scheduler (systemd timers) can call any API action incl. restart of all OpenVPN instances; schedule entries are config, survive upgrades
- [#9260](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9260) [feature, 2025, 0/1, -] **option for fcron to be used as cron daemon** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: scheduled jobs are systemd timers with Persistent=true (run missed jobs after downtime) as an option per job; no cron daemon choice needed.
- [#6567](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6567) [bug, 2023, 0/14, upstream] **Squid stops processing http requests after about 1 minute** -- w0 OUT-OF-SCOPE: Squid transparent proxy stall (upstream/plugin).
- [#7086](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7086) [feature, 2023, 4/3, support] **Add support of ssh3** -- w0 OUT-OF-SCOPE: ssh3 (QUIC SSH) experimental; keep OpenSSH; revisit if ssh3 stabilizes
- [#9034](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9034) [bug, 2025, 0/5, incomplete] **ACPI Error: Aborting method \_TZ.TZ00._TMP.** -- w0 N/A-LINUX: FreeBSD ACPI thermal noise on console; on Linux kernel loglevel on console is configurable (console.loglevel setting) and ACPI quirks are kernel params.
- [#8829](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8829) [bug, 2025, 1/2, -] **"vidconsole is unavailable" on OPNsense 25.1.9 with Intel UHD 730 (i915kms never loads)** -- w0 N/A-LINUX: FreeBSD i915kms/vt console failure; Linux i915 + simpledrm fallback. Console setting validated against present devices before apply.
- [#10141](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10141) [bug, 2026, 0/3, incomplete] **Python3.13 -"exited on signal 10 / 11 (no core dump - bad address)"** -- w0 N/A-LINUX: Python3.13 helper crashes on FreeBSD; sensr has no Python helpers in core (Rust services), crashes would surface via systemd-coredump + journald.
- [#6429](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6429) [feature, 2023, 0/2, -] **Web Proxy: Add strip_query_terms option to the config UI** -- w0 OUT-OF-SCOPE: Squid strip_query_terms option (proxy plugin).
- [#8666](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8666) [feature, 2025, 0/2, incomplete] **Monit Email SMTP server authentication.** -- w0 OUT-OF-SCOPE: Gmail OAuth for monit mail; sensr notification layer uses SMTP with app passwords plus webhooks (ntfy/Matrix/Slack) instead.
- [#5460](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5460) [bug, 2022, 0/1, -] **Web Proxy emits flawed configuration file for FTP proxy** -- w0 OUT-OF-SCOPE: Squid FTP proxy config emission (proxy plugin). Generic lesson: service config generators iterate all addresses of an interface, tested with multi-IP fixtures.
- [#5552](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5552) [support, 2022, 0/1, support] **su Permission denied** -- w0 N/A-LINUX: FreeBSD su/opnsense-shell login shell permission; sensr admin users have a normal shell/CLI client, no opnsense-shell.
- [#5951](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5951) [feature, 2022, 0/1, -] **Set Shutdown mode (off / halt)** -- w0 ADOPT: shutdown action offers poweroff vs halt (keep console) option; API /system/power accepts mode.

### 3.26 Plugins ecosystem (6)

- [#5868](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5868) [other, 2022, 0/4, support] **Squid Proxy leaking IP / Not Routing over OpenVPN** -- w1 OUT-OF-SCOPE: Squid egress via VPN; proxy is plugin territory; core offers policy routing of locally-originated traffic by fwmark for such services
- [#9100](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9100) [other, 2025, 1/3, incomplete] **os-ddclient config - Adjust cloudflare username/password tooltip** -- w1 ADOPT: DDNS provider definitions declare which credential fields apply (token-only vs user/pass) so the generated form hides irrelevant ones.
- [#10093](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10093) [bug, 2026, 0/3, -] **os-nut plugin: It is broken and kill the UPS on exit when set to netclient** -- w1 ADOPT: UPS (NUT) in core-adjacent plugin: netclient mode must not send FSD/kill-power to the UPS; shutdown command uses systemd poweroff; integration test shutdown sequence.
- [#6528](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6528) [bug, 2023, 0/7, support] **Squid Webproxy no longer binding on interface IPv6 IPs** -- w0 OUT-OF-SCOPE: Squid plugin IPv6 bind bug; lesson: all services take listen addresses as typed IPv4/IPv6 list from interface model, tested dual-stack.
- [#7411](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7411) [feature, 2024, 0/7, support] **Include python3 metapackage** -- w0 N/A-LINUX: FreeBSD pkg python version metapackage; Nix pins interpreter versions per closure so no multi-version package naming problem exists.
- [#9976](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9976) [support, 2026, 0/4, support] **OPNsense 26.1.2** -- w0 SUPPORT: student can't find os-clamav plugin -> UX: plugin catalog explains availability per release and marks deprecated/removed plugins with reason.

### 3.27 Other (19)

- [#5667](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5667) [other, 2022, 0/7, -] **GitHub Actions and unit testing** -- w3 ADOPT: CI on every PR runs cargo test/clippy/fmt + config-migration tests + e2e API tests; non-negotiable for a from-scratch project.
- [#9214](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9214) [support, 2025, 0/5, support] **Crash OPNsense** -- w1 SUPPORT: random crash on vSphere VMs -> ADOPT: crash dumps + kernel panic report collector (kdump/pstore) surfaced in GUI with export
- [#7178](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7178) [feature, 2024, 0/3, -] **Multipath TCP Support - There's a new porting attempt in progress by a University** -- w1 OUT-OF-SCOPE: MPTCP kernel support exists on Linux; aggregation-over-VPS is a plugin/VPN concern, not core; ensure nftables doesn't block MPTCP options
- [#9265](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9265) [support, 2025, 0/3, support] **Recent updates have slowed down page loading times, but not latency for gaming.** -- w1 SUPPORT: degraded page loads over days -> UX: diagnostics page with conntrack usage, DNS resolver latency, memory per daemon to find leaks; metrics exported.
- [#6120](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6120) [support, 2022, 0/2, incomplete] **massiv troughput and speed issue** -- w1 SUPPORT: throughput capped per peer -> UX: diagnostics to show shaper/queue state, offload settings, conntrack limits; perf regression test in CI across releases.
- [#6442](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6442) [support, 2023, 0/2, incomplete] **network throughput** -- w1 SUPPORT: small-packet (64B) forwarding rate -> ADOPT: publish pps benchmarks; defaults enable RSS/multiqueue, flowtable offload, and IRQ affinity for small-packet performance.
- [#7681](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7681) [bug, 2024, 0/2, -] **Cannot create a coherent snapshot on KVM via quiescing when Zenarmor is installed** -- w1 ADOPT: ship qemu-guest-agent and ensure all writable mounts are freezable (no FUSE/tmpfs pitfalls) so quiesced snapshots work; plugin data paths follow this rule.
- [#7966](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7966) [other, 2024, 0/2, incomplete] **High CPU usage under stress, not entirely unexpected - testing opportunity?** -- w1 ADOPT: ship opt-in perf profiling hooks (perf/eBPF, pprof-style flamegraphs for the Rust daemons) exposed in diagnostics for community-contributed profiling.
- [#8082](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8082) [other, 2024, 0/2, support] **Process Improvement - PHP Static Analysis** -- w1 N/A-LINUX: PHP static analysis; sensr CI uses cargo clippy -D warnings, cargo audit/deny, and TypeScript strict + eslint for the SPA.
- [#9445](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9445) [other, 2025, 0/2, support] **There appears no way to report a security vlnerability** -- w1 OUT-OF-SCOPE: project process; sensr should publish SECURITY.md with private reporting channel from day one
- [#7216](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7216) [feature, 2024, 0/1, -] **Update drill to have +short option** -- w1 ADOPT: ship `dig`/`doggo`-class DNS CLI tooling in the image; sensr CLI has `sensr dns query --short` backed by the API.
- [#7279](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7279) [support, 2024, 0/1, support] **Slow Download after upgrading to 24.1** -- w1 SUPPORT: slow downloads after upgrade -> UX: a connectivity diagnostics page (MTU/MSS test, shaper status, offload state, flowtable) to start troubleshooting.
- [#7612](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7612) [support, 2024, 0/1, support] **Video streaming apps buffering problem** -- w1 SUPPORT: streaming buffering, likely MTU/MSS or IPv6/DNS issue -> UX: built-in connectivity diagnostics (MTU path probe, DNS resolution test, PMTUD check).
- [#6457](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6457) [other, 2023, 0/11, support] **How can I install GOST encryption algorithms on OpenSense?** -- w0 OUT-OF-SCOPE: GOST cipher suites; sensr uses rustls/OpenSSL defaults; provider-specific national algorithms are plugin/provider territory.
- [#7042](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7042) [support, 2023, 0/5, support] **Page faults, panic, (kernel) and system instability** -- w0 N/A-LINUX: FreeBSD kernel panics on specific hardware; sensr relies on the Linux kernel and ships a kdump/pstore crash capture to the diagnostics page.
- [#9999](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9999) [support, 2026, 0/2, incomplete] **Keyboard/Mouse Disconnect + “GIANT-LOCKED” Error During OPNsense Boot on ESXi 8.0.3 (Dell PowerEdge R430)** -- w0 N/A-LINUX: FreeBSD GIANT-LOCKED USB on ESXi; Linux kernel driver story differs
- [#6541](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6541) [feature, 2023, 0/1, -] **Wi-Fi EasyMesh support** -- w0 OUT-OF-SCOPE: Wi-Fi EasyMesh controller is AP-vendor territory; not a firewall core feature.
- [#8126](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8126) [support, 2024, 0/1, support] **I am using UFS, but the memory usage is high on the host.** -- w0 SUPPORT: host RSS vs guest reported memory on Proxmox -> UX: dashboard memory widget distinguishes used/cache/free and documents balloon behaviour.
- [#9949](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9949) [other, 2026, 0/1, feature] **mvc: remove Phalcon in favour of only keeping the Volt framework** -- w0 N/A-LINUX: Phalcon/Volt PHP framework removal; sensr has no PHP templating layer (SPA + Rust API).

### 3b. Help-wanted issues closed as "completed" by the inactivity bot (432)

These carry `state_reason=completed` in the API but the final comment is the OPNsense-bot 180-day timeout; nothing shipped. Older issues (2015-2021) were closed this way before GitHub introduced `not_planned`. Listed for completeness; the ones with a feature-like title are the most relevant.


**Firewall rules / pf (20)**

- [#4478](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4478) [support, 2020, 0/16] **FW applying "default blocking rule" ignoring existent rules and "Bypass firewall rules for traffic on the same interface** -- w2 SUPPORT: asymmetric routing / state mismatch blamed on default deny -> UX: log view links each drop to the exact rule + state reason (no-state, TCP flags) with explanation
- [#3517](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3517) [feature, 2019, 0/7] **Block all IPv6 - Disable logging** -- w2 ADOPT: every implicit/system rule (block IPv6, bogons, anti-lockout) has a per-rule log toggle in config; no hidden unlogged/unlogged-only rules
- [#5426](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5426) [feature, 2021, 0/7] **Interface column under the Floating rules page** -- w1 ADOPT: floating/global rule grid includes an Interfaces column (chips) and is filterable by interface
- [#4374](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4374) [feature, 2020, 0/6] **If using bridges, hide members from Firewall => Rules menu** -- w2 ADOPT: bridge model has explicit 'filter on bridge vs members' flag; rule editor only offers interfaces that are actually filtering points (hide members when bridge-filtering)
- [#4680](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4680) [support, 2021, 0/6] **Traffic packet process / flow through FW services** -- w2 SUPPORT: packet-flow order question -> UX: ship packet-flow diagram (nft hooks, flowtable, IDS, shaper, VPN) in docs plus a per-packet trace tool (nft monitor trace).
- [#3795](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3795) [feature, 2019, 0/5] **Line separators to improve the organization of many entries (specifically Firewall Rules)** -- w2 ADOPT: rule list supports separator/section rows (title + color) stored in the ordered rule list; also collapsible groups in the grid.
- [#4386](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4386) [bug, 2020, 0/5] **Firewall Destination WAN_address should not include VIPs** -- w2 ADOPT: distinct macros: '<if> address' = primary interface address only, '<if> VIPs' = VRRP/alias addresses, '<if> all addresses' = both; documented.
- [#4644](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4644) [feature, 2021, 2/2] **Transition of metadata from live log to new firewall rules form** -- w2 ADOPT: live firewall log rows offer 'create rule from this' (prefill iface/proto/src/dst/port, choose pass/block) and 'add src to alias'.
- [#5180](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5180) [feature, 2021, 0/4] **Auto generated DHCP rule if DHCP is on, on that inteface.** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: enabling DHCP/DHCPv6 server on an interface auto-generates the matching input rule (visible as system rule) and removes it when disabled.
- [#4828](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4828) [feature, 2021, 0/3] **FR: create new rule on a special position** -- w2 ADOPT: rule create API accepts 'insert before/after <rule UUID>' / position index; GUI row menu 'add rule above/below'.
- [#4967](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4967) [feature, 2021, 1/2] **Auto set "Firewall Adaptive Timeouts" to FreeBSD defaults of start 60% and end 120% at boot** -- w1 N/A-LINUX: pf adaptive timeouts; nftables/conntrack has nf_conntrack_max + per-protocol timeouts; sensr sizes conntrack_max from RAM and exposes timeouts in schema.
- [#4974](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4974) [bug, 2021, 0/3] **rules processing problem with a tunnel through a tunnel** -- w1 TEST: nested tunnels (OpenVPN over WireGuard): rules attach to each interface via iifname/oifname exactly; integration test that wg0 rules never match tun0 traffic.
- [#3843](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3843) [bug, 2019, 0/2] **firewall schedules has incorrect character encoding (utf-8)** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: all strings are UTF-8 in JSON end-to-end; no urlencode-for-XML layer; property test round-trips non-ASCII descriptions through API/GUI.
- [#3887](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3887) [feature, 2020, 1/1] **Command to kill states on a cron job** -- w1 ADOPT: API/CLI action 'flush conntrack entries matching filter' (src/dst/proto/port) usable from scheduled tasks; also 'kill states on rule change' option.
- [#3998](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3998) [support, 2020, 0/2] **'quick' setting not applied to rules** -- w2 SUPPORT: confusion over 'quick' vs last-match semantics -> DESIGN: sensr rules are first-match only (nft accept/drop terminate); no per-rule quick flag to misread.
- [#5194](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5194) [bug, 2021, 0/2] **Generated DHCP rules are too wide** -- w2 ADOPT: auto DHCP rules are family-specific: v4 udp 67/68 from 0.0.0.0/net to iface; v6 udp 546/547 from fe80::/10 to ff02::1:2/link-local only.
- [#3709](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3709) [feature, 2019, 0/1] **firewall rules user-related** -- w2 ADOPT: identity-based rules: authenticated user/group (from portal/VPN/AD login) -> per-user nft set populated at login, referenced as rule source.
- [#3978](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3978) [bug, 2020, 0/1] **Firewall rules won't apply when one of the interfaces lacks an address on failover** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: NAT rules reference interface address via nftables named sets/maps updated by address events; a WAN without lease yields an empty set, not a ruleset load failure
- [#4039](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4039) [bug, 2020, 0/1] **GIF Tunnel :  Firewall rules only func in Group** -- w2 TEST: per-interface rules on tunnel devices (sit/gre) match via iifname exactly like physical ones; nft ruleset test over every interface kind
- [#5076](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5076) [bug, 2021, 0/1] **IP Do-Not-Fragment does not seem to be persistent** -- w1 N/A-LINUX: pf scrub no-df; Linux clears DF/MSS via nft 'tcp option maxseg size set rt mtu' + clamp; settings persist in schema and re-render on every apply.

**NAT (7)**

- [#4094](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4094) [bug, 2020, 0/21] **ICMP fragmentation required not returned to NAT origin** -- w3 ADOPT: PMTUD through NAT: ICMP frag-needed from tunnel MTU is conntrack-translated back to LAN origin; default MSS clamp to PMTU on tunnel ifaces; test
- [#4341](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4341) [feature, 2020, 0/5] **[Bug/FReq] Automatic NAT outbound rules' source for "'localhost'->'WAN'" rule should be 'This Firewall' instead of '127.** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: locally originated traffic (NTP, updates) is masqueraded via a 'self' rule matching output-chain traffic rather than 127.0.0.0/8; test firewall-originated egress
- [#4228](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4228) [support, 2020, 0/3] **netflix/stan chromecast not working on vanilla install** -- w1 SUPPORT: streaming/Chromecast broken on vanilla install (likely MSS/MTU or IGMP) -> UX: default outbound NAT + MSS clamping on PPPoE and a connectivity troubleshooter page
- [#4334](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4334) [bug, 2020, 1/1] **Outbound NAT with interface groups rotates between *source* addresses** -- w2 ADOPT: outbound NAT on an interface group expands to one rule per member with that member's own address (oifname -> snat to its addr); test no cross-interface source rotation
- [#4477](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4477) [feature, 2020, 1/1] **Feature: Ad SIP Inspection for RTP pinholes** -- w1 ADOPT: optional SIP ALG via nf_conntrack_sip/nf_nat_sip helper (per-rule 'ct helper set'), default off, with RTP pinhole expectations; document NAT-keepalive alternative.
- [#4619](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4619) [feature, 2021, 0/2] **NAT Edit - Add LAN and WAN address to Target Options** -- w1 ADOPT: NAT redirect target selector offers 'this firewall' plus every interface address/VIP as named choices (same address picker as rules)
- [#4033](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4033) [bug, 2020, 0/1] **NAT not applied to new OPT1 if.** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: automatic outbound NAT derived from config at plan time covers every interface in the model incl. newly added; test adding an OPT interface generates masquerade

**Aliases / tables / GeoIP (17)**

- [#4184](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4184) [feature, 2020, 0/21] **Automatic rule generation/Ability to automatically add IP's to Aliases** -- w2 ADOPT: dynamic ban sets: API endpoint to add IP+TTL to an nft set with timeout flag, driven by Suricata/auth-fail hooks (built-in fail2ban-like).
- [#4949](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4949) [feature, 2021, 1/20] **Add alias for IPv4 rfc1918 private addresses to default OPNsense installation** -- w2 ADOPT: ship built-in read-only aliases: rfc1918, bogons, rfc6598 (100.64/10), link-local, ula (fc00::/7), multicast; usable in rules from first boot.
- [#5223](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5223) [feature, 2021, 0/14] **New Dashboard Widget "URL Table IP Summary" and show info about URL Table IPs** -- w2 ADOPT: alias detail view: for each URL/feed alias show entry count, last fetch time/status, and 'which alias contains IP X' lookup; dashboard widget with per-alias hit counters
- [#3083](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3083) [feature, 2018, 0/5] **Feature request: Categories for Aliases.** -- w1 ADOPT: aliases carry an optional category/tag list in the schema (same tag model as rules); grid filters by category server-side.
- [#3776](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3776) [bug, 2019, 1/4] **Underscore in alias not a valid hostname** -- w2 ADOPT: host-alias validator accepts underscores in DNS labels (RFC 2181: any octet is legal in DNS names; only hostnames in RFC 952 sense are strict).
- [#4294](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4294) [feature, 2020, 0/5] **add all dns characters to alias names** -- w2 ADOPT: alias names allow '.', '-' (nft set names are internal UUID-derived, display name separate); alias grid search matches content values too.
- [#4679](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4679) [bug, 2021, 0/5] **Alias Creator Will Not Accept Host-names With An Underscore** -- w1 ADOPT: same as #3776 - permit underscore in alias hostnames; also disallow creating DHCP static names the alias validator will later reject (single shared validator).
