- [#4319](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4319) [feature, 2020, 0/3] **There is no API function to drop current connections after calling reconfigure alias API function** -- w2 ADOPT: alias/set update API has a 'flush matching conntrack entries' option (removed addresses -> conntrack -D by src/dst) so revoked access drops live sessions.
- [#4498](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4498) [feature, 2020, 0/3] **Support IP2Location Lite as alternate to Maxmind for GeoIP** -- w1 ADOPT: GeoIP alias source is pluggable (MaxMind GeoLite2 mmdb, IP2Location Lite CSV, DB-IP); core only needs a country->CIDR list importer.
- [#5403](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5403) [feature, 2021, 0/3] **Edit Alias enhancement suggestion** -- w1 ADOPT: alias entries are {address, description} pairs in the schema, edited as a two-column table (plus bulk paste) rather than a plain textarea.
- [#3848](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3848) [bug, 2019, 0/2] **Firewall/Diagnostics/pfTables truncates last alias entry in list** -- w1 TEST: alias-table diagnostics API returns exactly N entries for N-entry set (off-by-one in pfctl output parsing); nft set listing via netlink avoids text parsing.
- [#4239](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4239) [bug, 2020, 0/2] **filter_update dies with large blocklists** -- w3 ADOPT: alias/blocklist updater streams huge lists (FireHOL) into nft sets with bounded memory, incremental add/delete, and backoff on failure instead of crash-loop.
- [#4292](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4292) [bug, 2020, 0/2] **Outbound NAT rule breaks when renaming an Alias it is using** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: rules/NAT reference aliases by stable UUID not name; rename is a non-breaking field edit; referential integrity validated at apply
- [#4987](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4987) [feature, 2021, 0/2] **Firewall alias partial export and import function.** -- w1 ADOPT: per-object export/import (single alias or selection) as JSON via API; GUI 'export selected'/'import file' in alias grid.
- [#4014](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4014) [feature, 2020, 0/1] **Support dnsmasq and pf Tables** -- w2 ADOPT: FQDN aliases populated from the resolver's answers (Unbound ipset/dnsmasq-style hook into nft set with TTL timeout) so rules follow real client resolutions.
- [#4623](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4623) [feature, 2021, 0/1] **systemwide alias database** -- w2 ADOPT: aliases are one typed object in the config model referenced by UUID from firewall, NAT, VPN local/remote networks, shaper, portal - not firewall-only.
- [#4783](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4783) [bug, 2021, 0/1] **Adding invalid geoip source breaks aliases** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: per-alias URL fetch is isolated (one failing download logs a per-alias error and keeps the previous set content; never aborts the whole table refresh).

**Interfaces (VLAN/LAGG/bridge/PPPoE/wireless) (21)**

- [#2774](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/2774) [feature, 2018, 0/62] **Orange France are playing silly bu***** again.** -- w1 ADOPT: WAN presets per ISP quirk: Orange FR needs DHCP option 90 authentication string derived from user/pass - offer a 'DHCP auth (Orange)' option generator on the WAN.
- [#3904](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3904) [support, 2020, 0/23] **LACP Port Errors** -- w1 SUPPORT: LACP errors tied to NIC/switch; -> ADOPT: bonding (802.3ad) config exposes lacp_rate/xmit_hash and shows per-member LACP partner state + error counters in GUI
- [#3802](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3802) [bug, 2019, 1/16] **Disconnecting huawei e3372h Hilink modem from USB port crashes OPNsense** -- w2 ADOPT: hot-unplug of a USB WAN NIC is a netlink RTM_DELLINK event: gateway marked down, routes/rules referencing it withdrawn cleanly; chaos test unplugging an interface.
- [#3970](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3970) [bug, 2020, 0/7] **Updating an interface not possible with configured CARP IPs and IPv4 set to none** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: validation runs on candidate vs current config (no-op save always valid); VIP references checked against interface address-family config declaratively.
- [#2135](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/2135) [feature, 2018, 2/4] **Feature Request: cronjob to turn WLAN on/off** -- w1 ADOPT: schedule-driven interface/radio enable-disable (cron-like time ranges in config) for WLAN and other interfaces via API action
- [#4017](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4017) [bug, 2020, 0/4] **Problem with dhclient restarting** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: DHCP client lifecycle owned by one supervisor (networkd/own client) so no duplicate dhclient spawns; also fix: WAN 'defunct' detection and auto-recovery
- [#4073](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4073) [bug, 2020, 0/3] **Interface enable (disable) not working** -- w2 TEST: unticking 'enable' on an interface must bring the link admin-down and drop its addresses/routes; integration test asserts link state via netlink
- [#4167](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4167) [support, 2020, 0/3] **ESXI 7 - Add a 4 network adapter** -- w1 SUPPORT: adding a 4th NIC on ESXi -> UX: hot-added NICs appear as unassigned in interface list with 'assign' action; no console wizard needed.
- [#4435](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4435) [bug, 2020, 0/3] **Changing "speed and duplex" seems to have no effect** -- w1 TEST: interface speed/duplex setting is applied via ethtool/netlink and verified (link mode readback); integration test for forced 100-full
- [#5074](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5074) [support, 2021, 0/3] **2.5gbps modem intel x710 base-t slow throughput only 400-600mbps** -- w1 SUPPORT: X710 2.5GBASE-T negotiation/throughput -> UX: interface page shows ethtool link mode/advertised modes and allows forcing speed/autoneg; driver issue is upstream.
- [#5205](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5205) [feature, 2021, 0/3] **Improve MAC OUI vendor database handling and display for 'private' MAC addresses** -- w1 ADOPT: OUI database refreshed as a downloadable feed (like alias URL tables) with 'locally administered/random MAC' flagged instead of fake vendor
- [#5222](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5222) [support, 2021, 0/3] **How do i translate the same action from this TPLINK router config to OPNsense** -- w1 SUPPORT: translating vendor-router PPPoE+VLAN setup -> UX: WAN wizard with ISP presets (VLAN tag, PPPoE, MTU, DS-Lite) and inline explanation of each field
- [#4001](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4001) [bug, 2020, 0/2] **vxlan interface does not start after reboot** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: all virtual netdevs (vxlan, gre, vlan, bridge) rendered as networkd netdev units from config and created every boot; boot test for each type
- [#4173](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4173) [support, 2020, 0/2] **MAC Address Mismatch between ether and hwaddr** -- w1 SUPPORT: virtio NIC MAC vs VLAN parent MAC confusion -> ADOPT: VLAN/sub-interfaces inherit parent MAC; GUI shows both configured and hw MAC; spoofed MAC explicit field
- [#4286](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4286) [feature, 2020, 0/2] **simplify scheduled pppoe reconnect** -- w2 ADOPT: PPPoE interface option 'scheduled reconnect at HH:MM daily' expressed in config (not a cron hack); interface page shows its own device name
- [#4351](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4351) [support, 2020, 0/2] **Interface to Modem fails to start after reboot** -- w1 SUPPORT: LTE modem Ethernet link not coming up after reboot -> ADOPT: per-interface link watchdog (no carrier/DHCP for N min => bounce link) for unattended sites
- [#4371](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4371) [feature, 2020, 0/2] **WAN - Default DHCP, LAN Default Address** -- w1 ADOPT: first-boot console wizard sets LAN address/DHCP and WAN mode before bringing interfaces up; no implicit 192.168.1.1 default on the wire.
- [#5372](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5372) [other, 2021, 0/2] **DS-Lite Support** -- w2 ADOPT: DS-Lite (RFC6333) B4 client: ip4ip6 tunnel to AFTR learned via DHCPv6 option 64 (or static), auto default v4 route; common in DE/JP ISPs
- [#3705](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3705) [feature, 2019, 0/1] **Create tags (friendly names) for MAC addresses in ARP table** -- w2 ADOPT: MAC/host friendly-name tags stored in config and shown across ARP/NDP tables, DHCP leases, live log, states (one device registry)
- [#3818](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3818) [bug, 2019, 0/1] **[Bug] Wifi is broken in 20.1** -- w1 ADOPT: WLAN config is its own typed sub-object retained when assigned to any role; hostapd WPA settings validated and applied; AP+client modes tested on supported chipsets
- [#5154](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5154) [feature, 2021, 0/1] **Change workflow for wireless devices in UI** -- w1 ADOPT: single WLAN workflow: radio -> SSIDs (VAP) -> interface assignment in one typed object tree; hostapd config generated from it, no separate 'wireless device' page

**IPv6 / DHCPv6-PD / RA / NPTv6 (20)**

- [#4282](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4282) [bug, 2020, 0/18] **IPv6 connection is lost on the LAN interface after the tracked WAN interface (PPPoE) reconnected** -- w3 TEST: PPPoE reconnect re-triggers DHCPv6-PD and reapplies tracked LAN prefixes + RA; integration test for WAN down/up cycles with prefix change and unchanged
- [#4768](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4768) [other, 2021, 0/17] **Enable dhcp6c auto SLA-LEN correct** -- w3 ADOPT: DHCPv6-PD client derives tracked-interface prefix IDs automatically from the delegated prefix length (no manual SLA-LEN); GUI shows received prefix and lifetimes
- [#4909](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4909) [bug, 2021, 0/17] **IPv6 Track WAN "loses" the IPv6 address when the interface loses its Ethernet carrier** -- w3 TEST: carrier loss on parent NIC of tracked VLANs must not permanently drop GUA; addresses re-applied on link-up (or kept with keep_addr_on_down=1); netlink link-flap test
- [#4457](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4457) [bug, 2020, 0/9] **GIF0 interface doesn't display IPv6 address in console** -- w1 ADOPT: tunnel interfaces (sit/gif, HE tunnelbroker) are first-class interfaces: their IPv6 shows on console/dashboard and is available to dyndns/address consumers
- [#5198](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5198) [bug, 2021, 0/8] **IPv6 not updated on rtadvd when PPPoE IP/6rd IPv6 update** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: RA prefixes derive from address events (6rd/PD prefix change triggers RA regeneration + deprecation of old prefix); test 6rd prefix change path
- [#817](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/817) [feature, 2016, 4/1] **Feature: configurable IPv6 Link-Local address** -- w2 ADOPT: configurable static IPv6 link-local per interface (e.g. fe80::1) alongside auto LL; applied via netlink with addr_gen_mode none; shared LL for HA
- [#3531](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3531) [support, 2019, 1/3] **6rd; possibly missing direct connect route?** -- w1 SUPPORT: 6rd direct-route to same-ISP peers -> ADOPT: 6rd tunnel (sit) config sets on-link route for the 6rd prefix /BR-prefix so intra-ISP traffic bypasses the relay (RFC5969 §7)
- [#4656](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4656) [bug, 2021, 2/2] **dhcpdv6 uses the same prefix for delegations as it does for the lan(s) interface.** -- w3 ADOPT: DHCPv6-PD server (Kea pd-pools) must only hand out sub-prefixes from a range disjoint from the interface's own /64; validator rejects overlap.
- [#4276](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4276) [feature, 2020, 0/3] **Feature request: Configurable IPv6 Prefix Advertisements** -- w2 ADOPT: RA config supports multiple advertised prefixes per interface (GUA from PD + static ULA), each with own lifetimes/flags; dynamic prefix referenced by track-interface
- [#4556](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4556) [bug, 2020, 0/3] **IPv6 doesn't work with 2 ISP (Multi-WAN) configuration** -- w3 ADOPT: multi-WAN IPv6 source-address routing by default: per-WAN PD prefix gets an ip rule 'from <prefix> lookup <wan table>' so tracked LANs egress the right ISP
- [#5021](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5021) [support, 2021, 0/3] **I lose ipv6 after a few minutes** -- w2 SUPPORT: IPv6 dies after minutes (likely RA lifetime/firewall DHCPv6 client) -> DESIGN-AVOIDS: auto-allow DHCPv6 client/ICMPv6 for WAN; UX: PD lifetime/expiry shown live
- [#5105](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5105) [bug, 2021, 0/3] **Interface MTU is not applied to all routes for GIF tunnel** -- w1 TEST: configured MTU on sit tunnel applies to the device and all routes via it (no 1280 route-specific mtu); netlink route attribute test
- [#3662](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3662) [bug, 2019, 0/2] **19.7: IPv6 gif tunnel not transitioning away from "tentative" flag** -- w2 TEST: addresses on sit/gif tunnel devices must be added with nodad (no DAD on p2p tunnels) so they never remain tentative; netlink flag test
- [#4324](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4324) [bug, 2020, 0/2] **pppoe: IPv6 single gateway gets created and configured with link local address, while IPv6 configuration type=none** -- w2 TEST: PPPoE with IPv6=none must not create an IPv6 gateway from the link-local peer; test gateway derivation per address-family config
- [#4945](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4945) [feature, 2021, 0/2] **RTADV configuration** -- w2 ADOPT: WAN IPv6 mode options: accept_ra on/off, autoconf (SLAAC) on/off independent of DHCPv6, and RA router preference; config knob not sysctl hack
- [#5072](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5072) [other, 2021, 0/2] **ISP provides IPv6 delegated prefix only, so WAN doesn't have a public IPv6** -- w2 ADOPT: PD-only WAN mode: optionally assign an address from the delegated prefix (or ULA) to the firewall for outbound v6 (dyndns, updates); source-address selection documented
- [#5356](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5356) [bug, 2021, 0/2] **WireGuard IPv6 Tracking** -- w1 ADOPT: track-interface can source a /64 from any PD-receiving WAN and assign it to WireGuard/tunnel interfaces; schema allows tracking on virtual netdevs; test
- [#4151](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4151) [bug, 2020, 0/1] **DHCPv6 relay broken** -- w1 ADOPT: DHCPv6 relay (Kea dhcp6 relay or dhcrelay6) with validated upstream server + egress interface; schema validation of IPv6 literal with %scope; integration test
- [#4260](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4260) [support, 2020, 0/1] **IPv6 Firewall Rules doesn't work with  Firewall-Rules-WAN, only work with Firewall-Rules-Floating** -- w2 SUPPORT: IPv6 rules on PPPoE WAN not matching -> TEST: rules bound to pppoe interface match v6 traffic (interface-group/ifname resolution for dynamic ifaces)
- [#5022](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5022) [bug, 2021, 0/1] **Router Advertisements Validation/Default issue + Help Text Update** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: schema defaults (RA min/max interval) are real Option<T> with documented defaults; empty = default, cross-field validation (min<max) only when both set

**DHCP (ISC / Kea / dnsmasq) (23)**

- [#4985](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4985) [bug, 2021, 0/12] **DHCP leases are not listed as used** -- w1 TEST: leases from relayed/other-subnet clients (giaddr set) appear in the lease list; lease view queries Kea lease DB directly, not interface-filtered.
- [#4385](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4385) [feature, 2020, 1/8] **dhcp: vendor-classes** -- w2 ADOPT: Kea client-classes with vendor-class-identifier/test expressions and per-class option-data (VoIP VLAN option 132/43) in the schema and GUI.
- [#5308](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5308) [feature, 2021, 0/7] **Manage custom DHCPD configuration directly by web interface.** -- w2 ADOPT: Kea lease management API: list, delete (release) a lease, create reservation from lease; GUI action buttons on lease rows.
- [#4087](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4087) [feature, 2020, 0/6] **dhcpd / dhcpdv6 / radvd: "none" option for DNS servers** -- w2 ADOPT: DHCPv4/v6 and RA DNS options are Option<list>: explicit 'none' emits no DNS option (no RDNSS) instead of defaulting to the firewall address
- [#4444](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4444) [feature, 2020, 0/4] **feature: Adding the abbility to Assign static DHCP leases in the DHCP range** -- w2 ADOPT: Kea permits reservations inside the dynamic pool (reservations-out-of-pool=false); validator warns rather than rejects.
- [#4814](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4814) [feature, 2021, 3/1] **Allow network booting on different architectures within the DHCP gui** -- w2 ADOPT: netboot settings per architecture (Kea client-classes on option 93: BIOS, x86_64 EFI, arm64 EFI, iPXE) with separate boot filenames.
- [#5299](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5299) [feature, 2021, 0/4] **DHCPv4 Server: release IPs addresses for networks/subnets not directly connected to an interface** -- w2 ADOPT: Kea v4 shared-networks / remote subnets served via DHCP relay (giaddr-based subnet selection), so subnets not on a local interface can be served
- [#4770](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4770) [bug, 2021, 0/3] **DHCP6 service not handing out address after some time** -- w2 TEST: DHCPv6 server (Kea) keeps serving after PD prefix renew/rebind with same prefix; reservation by DUID survives; long-running integration test
- [#5390](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5390) [feature, 2021, 0/3] **Better DHCP Client Identifier (option 61) Support** -- w2 ADOPT: WAN DHCP client exposes client-identifier (option 61) as none/MAC/custom bytes, plus 'release lease now' action; DHCPv6 DUID likewise settable.
- [#3628](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3628) [bug, 2019, 0/2] **DHCP Release on the Interfaces Overview page does not send DHCPRELEASE** -- w2 ADOPT: 'release' action on DHCP/DHCPv6 WAN sends a real DHCPRELEASE/Release message (client API call) before stopping; verify with pcap integration test.
- [#4348](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4348) [feature, 2020, 1/1] **DHCP static mappings as alias for pf** -- w2 ADOPT: DHCP reservations are referenceable in firewall rules (source = reservation/host object) and a built-in dynamic alias 'all reservations on <iface>'.
- [#4410](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4410) [feature, 2020, 1/1] **Add device type under DHCP leases** -- w1 ADOPT: lease API exposes vendor-class-identifier, hostname, client-id; GUI shows a device-type column derived from it (and optional OUI vendor lookup).
- [#4863](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4863) [feature, 2021, 0/2] **virtual ip's and dhcpv4 server settings issue** -- w2 ADOPT: Kea per-reservation option-data (routers, dns) and per-pool options so fixed leases can use a VIP gateway different from the interface address
- [#5007](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5007) [feature, 2021, 0/2] **[Feature Request] Add toggle or filter on DHCP leases, or per interface** -- w1 ADOPT: lease grid with server-side filters: interface, state (active/expired), kind (dynamic/reserved); defaults persisted per user.
- [#5276](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5276) [feature, 2021, 0/2] **Allow custom subnet in dhcpv6** -- w2 ADOPT: DHCPv6 server pools/subnets configurable independently of the interface's tracked prefix (static ULA ranges alongside PD-derived), per Kea subnet6 model
- [#4055](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4055) [bug, 2020, 0/1] **dhcp server in relay mode, but dhcp static entries are active, preventing acces to firewall** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: DHCP per-interface mode is an enum server/relay/off; reservations only exist under a server config, so relay mode cannot leave stale static entries active.
- [#4192](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4192) [bug, 2020, 0/1] **DHCP dynamic leases removed when adding static lease.** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: Kea reservations are added via its control API without restarting the server, so dynamic leases stay visible/intact when a reservation is saved.
- [#4765](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4765) [bug, 2021, 0/1] **Unable to remove static DHCPv4 Address Mapping** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: reservations referencing an interface/subnet are validated as dangling on interface removal (block delete or cascade); no orphan objects hidden from GUI.
- [#4892](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4892) [bug, 2021, 0/1] **Static IP used when connecting from a separate interface** -- w2 ADOPT: DHCP reservations scoped to subnet (Kea host reservation in subnet4); same MAC may have different reservations per subnet; test host moving between VLANs
- [#4900](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4900) [feature, 2021, 0/1] **Add alias name/description for IP and MAC in DHCP lease page.** -- w1 ADOPT: lease grid joins hostname, reservation description, and alias/host-object name per IP/MAC; log views link IP -> lease/hostname.
- [#4978](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4978) [feature, 2021, 0/1] **dnsmasq for dhcp v4 and v6** -- w2 ADOPT: dnsmasq as optional integrated DHCPv4/v6+DNS backend; Kea default with built-in v4+v6 lease->DNS registration (hook or lease API poll).
- [#4982](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4982) [bug, 2021, 0/1] **DHCP Server: configuration for option 121 : bad conversion from string parameters (GUI) to the DCHP one.** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: DHCP option 121/249 classless routes typed as list of {prefix, router} in the schema; encoder emits correct octets; unit-tested against RFC 3442 examples.
- [#5028](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5028) [bug, 2021, 0/1] **Cannot create reservations on different interfaces / subnets for the same mac address** -- w2 ADOPT: allow same MAC reserved in multiple subnets (Kea supports per-subnet hosts) and ensure dynamic lease in other subnet still registers in DNS; test

**DNS (Unbound / dnsmasq / DDNS) (33)**

- [#4559](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4559) [bug, 2020, 2/17] **Allow dnsmasq port=0** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: typed Option<u16> port where 0 is a valid 'DNS disabled' value distinct from None/default; dnsmasq TFTP/DHCP-only mode supported.
- [#4270](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4270) [bug, 2020, 1/17] **Unbound crashing when WAN IP refreshes** -- w3 ADOPT: Unbound binds LAN/loopback (address-stable) and is not restarted on WAN address change; outgoing-interface updated via reload; PPPoE re-dial integration test.
- [#5121](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5121) [bug, 2021, 0/9] **Unbound stops forwarding to authoritative** -- w2 ADOPT: domain overrides render as forward-zone with forward-first off + domain-insecure; test that NXDOMAIN from roots never poisons the internal zone after TTL.
- [#3526](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3526) [bug, 2019, 0/7] **IPv4 addresse of new interface is added to the DNS A record of OPNsense FQDN** -- w2 ADOPT: the firewall's own FQDN resolves only to addresses of interfaces where the admin UI listens (or an explicit chosen address), not every listen interface of Unbound.
- [#4208](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4208) [bug, 2020, 0/7] **Unbound: "Domain override" leads to an invalid configuration when a "Custom Options" is specified** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: no free-text 'custom options' blob; every Unbound directive is a typed field, so generated config is validated (unbound-checkconf) inside apply.
- [#4362](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4362) [bug, 2020, 0/7] **Unbound Whitelist doesnt work** -- w2 TEST: DNSBL allowlist entry (exact + wildcard) removes matching blocklist domains before rendering RPZ/local-zone; unit test on the merge step.
- [#3457](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3457) [support, 2019, 0/6] **WPAD doesn't work with CNAME record in unbound DNS** -- w0 SUPPORT: WPAD needs an A record (Windows ignores CNAME for wpad) -> UX: DNS override form offers A/AAAA/CNAME types explicitly with a hint for wpad.
- [#5237](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5237) [feature, 2021, 4/2] **plugin: unbound SRV records overrides** -- w2 ADOPT: host overrides support arbitrary record types (SRV, TXT, CNAME, MX, PTR) via typed local-data entries, not just A/AAAA
- [#5355](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5355) [feature, 2021, 0/6] **Unbound: do not add entries for "ifconfig"** -- w2 ADOPT: same as #3526 - own hostname A/AAAA limited to admin-listen addresses or an explicit list; toggle to disable auto self-records entirely.
- [#4507](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4507) [feature, 2020, 0/5] **add some futures in unbound dns** -- w2 ADOPT: Unbound GUI exposes DoT upstreams (forward-tls-upstream + authname), aggressive-nsec, use-caps-for-id (0x20), qname-minimisation, serve-expired as typed fields.
- [#5256](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5256) [feature, 2021, 1/4] **Private Domains setting is hard to find under non-DNSBL use cases** -- w1 ADOPT: 'private-domain / rebind-protection exceptions' is a top-level DNS setting with help text, not buried under blocklist; auto-suggest for local search domains.
- [#3974](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3974) [support, 2020, 0/4] **Pixel phone outbound traffic or DNS lookups intermittently stop working** -- w0 SUPPORT: intermittent Pixel DNS/outbound stalls on old releases -> UX: per-client live DNS/conntrack diagnostics (query log filtered by client IP) to triage.
- [#4416](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4416) [support, 2020, 0/4] **Unbound responds to queries concerning DNSBL lists with servers rather than IPs?** -- w1 SUPPORT: DNSBL lookups fail via forwarded resolver -> UX: Unbound settings explain forward vs recursive mode and offer per-domain forward exceptions (bl.spamcop.net direct)
- [#5176](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5176) [feature, 2021, 0/4] **WIP: full dnsmasq support** -- w2 ADOPT: dnsmasq as full optional backend (DHCPv4/v6, RA, DNS) alongside Kea/Unbound; same schema objects (reservations, options) render to either backend.
- [#3727](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3727) [bug, 2019, 0/3] **Unbound DNS does not reload after adding new OpenVPN** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: Unbound access-control derived from all local networks incl. VPN tunnel ranges at plan time; adding a VPN triggers Unbound reload; test
- [#3861](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3861) [feature, 2019, 1/2] **No way to prevent OPNSense from performing relative DNS lookup for Host aliases** -- w2 ADOPT: alias hostname resolution uses FQDN-only lookups (trailing dot, no search-domain suffixing) via the Rust resolver; option for which resolver to use.
- [#4420](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4420) [feature, 2020, 1/2] **unbound: add checkbox for creation of PTR-records to "Host Overrides"** -- w1 ADOPT: per host-override 'create PTR' toggle (default on for first record per IP); reverse zones generated from the model.
- [#4427](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4427) [feature, 2020, 0/3] **unbound: add checkbox to make unbound-anchor use system-wide DNS servers** -- w1 ADOPT: DNSSEC trust-anchor update (unbound-anchor) can use configured forwarders or be disabled (bundled root key only) for isolated networks.
- [#6610](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6610) [feature, 2023, 0/3] **DNSBL source "justdomains.github.io" is too outdated. we need different sources** -- w2 ADOPT: built-in DNSBL source catalog is a signed JSON list updated independently of releases (hagezi, oisd, StevenBlack); stale sources flagged with last-modified date.
- [#4074](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4074) [bug, 2020, 0/2] **dhcp lease removes unbound override entry** -- w2 ADOPT: DHCP->DNS lease registration lives in its own Unbound local-zone/view and never removes user-defined host overrides (incl. wildcards); conflict shows a warning.
- [#4206](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4206) [support, 2020, 0/2] **Recent chrome bypassing unbound/DNS overrides due to DNSSEC** -- w1 SUPPORT: Chrome DoH bypass of local resolver -> UX: one-click 'disable DoH canary' (use-application-dns.net NXDOMAIN) + optional DoH-resolver blocklist (see #3967).
- [#4387](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4387) [feature, 2020, 0/2] **unbound: Host Overrides Type SRV** -- w2 ADOPT: host overrides support A, AAAA, CNAME, MX, SRV, TXT, PTR record types with typed fields per type (priority/weight/port/target for SRV).
- [#4413](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4413) [other, 2020, 0/2] **Services: Unbound DNS: Blacklist / unexpected behaviour** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: disabling blocklists triggers immediate regeneration of Unbound local-zone data in the apply plan (not next cron); test enable/disable round-trip
- [#4466](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4466) [bug, 2020, 0/2] **Unbound access list (ACL) discrepancy between gui and filesystem** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: Unbound access-control rendered from the typed model (deduped set of interface networks + user ACLs); generated config has a snapshot test vs the GUI view.
- [#4708](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4708) [support, 2021, 0/2] **Issues with resolving using getaddrinfo (two simultaneous DNS requests from same port)** -- w0 SUPPORT: glibc parallel A/AAAA from one port -> Unbound default handles it; UX: expose Unbound query log and 'serve-expired' to diagnose 5s stalls.
- [#5369](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5369) [support, 2021, 0/2] **Unbound DNS** -- w0 SUPPORT: shortnames without client search domain cannot resolve -> UX: DHCP pushes domain/search (option 15/119) by default; docs explain.
- [#3630](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3630) [feature, 2019, 0/1] **Unbound: allow forwarding to non-standard port** -- w2 ADOPT: Unbound forward/stub zones accept host@port and DoT (host@853#authname) upstreams in the schema; no 'custom options' escape hatch needed.
- [#3995](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3995) [bug, 2020, 0/1] **Unbound on 20.1.2: Forward to non-default @port not effective in Domain Override** -- w1 TEST: domain-override upstream 'addr@port' renders forward-addr with the port; snapshot test of generated unbound.conf for non-default ports.
- [#4052](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4052) [bug, 2020, 0/1] **Unbound crash: restarted then unable to read unbound.conf** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: service configs rendered atomically in apply (write temp, check with unbound-checkconf, rename); a restart never races a half-written config.
- [#4339](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4339) [feature, 2020, 0/1] **Allow disabling automatic subnet ACLs for Unbound to mitigate DNS-IP-proxy attacks** -- w1 ADOPT: Unbound ACLs: option to disable auto 'allow all interface subnets' and define explicit allow/deny/refuse per source network; default still auto.
- [#4506](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4506) [bug, 2020, 0/1] **Unbound Stops Resolving DNS Queries due to DHCP registrations** -- w2 ADOPT: DHCP lease -> Unbound registration via unbound-control is rate-limited/batched and failures never block resolution; Unbound health check with auto-restart.
- [#4984](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4984) [bug, 2021, 0/1] **Generate more/other reverse zones for DHCPv4 "Dynamic DNS" with classless subnets** -- w1 ADOPT: DHCP DDNS reverse zones generated for every /24 covered by a classless subnet (or RFC 2317 delegation for < /24), computed from the typed prefix.
- [#5236](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5236) [feature, 2021, 0/1] **plugin: unbound access-control-view settings** -- w1 ADOPT: Unbound views (access-control-view) in config model: per-source-network local-data/overrides so different clients get different answers

**Routing / gateways / multi-WAN (21)**

- [#2186](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/2186) [bug, 2018, 0/43] **wizard: WAN over PPPOE - default Route is not set correctly** -- w3 ADOPT: default route follows the PPPoE session (route installed from IPCP event via netlink, metric below physical WAN DHCP); integration test for PPPoE-over-DHCP-NIC.
- [#4149](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4149) [bug, 2020, 0/26] **Dpinger failing to properly recover on PPPOE recycling** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: gateway monitor re-binds on link/address netlink events (PPPoE re-up) instead of holding a dead socket; test monitor recovers after interface recreate
- [#3879](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3879) [bug, 2020, 0/20] **Problem with failover latency based** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: gateway health uses hysteresis (down/up thresholds + hold time) and group membership changes reload policy routes via fwmark; no monit-driven flapping; test
- [#3950](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3950) [bug, 2020, 0/10] **Routing via two gateways in same subnet fails** -- w2 TEST: static routes via two different gateways in the same subnet must install distinct next-hops (ip route add ... via X dev Y); netlink test asserts both
- [#5170](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5170) [bug, 2021, 0/7] **Multi-WAN - local services work only on main WAN** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: multi-WAN reply routing by default: inbound on WAN-N conntrack-marked, replies via WAN-N table (ip rule fwmark); test services on all WANs
- [#3917](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3917) [support, 2020, 0/6] **Can't activate Single Gateway (VPN)** -- w2 SUPPORT: tunnel gateway stays disabled (no far-end IP) -> ADOPT: p2p tunnel gateways (WireGuard/OpenConnect) allowed as interface-only next hop, no gateway IP needed
- [#3947](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3947) [feature, 2020, 1/4] **Assign static route to gateway group** -- w2 ADOPT: static routes accept a gateway group as next hop; route is re-pointed by the gateway health-checker on failover (ip route replace).
- [#4107](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4107) [bug, 2020, 0/5] **"Apply" button times out when a gateway is down** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: apply does no blocking network I/O (DNS, probes) in the request path; hostname aliases resolve async with cached fallback; test apply with WAN down
- [#4163](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4163) [feature, 2020, 0/5] **Monitor multiple IPs through/for a gateway** -- w2 ADOPT: gateway health-check with multiple monitor targets (next-hop + N upstream IPs), per-target latency/loss history and configurable down-vote quorum
- [#5238](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5238) [bug, 2021, 1/4] **Routing Problem when using two pppoe Connections from the same Internetprovider with the same PPP link target** -- w2 TEST: two PPPoE sessions with identical remote peer address must still get distinct routing tables (route via dev, not via IP); test duplicate peer IP
- [#4676](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4676) [bug, 2021, 0/4] **Gateway monitoring doesn't work for dynamic IP after upgrade to 21.1** -- w2 ADOPT: gateways with dynamic address (WireGuard/PPPoE) resolve the monitor target at runtime (peer address or configured monitor IP) instead of being skipped as invalid.
- [#4224](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4224) [bug, 2020, 0/3] **Duplicate entry of IPv4 upstream gateway after changing [WAN] interface IP address** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: console/API edits go through the same typed model with unique gateway keys per (interface, family); duplicate creation impossible by schema constraint
- [#3747](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3747) [bug, 2019, 0/2] **Default Gateway being de-configured during prolonged outages in dual-WAN failover scenario** -- w3 TEST: prolonged WAN outage in dual-WAN failover must never leave no default route; long-running failover test asserts default route present at every state transition
- [#4322](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4322) [other, 2020, 0/2] **select outgoing gateway/interface for traffic originating from opnsense itself / rules for loopback?** -- w1 ADOPT: firewall-originated traffic policy: per-service/source-address egress gateway via ip rule on output + SNAT consistency; diagnostics tools get an 'egress interface' picker
- [#4629](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4629) [bug, 2021, 0/2] **Gateway setting via the firewall rules is broken** -- w3 TEST: policy-routing rule with explicit gateway (fwmark + ip rule table) forwards traffic incl. return path (conntrack mark restore); integration test for rule-gateway
- [#3428](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3428) [other, 2019, 0/1] **dpinger, initial state** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: own gateway monitor emits explicit state machine (unknown->up/down) with first-probe event so apply waits for an initial verdict before computing routes
- [#3979](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3979) [feature, 2020, 0/1] **Reconnect/reset VPN client connections on failback after successful failover** -- w2 ADOPT: gateway-group failback event hook: optionally restart/re-resolve VPN client tunnels (OpenVPN/WG/IPsec) bound to a group so they return to the preferred WAN
- [#4207](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4207) [support, 2020, 0/1] **OPNSense not correctly monitoring backup gateway via another OPNSense router.** -- w1 SUPPORT: gateway monitor via a transit-link peer -> UX: per-gateway 'monitor IP' + 'monitor via source interface' fields and a live view of probe results/route used.
- [#4599](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4599) [feature, 2021, 0/1] **[FR] add route show to Diagnostics** -- w2 ADOPT: Diagnostics 'route lookup' (ip route get incl. fwmark/policy tables) showing chosen table, next-hop, interface and source address for a given dst/src/mark
- [#4876](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4876) [bug, 2021, 0/1] **Multi-WAN Sticky Connections on Wrong Interface** -- w2 TEST: load-balanced gateway groups with sticky (conntrack-based) must not emit WAN-sourced packets back on LAN; test NAT+mark consistency in multi-tier groups
- [#5226](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5226) [bug, 2021, 0/1] **MultiWAN reply-to not working as expected in some instances** -- w2 TEST: reply-to equivalent (conntrack mark -> per-WAN table) works for 3+ WANs including non-default ones with same upstream next-hop; multi-WAN integration test

**HA / CARP / config sync (13)**

- [#3708](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3708) [bug, 2019, 0/14] **HA Cluster: Failover does not force a reconnect of mobile clients to new master** -- w2 ADOPT: on VRRP master->backup transition, hooks terminate local IKE SAs (vici terminate) and VPN sessions bound to the VIP so clients reconnect to the new master.
- [#4000](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4000) [bug, 2020, 0/11] **problem with automatic sync of xmlrpc - encode idna message in log** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: HA replication over REST+JSON (UTF-8 native); peer hostname/IP validated as IDNA at save time with clear error; sync log shows per-object result.
- [#4106](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4106) [bug, 2020, 0/10] **Unbound crashes on Backup-Node after HA-Sync** -- w2 TEST: replicated config applied on backup must pass the same validation + service config check (unbound-checkconf) before restart; HA sync integration test with Unbound.
- [#4977](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4977) [bug, 2021, 0/8] **Default gateway lost on CARP master after failback on HA cluster setup with single public IP** -- w3 ADOPT: default route tied to the VRRP-tracked WAN: on master transition re-install route via netlink from gateway state machine; failback integration test.
- [#3476](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3476) [bug, 2019, 0/6] **CARP IPv6 / ND / CARP with IPv6 on WAN doesn't work as expected.** -- w3 ADOPT: VRRP IPv6 VIP: unsolicited NA on master transition, answer NS for the VIP from master only (keepalived); test off-link reachability after failover.
- [#4194](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4194) [feature, 2020, 0/6] **Make provision for fallover upon loss of gateway connection when using multiple WAN connections attached to different ro** -- w2 ADOPT: VRRP priority tracking of gateway health (track_script on WAN monitor) so the HA node with a working uplink becomes master
- [#4601](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4601) [feature, 2021, 0/5] **[FR] HA sync Interfaces too** -- w2 ADOPT: HA replicates interface definitions (VLANs, bridges, assignments) with a per-node device mapping table (eth0 <-> enp1s0) and node-local address overrides.
- [#4897](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4897) [feature, 2021, 1/4] **Add option to configure AdvRASrcAddress and DeprecatePrefix for radvd** -- w3 ADOPT: RA source address = VRRP virtual link-local (AdvRASrcAddress) and deprecate-prefix on failover; RA daemon tied to VRRP state transitions
- [#5085](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5085) [feature, 2021, 0/5] **All dynamic DNS updates conditional on CARP status** -- w2 ADOPT: dynamic DNS updater (and other egress-side jobs) have 'only when VRRP master' condition; backup node stops updating on demotion.
- [#5346](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5346) [bug, 2021, 0/4] **Unexpected behavior when syncing pf-configuration** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: HA config replication is by stable interface IDs/names, not creation order; conntrackd state sync keyed on interface names mapped between peers.
- [#4864](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4864) [feature, 2021, 0/3] **Feature Request: dhcp relay HA mode** -- w2 ADOPT: DHCP relay (and other active-only services) have 'run on VRRP master only' flag; keepalived notify scripts start/stop them; config replicated.
- [#5336](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5336) [feature, 2021, 0/3] **HA setup sync certificates** -- w2 ADOPT: ACME-issued certs are config objects -> replicated by HA sync automatically; renewal on master triggers sync + service reload on backup.
- [#4307](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4307) [bug, 2020, 1/1] **Feature request: Add DHCP Relay configuration sync via XMLPRC** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: HA config replication syncs the whole declarative config (selected subtrees), so no per-feature XMLRPC sync omissions like DHCP relay.

**VPN: WireGuard (3)**

- [#5307](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5307) [bug, 2021, 5/13] **Egress gateway default is incorrect under NAT port forward auto-associated firewall rules on wireguard interfaces** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: inbound connections on any non-default interface (WireGuard incl.) get conntrack mark -> reply via that interface's table by default; test port forward on wg0
- [#4973](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4973) [support, 2021, 0/13] **ping problem** -- w1 SUPPORT: WireGuard ping/allowed-ips confusion -> UX: WireGuard peer form validates AllowedIPs overlap and shows live handshake/transfer per peer with route preview
- [#4490](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4490) [other, 2020, 0/2] **Wireguard gateway doesn't reset firewall state when goes up & down** -- w3 ADOPT: kill-switch semantics: when a policy-routed gateway (WireGuard) goes down, flush conntrack entries for that fwmark/table and block (no fallback) unless configured

**VPN: IPsec (34)**

- [#1773](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/1773) [bug, 2017, 0/34] **NAT before IPsec: limitations and workarounds** -- w2 ADOPT: NAT-before-IPsec via nftables (snat in postrouting matched on xfrm policy / VTI oif) decoupled from identifier type; works with DN/FQDN identifiers; test
- [#5241](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5241) [feature, 2021, 0/15] **IPSec: Parameters and Description in the IPsec configuration dialog should be improved** -- w1 ADOPT: IPsec form uses strongSwan's own terminology (pubkey, eap-mschapv2, psk, local/remote) with accurate descriptions generated from schema docs
- [#5456](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5456) [support, 2022, 0/15] **IPSec site-to-site is not connecting** -- w1 SUPPORT: route-based IPsec + BGP not connecting (likely TS 0/0 + firewall) -> UX: VTI wizard preconfigures TS 0.0.0.0/0, tunnel addresses, allow rules, and a connection debug view
- [#3728](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3728) [bug, 2019, 0/12] **All traffic blocked with route-based IPSec** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: route-based IPsec uses XFRM interfaces with explicit routes only; never install 0.0.0.0/1 + 128/1 split routes; test enabling VTI does not alter default routing
- [#3732](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3732) [bug, 2019, 0/12] **Selection of Carp Adresses in ipsec** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: IPsec local address selector enumerates all VIPs of an interface from the model (list type), not a per-interface single value.
- [#4438](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4438) [support, 2020, 0/9] **ipsec ike1 hybrid-rsa xauth failed** -- w0 OUT-OF-SCOPE: IKEv1 hybrid-RSA/XAuth is legacy; sensr supports IKEv2 only (EAP-MSCHAPv2/EAP-RADIUS/EAP-TLS) with clear docs
- [#3781](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3781) [support, 2019, 0/8] **IPSec IKEv2 Mobile Windows 10 letsencript intermidiate cert issue** -- w2 SUPPORT: Windows IKEv2 requires intermediate CA sent -> ADOPT: certificate store tracks chains; strongSwan gets full chain (certs + intermediates) auto-installed from ACME
- [#3794](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3794) [feature, 2019, 0/7] **Allow the administrator to specify multiple IPsec IKE/SA algorithms** -- w3 ADOPT: IKE/ESP proposals are lists of full proposal strings (multiple encryption+integrity+DH combos) per connection, matching swanctl syntax
- [#3580](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3580) [bug, 2019, 0/6] **Route-based IPsec / SNAT / packets are not entering the tunnel** -- w2 TEST: SNAT/1:1 NAT onto route-based IPsec (xfrm/VTI interface) enters tunnel; nft postrouting on xfrm oif test; document ordering of NAT vs xfrm lookup
- [#5032](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5032) [bug, 2021, 0/6] **IPSec Tunnel between Sophos UTM and OPNSense does not reconnect after Sophos Reboot** -- w2 ADOPT: expose close_action/start_action and 'reauth on DELETE' for site-to-site so tunnel re-initiates after peer sends CLEAR/DELETE; default start+trap for initiators
- [#4183](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4183) [feature, 2020, 0/5] **ipsec Phase2 config, multiple left/rightsubnet** -- w3 ADOPT: child SA local/remote traffic selectors are lists of networks (swanctl local_ts/remote_ts comma lists); one child per policy set, not per subnet
- [#4618](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4618) [bug, 2021, 0/5] **Unbound crash leads to not working IPsec tunnels and Interface problems** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: IPsec status/API never depends on local resolver; FQDN peers resolved by strongSwan itself with cached fallback; Unbound crash isolated via systemd restart
- [#3707](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3707) [feature, 2019, 0/4] **Implement closeaction=restart on ipsec.conf as an option during Phase 1 configuration** -- w2 ADOPT: expose swanctl close_action (none/trap/start) and dpd_action per connection; default start+close_action=start for site-to-site initiators
