- [#2424](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/2424) [feature, 2018, 0/1] **Allow more special values for `addr` in ipfw rules (Traffic Shaping)** -- w1 ADOPT: shaper classifier matches reuse firewall address model: 'this firewall' (fib/local), aliases as nft sets, interface networks; same address picker as rules
- [#4035](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4035) [support, 2020, 0/1] **The traffic shaper isn't working properly** -- w2 SUPPORT: fair sharing breaks when real link rate < configured -> ADOPT: CAKE with autorate-ingress / optional auto bandwidth measurement; docs note on setting ~90% of real rate
- [#4377](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4377) [feature, 2020, 0/1] **Please add routed IPsec interfaces to Traffic Shaper** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: shaper attaches qdisc to any netdev incl. XFRM/VTI interfaces; route-based IPsec is a real interface so shaping/QoS (voip DSCP) applies
- [#5048](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5048) [bug, 2021, 0/1] **QoS upload shaper does not match subnet clients for upload, download = OK?** -- w2 SUPPORT: upload shaping never matched (direction/source confusion post-NAT) -> DESIGN-AVOIDS: shaper classifies pre-NAT via conntrack mark in forward chain; test both directions

**Auth / users / 2FA / SSH (12)**

- [#5303](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5303) [feature, 2021, 0/17] **Feature Request: "Remote" Groups** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: groups are config-model objects (not Unix groups): any length/chars; 'remote group' = LDAP DN/claim -> role mapping, no import needed.
- [#4349](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4349) [feature, 2020, 0/15] **Selection possibility for multiple LDAP servers while initiating an import** -- w1 ADOPT: user import/sync is per auth-server (select which LDAP source), not implicitly the first configured one.
- [#4660](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4660) [feature, 2021, 0/7] **Feature Request: LDAP: Automatically scheduled re-import** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: privileges bind to remote groups via claim/DN mapping evaluated at each login, so removing AD membership revokes access immediately; no import/re-sync job.
- [#3481](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3481) [feature, 2019, 0/4] **Feature request: HTML5-Application-Portal** -- w0 OUT-OF-SCOPE: HTML5 RDP/VNC/SSH portal (Guacamole) is an app, not firewall core; possible plugin.
- [#3940](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3940) [feature, 2020, 0/3] **Add possibility to add multiple 2FA token devices or to create emergency backup codes** -- w2 ADOPT: multiple TOTP/WebAuthn devices per user + one-time recovery codes generated at enrollment; CLI/console path to reset 2FA.
- [#5283](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5283) [feature, 2021, 0/3] **Feature Request: New Access Server - "LDAP + Radius"** -- w2 ADOPT: composable auth chains: password backend (LDAP/local) + second-factor backend (TOTP, RADIUS PAP OTP, etc.) as independent typed steps, not fixed combos.
- [#3749](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3749) [feature, 2019, 1/1] **Automatically strip/ignore domain in login form** -- w2 ADOPT: LDAP auth server option 'strip @domain / map UPN' and allow '@' in usernames (no Unix-login-name constraint since users are not system accounts).
- [#3956](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3956) [support, 2020, 0/2] **StartTLS authentication won't work with LDAP/AD** -- w2 SUPPORT: LDAP StartTLS trust failure -> UX: 'test connection' button returning the exact TLS/bind error and the CA chain seen, instead of a silent empty container list.
- [#4963](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4963) [bug, 2021, 0/2] **ldap / ad user import is only supported for the first server** -- w1 ADOPT: same as #4349 - import/sync is per auth-server, each with own base DN/filter.
- [#5255](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5255) [bug, 2021, 0/2] **Automatic user creation on LDAP does not read properties** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: one user-provisioning function (sync name/email/groups) is shared by manual import, scheduled sync and auto-create-on-login; no duplicated code paths.
- [#4673](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4673) [bug, 2021, 0/1] **Ldap Group mapping does not work in Tester** -- w2 ADOPT: auth-server 'test login' returns the full resolved identity (groups after mapping, attributes) using the exact same code path as a real login.
- [#5009](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5009) [bug, 2021, 0/1] **OPNSense ldap imported users with restricted groups and OTP seed assigned ignores groups** -- w3 TEST: VPN server bound to group X rejects LDAP-imported user with TOTP who is not in X; group enforcement runs after every auth backend incl. LDAP+TOTP composite.

**Certificates / trust (12)**

- [#1718](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/1718) [feature, 2017, 2/11] **Feature Request: Support client-side SSL/TLS certificates in the web GUI** -- w2 ADOPT: optional mTLS client-certificate auth for the admin UI/API (map cert CN/SAN to user), combinable with TOTP; also API key alternative.
- [#2497](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/2497) [feature, 2018, 0/10] **[FR] www/nginx: assign certificates to users or in the other direction to allow TLS logins** -- w1 ADOPT: same as #1718 - client-cert login for admin UI with explicit cert->user binding (not loose CN match) and a trusted-CA setting.
- [#3968](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3968) [bug, 2020, 1/9] **CA/Cert Generation: Optional fields are treated as mandatory** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: cert/CA schema marks only CN (or SAN) required; all other DN fields optional with sane omission; generated form enforces exactly the schema.
- [#4924](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4924) [feature, 2021, 0/4] **Mutual TLS (mTLS / SSL client authentication) Support** -- w2 ADOPT: admin UI/API mTLS modes disabled/optional/required with trusted client CA list, CRL/OCSP check, cert->user mapping; optional per-listener.
- [#5060](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5060) [feature, 2021, 0/3] **Add Interface for TLS policies** -- w2 ADOPT: central TLS policy objects (min version, cipher suites, curves, ALPN) referenced by every TLS-terminating service (GUI, VPN, portal, plugins).
- [#2908](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/2908) [feature, 2018, 0/2] **system/trust: Support EC keys** -- w2 ADOPT: internal CA/cert key types RSA (2048/3072/4096), ECDSA (P-256/P-384), Ed25519; key type is a schema enum, default ECDSA P-256; UI filters by CA type.
- [#4366](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4366) [feature, 2020, 0/2] **Allow for encrypted private keys when defining an existing CA.** -- w1 ADOPT: import of encrypted PEM/PKCS#8/PKCS#12 keys with passphrase field; key stored re-encrypted under the sensr secret store.
- [#8736](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8736) [bug, 2025, 0/2] **Certificate Reissue,  aexisting DNS domian name will be vorget** -- w1 TEST: 'reissue certificate' pre-fills all SAN types (DNS, IP, email, URI) from the existing cert; round-trip unit test on SAN parsing.
- [#2432](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/2432) [feature, 2018, 0/1] **Feature Request: bulk certificate import and management** -- w1 ADOPT: bulk cert import (zip/PEM bundle, auto-pair cert+key by filename), expiry/weak-key/SHA1 badges in the grid, deep links from VPN forms to trust store.
- [#4367](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4367) [bug, 2020, 0/1] **Certificate import does not check whether the provided private key is encrypted as it would in #4366.** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: one key parser (rustls-pemfile/pkcs8) validates CA and cert imports alike; encrypted key without passphrase is rejected at the API with a clear error.
- [#4999](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4999) [bug, 2021, 0/1] **OpenVPN / Client export wrong/corrupted p12 when private key is EC** -- w2 TEST: PKCS#12 export for EC (secp384r1) keys yields a bundle importable by OpenVPN/Windows; unit tests per key type and curve
- [#5001](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5001) [feature, 2021, 0/1] **Add an API service to replace an existing X509 certificate** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: certificates are schema objects with stable UUIDs -> PUT /certificates/{id} replaces cert+key; services referencing it reload in the same apply.

**API / MVC / configd / CLI (8)**

- [#3826](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3826) [feature, 2019, 5/7] **API: firewall rules** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: API-first design - firewall rules CRUD exists day one with OpenAPI spec; Terraform provider can be generated from the schema.
- [#4062](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4062) [feature, 2020, 7/1] **API: DHCP settings and static mappings** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: DHCP (Kea) config incl. reservations is in the schema -> CRUD API generated; add scoped API keys so a tenant can manage only their reservations.
- [#4355](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4355) [feature, 2020, 4/2] **Api support for vlan / interfaces** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: interfaces/VLANs are schema objects -> full API; creating a VLAN+interface+DHCP+rules is one declarative apply.
- [#3878](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3878) [feature, 2020, 0/2] **API: VPN Status** -- w2 ADOPT: /status/vpn/ipsec endpoints expose IKE SA + child SA state (from vici), plus OpenVPN/WireGuard peer state, for monitoring; also Prometheus metrics.
- [#4740](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4740) [bug, 2021, 0/2] **Assign gateway group to rules using the firewall automation feature** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: rule.gateway references a typed enum of gateway/gateway-group IDs from the same schema the GUI uses; API cannot lag GUI options.
- [#5041](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5041) [feature, 2021, 1/1] **Managing powerd settings through OPNsense API** -- w1 ADOPT: CPU power/frequency governor is a schema setting (system.power: governor, min/max) so it is API-settable for UPS/low-power automation.
- [#5265](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5265) [bug, 2021, 0/2] **API Error while setting Suricata Rules** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: typed schema-generated API rejects malformed payloads with a 400 and field path instead of a model-internal exception; add API fuzz test on IDS rule endpoints
- [#4746](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4746) [feature, 2021, 0/1] **Add new configd parameter to reload templates before command execution** -- w1 N/A-LINUX: configd template-reload semantics; sensr renders service configs from the model inside the transactional apply, so cron/actions never see stale templates.

**GUI / UX / i18n (39)**

- [#4053](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4053) [bug, 2020, 1/12] **IDS alerts page navigation items not updated correctly** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: one shared grid component with server-side paging (page/size/total) used by alerts view too; pagination e2e test.
- [#8264](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8264) [feature, 2025, 11/1] **Automatic switching between light and dark themes based on browser or system’s light/dark mode** -- w2 ADOPT: theme setting auto/light/dark following prefers-color-scheme by default; per-user, persisted.
- [#1916](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/1916) [feature, 2017, 0/8] **Feature request: search for specific settings** -- w2 ADOPT: same as #4343 - global search over schema field names/labels/help text and current values, deep-linking to the field.
- [#4875](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4875) [bug, 2021, 0/8] **Inconsistent UI for L3 menu items** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: single SPA navigation model (sidebar for sections, tabs for sub-forms) generated uniformly from the schema's module tree; no per-page variants.
- [#4007](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4007) [bug, 2020, 0/7] **Firefox requires login twice before it logges in.** -- w1 TEST: login works first try when the same browser holds sessions/cookies for several firewall hosts (cookie name/path/host scoping, CSRF token not shared).
- [#3719](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3719) [bug, 2019, 0/6] **Bootstrap-select not rendered properly in Firefox** -- w1 ADOPT: SPA uses native/accessible select components tested across Firefox/Chrome/Safari in CI (Playwright matrix); no bootstrap-select.
- [#4010](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4010) [support, 2020, 0/6] **PR_CONNECT_RESET_ERROR on apply modification** -- w2 SUPPORT: apply restarted the web stack and dropped the session -> DESIGN-AVOIDS: apply never restarts the API/GUI server; results stream back over the same connection
- [#2583](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/2583) [feature, 2018, 0/5] **UI - Firewall/Rules representation/rendering** -- w2 ADOPT: rules grid: dense responsive layout, column chooser, compact icon column, horizontal space usage on wide screens; UX-tested at 1400 and 2560px.
- [#3731](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3731) [bug, 2019, 0/5] **form POST fails in FF when accessing GUI with IPv6 literals and 1st party isolation enabled** -- w1 TEST: SPA + API auth must work when accessed via IPv6 literal host ([2001:db8::1]) incl. cookies/CSRF with Firefox first-party isolation; e2e test matrix
- [#4018](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4018) [bug, 2020, 0/5] **Dashboard notices do not fit on line** -- w1 ADOPT: notices panel wraps/truncates with full text on expand; responsive SPA layout tested at narrow widths.
- [#4766](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4766) [feature, 2021, 1/4] **Larger drop down menus for higher resolution screens** -- w1 ADOPT: UI density/scale preference (compact/normal/large) per user; dropdowns are searchable comboboxes sized by viewport height.
- [#4185](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4185) [bug, 2020, 0/4] **IPs in ARP table not sorted numerically** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: grids sort server-side on typed columns (IpAddr, not string) so ARP/lease/state tables sort numerically by construction
- [#4253](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4253) [bug, 2020, 0/4] **GSA reports SSL/TLS: Missing `secure` and `httponly` Cookie Attribute** -- w3 ADOPT: session cookie set with Secure, HttpOnly, SameSite=Strict, __Host- prefix; HSTS header; security headers audited by a test.
- [#4345](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4345) [bug, 2020, 0/4] **[Bug/FReq] Search box is missing on tablet in portrait mode even if there is enough space for it** -- w1 ADOPT: global search is always reachable (icon expanding to field on narrow widths, keyboard shortcut), not hidden by breakpoint.
- [#4668](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4668) [feature, 2021, 0/4] **Discard Button** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: candidate config has 'apply' and 'discard' (drop pending diff) actions; pending-changes banner shows the diff and both buttons.
- [#4682](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4682) [bug, 2021, 0/4] **Log pages do not advance (fastforward) to end (last page),** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: log viewer uses server-side cursor pagination with total count and 'jump to last'; generic grid component tested for first/last navigation
- [#79](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/79) [other, 2015, 1/2] **system: setup wizard should also cover IPv6, add help texts** -- w2 ADOPT: setup wizard configures IPv6 (WAN DHCPv6-PD/static, LAN track-interface + RA) alongside IPv4 with inline help for each choice
- [#3926](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3926) [bug, 2020, 0/3] **OpenVPN Server config / Checkbox not working** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: optional fields are Option<T> in schema; unchecking/clearing serializes None and apply removes the push option; form round-trip test
- [#4343](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4343) [feature, 2020, 0/3] **[Bug/Q] Trying to find Secure Shell / SSH settings with the search box does not return anything** -- w2 ADOPT: global search indexes field labels/help text of generated forms (from the schema), not just menu entries; result deep-links to the field.
- [#4620](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4620) [bug, 2021, 0/3] **wrong error message when we have a whitespace in front** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: typed IpAddr fields trim/normalize input at the schema layer; validation error names the offending field and raw value; add parser tests for whitespace
- [#4678](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4678) [bug, 2021, 0/3] **OpenVPN Server Client Settings cannot be deactivated and saved** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: typed optional fields serialize None when cleared; form submits full object, not only changed keys; e2e test clearing DNS push fields
- [#5004](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5004) [feature, 2021, 0/3] **System: Diagnostics: Activity numeric sort order for most colums** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: process/activity grid served as typed JSON (ints, bytes, durations) and sorted server-side numerically; shared grid handles unit-formatted columns
- [#3819](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3819) [feature, 2019, 0/2] **Table Column Sorting Feature Request** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: every grid is the same generated component with server-side sort/search/paging via API; no legacy static tables.
- [#3986](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3986) [bug, 2020, 0/2] **Interfaces assignment / Include description for IPSEC and OpenVPN tunnels** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: interface assignment picker lists device + owning object description (tunnel name) since tunnels are typed config objects linked to netdevs
- [#4342](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4342) [bug, 2020, 0/2] **[CUp] Sidebar and button missing when hiding it in portrait mode and changing to landscape mode afterwards** -- w1 TEST: responsive layout e2e - rotate viewport portrait<->landscape with sidebar hidden; sidebar toggle always reachable.
- [#4448](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4448) [feature, 2020, 0/2] **Feature proposal: Sort dropdowns of RRD items alphabetically in Diagnostics/systemhealth.volt** -- w1 ADOPT: all GUI dropdowns of interfaces/gateways/series are natural-sorted by display name server-side; generated select component enforces it
- [#4818](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4818) [bug, 2021, 0/2] **"HTTP_REFERER enforcement" activated by default blocks access to WebUI** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: CSRF protection via SameSite cookie + token, not Referer/Host enforcement; admin listen hostnames and access settings editable from console menu/CLI.
- [#4829](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4829) [feature, 2021, 1/1] **FR: add a search function for "where used"** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: typed references in the schema give a reverse index -> GET /objects/{id}/references ('where used') for aliases, certs, gateways, interfaces; shown in GUI.
- [#4882](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4882) [bug, 2021, 0/2] **System: Wizard: Configure LAN Interface dhcp** -- w1 ADOPT: setup wizard supports LAN as DHCP client (edge/lab cases) without crashing; schema allows any interface mode in wizard; e2e test
- [#4910](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4910) [feature, 2021, 0/2] **Redesign the standard template?** -- w0 ADOPT: compact page header (breadcrumb + actions on one line); no redundant content-head block.
- [#5047](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5047) [feature, 2021, 0/2] **"Apply Changes" in global header.** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: single global 'pending changes' banner (candidate config diff) on every page with apply/discard; see #4668.
- [#5339](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5339) [feature, 2021, 0/2] **Sticky Sidebar Collapsed/Expanded State** -- w1 ADOPT: sidebar collapsed state and other UI prefs persisted per user (server-side preferences API, localStorage fallback).
- [#5401](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5401) [other, 2021, 0/2] **OpenVPN Server Configuration** -- w2 ADOPT: destructive quick actions (disable/stop a service, delete) in grids require confirmation; icons unambiguous (toggle switch, not arrow)
- [#3159](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3159) [feature, 2019, 0/1] **Feature Request:- Copy feature for ALL values in an input box for ease of transfer.** -- w1 ADOPT: every list/multi-value field in generated forms has copy-to-clipboard and text-mode (one per line) editing; config export covers machine transfer.
- [#3889](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3889) [feature, 2020, 0/1] **Implement help request directly in the web ui's different page** -- w0 OUT-OF-SCOPE: in-UI community help/pastebin of config sections; instead provide 'copy anonymized config section as JSON' for forum posts.
- [#4269](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4269) [bug, 2020, 0/1] **pfTables quick address: Exception thrown** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: generated forms validate client-side from the schema before submit; API returns structured 400 field errors, never an uncaught exception page.
- [#4340](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4340) [feature, 2020, 0/1] **[CUp/FReq] Network Timeout on "Interfaces: Overview" page (./status_interfaces.php) when reloading PPPoE** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: interface reload is an async API job; GUI polls job status / streams events instead of blocking the HTTP request until PPPoE reconnects
- [#4414](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4414) [feature, 2020, 0/1] **NAT Port Forwarding GUI page minor change with big impact** -- w1 ADOPT: port-forward form shows source address/port by default (collapsible 'Advanced' labeled clearly) ; generated forms label hidden sections as 'Source filter' not 'Show'
- [#4732](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4732) [feature, 2021, 0/1] **Change Request: Categories colors** -- w0 ADOPT: category color swatch rendered as a leading badge/chip before the description in rule grids.

**Dashboard / widgets (11)**

- [#4283](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4283) [feature, 2020, 0/12] **widget "interfaces" improvement: use space for interface name, show netmask, show config type** -- w1 ADOPT: interfaces widget with user-selectable columns: name, status, speed, IPv4/prefix, IPv6/prefix, config type (static/DHCP/PPPoE).
- [#3115](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3115) [feature, 2019, 0/7] **Feature Request: Traffic Widget** -- w1 ADOPT: traffic widget has per-widget interface selection persisted per user; widget config stored in user preferences via API.
- [#4217](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4217) [feature, 2020, 0/7] **Dashboard Interfaces Widget Table Auto Sizing** -- w1 ADOPT: interfaces widget uses auto-width columns (CSS grid), long names wrap gracefully; no hardcoded percentages.
- [#3915](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3915) [feature, 2020, 0/6] **Keep the lobby traffic graphs persistent** -- w2 ADOPT: traffic graphs backfill from the metrics store (Prometheus-style ring buffer) so dashboard shows last N minutes on open, not an empty graph.
- [#3928](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3928) [feature, 2020, 0/4] **LAGG & VLAN status on dashboard** -- w1 ADOPT: interface status API exposes topology (VLAN id, parent, bond members with per-member link state/speed); dashboard renders it as a tree.
- [#4426](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4426) [feature, 2020, 3/1] **[Feature Request]: Display average core temp instead of individual temps in Dashboard widget.** -- w0 ADOPT: thermal widget aggregation option (per-core / package / average) from hwmon; metrics exported to Prometheus too.
- [#3561](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3561) [feature, 2019, 1/2] **Feature request: Dashboard widget for intrusion detection alerts** -- w1 ADOPT: IDS alerts widget (last N eve.json alerts, severity colored, link to alerts page) in the dashboard widget library.
- [#2479](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/2479) [other, 2018, 1/1] **Auto update OpenVPN widget on dashboard** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: all dashboard widgets subscribe to a live event stream (SSE/WebSocket) from the API; VPN session changes push instantly
- [#4647](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4647) [feature, 2021, 0/2] **Widget for Interface totals** -- w1 ADOPT: historical per-interface totals widget (today/week/month bytes) backed by the built-in metrics store with retention.
- [#4851](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4851) [feature, 2021, 0/2] **OPNsense Single Pane of Glass** -- w1 ADOPT: drag-and-drop grid dashboard (resizable widgets, multiple columns/rows) with per-user layout saved via API.
- [#4551](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4551) [feature, 2020, 0/1] **System log widget: transit to clientside** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: all widgets are SPA components polling/streaming the log API (journald query with filters) -> auto-update + client-side filter by default.

**Logging / reporting / monitoring (22)**

- [#4121](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4121) [bug, 2020, 0/13] **Remote Syslog keeps sending to old target** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: log shipper config fully regenerated from the declarative model on each apply (no stale drop-in files); test removal of a target stops shipping
- [#6622](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6622) [feature, 2023, 0/6] **Possibility to change color scheme of graphs in "Reporting: Traffic"** -- w1 ADOPT: charts use a fixed accessible categorical palette with distinct hues + legend toggle/isolate per series; color scheme selectable in theme
- [#2063](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/2063) [feature, 2018, 1/4] **Feature Request: Insight, Details Report - Add an option to display hostnames instead of IPs on Insight Details report** -- w1 ADOPT: flow/NetFlow reports have a sticky per-user 'resolve hostnames' toggle (reverse DNS + DHCP lease names cached).
- [#3703](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3703) [feature, 2019, 0/4] **NetFlow - Virtual WAN IPs** -- w1 ADOPT: NetFlow/IPFIX 'WAN addresses' for direction classification include VIPs/aliases automatically (derived from the interface's address list).
- [#4639](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4639) [bug, 2021, 0/4] **Insight: Interface totals (bit/sec) IN graph disappears after setting dropdown to "Last Year, 24 hour average"** -- w1 TEST: metrics time-series downsampling (1y/24h avg) must return both in/out series; unit test over the aggregation query for every resolution
- [#5345](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5345) [feature, 2021, 0/4] **Template support for remote syslog logging** -- w2 ADOPT: remote log export formats selectable per target: RFC5424 syslog, JSON (structured fields), CEF; firewall log fields are named keys not CSV
- [#4054](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4054) [feature, 2020, 0/3] **Filterlog: Rule Number May Change - need tracker ID for log collectors** -- w3 ADOPT: every rule (user and system) has a stable UUID; nft log prefix carries it; firewall log JSON includes rule_id, label, interface; never reused after delete.
- [#5191](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5191) [feature, 2021, 0/3] **Allow logging on a specific fw rule to bypass filter.log and directly go to remote syslog server** -- w2 ADOPT: per-rule log target option (local, remote-only, both) via distinct nft log group -> separate nflog pipeline to remote syslog/OTLP, bypassing local store.
- [#3924](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3924) [feature, 2020, 0/2] **Add source IP address parameter while setting up a logging destination** -- w2 ADOPT: remote log target (syslog/OTLP) has optional source address / bind interface field; validated against configured addresses
- [#4421](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4421) [feature, 2020, 0/2] **OpenVPN interface is missing as a selectable option in Live View** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: live firewall log filter lists every netdev the rules reference (tun/wg/xfrm) from the model, not only assigned physical interfaces
- [#4808](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4808) [bug, 2021, 0/2] **Throughput drop when traffic graph is open** -- w2 ADOPT: live traffic view reads /proc/net/dev + conntrack top-talkers via netlink sampling, never pcap on the data path; benchmark test for throughput impact.
- [#4870](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4870) [bug, 2021, 0/2] **Top Talkers: No IPv6 talkers shown** -- w2 TEST: traffic/top-talkers accounting (NetFlow/conntrack) must include IPv6 flows; test asserts v6 flows appear
- [#4997](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4997) [bug, 2021, 0/2] **Memory leak/crash when opening IPSec logs after clearing them** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: log clear is an API action on journald/log store, not file truncation under an active reader; log viewer uses bounded queries; test clearing while viewing
- [#5080](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5080) [bug, 2021, 0/2] **Traffic top talkers in and out same value  (Read 28 times)** -- w1 TEST: top-talkers per-host in/out totals computed from directional flow records; unit test that in != out for asymmetric traffic
- [#4164](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4164) [feature, 2020, 0/1] **Add Uplink (pppoe) (historical) connectivity logging** -- w2 ADOPT: structured link/PPPoE session event log (up/down, cause, duration) with history view and Prometheus metrics for uplink availability
- [#4203](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4203) [feature, 2020, 0/1] **Netflow destination neither allows DNS nor IPv6** -- w2 ADOPT: NetFlow/IPFIX collector target is typed SocketAddr-or-hostname + port, supports IPv6 literals ([::1]:2055) and DNS names
- [#4586](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4586) [feature, 2021, 0/1] **More flexible logfile settings** -- w1 ADOPT: logs in journald with stable names; export/rotation by size or time via journald + optional file sink with logrotate-style compression; UI download of archives
- [#4662](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4662) [feature, 2021, 0/1] **Featrure Request Monitoring Endpoint** -- w3 ADOPT: Prometheus /metrics + JSON status API covering gateways, interfaces, services, DHCP leases, CPU/mem/temp; no agent needed for Zabbix/Grafana
- [#4701](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4701) [bug, 2021, 0/1] **Insight does not work if network interface is 802.1q** -- w1 TEST: flow accounting (NetFlow/IPFIX exporter) must capture traffic on VLAN sub-interfaces; integration test with tagged interfaces
- [#4781](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4781) [other, 2021, 0/1] **Top Talkers Tab: Lookup Hostnames Checkbox** -- w1 ADOPT: 'resolve hostnames' toggle available on every address-bearing live view (top talkers, states, log) via shared reverse-lookup/device-registry service
- [#4782](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4782) [feature, 2021, 0/1] **GUI updates for OPNsense - Reporting - Insight - Details window** -- w0 ADOPT: flow-report filter controls are generated from the same column definitions as the table (same order, same labels).
- [#4831](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4831) [bug, 2021, 0/1] **The filesystem full error makes flowd_aggregate die while there is a lot of space left** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: flow aggregation is a supervised systemd service with restart=on-failure; sqlite 'disk full' means tmpfs/inode limits -> store in proper state dir, alert on failure

**Firmware / upgrades / installer / kernel (4)**

- [#4128](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4128) [bug, 2020, 0/17] **No traffic after update to 20.1.7** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: NixOS generations: boot menu rollback to previous generation works offline (no mirror fetch needed) when an upgrade breaks PPPoE routing.
- [#4289](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4289) [bug, 2020, 0/3] **Fail update 20.7 on XCP-NG** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: atomic generation switch with boot-menu rollback; XCP-NG/Xen guest kernel modules part of the tested image matrix (CI boots qcow on QEMU/Xen).
- [#4417](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4417) [bug, 2020, 0/2] **External SSL Certificate Broken after Update to 2.7.3** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: atomic NixOS-generation upgrade cannot leave the web server with a reverted cert; upgrade progress survives GUI reload via API job status.
- [#3965](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3965) [feature, 2020, 0/1] **proxy support for firmware/plugin updates** -- w2 ADOPT: system-wide outbound proxy setting (http/https/no_proxy) applied to Nix substituters, blocklist/alias fetchers, GeoIP, time sync of upgrade metadata.

**Backups / config (11)**

- [#4570](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4570) [feature, 2021, 0/14] **Config.xml - password not imported from pfSense** -- w2 ADOPT: pfSense/OPNsense config.xml importer as a one-way migration tool: maps fields, resets unmigratable passwords with console prompt, reports unmapped items.
- [#3042](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3042) [feature, 2018, 2/7] **Easy way to revert changes, especially for remote locked-out scenarios** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: transactional apply with 'commit-confirmed' timer (auto-rollback if not confirmed within N min) + config history; candidate vs running config built in.
- [#4275](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4275) [bug, 2020, 0/6] **Restoring a Backup with VLANs makes system unreachable** -- w3 ADOPT: restore on hardware with different NIC names enters an interface-reassignment step (GUI + console) before apply; interfaces use stable IDs, VLAN parents remap.
- [#4337](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4337) [bug, 2020, 0/4] **nextcloud backup error** -- w1 ADOPT: Nextcloud/WebDAV backup uses plain WebDAV with app-password; 'test' button surfaces HTTP status + server body; no OCS username lookup dependency.
- [#3695](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3695) [feature, 2019, 0/3] **Fallback configuration activated by timer or missing commit.** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: commit-confirmed apply (auto-rollback to previous generation if not confirmed within N min); see #3042.
- [#4105](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4105) [feature, 2020, 0/3] **uniqid for old IPSEC Ph2 entries missing** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: every config object has a UUID enforced by schema; migrations assign ids to legacy objects; no id-less entries possible
- [#4281](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4281) [bug, 2020, 0/3] **Unable to disable "Do not backup RRD data." in 'System: Configuration: Backups'** -- w1 ADOPT: remote backup targets (Nextcloud/WebDAV/S3/git) share one bundle format with explicit include flags (config, metrics history) honored like local download.
- [#4546](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4546) [feature, 2020, 0/3] **Automatic backup with rrd data** -- w1 ADOPT: scheduled remote backups accept the same include options (metrics/RRD-equivalent, DHCP leases, captive portal db) as manual export.
- [#3948](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3948) [bug, 2020, 0/2] **Password for OpenDNS service stored in plaintext in config.xml file** -- w3 ADOPT: schema marks fields as `secret`; secrets are encrypted at rest (age/sops-style key) and redacted in API responses/exports; no field can be plaintext by oversight.
- [#3872](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3872) [feature, 2020, 0/1] **System: Configuration: Defaults: allow reboot as well as shutdown + confirmation dialog** -- w1 ADOPT: factory-reset action offers 'reset and reboot' vs 'reset and power off' with confirmation; API action with explicit parameter
- [#4333](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4333) [bug, 2020, 0/1] **password-less Google Drive Restore won't work.** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: remote backup requires an encryption passphrase (or explicit 'unencrypted' choice); restore detects encryption from bundle header, never silently encrypts.

**System / services / hardware (13)**

- [#4785](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4785) [support, 2021, 0/12] **CPU Load - Opnsense/BSD doesn't shows.** -- w1 SUPPORT: hidden CPU load from NIC interrupts -> ADOPT: per-CPU softirq/IRQ and per-NIC queue stats in system health; RSS/RPS guidance for VF/SR-IOV NICs
- [#4003](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4003) [support, 2020, 0/7] **High CPU usage when PC downloading** -- w1 SUPPORT: high interrupt CPU under download on i386 box -> ADOPT: dashboard shows softirq/IRQ breakdown per NIC queue, RSS/RPS tuning and offload status so users can diagnose
- [#4437](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4437) [bug, 2020, 0/7] **APU's do not restart after a power fail (v 12.7) but 19.1 is ok** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: NixOS-style read-only root + journaled fs (ext4/btrfs/ZFS) and tmpfs for volatile state; power-loss boots cleanly without fsck prompts; test by hard power cycle
- [#4646](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4646) [feature, 2021, 3/2] **Watchdog Based on Monit** -- w3 DESIGN-AVOIDS: every enabled service is a systemd unit with Restart=on-failure + health check; dashboard shows restart counts; no separate monit layer
- [#3681](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3681) [feature, 2019, 0/4] **Adding new interface should restart services bound to "all interfaces"** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: apply-plan computes service dependencies on interface set; adding an interface restarts/reconfigures listeners (Unbound, NTP) bound to 'all'; add test
- [#3397](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3397) [feature, 2019, 0/3] **Feature request: Webserver - Proxy Auto-Config** -- w1 ADOPT: auxiliary HTTP endpoints (PAC/WPAD, captive portal, ACME) served on separate listener/port from the management API, independently bound per interface
- [#4144](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4144) [support, 2020, 0/2] **NTP: serial GPS does not get initialized?** -- w0 SUPPORT: GPS NMEA init string vs baud mismatch -> ADOPT: NTP GPS refclock config validates that init string baud equals serial baud; or use chrony+gpsd with SHM
- [#4256](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4256) [support, 2020, 0/2] **Opnsense stop working after vmware snapshot,need to reboot** -- w1 SUPPORT: vmxnet3 broken after VMware memory snapshot -> TEST: virt smoke tests (vmxnet3, virtio) incl. snapshot/resume; interface watchdog bounces stuck NICs
- [#5174](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5174) [other, 2021, 0/2] **serialspeed 15000000 support** -- w1 ADOPT: console/serial speed configurable incl. 1500000 baud (Rockchip RK3399 boards like NanoPi R4S); arm64 images first-class
- [#3833](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3833) [feature, 2019, 0/1] **Change defaults for SSH Listen Interfaces** -- w2 ADOPT: SSH defaults: disabled; when enabled default listen = LAN-class interfaces only, key-only auth, no root password login.
- [#4686](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4686) [support, 2021, 0/1] **Reboot cycle when filesystem repair is required** -- w2 SUPPORT: fsck reboot loop after crashes -> DESIGN-AVOIDS: journaled/COW fs with automatic repair at boot, pstore crash logs, no manual fsck prompts on console
- [#4824](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4824) [bug, 2021, 0/1] **Bootup recovery seems to be over enthusiastic sometimes** -- w0 N/A-LINUX: FreeBSD UFS boot recovery of /etc files; NixOS immutable store + declarative users means no 'recover base' step exists.
- [#5167](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5167) [feature, 2021, 0/1] **Allow for CPU affinity configurations to limit the # of cores assigned to non-routing/firewall tasks** -- w1 ADOPT: CPU affinity/cgroup CPU weights: pin dataplane IRQs/Suricata to chosen cores and limit auxiliary services (metrics, flow aggregation) via systemd slices

**Plugins ecosystem (13)**

- [#4873](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4873) [support, 2021, 0/9] **Proxy Client can't reach website as CA is unknown (too new ??? )** -- w0 OUT-OF-SCOPE: Squid missing intermediate CA (AIA fetching); proxy plugin. Core lesson: ship up-to-date system CA bundle via NixOS cacert.
- [#4979](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4979) [bug, 2021, 0/7] **Squid proxy blacklistS (Remote Access Control Lists)** -- w0 OUT-OF-SCOPE: squid remote ACL blacklist fetching (proxy plugin)
- [#5315](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5315) [bug, 2021, 0/5] **rc.reload_all causes boots to be stuck when used with ZeroTier** -- w2 DESIGN-AVOIDS: services are systemd units with dependency ordering and timeouts; no global reload_all that blocks boot on a plugin; boot watchdog test
- [#3710](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3710) [feature, 2019, 1/3] **Add an option to proxy to select the outgoing interface** -- w1 OUT-OF-SCOPE: proxy plugin outbound-interface option; but core must let locally-originated traffic (proxy) be matched by firewall rules (nft output chain with uid/cgroup match)
- [#5282](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5282) [other, 2021, 0/4] **I need pdo library of sqlite3 for PHP** -- w0 N/A-LINUX: PHP PDO sqlite; sensr plugins are Rust/Nix modules with a documented storage API.
- [#4034](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4034) [bug, 2020, 0/3] **fetchACLs.py return http code 406** -- w1 ADOPT: all outbound HTTP fetchers (alias URLs, blocklists, GeoIP) send a sane 'sensr/<ver>' User-Agent and surface HTTP status errors per source.
- [#4464](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4464) [support, 2020, 0/3] **Proxy Transparente ssl man in the middle** -- w0 OUT-OF-SCOPE: Squid SSL-bump issuer-chain errors; proxy plugin territory.
- [#3884](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3884) [feature, 2020, 0/2] **Aggregate squid localnet src ips** -- w1 DESIGN-AVOIDS: 'local networks' helper returns aggregated/deduplicated CIDRs (ipnet merge) for any consumer (proxy, Unbound ACL, portal).
- [#4350](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4350) [bug, 2020, 0/2] **Transparent proxy - issues with  cyrillic text in websites** -- w0 OUT-OF-SCOPE: squid transparent proxy charset handling (proxy plugin territory)
- [#4463](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4463) [support, 2020, 0/2] **Proxy Transparente ssl man in the middle  + Windows Update** -- w0 OUT-OF-SCOPE: Squid SSL inspection vs Windows Update (CRL/AIA fetch denied); proxy plugin territory.
- [#2812](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/2812) [feature, 2018, 0/1] **PAC: make default proxy configurable instead of direct** -- w0 OUT-OF-SCOPE: PAC file generator belongs to a proxy plugin; if provided, default-proxy selection is a required option
- [#3394](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3394) [feature, 2019, 0/1] **Feature request: Squid - SSL-Filtering per Interface** -- w0 OUT-OF-SCOPE: Squid SSL-bump per interface is a proxy-plugin concern; core is not a web proxy.
- [#4412](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4412) [feature, 2020, 0/1] **Allowing Squid Proxy to Serve Apple iOS Updates as well** -- w0 OUT-OF-SCOPE: Squid cache tuning for iOS updates; proxy plugin.

- [#5440](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5440) [support, 2021, 0/6] **Entire router stops responding periodically** -- w1 SUPPORT: periodic hard hang on specific hardware (NIC/driver) -> ADOPT: hardware watchdog (systemd watchdog + pstore crash logs) to diagnose and auto-recover

- [#3757](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3757) [support, 2019, 0/3] **Dmesg spammed with promiscuous mode disabled and enabled** -- w0 SUPPORT: hardware instability misread as promisc-mode log spam -> UX: expose promisc toggles as a single structured log event; health page flags repeated hangs.
- [#4624](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4624) [other, 2021, 1/1] **Consider using https://reuse.software/ to reduce the license header in most files** -- w1 ADOPT: SPDX-License-Identifier headers + REUSE-compliant repo layout from day one (MIT/Apache-2.0 dual).

## 4. Most-wanted feature requests (top 100 by reactions + comments, any state)

"Shipped?" is derived from state_reason plus the last comments on the issue (fetched separately): **yes** = closed completed with a fix/feature merged; **yes (vN)** where a version was named; **partial**; **no (declined)** = not_planned; **no (timed out)** = closed by the inactivity bot; **open**. Hand-verified notes override the heuristic where the thread shows the real outcome.

### 4.1 Reading the most-wanted list

- **IPv6 dominates the top of the list.** Of the 15 most-discussed feature requests, eight are IPv6 (tracking the WAN itself in DHCPv6 mode #5630, prefix lifetimes #9521, NPTv6 with tracked prefixes #5284/#6158, SLAAC track-interface #2394, 6rd #1501, RENEW/REBIND handling #6522, multi-PD IAID #7647). OPNsense shipped all of them eventually, but each took 1-4 years and several follow-up bug threads (see section 5, class "IPv6 PD fragility"). sensr must treat the PD lifecycle as a first-class state machine from day one, not as scripts around a client daemon.
- **Default-gateway and multi-WAN semantics** (#2279, #6803, #6231, #7163, #7335) are the second cluster. OPNsense shipped gateway-group default switching (19.1.8) and priority-based defaults (24.1) but declined multiple monitor IPs per gateway (#7163).
- **The Kea -> Unbound lease registration gap** (#7475 rx25, #7307 rx22) is the highest-reaction unmet request in the whole tracker. OPNsense's position: Kea is not meant to feed Unbound; use dnsmasq (25.1 "dnsmasq as DHCP + DNS" direction) or a Business Edition authoritative DNS plugin. sensr: Kea lease hooks -> Unbound local-data (A/AAAA/PTR) must work out of the box.
- **Login 2FA field** (#6310 rx18 closed, #8239 rx8 open) is the highest-reaction GUI request and still unresolved after three years; a community client-side plugin exists.
- **WAN/PPPoE following CARP** (#7333 rx11 closed, #10936 c36 open, #3185 shipped for PPPoE connect/disconnect) shows HA with a single public IP or dialup WAN is a long-standing weak spot.
- **Hidden auto-rules** (#7127 c38, #7783 rx7) were declined firmly and the thread locked; this is a philosophical difference sensr can exploit.
- Declined for scope reasons but worth having in sensr: FIDO2/WebAuthn (#3935), user/group-based rules (#617), scheduled updates (#1798), MAP-T/MAP-E (#4983), RFC1918 default alias (#4949), remote groups (#5303), dnsmasq multi-tag hosts (#9067), floating rule on single interface in the new rules UI (#9652), OpenVPN `port-share` and other instance options (#6758), OpenVPN credentials from management interface (#7366).
- Shipped-but-partial and worth doing right: fq_codel/CAKE SQM (#505; CAKE impossible on FreeBSD, trivial on Linux), NAT64/DNS64 (#167; plugin only), CSR signing with SANs (#1850), L7 DPI (#1887), reply-to semantics (#3952).

### 4.2 Shipped-status summary for the top 100

Counted from the table above: **shipped** 62, **n/a or dropped** 11, **partial** 9, **declined** 8, **timed out** 6, **open** 4 (of 100). 'n/a or dropped' covers support tickets mislabelled as features, meta tickets and items dropped as technically impossible.

Everything in the "no" and "partial" rows is listed again in section 6 where it matters for sensr.


## 5. Recurring bug classes in closed bugs


Base: 3,326 closed issues classified as bugs (927 carry the `bug` label; the rest are label-less or `support`/`incomplete` reports whose template or title describes a defect). Classes were built with title-first regular expressions (`bugclasses2.py`); an issue may belong to several classes; counts are approximate and biased toward precision. "Examples" are the most-discussed issues in the class.

| class | issues | of which `bug`-labelled | trend |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| GUI/grid/form defects | 491 | 159 | rising with MVC/bootgrid migration (69 in 2025, 68 in 2026) |
| DNS resolver (Unbound/dnsmasq) start/registration/override | 324 | 85 | rising (54 in 2025) |
| Interface reconfigure races / boot ordering / "works after manual restart" | 266 | 67 | flat ~25/yr for 10 years |
| Upgrade regressions ("since X.Y") | 216 | 52 | rising (38 in 2025, 32 in 2026) |
| Service dead or stale (not restarted, crash loop, 100% CPU) | 179 | 32 | rising |
| DHCP lease / static map / Kea config | 172 | 45 | spike after Kea introduction (33 in 2024, 35 in 2026) |
| Gateway monitoring / default route / multi-WAN | 168 | 46 | flat |
| IPv6 PD / RA / track-interface fragility | 161 | 45 | flat-rising, 23/yr |
| Auth / session / LDAP / privilege | 160 | 45 | rising |
| PHP runtime errors (fatal, undefined index, white page) | 137 | 47 | persistent |
| Certificate / TLS | 135 | 40 | |
| API / MVC / configd inconsistencies | 131 | 58 | rising with API adoption |
| Alias / table resolution (URL tables, GeoIP, nested) | 111 | 30 | |
| Boot / installer / ZFS / console | 104 | 19 | |
| Hardware / driver (NIC, SFP, wireless, hypervisor) | 99 | 13 | mostly FreeBSD-upstream |
| MTU / MSS / PPPoE | 96 | 18 | |
| Performance / throughput | 95 | 22 | |
| HA / CARP / XMLRPC sync | 84 | 19 | |
| Backup / restore | 68 | 20 | |
| pf reload drops states / `reply-to` / sticky | 51 | 12 | spike in 26.1 (12 in 2026) |
| GUI/backend validation mismatch (explicit) | 49 | 25 | 15 in 2026 |
| Config migration breakage | 43 | 16 | 14 in 2026 (MVC migrations of rules/VIPs/aliases) |
| Log rotation / disk full | 20 | 3 | |

Below: for each class that carries an architectural lesson, what breaks in OPNsense, why, sensr's mitigation and the regression test. Classes that are purely FreeBSD-driver or hardware-specific are summarised briefly.

### 5.1 Config migration breakage (43 explicit; many more hide in "upgrade regressions")

**What breaks.** Each OPNsense release migrates parts of `config.xml` from legacy (PHP arrays) to MVC models. Migrations drop or mangle data: WireGuard peer allowed-IPs lost on 23.7.6 ([#6934](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6934)), Unbound overrides cleared on 22.1.5 ([#5693](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5693)), VIP MVC UI broken for certain setups ([#6422](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6422)), static aliases empty after 26.7.1 ([#10596](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10596)), Unbound ACL default flipped from allow to deny on upgrade ([#10155](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10155)), legacy DHCP prefix merge ([#7046](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7046)), log settings reset to RAM disk on 22.7 wiping logs ([#5924](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5924)), schedules not applied in the new rules UI ([#9690](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9690)), IPv6 no-release migration breaking LAGG ([#9681](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9681)), cron command removal silently mapping to another command ([#7197](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7197), [#10317](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10317)).

**Why.** Schema is implicit (XML + PHP code paths), migrations are ad-hoc PHP run at boot after the upgrade, untested against real-world configs, and there is no "diff of what the migration changed".

**sensr mitigation.** Versioned typed schema; every schema change ships a migration function; CI runs all migrations against a corpus of fixture configs (one per prior release, including exotic setups: PPPoE+VLAN, HA pair, PD-only WAN, multi-WAN with VPN gateways) and asserts a semantic diff of zero except the intended change. Migration output is stored as a new config generation with a human-readable diff shown after upgrade. Unknown fields are preserved, not dropped.

**Regression test.** `migrations::corpus` test: for each fixture `vN.toml`, migrate to HEAD, render all backends (nftables, Kea, Unbound, strongSwan, networkd), compare to golden output; fail on any dropped object count (rules, aliases, overrides, peers, reservations). Plus property test: `migrate(serialize(x)) == x` for random configs of the current version.

### 5.2 Upgrade regressions (216)

**What breaks.** Behaviour changes with a point release: dhclient-script regression 19.1 ([#3197](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3197)), WAN MTU set to 576 on 19.1 ([#3173](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3173)), Unbound stops at boot after 22.7 ([#6028](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6028)), ISC DHCPv4 stops registering leases in Unbound 24.7.8 ([#8075](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8075), 40 comments; also [#7376](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7376)), VLAN traffic not reaching VLAN interfaces after 26.7.3 ([#10810](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10810)), alias rules no longer matching after 25.7.3 ([#9198](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9198)), HAProxy sync parse error ([#4533](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4533), 94 comments), freeradius package stuck mid-update ([#6167](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6167)), mixed-ABI repo state on major upgrade ([#10622](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10622)), DNAT auto-rules failing in multi-gateway setups after 26.1 ([#9702](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9702), 55 comments).

**Why.** No end-to-end tests on real network scenarios before release; pkg-based upgrades can half-apply; config regeneration differs between "fresh save" and "boot after upgrade".

**sensr mitigation.** NixOS generations: the complete closure is downloaded and verified before activation; activation is atomic; a post-boot health check (API reachable on management interface, configured interfaces present with addresses, enabled units active) auto-rolls back to the previous generation on failure. A release gate runs the scenario e2e suite (below) in VMs. Rendering is a pure function of config so boot-after-upgrade and save produce identical backend files.

**Regression test.** CI "upgrade lab": boot previous release image with each fixture config in a VM/netns lab (WAN DHCP, PPPoE, PD, VLANs, multi-WAN, HA pair), upgrade to the candidate, assert: ping through NAT, DNS resolution, DHCP lease on LAN, VPN tunnel up, management reachable; then verify rollback by injecting a failing health check.

### 5.3 IPv6 PD fragility (161)

**What breaks.** radvd stops announcing after a while ([#4338](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4338), 219 comments, 8 reactions, the single most-discussed bug), radvd not advertising GUA after reboot/prefix change ([#6637](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6637)), track-interface sometimes fails after PPPoE reconnect ([#6223](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6223)), IP alias on tracking LAN breaks tracking ([#3310](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3310), 92 comments), multiple dhcp6c/dhcpd6 instances ([#6263](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6263)), dhcp6c does not pick up routes ([#1668](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/1668)), WAN_DHCP6 gateway in multi-WAN ([#3604](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3604)), empty dhcp6c.conf on PPPoE ([#9153](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9153)), IA_PD renewal failing hourly ([#9546](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9546)), clients losing IPv6 after nightly PPPoE reconnect because old prefix is not deprecated (RFC 9096, [#9261](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9261)), stale leases for old prefix ([#5735](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5735)), /62 prefix generating a /62 RA ([#9523](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9523)), PD-only WAN without IA_NA not routing ([#9853](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9853)), DS-Lite not coming up after reboot ([#7713](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7713), 83 comments, open).

**Why.** PD handling is distributed over dhcp6c hook scripts, `rc.newwanip`/`rc.newwanipv6`, PHP interface code, radvd config regeneration and dhcpd6 regeneration, each with its own idea of the current prefix and no shared state machine; the client (WIDE dhcp6c) is old and its lifetimes were ignored until 26.1.

**sensr mitigation.** A single PD state machine owned by the network daemon: per WAN `DelegatedPrefix { prefix, preferred_until, valid_until, iaid }` persisted across reboot; all consumers (track-interface addresses, RA, Kea v6 subnets, NPTv6, firewall sets, Suricata HOME_NET, prefix-relative reservations, dynamic-prefix aliases) are derived views re-rendered in one reconcile on change. Prefix change performs deprecate (lifetime 0 for the old prefix for a configurable period, RFC 7084 L-13 / RFC 9096) then remove. One RA daemon, one DHCPv6 server, supervised by systemd. Odd prefix lengths (/62, /60, /56, /48) always carve /64s with a validated subnet-id. PD-only WAN supported.

**Regression test.** netns lab with a scripted upstream DHCPv6-PD server: (1) PPPoE reconnect with a new prefix: LAN clients see old prefix deprecated and the new one within one RA interval; Kea v6 subnets and nft sets updated; no stale addresses after valid lifetime. (2) Reboot: PD restored from persistence and renewed, LAN reachable within N seconds. (3) 72-hour soak with T1 renewals: no address loss, RA still periodic. (4) /62 and /56 delegations carve /64s. (5) PD-only (no IA_NA) WAN routes outbound v6.

### 5.4 Interface reconfigure races and boot ordering (266)

**What breaks.** Services started before their interface has an address: Unbound not starting at boot ([#6028](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6028), [#7684](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7684), [#7982](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7982)), dnsmasq failing at boot ([#8895](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8895)), interfaces randomly not coming up at boot ([#10403](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10403)), bridge membership racing VLAN creation ([#10820](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10820)), PPPoE over VLAN not dialled at boot ([#11069](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/11069)), WAN never gets IPv4 after reboot because `rc.newwanip` looked up the wrong device ([#10877](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10877)), static route to VTI not configured after reboot ([#3414](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3414), 84 comments), `rc.newwanip` kills CARP on every other call ([#2486](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/2486)), no DNS for 10 minutes on interface state change ([#7426](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7426)), DHCP renew to the same IP restarting OpenVPN/Unbound and dropping routes ([#6816](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6816), [#7191](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7191), [#8020](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8020), [#10988](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10988)), disabling an interface not removing routes ([#6134](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6134)), multiple services failing to auto-start after reboot on 26.7.2 ([#10723](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10723)).

**Why.** Event handling is shell/PHP hook scripts (`rc.newwanip`, `rc.linkup`, devd) that re-run whole "configure" functions with no dependency graph, no idempotence check and no locking; service start order is rc.d-sequential without readiness.

**sensr mitigation.** Declarative network state applied by a planner that orders netlink operations by dependency (physical -> VLAN -> bond/bridge -> PPPoE -> addresses -> routes -> tunnels); services are systemd units with `After=`/`BindsTo=` on the device/address they need and `Restart=on-failure`; address/route events arrive via netlink into a single reconciler that computes a diff and only restarts consumers whose inputs changed (DHCP renew with identical lease = no-op). No shell hooks.

**Regression test.** Cold-boot e2e for each topology fixture (PPPoE-over-VLAN WAN, bridge of VLANs, LAGG+VLANs, WireGuard with bound gateway): all enabled units active within 60 s, LAN clients resolve/route, repeat 20 times to catch order races. Event test: simulate DHCP renew with same lease -> assert zero restarts and zero route churn (journal and `ip monitor` captured); simulate renew with changed gateway -> exactly the dependent services reload.

### 5.5 pf reload drops states / return-path semantics (51, plus the 168 gateway-monitoring issues)

**What breaks.** Applying rules or alias updates interrupts traffic ([#5788](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5788), [#5381](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5381), [#780](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/780)); failover kills states even when no viable target exists ([#9789](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9789)); `reply-to` surprises: multi-WAN gateways not respecting reply-to in 26.1.8 ([#9806](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9806), 96 comments), "disable reply-to" not working ([#9785](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9785)), sticky connections dropping ([#5869](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5869)), pfsync + multiqueue dropping TCP ([#8059](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8059)), `update_tables.py` failing silently ([#4266](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4266)), captive-portal idle timeout acting as hard timeout ([#9902](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9902)).

**Why.** pf has one ruleset; a reload re-evaluates everything, and policy routing is encoded inside rules (`route-to`/`reply-to`), so routing changes force rule reloads and vice versa; errors from `pfctl` were swallowed.

**sensr mitigation.** nftables atomic ruleset replacement (`nft -f` with flush inside one transaction, validated with `nft -c` in the plan step) never drops conntrack entries; alias updates touch only named sets (`nft add element`), never the ruleset. Policy routing lives in the routing layer (fwmark + `ip rule` tables), reply routing via conntrack mark saved at ingress, so gateway state changes rewrite routing tables only and never reload rules. State killing on gateway change is explicit and scoped (flush conntrack entries by mark/oif of the failed WAN only, and only if a viable alternative exists). Every backend command's stderr is captured and surfaces as an apply error.

**Regression test.** Throughput/stateful test in the lab: long-lived TCP flow + UDP stream across the firewall while (a) applying 50 rule changes, (b) refreshing a 100k-entry URL alias, (c) failing and restoring WAN1 in a two-WAN group; assert zero TCP resets and bounded packet loss; assert replies to inbound connections on WAN2 leave via WAN2 (pcap on both WANs).

### 5.6 GUI/backend validation mismatch and silent acceptance (49 explicit, plus ~140 PHP runtime errors and much of the 491 GUI class)

**What breaks.** The GUI accepts input the backend cannot use or renders invalid daemon configs: protocol=any generating invalid pf syntax silently dropping the rule ([#10783](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10783)), DNAT with IPv4+IPv6 breaking the whole ruleset at boot ([#10755](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10755)), Kea pool outside subnet accepted ([#11126](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/11126)), duplicate Kea reservation stopping Kea ([#7477](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7477)), malformed MAC ([#7765](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7765)), Kea `subnet4.id=0` rejected by Kea 3 ([#10714](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10714)), lease hostnames with spaces/invalid labels breaking Unbound ([#5550](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5550), [#9758](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9758)), wildcard override preventing Unbound start ([#4049](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4049), [#8051](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8051)), radvd.conf syntax error on RDNSS ([#6819](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6819)), whitespace not trimmed ([#2217](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/2217)), deleting a referenced cert crashing a page ([#6356](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6356)), deleting an interface group with rules ([#8773](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8773)), LDAP sync deleting all users when the server is unreachable ([#10129](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10129)), PHP fatal/undefined-index white pages ([#3231](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3231), [#6969](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6969), [#9664](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9664)).

**Why.** Validation is duplicated between JS, PHP form code and the model XML; backends are rendered from templates with string interpolation and started without a syntax check; references are by name/index without integrity.

**sensr mitigation.** One schema with typed newtypes (`MacAddr`, `DnsLabel`, `Cidr`, `PortRange`, `Protocol` enum), cross-object validators (pool within subnet, no overlapping subnets, reservations unique) and referential integrity (UUID references; delete refused when referenced, with the list of dependants). The GUI form is generated from the schema so it cannot accept what the model rejects. Every rendered backend config is dry-run checked by its own tool (`nft -c`, `kea-dhcp4 -t`, `unbound-checkconf`, `swanctl --load-all` on a test socket, `openvpn --config --test`) before the transaction commits; a failing dry-run aborts the whole apply with the daemon's message attached to the offending object.

**Regression test.** Fuzz/property tests over the schema: for random valid configs, every renderer output passes its dry-run checker (`proptest` + the real checkers in CI). Golden tests for every historically broken case above. API contract test: every mutation with invalid input returns 4xx with a field-addressed error, never 200.

### 5.7 Service dead or stale (179) and DNS resolver start/registration (324)

**What breaks.** radvd stops announcing ([#4338](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4338)), Unbound not honoring outgoing interfaces ([#5329](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5329), 76 comments), Unbound crashing on WAN IP refresh ([#4270](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4270)) or when internet is lost ([#9322](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9322)), overrides not resolving until restart ([#9587](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9587)), apply leaving stale runtime files ([#10144](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10144)), blocklist download failure unloading old list ([#6866](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6866)), DoT not using port 853 ([#9830](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9830)), dpinger not recovering after PPPoE recycle ([#4149](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4149)), gateway monitoring dying randomly ([#7027](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7027), 84 comments, open), dhcrelay at 100% CPU ([#7471](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7471)), OpenVPN status wrong ([#3223](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3223)), Unbound restart cutting management access ([#10026](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10026)), DHCP->Unbound registration intermittently stopping ([#3478](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3478), [#8075](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8075)).

**sensr mitigation.** All daemons supervised (`Restart=on-failure`, watchdog where supported), health exposed as metrics and alerts; Unbound bound with `interface-automatic`/wildcard listen plus `ip_freebind` so address churn needs no restart; overrides and lease registrations pushed via `unbound-control local_data` (no restart); blocklist compile is transactional (keep last-good); gateway health checker is an in-process supervised task with its own metrics; lease registration is a tested Kea hook -> resolver path.

**Regression test.** Chaos suite: toggle WAN carrier, change WAN address, kill each daemon once; assert recovery within SLA and that unrelated services never restart (journal diff). Lease registration e2e: new lease resolvable within 1 s; expiry removes the record.

### 5.8 Multi-WAN and gateway monitoring (168)

**What breaks.** Failover fails on cable unplug ([#4160](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4160)), monitor route not re-added ([#5956](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5956)), default gateway chosen although not upstream ([#3966](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3966)), editing auto-generated gateways breaks routing ([#7224](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7224)), failover to fallback fails with dual stack ([#7335](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7335)), no failback ([#9316](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9316)), forced-gateway rules falling back to default when gateway down, a security issue ([#8189](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8189)), same gateway IP on two WANs unsupported ([#6576](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6576)), DHCP renew on backup WAN failing for lack of route ([#9889](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9889)), WireGuard handshake broken by missing route ([#7364](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7364), 55 comments).

**sensr mitigation.** Per-WAN routing table, interface-bound probes (multiple targets, ICMP/TCP/HTTP, quorum policy), DHCP/PPPoE clients bind to their device; gateway groups compile to fwmark tables; "drop if down" option for policy rules; explicit failback with optional conntrack flush; duplicate upstream IPs are fine by construction.

**Regression test.** Two-WAN lab (one DHCP, one PPPoE) with gateway group tiers: unplug WAN1 (carrier), blackhole WAN1 (carrier up, no replies), restore; assert switch and failback times, policy-routed rule with "drop if down" never leaks via WAN2, backup WAN DHCP renew succeeds while WAN1 is default.

### 5.9 Aliases / tables (111)

**What breaks.** URL table updates failing silently or via cron ([#10324](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10324), [#2042](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/2042)), GeoIP download broken ([#9374](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9374)), port aliases not working after update ([#7017](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7017)), large tables exceeding pf table size ([#4669](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4669)), BGP/URL aliases empty after restore ([#7075](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7075)), nested/host alias resolution gaps ([#2590](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/2590), [#6678](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6678)).

**sensr mitigation.** Aliases compile to named nft sets with explicit type (ipv4_addr/ipv6_addr/inet_service, interval flag); refresh jobs are supervised tasks with per-alias last-success/last-error status in API and GUI; failed downloads keep the last-good set; restore from backup triggers an immediate refresh; set sizes are unbounded by default.

**Regression test.** Alias e2e: URL table from a local HTTP fixture, host alias with multiple A/AAAA, nested alias with exclusion; verify set membership after apply, after fixture change + refresh, after HTTP failure (membership unchanged, error visible), after restore from backup.

### 5.10 HA / CARP sync (84)

**What breaks.** XMLRPC sync parse errors ([#4533](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/4533)), split-brain on link flap / disabled preemption ([#2780](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/2780), [#1549](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/1549)), maintenance mode no-op ([#5930](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5930)), IPv6 CARP both master ([#3411](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/3411)), pfsync corrupting standby via interface index mismatch ([#8971](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8971)), backup node not stopping services ([#5200](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5200)), WireGuard/IPsec/OpenVPN unaware of CARP ([#7013](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7013), [#6473](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6473)), radvd split-brain ([#9873](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9873)).

**sensr mitigation.** VRRPv3 via keepalived with interface and script tracking; config replication is a typed API transaction of UUID-keyed objects with node-local fields excluded; services declare `ha_placement = master_only | both`; RA and VPN follow VRRP notify hooks; conntrackd for state sync; maintenance mode is an explicit priority demotion visible in API.

**Regression test.** Two-node lab: fail master (power, link, demote), assert backup takes VIPs, RA, DHCP, VPN within N seconds, no dual-master; replicate a config change on master and assert backup renders identical backend configs minus node-local fields.

### 5.11 Logging / disk full (20) and performance of reporting (95 performance issues, several caused by logging)

**What breaks.** flowd.log not rotating ([#5469](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/5469)), Suricata EVE filling disks ([#7200](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7200), [#9385](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9385)), dashboard sluggish with disk logging ([#7829](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7829)), reporting queries dropping throughput ([#8272](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/8272)), log size unit wrong ([#7397](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7397)), hostwatch log flood ([#9624](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9624)).

**sensr mitigation.** journald with `SystemMaxUse`/`SystemKeepFree`; flow and alert stores are size-bounded databases with retention; a disk-free floor disables file sinks and alerts before root fills; collectors run in a low-priority cgroup off the dataplane.

**Regression test.** Fill test: generate 10x normal log/flow volume for an hour on a small disk; assert free space never drops below the floor and the dataplane throughput stays within 5%.

### 5.12 Certificates / TLS (135), auth (160), backup/restore (68), API (131)

Lessons captured as items in sections 2/3/6: CRL changes must propagate to all consumers ([#7935](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7935)); CA chains included in exports ([#1487](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/1487)); deletion of referenced certs refused; LDAP sync must be fail-safe ([#10129](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10129)); OTP QR encoding ([#9070](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9070)); anti-lockout must bind to the management interface, never WAN ([#7372](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7372)); restore must re-trigger derived state (alias downloads, nextuid, firewall groups [#9866](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9866)); API errors must be real HTTP errors ([#10418](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10418)); every endpoint has an ACL ([#10351](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10351)); secrets never serialized ([#10115](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10115)); concurrent edits detected ([#6586](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/6586), [#7954](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/7954)).

### 5.13 FreeBSD-specific classes sensr sidesteps (not free of risk on Linux)

Hardware/driver (99: `ix` no carrier, Realtek+netmap panics, Denverton, SFP renumbering [#10730](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/10730)), boot/installer/ZFS (104), MTU/MSS/PPPoE (96: RFC4638 baby jumbo, MSS clamping per address family [#9398](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9398), PMTUD regression [#9225](https://github.com/opnsense/core/issues/9225)), and PHP runtime errors (137) do not transfer as such. The transferable lessons: stable hardware-bound interface naming (never probe order), MSS clamp derived per family from MTU (`tcp option maxseg size set rt mtu`), PPPoE MTU 1500 via RFC4638 when the parent supports it, serial console persisted everywhere, unattended installer with answer file, and a kernel/driver matrix tested in CI on the supported appliance set.


## 6. Prioritized: 40 most impactful items for sensr


Ranking criteria: (1) how many OPNsense issues the item would have prevented or satisfied (counts from sections 3 and 5), (2) engagement on the underlying issues, (3) whether OPNsense declined or cannot do it on FreeBSD (differentiation), (4) architectural leverage (must be decided before code exists). IDs are stable; later documents reference them as `ISS-C-NNN`.

| ID | priority | item | evidence (opnsense/core) | sensr requirement |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| ISS-C-001 | P0 | **Transactional whole-config apply with dry-run validation of every rendered backend** | 491 GUI-defect + 49 validation + 137 PHP-error + 43 migration bugs; #10783, #10755, #11126, #7477, #10714, #5550, #4049, #6633, #10144, #10906 | validate -> render all backend files -> `nft -c`/`kea -t`/`unbound-checkconf`/etc. -> atomic swap -> rollback; one object can never break the ruleset or stop a daemon |
| ISS-C-002 | P0 | **Single PD/IPv6 prefix state machine** with persistence and derived consumers | 161 IPv6-PD bugs; #4338 (219c), #6637, #6223, #3310, #6263, #9261, #9523, #9853, #5735, #11028, #10598, #7713 | prefix lifetimes tracked; RA, Kea6, track-interface, NPTv6, nft sets, HOME_NET, prefix-relative reservations re-rendered in one reconcile; RFC 9096 deprecation; /62-/48 carving; PD-only WAN |
| ISS-C-003 | P0 | **Dependency-ordered network bring-up and event reconciler** (no shell hooks) | 266 race/boot bugs; #10403, #10820, #11069, #10877, #3414, #2486, #7426, #10723, #8895, #7684 | planner orders netlink ops; systemd units bound to devices/addresses; DHCP renew with same lease is a no-op (#6816/#7191/#8020/#10988) |
| ISS-C-004 | P0 | **Versioned schema + CI-tested migrations against a fixture corpus** | 43 migration + 216 upgrade regressions; #6934, #5693, #10596, #10155, #6422, #7046, #9690, #7197 | every schema change ships a migration; corpus of real configs per prior version; semantic diff gate; unknown fields preserved |
| ISS-C-005 | P0 | **Atomic upgrades with health-check rollback** (NixOS generations) | #6167, #7275, #6127, #10622, #6959, #3173, #10810; scheduled updates declined (#1798 -> BE) | closure verified before switch; post-boot health check; auto-rollback; scheduled/unattended upgrades in core, not an enterprise upsell |

### 6.1 What to decide first (architecture-gating)

ISS-C-001, 002, 003, 004, 006, 007, 017 must be settled before the first line of feature code: they define the config model, the apply pipeline, the network reconciler, the routing design and interface identity. Everything else in the list is a feature that fits inside those decisions. ISS-C-022 (the scenario lab) should exist before the second feature lands; most of the 3,300 closed OPNsense bugs would have been caught by a cold-boot, upgrade or failover scenario test.

### 6.2 What OPNsense will not do that sensr can (differentiators)

Visible/overridable auto-rules (ISS-C-008), Kea -> resolver registration (ISS-C-009), dialup/VPN HA placement (ISS-C-010), multi-target gateway checks (ISS-C-011), dedicated OTP field and FIDO2 (ISS-C-013), scheduled upgrades and unattended install in core (ISS-C-005, 024), CAKE SQM (ISS-C-027), NAT64/MAP-E/DS-Lite (ISS-C-026), floating rule on one interface (ISS-C-034), user-aware policies (ISS-C-035), config importer (ISS-C-039).
